== @PSA

[.book-title]
The Book of Psalms

=== @1

[discrete.major]
==== BOOK I

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 1—41>>)

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Two Paths

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 5:3–12; Luke 6:20–23>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __is__ the man that ##walketh##note:x[<<Prov 4:14–15; 13:20>>] not in the counsel of the ##ungodly##note:tn[or, wicked], nor standeth in the way of sinners, nor ##sitteth##note:x[<<Ps 26:4–5>>] in the seat of the scornful.
@ ##But his##note:x[<<Ps 119:11, 92>>] delight __is__ in the law of the LORD; and in his law doth he ##meditate##note:x[<<Josh 1:8>>] day and night.
@ And he shall be like a ##tree##note:x[<<Jer 17:8; Ezek 47:12>>] planted by the rivers of water, that ##bringeth##note:x[<<Ps 92:14>>] forth his fruit in his season; his leaf also shall not ##wither##note:tn[Heb. fade]; and whatsoever he doeth shall prosper.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ungodly __are__ not so: but __are__ ##like##note:x[<<Job 21:18; Ps 35:5; Matt 3:12>>] the chaff which the wind driveth away.
@ Therefore the ungodly ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 5:5>>] not stand in the judgment, nor ##sinners##note:x[<<Mal 3:18; Matt 25:46>>] in the congregation of the righteous.
@ For the LORD ##knoweth##note:x[<<John 10:14; 2 Tim 2:19>>] the way of the righteous: but the ##way of the ungodly##note:x[<<Prov 15:9>>] shall perish.

=== @2

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Triumphant Messiah

[.parallel]
(<<Acts 4:23–31>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Why##note:x[<<Ps 46:6; Acts 4:25–27>>] do the heathen ##rage##note:tn[or, tumultuously assemble], and the ##people##note:x[<<Rev 17:14>>] ##imagine##note:tn[Heb. meditate] a vain thing?
@ The ##kings##note:x[<<Rev 17:12–14>>] of the earth set themselves, and the ##rulers##note:x[<<Matt 26:59; 27:1>>] take counsel together, against the LORD, and against his anointed, __saying__,
@ ##Let us break their bands asunder, and cast away their cords from us.##note:x[<<Jer 5:5; Luke 19:14, 27>>]
@ He that sitteth in the heavens ##shall laugh##note:x[<<Ps 37:13; 59:8; Prov 1:26>>]: the Lord shall have them in derision.
@ ##Then##note:x[<<Isa 11:4; Rev 19:15>>] shall he speak unto them in his wrath, and ##vex##note:tn[or, trouble] them in his ##sore##note:x[<<Ps 110:5–6>>] displeasure.
@ ##Yet##note:x[<<Ps 45:6; Matt 28:18>>] have I ##set##note:tn[Heb. anointed] my king upon ##my holy hill of Zion##note:x[<<Rev 14:1>>].note:tn[__upon…__: Heb. upon Zion, the hill of my holiness]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will declare the decree: the LORD hath said unto me, ##Thou##note:x[<<Acts 13:33; Heb 1:5; 5:5>>] __art__ my Son; this day have I begotten thee.note:tn[__the decree__: or, for a decree]
@ ##Ask##note:x[<<John 17:4–5>>] of me, ##and I##note:x[<<Ps 22:27; 72:8>>] shall give __thee__ the heathen __for__ thine inheritance, and the uttermost parts of the earth __for__ thy possession.
@ note:x[<<Ps 89:23; Rev 2:26–27; 12:5>>]Thou shalt break them with a rod of iron; thou shalt dash them in pieces like a potter’s vessel.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Be wise##note:x[<<Hos 14:9>>] now therefore, ##O##note:x[<<Ps 45:12; Isa 60:10–11>>] ye kings: be instructed, ye judges of the earth.
@ ##Serve##note:x[<<Heb 12:28–29>>] the LORD with fear, and ##rejoice##note:x[<<Phil 2:12; Heb 12:25>>] with trembling.
@ ##Kiss##note:x[<<John 5:23>>] the Son, lest he be angry, and ye perish __from__ the way, when his wrath is kindled but a little. ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 34:8; 40:4>>] __are__ all they that put their trust in him.

=== @3

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David, when he fled from Absalom his son.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Deliver Me, O LORD!

[.parallel]
(<<2 Samuel 15:13–29>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ LORD, ##how##note:x[<<2 Sam 16:15; 17:11–13>>] are they increased that trouble me! ##many##note:x[<<Ps 17:7>>] __are__ they that rise up against me.
@ Many __there be__ which say of my soul, __There is__ ##no##note:x[<<Ps 42:3, 10; 71:11>>] help for him in God. Selah.
@ But thou, O LORD, __art__ ##a shield##note:x[<<Gen 15:1; Ps 28:7>>] ##for##note:tn[or, about] me; my glory, and ##the##note:x[<<Ps 27:6>>] lifter up of mine head.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I cried##note:x[<<Ps 91:15; Isa 65:24>>] unto the LORD with my voice, and ##he##note:x[<<Ps 34:4>>] heard me out of his holy hill. Selah.
@ ##I laid##note:x[<<Ps 4:8; Prov 3:24>>] me down and slept; I awaked; for ##the##note:x[<<Ps 4:8>>] LORD sustained me.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 27:1–3; 118:10–12; Rom 8:31>>] not be afraid of ten thousands of people, that have set __themselves__ against me round about.
@ Arise, O LORD; save me, O my God: for ##thou##note:x[<<Job 16:10; 29:17; Ps 58:6>>] hast smitten all mine enemies __upon__ the cheek bone; thou hast broken the teeth of the ungodly.
@ ##Salvation##note:x[<<Ps 37:39–40; Isa 43:11; Jonah 2:9>>] __belongeth__ unto the LORD: thy blessing __is__ upon thy people. Selah.

=== @4

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician on Neginoth, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Answer Me When I Call!

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Hear me when I call, O God of my righteousness: ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 40:1–3; 2 Cor 1:10>>] hast enlarged me __when I was__ in distress; ##have mercy upon me##note:x[<<Exod 34:6–7>>], and hear my prayer.note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]note:tn[__have…__: or, be gracious unto me]
@ O ye sons of men, ##how##note:x[<<Prov 1:22>>] long __will ye turn__ ##my glory##note:x[<<Ps 3:3; Hos 4:7>>] into shame? __how long__ will ye love vanity, __and__ seek after leasing? Selah.
@ But know note:x[<<2 Thess 2:13–14>>]that ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 34:15; John 15:16>>] hath set apart him that is godly for himself: the LORD will hear when I call unto him.
@ Stand in awe, and ##sin##note:x[<<Eph 4:26>>] not: ##commune##note:x[<<Ps 63:6; 77:6>>] with your own heart upon your bed, and be still. Selah.
@ ##Offer##note:x[<<Ps 51:19; Heb 13:15–16>>] the sacrifices of righteousness, and ##put##note:x[<<Ps 37:3>>] your trust in the LORD.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ __There be__ many that say, Who will shew us __any__ good? LORD, ##lift##note:x[<<Num 6:26; Ps 80:7, 19>>] thou up the light of thy countenance upon us.
@ Thou hast ##put##note:x[<<Ps 37:4; 63:2–5>>] gladness in my heart, more than in ##the time##note:x[<<Isa 9:3>>] __that__ their corn and their wine increased.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Job 11:18–19; Ps 3:5; Prov 3:24>>] both lay me down in peace, and sleep: for thou, LORD, only makest me dwell in safety.

=== @5

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Nehiloth, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Give Ear to My Words

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Give##note:x[<<Ps 54:2; 1 John 5:14–15>>] ear to my words, O LORD, ##consider my##note:x[<<Ps 19:14>>] meditation.
@ Hearken ##unto the##note:x[<<Ps 3:4>>] voice of my cry, ##my King##note:x[<<Ps 145:1>>], and my God: for ##unto thee##note:x[<<Ps 65:2>>] will I pray.
@ ##My voice shalt thou hear in the morning, O LORD; in the morning will I direct __my prayer__ unto thee, and will look up.##note:x[<<Ps 88:13; 119:147; 130:6>>]
@ For thou __art__ not a ##God##note:x[<<Ps 50:21; Mal 2:17>>] that hath pleasure in wickedness: neither shall ##evil##note:x[<<Heb 12:14>>] dwell with thee.
@ The foolish shall not ##stand##note:x[<<Ps 1:5>>] in thy sight: ##thou##note:x[<<Prov 6:16–19; Hos 9:15>>] hatest all workers of iniquity.note:tn[__in…__: Heb. before thine eyes]
@ Thou shalt ##destroy##note:x[<<Rev 21:8; 22:15>>] them that speak leasing: the LORD will abhor ##the bloody##note:x[<<Ps 55:23>>] and deceitful man.note:tn[__bloody…__: Heb. man of bloods and deceit]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But as for me, I will come __into__ thy house in the multitude of thy mercy: __and__ in thy fear will ##I worship##note:x[<<Ps 132:7; 138:2; Heb 4:16>>] toward thy holy temple.note:tn[__thy holy…__: Heb. the temple of thy holiness]
@ ##Lead##note:x[<<Ps 25:4–5>>] me, O LORD, in thy righteousness because of ##mine##note:x[<<Ps 27:11>>] enemies; ##make##note:x[<<Ps 27:11>>] thy way straight before my face.note:tn[__mine…__: Heb. those which observe me]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Jer 9:3–6; Rom 3:13>>] __there is__ no ##faithfulness##note:tn[or, stedfastness] in their mouth; their inward part __is__ very wickedness; their ##throat##note:x[<<Rom 3:13>>] __is__ an open sepulchre; they flatter with their tongue.note:tn[__their mouth__: Heb. his mouth, that is, the mouth of any of them]note:tn[__very…__: Heb. wickednesses]
@ Destroy thou them, O God; ##let##note:x[<<2 Sam 17:14; Ps 140:9–10>>] them fall by their own counsels; cast them out in the multitude of their transgressions; for ##they##note:x[<<Isa 63:10>>] have rebelled against thee.note:tn[__Destroy…__: or, Make them guilty]note:tn[__by…__: or, from their counsels]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 68:3; Rev 19:1–7>>] let all those that put their trust in thee rejoice: let them ever shout for joy, because thou defendest them: let them also that ##love##note:x[<<Rom 8:28>>] thy name be joyful in thee.note:tn[__defendest…__: Heb. coverest over, or, protectest them]
@ For thou, LORD, ##wilt##note:x[<<Ps 32:10>>] ##bless##note:x[<<Ps 115:13>>] the righteous; with favour wilt thou ##compass##note:tn[Heb. crown] him as __with__ a ##shield##note:x[<<Ps 84:11>>].

=== @6

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician on Neginoth upon Sheminith, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Do Not Rebuke Me in Your Anger

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 38:1–22>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD, ##rebuke##note:x[<<Ps 38:1; Jer 10:24; 46:28>>] me not in thine anger, neither chasten me in thy hot displeasure.note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]note:tn[__Sheminith__: or, the eighth]
@ Have mercy upon me, O LORD; for I __am__ weak: ##O LORD, heal##note:x[<<Ps 30:2; Jer 17:14; Hos 6:1>>] me; for my bones are vexed.
@ My soul is also sore vexed: but thou, O LORD, ##how##note:x[<<Ps 90:13>>] long?
@ Return, O LORD, ##deliver##note:x[<<Ps 17:13; 116:4>>] my soul: oh save me ##for##note:x[<<Dan 9:18>>] thy mercies’ sake.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 88:10–12; 115:17>>] in death __there is__ no remembrance of thee: ##in the##note:x[<<Eccl 9:10>>] grave who shall give thee thanks?
@ ##I am##note:x[<<Ps 38:9; 69:3>>] weary with my groaning; all the night make I my bed to swim; ##I water##note:x[<<Ps 42:3>>] my couch with my tears.note:tn[__all…__: or, every night]
@ ##Mine##note:x[<<Job 17:7; Ps 38:10>>] eye is consumed because of grief; it waxeth old because of all mine enemies.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Depart##note:x[<<Ps 119:115; Luke 13:27>>] from me, all ye workers of iniquity; ##for##note:x[<<Ps 145:18>>] the LORD hath heard the voice of my weeping.
@ The LORD ##hath heard##note:x[<<Ps 66:19–20; 118:5; 120:1>>] my supplication; the LORD will receive my prayer.
@ ##Let all##note:x[<<Ps 86:17; Jer 20:11>>] mine enemies be ashamed and sore vexed: let them ##return##note:x[<<Mal 3:18>>] __and__ be ashamed suddenly.

=== @7

[.psalm-title]
Shiggaion of David, which he sang unto the LORD, concerning the words of Cush the Benjamite.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Take Refuge in You

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Hab 3:1>>]O LORD my God, in thee do I put my trust: ##save##note:x[<<Ps 31:15; 1 Pet 4:19>>] me from all them that persecute me, and deliver me&#58;note:tn[__words__: or, business]
@ ##Lest##note:x[<<Isa 38:13>>] he tear my soul ##like##note:x[<<2 Tim 4:17>>] a lion, ##rending##note:x[<<Ps 50:22>>] __it__ in pieces, while __there is__ none to deliver.note:tn[__none…__: Heb. not a deliverer]
@ O LORD my God, ##if I##note:x[<<1 Sam 26:18–19; Ps 59:3>>] have done this; ##if there##note:x[<<1 Sam 24:11>>] be iniquity in my hands;
@ ##If I##note:x[<<Ps 55:20; 109:5>>] have rewarded evil unto him that was at peace with me; (yea, I have delivered him that ##without##note:x[<<1 Sam 24:17–19>>] cause is mine enemy:)
@ Let the enemy persecute my soul, and take __it;__ yea, let him ##tread##note:x[<<Mal 4:3>>] down my life upon the earth, and lay mine honour in the dust. Selah.
@ ##Arise##note:x[<<Ps 3:7; 35:23>>], O LORD, in thine anger, lift up thyself because of the rage of mine enemies: and ##awake##note:x[<<Ps 44:23>>] for me __to__ the judgment __that__ thou hast commanded.
@ So shall the congregation of the people compass thee about: for their sakes therefore return thou on high.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 96:13; Rom 14:10–12>>] shall ##judge##note:x[<<Ps 43:1>>] the people: judge me, O LORD, according to my righteousness, and according to mine integrity __that is__ in me.
@ Oh let the wickedness of the wicked come to an end; but establish the just: ##for##note:x[<<Jer 11:20; 17:10; 20:12>>] the righteous God trieth the hearts and reins.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##My##note:x[<<Gen 15:1; Ps 84:11>>] defence __is__ of God, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 125:4>>] saveth the upright in heart.note:tn[__My…__: Heb. My buckler is upon God]
@ ##God judgeth the righteous, and God is angry __with the wicked__ every day.##note:x[<<Ps 7:8; 94:15>>]note:tn[__judgeth…__: or, is a righteous judge]
@ ##If##note:x[<<Isa 55:6–7; Matt 3:10; Acts 3:19>>] he turn not, he will whet his sword; he hath bent his bow, and made it ready.
@ He hath also prepared for him the instruments of death; he ##ordaineth##note:x[<<Hab 3:13>>] his arrows against the ##persecutors##note:x[<<2 Thess 1:6>>].
@ ##Behold, he travaileth with iniquity, and hath conceived mischief, and brought forth falsehood.##note:x[<<Job 15:35; Isa 33:11; Jas 1:15>>]
@ He made a pit, and digged it, ##and is##note:x[<<Job 4:8; Ps 141:10; Prov 26:27>>] fallen into the ditch __which__ he made.note:tn[__He made a pit__: Heb. He hath digged a pit]
@ ##His mischief shall return upon his own head, and his violent dealing shall come down upon his own pate.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 2:32; Esth 9:25; Ps 37:12–13>>]
@ I will praise the LORD ##according##note:x[<<Ps 71:15–16; 98:2>>] to his righteousness: and will sing praise to the name of the LORD ##most##note:x[<<Ps 9:2>>] high.

=== @8

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Gittith, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== How Majestic Is Your Name!

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD ##our##note:x[<<Ps 8:9>>] Lord, ##how##note:x[<<Ps 113:2–4; 148:13>>] excellent __is__ thy name in all the earth! who hast set thy glory above the heavens.
@ ##Out##note:x[<<Matt 21:16>>] of the mouth of babes and sucklings hast thou ##ordained##note:tn[Heb. founded] ##strength##note:x[<<Isa 40:31; 2 Cor 12:9–10>>] because of thine enemies, that thou mightest still the enemy and the avenger.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##When##note:x[<<Ps 111:2>>] I consider thy heavens, the ##work##note:x[<<Gen 1:1; Ps 33:6>>] of thy fingers, the moon and the stars, which thou hast ordained;
@ ##What##note:x[<<Job 7:17; Ps 144:3; Heb 2:6–9>>] is man, that thou art mindful of him? and the son of man, that thou visitest him?
@ For ##thou##note:x[<<Heb 2:7, 9>>] ##hast##note:x[<<Heb 2:9>>] made him a little lower than the angels, and hast crowned him with glory and honour.
@ Thou ##madest##note:x[<<Gen 1:26, 28; Matt 28:18>>] him to have dominion over the works of thy hands; thou hast put all __things__ under his feet:
@ All sheep and oxen, yea, and the beasts of the field;note:tn[__All…__: Heb. Flocks and oxen all of them]
@ ##The fowl##note:x[<<Gen 1:20–25; Ps 148:10>>] of the air, and the fish of the sea, __and whatsoever__ passeth through the paths of the seas.
@ ##O LORD our Lord, how excellent __is__ thy name in all the earth!##note:x[<<Ps 8:1>>]

=== @9

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Muthlabben, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Give Thanks to the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 145:1–3>>] __thee__, O LORD, ##with my##note:x[<<Ps 86:12>>] whole heart; I will ##shew##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:24>>] forth all thy marvellous works.
@ ##I will be##note:x[<<Ps 5:11>>] glad and rejoice in thee: I will sing praise to thy name, ##O thou##note:x[<<Ps 7:17; 83:18>>] most High.
@ When mine enemies are turned back, ##they shall##note:x[<<Ps 76:7; 80:16; 2 Thess 1:9>>] fall and perish at thy presence.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 140:12>>] thou hast ##maintained##note:x[<<Ps 98:9; 1 Pet 2:23>>] my right and my cause; thou satest in the throne judging right.note:tn[__maintained…__: Heb. made my judgment]note:tn[__judging right__: Heb. judging in righteousness]
@ Thou hast ##rebuked##note:x[<<Rev 19:15>>] the heathen, thou hast ##destroyed##note:x[<<Mal 4:3>>] the wicked, thou hast ##put out##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>] their name for ever and ever.
@ O thou enemy, ##destructions##note:x[<<Exod 14:13; 1 Cor 15:26>>] are come to a perpetual end: and thou hast destroyed cities; their ##memorial##note:x[<<Isa 14:22–23>>] is perished with them.note:tn[__O thou…__: or, The destructions of the enemy are come to a perpetual end: and their cities hast thou destroyed, etc]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Heb 13:8>>] the LORD shall endure for ever: ##he hath##note:x[<<Ps 103:19; Rev 20:11>>] prepared his throne for judgment.
@ ##And he shall judge the world in righteousness, he shall minister judgment to the people in uprightness.##note:x[<<Ps 96:13; 98:9; Rev 20:12–13>>]
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 91:1–2; Prov 18:10; Nah 1:7>>] also will be a refuge for the oppressed, a refuge in times of trouble.note:tn[__a refuge__: Heb. an high place]
@ And they that ##know##note:x[<<Ps 91:14; Prov 18:10>>] thy name will ##put##note:x[<<Isa 26:3–4>>] their trust in thee: for thou, LORD, hast not forsaken them that seek thee.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Sing##note:x[<<Ps 96:1–2; 148:1–5>>] praises to the LORD, which dwelleth in Zion: ##declare##note:x[<<Ps 107:22>>] among the people his doings.
@ ##When##note:x[<<Rev 16:6>>] he maketh inquisition for blood, he remembereth them: ##he forgetteth##note:x[<<Exod 3:7; Ps 34:6>>] not the cry of the ##humble##note:tn[or, afflicted].
@ Have mercy upon me, O LORD; consider my trouble __which I suffer__ of them that hate me, ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 30:3>>] that liftest me up from the gates of death:
@ ##That##note:x[<<Ps 106:2>>] I may shew forth all thy praise in the gates of the daughter of Zion: ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 13:5; 20:5>>] rejoice in thy salvation.
@ ##The heathen are sunk down in the pit __that__ they made: in the net which they hid is their own foot taken.##note:x[<<Ps 7:15–16; 35:8; 57:6>>]
@ The LORD is ##known##note:x[<<Exod 7:5; Ps 83:17–18>>] __by__ the judgment __which__ he executeth: the ##wicked##note:x[<<Prov 12:13>>] is snared in the work of his own hands. ##Higgaion##note:tn[that is, Meditation]. Selah.
@ ##The wicked##note:x[<<Rom 2:8–9; Rev 21:8>>] shall be turned into hell, __and__ all the nations that ##forget##note:x[<<Ps 50:22>>] God.
@ ##For the##note:x[<<Ps 12:5; Jas 2:5>>] needy shall not alway be forgotten: the ##expectation##note:x[<<Prov 23:18>>] of the poor shall __not__ perish for ever.
@ ##Arise##note:x[<<Ps 3:7; 44:23, 26>>], O LORD; let not man prevail: let the heathen be judged in thy sight.
@ ##Put##note:x[<<Jer 32:40>>] them in fear, O LORD: __that__ the nations ##may##note:x[<<Ps 82:6–7; Isa 31:3>>] know themselves __to be but__ men. Selah.

=== @10

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Perils of the Pilgrim

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Why ##standest##note:x[<<Ps 22:1>>] thou afar off, O LORD? __why__ ##hidest##note:x[<<Ps 13:1–3; 27:9>>] thou __thyself__ in times of trouble?
@ ##The wicked##note:x[<<Ps 36:11; 59:12>>] in __his__ pride doth persecute the poor: ##let##note:x[<<Ps 7:16>>] them be taken in the devices that they have imagined.note:tn[__The wicked…__: Heb. In the pride of the wicked he doth persecute]
@ note:x[<<Zech 11:5–8; Luke 12:19>>]For the wicked ##boasteth##note:x[<<Ps 94:4>>] of his ##heart’s##note:tn[Heb. soul’s] desire, and blesseth the covetous, __whom__ the LORD abhorreth.note:tn[__blesseth…__: or, the covetous blesseth himself, he abhorreth the LORD]
@ The wicked, through the pride of his countenance, will not seek __after__ ##__God__##note:x[<<Ps 14:1>>]__:__ God __is__ not in all his ##thoughts##note:x[<<Acts 8:22; Rom 1:28>>].note:tn[__God is…__: or, all his thoughts are, There is no God]
@ His ways are always grievous; ##thy judgments##note:x[<<Isa 26:11>>] __are__ far above out of his sight: __as for__ all his enemies, he puffeth at them.
@ He hath ##said##note:x[<<Matt 24:48>>] in his heart, I shall ##not##note:x[<<Eccl 8:11; Isa 56:12>>] be moved: for __I shall__ ##never##note:tn[Heb. unto generation and generation] __be__ in adversity.
@ His mouth is full of cursing note:x[<<Ps 7:14>>]and ##deceit##note:tn[Heb. deceits] and fraud: under his tongue __is__ ##mischief##note:x[<<Ps 7:14; 140:9>>] and ##vanity##note:tn[or, iniquity].
@ He ##sitteth##note:x[<<Hab 3:14>>] in the lurking places of the villages: in the secret places doth he murder the innocent: his eyes are privily set against the poor.note:tn[__are…__: Heb. hide themselves]
@ ##He lieth##note:x[<<Ps 17:12; 59:3; Mic 7:2>>] in wait ##secretly##note:tn[Heb. in the secret places] as a lion in his den: he lieth in wait to catch the poor: he doth catch the poor, when he draweth him into his net.
@ He croucheth, __and__ ##humbleth##note:x[<<1 Sam 18:21–26; 23:21–22>>] himself, that the poor may fall by his strong ones.note:tn[__He…__: Heb. He breaketh himself]note:tn[__by…__: or, into his strong parts]
@ He hath ##said##note:x[<<Ps 10:6>>] in his heart, ##God##note:x[<<Ps 94:7; Ezek 8:12>>] hath forgotten: he hideth his face; he will never see __it__.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Arise, O LORD; O God, ##lift##note:x[<<Ps 94:2; Mic 5:9>>] up thine hand: ##forget##note:x[<<Ps 9:12>>] not the ##humble##note:tn[or, afflicted].
@ Wherefore doth the wicked ##contemn##note:x[<<Luke 10:16>>] God? he hath said in his heart, Thou wilt not require __it__.
@ Thou hast seen __it;__ for thou beholdest mischief and spite, to requite __it__ with thy hand: ##the poor##note:x[<<Ps 55:22>>] ##committeth##note:tn[Heb. leaveth] himself unto thee; thou art the ##helper##note:x[<<Ps 68:5; 146:9>>] of the fatherless.
@ ##Break##note:x[<<Job 38:15; Ps 37:17>>] thou the arm of the wicked and the evil __man:__ ##seek##note:x[<<Zeph 1:12>>] out his wickedness __till__ thou find none.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 29:10; Jer 10:10; 1 Tim 1:17>>] __is__ King for ever and ever: the heathen are perished out of his land.
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 145:19>>], thou hast heard the desire of the ##humble##note:x[<<2 Chr 34:27>>]: thou wilt ##prepare##note:tn[or, establish] their heart, thou wilt ##cause##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:12>>] thine ear to hear:
@ To ##judge##note:x[<<Ps 82:3; Isa 11:4>>] the fatherless and the oppressed, that the man of the earth may no more ##oppress##note:tn[or, terrify].

=== @11

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== In the LORD I Take Refuge

[.parallel]
(<<Habakkuk 1:12–17>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##In the##note:x[<<Ps 56:11; Isa 26:3–4>>] LORD put I my trust: ##how##note:x[<<1 Sam 23:14>>] say ye to my soul, Flee __as__ a bird to your mountain?
@ ##For, lo##note:x[<<Ps 37:14; 64:3–4; Jer 9:3>>], the wicked bend __their__ bow, they make ready their arrow upon the string, that they may ##privily##note:tn[Heb. in darkness] shoot at the upright in heart.
@ ##If the##note:x[<<Ps 82:5; Isa 58:12; 2 Tim 2:19>>] foundations be destroyed, what can the righteous do?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 2:4; 103:19>>]##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 18:6>>] __is__ in his holy temple, the LORD’s throne __is__ in heaven: his eyes behold, his eyelids try, the children of men.
@ The LORD ##trieth##note:x[<<Ps 7:9; Jas 1:12>>] the righteous: but the ##wicked##note:x[<<Prov 6:16–19>>] and him that loveth violence his soul hateth.
@ ##Upon##note:x[<<Gen 19:24; Ezek 13:13; 38:22>>] the wicked he shall rain snares, fire and brimstone, and an horrible tempest: __this shall be__ the portion of their cup.note:tn[__an horrible…__: or, a burning tempest]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 45:7>>] the righteous LORD loveth righteousness; ##his##note:x[<<Job 36:7; 1 Pet 3:12>>] countenance doth behold the upright.

=== @12

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Sheminith, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Godly Are No More

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Help##note:tn[or, Save], LORD; for the ##godly##note:x[<<Isa 57:1; Jer 5:1; Matt 24:12>>] man ceaseth; for the faithful fail from among the children of men.note:tn[__upon…__: or, upon the eighth]
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 41:6; Jer 9:8>>] speak vanity every one with his neighbour: __with__ ##flattering##note:x[<<Rom 16:18>>] lips __and__ with a double heart do they speak.note:tn[__a double…__: Heb. an heart and an heart]
@ The LORD shall cut off all flattering lips, __and__ the ##tongue##note:x[<<Dan 7:8; Rev 13:5>>] that speaketh note:x[<<Prov 18:21>>]##proud##note:tn[Heb. great] things:
@ ##Who##note:x[<<Exod 5:2; Job 21:14–15>>] have said, ##With##note:x[<<Jas 3:5–6>>] our tongue will we prevail; our lips __are__ our own: who __is__ lord over us?note:tn[__are…__: Heb. are with us]
@ For the ##oppression##note:x[<<Ps 146:7–8; Prov 14:31; Jas 5:4>>] of the poor, for the sighing of the needy, now will I arise, saith the LORD; I will set __him__ in safety __from him that__ puffeth at him.note:tn[__puffeth…__: or, would ensnare him]
@ The ##words##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:31; Ps 18:30; Prov 30:5>>] of the LORD __are__ pure words: __as__ silver tried in a furnace of earth, purified seven times.
@ ##Thou shalt##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; Ps 121:8>>] keep them, O LORD, thou shalt preserve ##them from##note:tn[Heb. him, etc: that is, every one of them, etc] this generation for ever.
@ The ##wicked##note:x[<<Prov 29:12>>] walk on every side, ##when##note:x[<<Mark 14:63–65>>] the vilest ##men##note:tn[Heb. of the sons of the men] are exalted.

=== @13

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== How Long, O LORD?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ How long wilt thou ##forget##note:x[<<Lam 5:20>>] me, O LORD? for ever? how long ##wilt thou hide##note:x[<<Job 13:24, 24>>] thy face from me?note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]
@ How long shall I ##take##note:x[<<Jer 15:18>>] counsel in my soul, __having__ ##sorrow##note:x[<<Jer 8:18>>] in my heart daily? how long shall mine ##enemy##note:x[<<Mic 7:8–10>>] be exalted over me?
@ note:x[<<Jer 51:39>>]Consider __and__ hear me, O LORD my God: ##lighten##note:x[<<Ezra 9:8; Ps 18:28>>] mine eyes, lest I sleep the __sleep of__ death;
@ ##Lest##note:x[<<Ps 25:2>>] mine enemy say, ##I have##note:x[<<Jer 1:19>>] prevailed against him; __and__ those that trouble me rejoice ##when##note:x[<<Prov 12:3>>] I am moved.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Isa 12:2; Jude 1:21>>] I have trusted in thy mercy; ##my heart##note:x[<<Hab 3:18>>] shall rejoice in thy salvation.
@ ##I##note:x[<<Ps 21:13>>] will sing unto the LORD, because ##he##note:x[<<Ps 116:7; 119:7>>] hath dealt bountifully with me.

=== @14

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Fool Says There Is No God

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 53:1–6; Isaiah 59:1–17; Romans 3:9–20>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The fool hath said in his heart, ##__There__##note:x[<<Rom 3:10–12>>] __is__ ##no##note:x[<<Ps 10:4>>] God. They are corrupt, they have done ##abominable##note:x[<<Rom 1:21–32>>] works, __there is__ none that doeth good.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 33:13–14>>] looked down from heaven upon the children of men, to see if there were ##any##note:x[<<Rom 3:11>>] that did understand, __and__ ##seek##note:x[<<Heb 11:6>>] God.
@ They are ##all gone##note:x[<<Isa 53:6; Rom 3:10–12>>] aside, they are __all__ together become ##filthy##note:tn[Heb. stinking]: ##__there__##note:x[<<Rom 3:10>>] __is__ none that doeth good, no, not one.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Have##note:x[<<Rom 1:28>>] all the workers of iniquity no knowledge? who ##eat up##note:x[<<Jer 10:25>>] my people __as__ they eat bread, ##and##note:x[<<Ps 79:6>>] call not upon the LORD.
@ There were they in great fear: for God __is__ in ##the generation##note:x[<<Ps 24:6; 73:15; 112:2>>] of the righteous.note:tn[__were…__: Heb. they feared a fear]
@ Ye have shamed the counsel of the poor, because the ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 9:9>>] __is__ his refuge.
@ ##Oh##note:x[<<Ps 53:6>>] that the salvation of Israel __were come__ out of Zion! when the LORD bringeth back the captivity of his people, Jacob shall rejoice, __and__ Israel shall be glad.note:tn[__Oh…__: Heb. Who will give]

=== @15

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Who May Dwell on Your Holy Mountain?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 61:4; 84:4; 92:13>>], who shall ##abide##note:tn[Heb. sojourn] in thy tabernacle? who shall dwell in thy holy hill?
@ ##He##note:x[<<Prov 28:18>>] that walketh uprightly, and worketh righteousness, and ##speaketh##note:x[<<Eph 4:25; Col 3:9>>] the truth in his heart.
@ __He that__ ##backbiteth##note:x[<<Ps 101:5–8>>] not with his tongue, nor ##doeth##note:x[<<Matt 7:12; Rom 13:10>>] evil to his neighbour, nor taketh up a reproach against his neighbour.note:tn[__taketh…__: or, receiveth, or, endureth]
@ In whose eyes a vile person is contemned; ##but##note:x[<<Ps 101:6>>] he honoureth them that fear the LORD. __He that__ ##sweareth##note:x[<<Judg 11:35; Matt 5:33>>] to __his own__ hurt, and changeth not.
@ __He that__ ##putteth##note:x[<<Exod 22:25; Ezek 22:12>>] not out his money to usury, ##nor taketh##note:x[<<Deut 16:19>>] reward against the innocent. He that doeth these __things__ shall never be moved.

=== @16

[.psalm-title]
Michtam of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Presence of the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<Acts 2:14–36>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 56:1>>]##Preserve##note:x[<<Ps 17:8>>] me, O God: ##for##note:x[<<Isa 26:3–4>>] in thee do I put my trust.note:tn[__Michtam__: or, A golden Psalm]
@ __O my soul__, ##thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 31:14; 89:26>>] said unto the LORD, Thou __art__ my Lord: ##my goodness##note:x[<<Ps 73:25–26>>] __extendeth__ not to thee;
@ ##__But__##note:x[<<Heb 6:10>>] to the saints that __are__ in the earth, and __to__ ##the excellent##note:x[<<Prov 12:26>>], ##in whom##note:x[<<Prov 13:20>>] __is__ all my delight.
@ ##Their##note:x[<<Ps 32:10>>] sorrows shall be multiplied __that__ hasten __after__ another __god:__ their drink offerings of blood will I not offer, nor ##take##note:x[<<Exod 23:13; Josh 23:7>>] up their names into my lips.note:tn[__hasten…__: or, give gifts to another]
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 73:26; 142:5; Lam 3:24>>] __is__ the portion of mine inheritance and of my cup: thou maintainest my lot.note:tn[__of mine…__: Heb. of my part]
@ ##The lines##note:x[<<Ps 78:55>>] are fallen unto me ##in pleasant##note:x[<<Ps 21:1–3>>] __places;__ yea, ##I have##note:x[<<Jer 3:19>>] a goodly heritage.
@ I will bless the LORD, ##who hath##note:x[<<Isa 50:4>>] given me counsel: my reins also instruct me ##in the##note:x[<<Ps 17:3; 63:6>>] night seasons.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I have##note:x[<<Acts 2:25–28>>] set the LORD always before me: because ##__he is__##note:x[<<Ps 73:23>>] at my right hand, ##I shall##note:x[<<Ps 62:6>>] not be moved.
@ Therefore my heart is glad, and ##my glory##note:x[<<Ps 30:12; 57:8; Acts 2:26>>] rejoiceth: my flesh also shall rest in hope.note:tn[__rest…__: Heb. dwell confidently]
@ For thou wilt not leave ##my##note:x[<<Ps 49:15>>] soul in hell; ##neither##note:x[<<Acts 2:27–31; 13:35–38>>] wilt thou suffer thine Holy One to see corruption.
@ Thou wilt shew me the path of life: ##in thy##note:x[<<Acts 2:28; Jude 1:24; Rev 7:15–17>>] presence __is__ fulness of joy; at thy right hand __there are__ pleasures for evermore.

=== @17

[.psalm-title]
A Prayer of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Hear My Righteous Plea

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<1 John 3:21>>] the right, O LORD, ##attend##note:x[<<Ps 61:1; 142:6>>] unto my cry, give ear unto my prayer, __that goeth__ not out of feigned lips.note:tn[__the right__: Heb. justice]note:tn[__not…__: Heb. without lips of deceit]
@ ##Let my##note:x[<<Ps 37:6; 2 Thess 1:6–9>>] sentence come forth from thy presence; let thine eyes behold the ##things##note:x[<<Ezek 18:25>>] that are equal.
@ Thou hast ##proved##note:x[<<Job 23:10; Ps 26:2>>] mine heart; thou hast visited __me__ in the night; thou hast tried me, __and__ shalt find nothing; ##I am##note:x[<<Acts 11:23>>] purposed __that__ my mouth shall not transgress.
@ Concerning the ##works##note:x[<<1 Cor 3:3>>] of men, by the ##word##note:x[<<Ps 119:9–11>>] of thy lips I have kept __me from__ the paths of the ##destroyer##note:x[<<1 Pet 5:8>>].
@ ##Hold##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; Ps 119:133; 121:3>>] up my goings in thy paths, __that__ my footsteps slip not.note:tn[__slip…__: Heb. be not moved]
@ ##I have##note:x[<<Ps 55:16; 66:19–20; 116:2>>] called upon thee, for thou wilt hear me, O God: incline thine ear unto me, __and hear__ my speech.
@ ##Shew##note:x[<<Ps 31:21>>] thy marvellous lovingkindness, O thou that ##savest##note:x[<<Ps 5:11–12>>] ##by thy##note:x[<<Ps 60:5>>] right hand them which put their trust __in thee__ from those that rise up __against them__.note:tn[__by thy…__: or, them which trust in thee from those that rise up against thy right hand]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Keep me as the ##apple##note:x[<<Deut 32:10>>] of the eye, ##hide##note:x[<<Ps 91:1, 4>>] me under the shadow of thy wings,
@ From the wicked that ##oppress##note:tn[Heb. waste] me, __from__ my ##deadly enemies##note:x[<<Ps 35:4, 7>>], __who__ compass me about.note:tn[__my…__: Heb. my enemies against the soul]
@ They are inclosed in their own fat: ##with##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:3; Ps 31:18; 123:4>>] their mouth they speak proudly.
@ They have now compassed us in our steps: they have ##set##note:x[<<Prov 6:13–14>>] their eyes bowing down to the earth;
@ ##Like##note:x[<<Ps 7:2>>] as a lion __that__ is greedy of his prey, and as it were a young lion ##lurking##note:tn[Heb. sitting] in secret places.note:tn[__Like…__: Heb. The likeness of him (that is, of every one of them) is as a lion that desireth to ravin]
@ ##Arise##note:x[<<Ps 3:7; 7:6; 44:26>>], O LORD, disappoint him, cast him down: deliver my soul from the wicked, ##__which is__##note:tn[or, by] thy sword&#58;note:tn[__disappoint…__: Heb. prevent his face]
@ From men ##__which are__##note:tn[or, by] thy hand, O LORD, from ##men of##note:x[<<Luke 16:8; John 17:14>>] the world, __which have__ their portion in __this__ life, and whose ##belly##note:x[<<Job 22:18>>] thou fillest with thy hid __treasure:__ they are full of children, and leave the rest of their __substance__ to their babes.note:tn[__they…__: or, their children are full]
@ As for me, ##I will##note:x[<<2 Cor 3:18>>] behold thy face in righteousness: ##I shall##note:x[<<Ps 16:11; Matt 5:6>>] be satisfied, when I awake, with thy likeness.

=== @18

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __A Psalm__ of David, the servant of the LORD, who spake unto the LORD the words of this song in the day __that__ the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies, and from the hand of Saul: And he said,

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is My Rock

[.parallel]
(<<2 Samuel 22:1–51>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<2 Sam 22:1–51>>]##I will##note:x[<<1 John 4:19>>] love thee, O LORD, ##my##note:x[<<Col 1:11>>] strength.
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 62:7>>] __is__ my rock, and my ##fortress##note:x[<<Ps 144:2>>], and my deliverer; my God, my strength, in whom I will trust; my buckler, and the horn of my salvation, __and__ my ##high##note:x[<<Prov 18:10>>] tower.note:tn[__my strength__: Heb. my rock]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 62:8>>] call upon the LORD, __who is worthy__ to be praised: ##so shall##note:x[<<Ps 91:15; Luke 1:71>>] I be saved from mine enemies.
@ The ##sorrows##note:x[<<Ps 116:3; Mark 14:33–34; 2 Cor 1:9>>] of death compassed me, and the floods of ungodly men made me afraid.note:tn[__ungodly men__: Heb. Belial]
@ note:x[<<Ps 86:13>>]The ##sorrows##note:tn[or, cords] of hell compassed me about: the snares of death prevented me.
@ In my ##distress##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; 130:1–2>>] I called upon the LORD, and cried unto my God: he ##heard##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:7>>] my voice out of his temple, and my cry came before him, __even__ into his ears.
@ Then the ##earth##note:x[<<Acts 4:31>>] shook and trembled; the ##foundations##note:x[<<Ezek 38:19–20>>] also of the hills moved and were shaken, because he was wroth.
@ There ##went##note:x[<<Ps 11:6; 21:9>>] up a smoke out of his nostrils, and ##fire##note:x[<<Nah 1:5–6>>] out of his mouth devoured: coals were kindled by it.note:tn[__out of his nostrils__: Heb. by his, etc]
@ ##He bowed##note:x[<<Ps 144:5–15>>] the heavens also, and came down: and darkness __was__ under his feet.
@ And he rode upon a cherub, and did fly: yea, ##he did fly##note:x[<<Ps 104:3>>] upon the wings of the wind.
@ He made darkness his secret place; his pavilion round about him __were__ dark waters __and__ ##thick##note:x[<<Deut 4:11; Ps 97:2>>] clouds of the skies.
@ At the brightness __that was__ before him his thick clouds passed, ##hail##note:x[<<Josh 10:11; Rev 16:21>>] __stones__ and coals of fire.
@ The LORD also ##thundered##note:x[<<1 Sam 7:10; Ps 104:7>>] in the heavens, and the Highest gave his voice; hail __stones__ and coals of fire.
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Hab 3:11>>], he sent out his arrows, and scattered them; and ##he shot##note:x[<<Ps 144:6>>] out lightnings, and discomfited them.
@ Then the ##channels##note:x[<<Exod 15:8; Ps 106:9>>] of waters were seen, and the ##foundations##note:x[<<Mic 6:2>>] of the world were discovered at thy rebuke, O LORD, at the blast of the breath of thy nostrils.
@ ##He sent##note:x[<<Ps 57:3; 144:7>>] from above, he took me, he ##drew##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:17>>] me out of ##many##note:tn[or, great] waters.
@ He delivered me from my ##strong##note:x[<<Heb 2:14–15>>] enemy, and from ##them##note:x[<<Job 16:9>>] which hated me: for ##they were##note:x[<<Ps 35:10>>] too strong for me.
@ They prevented ##me in##note:x[<<Deut 32:35; Jer 18:17; Obad 1:10–14>>] the day of my calamity: but the LORD was my stay.
@ He ##brought##note:x[<<Ps 18:36; 31:8; 118:5>>] me forth also into a large place; he delivered me, because he delighted in me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD ##rewarded##note:x[<<Ps 58:11; Matt 6:4>>] me according to my righteousness; according to the ##cleanness##note:x[<<Ps 24:4>>] of my hands hath he recompensed me.
@ ##For I##note:x[<<Ps 17:4; 26:1>>] have kept the ways of the LORD, and ##have not##note:x[<<Ps 119:102>>] wickedly departed from my God.
@ ##For all##note:x[<<Ps 119:13, 128>>] his judgments __were__ before me, and ##I did##note:x[<<Ps 119:112>>] not put away his statutes from me.
@ I was also ##upright##note:x[<<Ps 11:7>>] ##before##note:tn[Heb. with] him, and ##I kept##note:x[<<Matt 5:29–30; 18:8–9>>] myself from mine iniquity.
@ Therefore hath ##the LORD recompensed me##note:x[<<Ruth 2:12; Matt 10:41–42>>] according to my righteousness, according to the cleanness of my hands in his eyesight.note:tn[__in…__: Heb. before his eyes]
@ ##With the##note:x[<<Matt 18:33–35>>] merciful thou wilt shew thyself merciful; with an upright man thou wilt shew thyself upright;
@ With the pure thou wilt shew thyself pure; and with the ##froward##note:x[<<Lev 26:23–24; Prov 3:34; Jas 2:13>>] thou wilt shew thyself froward.note:tn[__shew thyself froward__: or, wrestle]
@ For thou wilt ##save##note:x[<<Isa 57:15; 66:2; Jas 2:5>>] the afflicted people; but wilt bring down high looks.
@ For ##thou wilt##note:x[<<Job 29:3>>] light my note:x[<<2 Sam 22:29>>]##candle##note:tn[or, lamp]: the LORD ##my God##note:x[<<Isa 42:16>>] will enlighten my darkness.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ For ##by thee##note:x[<<Eph 6:10–13>>] I have run through a troop; and ##by my God##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:30; 2 Cor 12:9–10>>] have I leaped over a wall.note:tn[__run…__: or, broken]
@ __As for__ God, ##his way##note:x[<<Deut 32:4; 2 Sam 22:31>>] __is__ perfect: the word of the LORD is note:x[<<Prov 30:5>>]##tried##note:tn[or, refined]: he __is__ a buckler to all those that trust in him.
@ ##For who __is__ God save the LORD? or who __is__ a rock save our God?##note:x[<<Deut 32:31, 39; Ps 86:8>>]
@ __It is__ God that ##girdeth##note:x[<<Ps 28:7; Isa 45:5; 2 Cor 3:5>>] me with strength, and maketh my way perfect.
@ He maketh my feet like hinds’ __feet__, and setteth me upon my ##high##note:x[<<Deut 32:13; 33:29; Hab 3:19>>] places.
@ He ##teacheth##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:36; Ps 144:1>>] my hands to war, ##so that##note:x[<<Ps 46:9>>] a bow of steel is broken by mine arms.
@ Thou hast also given me the ##shield##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:36; Ps 28:7>>] of thy salvation: and thy right hand hath holden me up, and thy ##gentleness##note:x[<<Jas 3:17–18>>] hath made me great.note:tn[__thy gentleness…__: or, with thy meekness thou hast multiplied me]
@ Thou hast ##enlarged##note:x[<<Job 36:16>>] my steps under me, that my ##feet##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:37; Prov 4:12>>] did not slip.note:tn[__my feet__: Heb. mine ankles]
@ I have pursued mine enemies, and overtaken them: neither did I turn again till they were consumed.
@ I have wounded them that they were not able to rise: they are fallen under my feet.
@ For thou hast ##girded##note:x[<<Ps 18:32>>] me with strength unto the battle: thou hast note:x[<<2 Sam 22:40; Isa 45:14>>]##subdued##note:tn[Heb. caused to bow] under me those that rose up against me.
@ Thou hast also given me the necks of mine enemies; that I might destroy them that hate me.
@ ##They cried, but __there was__ none to save __them: even__ unto the LORD, but he answered them not.##note:x[<<Prov 1:28>>]
@ Then did I beat them small as the dust before the wind: I did ##cast##note:x[<<Isa 10:6>>] them out as the dirt in the streets.
@ Thou hast delivered me ##from##note:x[<<2 Sam 3:1>>] the strivings of the people; __and__ thou hast ##made##note:x[<<Isa 52:15>>] me the head of the heathen: ##a people##note:x[<<Isa 55:5>>] __whom__ I have not known shall serve me.
@ As soon as they hear of me, they shall obey me: the note:x[<<Ps 66:3; 81:15>>]##strangers##note:tn[Heb. sons of the stranger] ##shall submit##note:x[<<Ps 81:15>>] themselves unto me.note:tn[__As soon…__: Heb. At the hearing of the ear]note:tn[__submit…__: or, yield feigned obedience]
@ The note:x[<<Mic 7:17>>]##strangers##note:tn[Heb. sons of the stranger] shall fade away, and be afraid out of their close places.
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:47>>] liveth; and ##blessed##note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>] __be__ my rock; and let the God of my salvation be ##exalted##note:x[<<Ps 57:5>>].
@ __It is__ God that note:x[<<Rom 12:19>>]##avengeth##note:tn[Heb. giveth avengements for] me, and note:x[<<Ps 47:3>>]##subdueth##note:tn[or, destroyeth] the people under me.
@ He delivereth me from mine enemies: yea, thou liftest me up above those that rise up against me: thou hast delivered me from the ##violent man##note:x[<<Ps 86:14; 140:1, 4>>].note:tn[__violent…__: Heb. man of violence]
@ Therefore ##will I give thanks##note:x[<<Rom 15:9>>] unto thee, O LORD, among the heathen, and ##sing##note:x[<<Rom 15:9>>] praises unto thy name.note:tn[__give…__: or, confess]
@ ##Great##note:x[<<Ps 89:3–4; 144:10>>] deliverance giveth he ##to his##note:x[<<Ps 132:10>>] king; and sheweth mercy to his anointed, to David, and to his seed for evermore.

=== @19

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Heavens Declare the Glory of God

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##The heavens##note:x[<<Ps 8:3; 148:3–4; Rom 1:19–20>>] declare the glory of God; and the firmament sheweth his handywork.
@ note:x[<<Ps 74:16>>]##Day unto##note:x[<<Ps 24:7–10; 134:1–3>>] day uttereth speech, and night unto night sheweth knowledge.
@ ##__There__##note:x[<<Deut 4:19>>] __is__ no speech nor language, __where__ their voice is not heard.note:tn[__where…__: or, without these their voice is heard: Heb. without their voice heard]
@ ##Their##note:x[<<Ps 98:3; Rom 10:18>>] ##line##note:tn[or, rule, or, direction] is gone out through all the earth, and their words to the end of the world. ##In them##note:x[<<Gen 1:14–18>>] hath he set a tabernacle for the sun,
@ Which __is__ as a ##bridegroom##note:x[<<Isa 61:10; 62:5>>] coming out of his chamber, __and__ ##rejoiceth##note:x[<<Phil 3:13–14>>] as a strong man to run a race.
@ ##His going##note:x[<<Eccl 1:5>>] forth __is__ from the end of the heaven, and his ##circuit##note:x[<<Job 22:14>>] unto the ends of it: and there is nothing hid from the heat thereof.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##law##note:tn[or, doctrine] of the LORD __is__ ##perfect##note:x[<<Ps 111:7>>], ##converting##note:tn[or, restoring] the soul: the testimony of the LORD __is__ ##sure##note:x[<<Ps 111:7>>], ##making##note:x[<<Ps 119:130>>] wise the simple.
@ The statutes of the LORD __are__ right, ##rejoicing##note:x[<<Jer 15:16>>] the heart: the commandment of the LORD ##__is__ pure##note:x[<<Ps 12:6>>], ##enlightening##note:x[<<Ps 119:105>>] the eyes.
@ ##The fear##note:x[<<1 Sam 12:24>>] of the LORD __is__ clean, enduring for ever: the ##judgments##note:x[<<Ps 119:137–138, 142>>] of the LORD __are__ ##true##note:tn[Heb. truth] __and__ righteous altogether.
@ More to be desired __are they__ ##than gold##note:x[<<Ps 119:72, 127>>], yea, than much fine gold: ##sweeter##note:x[<<Ps 119:103>>] also than honey and the honeycomb.note:tn[__the honeycomb__: Heb. the dropping of honeycombs]
@ ##Moreover##note:x[<<Ps 119:11>>] by them is thy servant warned: __and__ in ##keeping##note:x[<<Jas 1:25; 2 John 1:8>>] of them __there is__ great reward.
@ Who ##can##note:x[<<Job 6:24>>] understand __his__ errors? cleanse thou me from ##secret##note:x[<<Ps 90:8; 139:23–24>>] __faults__.
@ Keep back thy servant also from presumptuous __sins;__ ##let##note:x[<<Ps 119:133; Rom 6:12–14>>] them not have dominion over me: then shall I be ##upright##note:x[<<Ps 7:10>>], and I shall be innocent from the great transgression.note:tn[__the great__: or, much]
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Ps 51:15; 119:108; 1 Pet 2:5>>] the words of my mouth, and the meditation of my heart, be acceptable in thy sight, O LORD, my ##strength##note:tn[Heb. rock], and my redeemer.

=== @20

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Day of Trouble

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD ##hear##note:x[<<Ps 138:7>>] thee in the day of trouble; the ##name##note:x[<<Prov 18:10>>] of the God of Jacob ##defend##note:x[<<Ps 91:14>>] thee;note:tn[__defend…__: Heb. set thee on an high place]
@ Send thee help ##from##note:x[<<1 Kgs 6:16; 8:44–45; 2 Chr 20:8–9>>] the sanctuary, and ##strengthen##note:tn[Heb. support] thee out of Zion;note:tn[__thee help__: Heb. thy help]
@ Remember all thy offerings, and note:x[<<2 Chr 7:1>>]##accept##note:tn[Heb. turn to ashes: or, make fat] thy burnt sacrifice; Selah.
@ ##Grant thee according to thine own heart, and fulfil all thy counsel.##note:x[<<Ps 37:4; 145:19; Matt 21:22>>]
@ We will ##rejoice##note:x[<<Isa 25:9>>] in thy salvation, ##and in##note:x[<<Exod 17:15; Ps 60:4>>] the name of our God we will set up __our__ banners: the LORD fulfil all thy petitions.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Now##note:x[<<Ps 18:50; 28:8>>] know I that the LORD saveth his anointed; he will hear him from his holy heaven ##with##note:x[<<Acts 5:31>>] the saving strength of his right hand.note:tn[__his holy…__: Heb. the heaven of his holiness]note:tn[__with…__: Heb. by the strength of the salvation of]
@ ##Some __trust__##note:x[<<Prov 21:31; Isa 31:1; Jer 17:5>>] in chariots, and some in horses: but we will remember the name of the LORD our God.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 34:21–22>>] are brought down and fallen: ##but we##note:x[<<Ps 125:1; Jer 17:7–8>>] are risen, and stand upright.
@ ##Save##note:x[<<Ps 118:25–26>>], LORD: ##let##note:x[<<Ps 5:2; 44:4>>] the king hear us when we call.

=== @21

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== After the Battle

[.parallel]
(<<Proverbs 21:1–31>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##The king##note:x[<<Matt 2:2>>] shall ##joy##note:x[<<Ps 28:7>>] ##in thy##note:x[<<Heb 12:2>>] strength, O LORD; and in thy salvation how greatly shall he rejoice!
@ note:x[<<Ps 2:8–9; 20:4–5>>]Thou hast given him his heart’s desire, and hast not withholden the request of his lips. Selah.
@ For thou preventest him with the ##blessings##note:x[<<Ps 31:19; Eph 1:3>>] of goodness: thou ##settest##note:x[<<2 Sam 12:30>>] a crown of pure gold on his head.
@ He ##asked##note:x[<<Ps 119:77>>] life of thee, __and__ thou gavest __it__ him, __even__ ##length##note:x[<<Ps 91:16; Rev 1:18>>] of days for ever and ever.
@ His ##glory##note:x[<<Heb 8:1; Rev 5:8–13>>] __is__ great in thy salvation: ##honour##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:22>>] and majesty hast thou laid upon him.
@ For thou hast made him most blessed for ever: thou hast ##made him exceeding glad##note:x[<<Ps 16:11; 45:7; Acts 2:28>>] with thy countenance.note:tn[__made him most…__: Heb. set him to be blessings]note:tn[__made him exceeding…__: Heb. make him glad with joy]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##For the##note:x[<<Ps 18:2; 91:2>>] king trusteth in the LORD, and through the mercy of the most High ##he shall##note:x[<<Ps 16:8>>] not be moved.
@ ##Thine hand shall find out all thine enemies: thy right hand shall find out those that hate thee.##note:x[<<Ps 110:1–2; Luke 19:27; 1 Cor 15:25>>]
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Mal 4:1; 2 Thess 1:8>>] shalt make them as a fiery oven in the time of thine anger: the LORD shall swallow them up in his wrath, and ##the fire##note:x[<<Ps 18:8>>] shall devour them.
@ ##Their fruit shalt thou destroy from the earth, and their seed from among the children of men.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 13:34; Ps 37:28; 109:13>>]
@ For they intended evil against thee: they ##imagined##note:x[<<Ps 2:1; 10:2; 31:13>>] a mischievous device, __which__ they are not able __to perform__.
@ Therefore shalt thou ##make##note:x[<<Lam 3:12>>] them turn their ##back##note:tn[Heb. shoulder], __when__ ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Ps 64:7>>] make ready __thine arrows__ upon thy strings against the face of them.note:tn[__shalt thou…__: or, thou shalt set them as a butt]
@ ##Be thou##note:x[<<Ps 57:11>>] exalted, LORD, in thine own strength: ##__so__ will##note:x[<<Rev 16:5–7; 19:1–6>>] we sing and praise thy power.

=== @22

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Aijeleth Shahar, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Psalm of the Cross

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 27:32–56; Mark 15:21–41; Luke 23:26–43; John 19:16–30>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##My God##note:x[<<Matt 27:46; Mark 15:34>>], my God, why hast thou forsaken me? __why art thou so__ far from helping me, __and from__ the ##words##note:x[<<Heb 5:7>>] of my roaring?note:tn[__Aijeleth…__: or, the hind of the morning]note:tn[__helping…__: Heb. my salvation]
@ O my God, ##I cry##note:x[<<Ps 42:3; Luke 18:7>>] in the daytime, but thou hearest not; and ##in the night##note:x[<<Luke 18:7>>] season, and am not silent.note:tn[__am…__: Heb. there is no silence to me]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Isa 6:3>>] thou __art__ holy, __O thou__ ##that##note:x[<<Deut 10:21; Ps 50:23>>] inhabitest the praises of Israel.
@ ##Our fathers trusted in thee: they trusted, and thou didst deliver them.##note:x[<<Gen 15:6; Exod 14:13–14, 31>>]
@ They cried unto thee, ##and were##note:x[<<Isa 49:23; Rom 9:33; 10:11>>] delivered: they trusted in thee, and were not confounded.
@ But ##I __am__##note:x[<<Job 25:6; Isa 41:14>>] a worm, and no man; ##a reproach##note:x[<<Isa 53:3>>] of men, and despised of the people.
@ All they that see me ##laugh##note:x[<<Matt 27:39; Mark 15:29>>] me to scorn: they shoot out the lip, they ##shake##note:x[<<Matt 27:39–40>>] the head, __saying__,note:tn[__shoot…__: Heb. open]
@ ##He trusted##note:x[<<Matt 27:42–43>>] on the LORD __that__ he would deliver him: ##let him##note:x[<<Ps 91:14; Mark 15:30–32>>] deliver him, seeing he delighted in him.note:tn[__He trusted…__: Heb. He rolled himself on]note:tn[__seeing…__: or, if he delight in]
@ But thou __art__ he ##that took##note:x[<<Ps 71:6; Isa 49:1–2>>] me out of the womb: ##thou didst##note:x[<<Ps 71:17>>] make me hope __when I was__ upon my mother’s breasts.note:tn[__didst…__: or, kept me in safety]
@ I was ##cast##note:x[<<Isa 49:1>>] upon thee ##from##note:x[<<Jer 1:5; Gal 1:15>>] the womb: thou __art__ my God from my mother’s belly.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Be not##note:x[<<Ps 10:1; 71:12; John 16:32>>] far from me; for trouble __is__ near; for __there is__ none to help.note:tn[__none…__: Heb. not a helper]
@ ##Many##note:x[<<Ps 68:30>>] bulls have compassed me: ##strong##note:x[<<Amos 4:1–3; Acts 4:27>>] __bulls__ of Bashan have beset me round.
@ They ##gaped##note:x[<<Job 16:10; Ps 35:21>>] upon me __with__ their mouths, ##__as__ a##note:x[<<1 Pet 5:8>>] ravening and a roaring lion.note:tn[__gaped…__: Heb. opened their mouths against me]
@ I am poured out like water, and ##all##note:x[<<Ps 22:17; Dan 5:6>>] my bones are out of joint: my ##heart##note:x[<<Job 23:16>>] is like wax; it is melted in the midst of my bowels.note:tn[__out of…__: or, sundered]
@ My strength is dried up like a potsherd; and my ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 69:3, 21; John 19:28>>] cleaveth to my jaws; and thou hast brought me into the dust of death.
@ For dogs have compassed me: the assembly of the wicked have inclosed me: ##they pierced##note:x[<<Zech 12:10; Matt 27:35; John 19:37>>] my hands and my feet.
@ ##I may##note:x[<<Job 33:21>>] tell all my bones: they ##look##note:x[<<Matt 27:39–41; Luke 23:35>>] __and__ stare upon me.
@ ##They part my garments among them, and cast lots upon my vesture.##note:x[<<Matt 27:35; Mark 15:24; Luke 23:34>>]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 22:11>>] be not thou far from me, O LORD: ##O my##note:x[<<Ps 40:13, 17>>] strength, haste thee to help me.
@ Deliver my ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 17:13; Zech 13:7>>] from the sword; ##my darling##note:x[<<Ps 35:17>>] from the ##power##note:tn[Heb. hand] of the dog.note:tn[__my darling__: Heb. my only one]
@ Save ##me from##note:x[<<John 14:30; 2 Tim 4:17; 1 Pet 5:8>>] the lion’s mouth: for thou hast heard me from the horns of the unicorns.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Heb 2:11–12>>] declare thy name unto ##my brethren##note:x[<<Matt 12:48–49; Rom 8:29>>]: in the midst of the congregation will I praise thee.
@ ##Ye that##note:x[<<Ps 135:19–20>>] fear the LORD, praise him; ##all ye##note:x[<<Ps 135:19–20>>] the seed of Jacob, ##glorify##note:x[<<Rev 15:4>>] him; and fear him, all ye the seed of Israel.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Isa 50:6–9>>] he hath not despised nor abhorred the affliction of the afflicted; neither hath he hid his face from him; ##but##note:x[<<Ps 116:3–6; Heb 5:7>>] when he cried unto him, he heard.
@ ##My praise##note:x[<<Ps 35:18; 40:9–10; 111:1>>] __shall be__ of thee in the great congregation: I will pay my vows before them that fear him.
@ ##The meek##note:x[<<Ps 69:32; Isa 65:13>>] shall eat and be satisfied: they shall praise the LORD that seek him: ##your##note:x[<<Ps 69:32>>] heart shall live for ever.
@ ##All the ends##note:x[<<Ps 86:9; Isa 45:22>>] of the world shall remember and turn unto the LORD: ##and all##note:x[<<Rev 7:9–12>>] the kindreds of the nations shall worship before thee.
@ note:x[<<Ps 47:7–8; Dan 7:14; Zech 14:9>>]For the kingdom __is__ the LORD’s: and he __is__ the governor among the nations.
@ ##All __they that__##note:x[<<Isa 26:19; Phil 2:10>>] __be__ fat upon earth ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 45:12>>] eat and worship: all they that go down to the dust shall bow before him: and none can keep alive his own soul.
@ ##A seed##note:x[<<Heb 2:13>>] shall serve him; ##it shall##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:9>>] be accounted to the Lord for a generation.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 78:6; 86:9>>] shall come, and shall declare ##his righteousness##note:x[<<Rom 1:17>>] unto a people that shall be born, that he hath done __this__.

=== @23

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is My Shepherd

[.parallel]
(<<Ezekiel 34:11–24; John 10:1–21>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD __is__ ##my shepherd##note:x[<<Isa 40:11; John 10:11>>]; ##I shall not want##note:x[<<Phil 4:19>>].
@ He maketh me to lie down in ##green pastures##note:x[<<Ezek 34:13–14>>]: he ##leadeth me##note:x[<<Rev 7:17; 22:1>>] beside the still waters.note:tn[__green…__: Heb. pastures of tender grass]note:tn[__still…__: Heb. waters of quietness]
@ He ##restoreth my soul##note:x[<<Ps 19:7; 51:10>>]: he ##leadeth##note:x[<<Isa 42:16>>] me in the paths of righteousness for his name’s sake.
@ Yea, though I walk through the valley of the shadow of death, ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 118:6; 138:7; Isa 41:10>>] fear no evil: for thou __art__ with me; thy rod and thy staff they comfort me.
@ Thou preparest a table before me in the presence of mine enemies: note:x[<<Ps 45:7; 92:10>>]thou ##anointest##note:tn[Heb. makest fat] my head with oil; ##my cup##note:x[<<Eph 3:20>>] runneth over.
@ Surely ##goodness##note:x[<<Ps 36:7–10; 2 Tim 4:18>>] and mercy shall follow me all the days of my life: ##and I##note:x[<<2 Cor 5:1>>] will dwell in the house of the LORD for ever.note:tn[__for ever__: Heb. to length of days]

=== @24

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Earth Is the LORD’s

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##earth##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:11; 1 Cor 10:26>>] __is__ the LORD’s, and the fulness thereof; the ##world##note:x[<<Ps 89:11>>], and they that dwell therein.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 104:5–6; 136:6>>] he hath founded it upon the seas, ##and##note:x[<<Jer 5:22>>] established it upon the floods.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Who##note:x[<<Ps 15:1>>] shall ascend into the hill of the LORD? or who shall ##stand##note:x[<<Mal 3:1; Heb 12:28>>] in his holy place?
@ ##He that##note:x[<<Jas 4:8>>] hath clean hands, and a ##pure##note:x[<<Ps 51:10; Matt 5:8>>] heart; who hath not lifted up his soul unto vanity, nor sworn deceitfully.note:tn[__He…__: Heb. The clean of hands]
@ He shall ##receive##note:x[<<Eph 1:3>>] the blessing from the LORD, and ##righteousness##note:x[<<Phil 3:9>>] from the ##God##note:x[<<Titus 3:4–6>>] of his salvation.
@ This __is__ the generation of them ##that seek##note:x[<<Ps 27:8; 105:4>>] him, that seek thy face, O Jacob. Selah.note:tn[__O Jacob__: or, O God of Jacob]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Lift##note:x[<<Ps 118:19–20; Isa 26:2>>] up your heads, O ye gates; and be ye lift up, ye everlasting doors; and the ##King##note:x[<<Rev 4:11>>] of glory shall come in.
@ Who __is__ this King of glory? ##The LORD strong##note:x[<<Ps 45:3–6; Rev 6:2; 19:11–21>>] and mighty, the LORD mighty in battle.
@ Lift up your heads, O ye gates; even lift __them__ up, ye everlasting doors; and the King of glory shall come in.
@ Who is this King of glory? ##The LORD##note:x[<<Isa 54:5>>] of hosts, ##he __is__##note:x[<<Matt 25:34; Titus 2:13>>] the King of glory. Selah.

=== @25

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== To You I Lift Up My Soul

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Unto thee, O LORD, ##do I##note:x[<<Ps 24:4; 86:4; 143:8>>] lift up my soul.
@ ##O##note:x[<<Ps 22:5; Rom 10:11>>] my God, I trust in thee: let me not be ashamed, ##let not##note:x[<<Ps 41:11>>] mine enemies triumph over me.
@ Yea, let none that ##wait##note:x[<<Isa 40:31; Lam 3:25; Mic 7:7>>] on thee be ashamed: let them be ashamed which transgress without cause.
@ ##Shew me thy ways, O LORD; teach me thy paths.##note:x[<<Ps 27:11; 86:11; 143:8>>]
@ ##Lead##note:x[<<Ps 25:10>>] me in thy truth, and teach me: for thou __art__ the God of my salvation; ##on thee##note:x[<<Prov 8:34; Isa 30:18>>] do I wait all the day.
@ Remember, O LORD, ##thy tender mercies##note:x[<<Col 3:12>>] and thy lovingkindnesses; ##for they##note:x[<<Ps 103:17; Luke 1:50>>] __have been__ ever of old.note:tn[__tender…__: Heb. bowels]
@ ##Remember##note:x[<<Isa 38:17; 43:25; Heb 10:16–18>>] not the sins of my youth, nor my transgressions: according to thy mercy remember thou me for thy goodness’ sake, O LORD.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Good and upright __is__ the LORD: therefore will he ##teach##note:x[<<John 6:44–45; Eph 1:17–18; Jas 1:5>>] sinners in the way.
@ The ##meek##note:x[<<Matt 5:5>>] will he ##guide##note:x[<<Ps 23:3; Prov 3:5–6>>] in judgment: and the meek will he teach his way.
@ All ##the paths##note:x[<<Rom 8:28>>] of the LORD __are__ ##mercy##note:x[<<Jas 5:11>>] and truth unto such as ##keep##note:x[<<Heb 8:8–12>>] his covenant and his testimonies.
@ For ##thy##note:x[<<Ps 79:9; Isa 43:25; 1 John 2:12>>] name’s sake, O LORD, pardon mine iniquity; for it __is__ great.
@ What man __is__ he that feareth the LORD? ##him##note:x[<<Ps 32:8; 37:23; John 7:17>>] shall he teach in the way __that__ he shall choose.
@ ##His soul##note:x[<<Prov 1:33; 19:23>>] shall dwell at ease; and his seed shall ##inherit##note:x[<<Ps 37:11>>] the earth.note:tn[__dwell…__: Heb. lodge in goodness]
@ The ##secret##note:x[<<John 7:17; 14:21–23; 15:15>>] of the LORD __is__ with them that fear him; and he will shew them his covenant.note:tn[__and…__: or, and his covenant to make them know it]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Mine##note:x[<<Ps 141:8>>] eyes __are__ ever toward the LORD; for he shall ##pluck##note:tn[Heb. bring forth] my feet ##out##note:x[<<Ps 31:4; 124:7–8>>] of the net.
@ ##Turn##note:x[<<Ps 86:16; Mic 7:19>>] thee unto me, and have mercy upon me; ##for I##note:x[<<Dan 9:17>>] __am__ desolate and afflicted.
@ ##The troubles of my heart are enlarged: __O__ bring thou me out of my distresses.##note:x[<<Ps 34:19; 2 Cor 4:8–9>>]
@ ##Look##note:x[<<2 Sam 16:12; Ps 119:132>>] upon mine affliction and my pain; and forgive all my sins.
@ Consider mine enemies; for they are many; and they hate me with cruel hatred.note:tn[__cruel…__: Heb. hatred of violence]
@ ##O##note:x[<<Ps 17:8; 121:7>>] keep my soul, and deliver me: ##let##note:x[<<Ps 71:1–2>>] me not be ashamed; for I put my trust in thee.
@ ##Let integrity and uprightness preserve me; for I wait on thee.##note:x[<<Ps 26:1; 41:12; Prov 11:3>>]
@ ##Redeem Israel, O God, out of all his troubles.##note:x[<<Ps 130:8>>]

=== @26

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Vindicate Me, O LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Judge##note:x[<<Ps 7:8>>] me, O LORD; ##for##note:x[<<Prov 20:7>>] I have walked in mine integrity: I have ##trusted##note:x[<<Ps 28:7>>] also in the LORD; __therefore__ I shall not slide.
@ ##Examine me, O LORD, and prove me; try my reins and my heart.##note:x[<<Ps 7:9; 17:3; 139:23–24>>]
@ ##For##note:x[<<2 Cor 8:9>>] thy lovingkindness __is__ before mine eyes: ##and##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:3; 1 John 1:7>>] I have walked in thy truth.
@ ##I have not sat with vain persons, neither will I go in with dissemblers.##note:x[<<Ps 1:1; Prov 13:20; Jer 15:17>>]
@ I have ##hated##note:x[<<Ps 31:6; 101:3–8; 139:21–22>>] the congregation of evil doers; and will not sit with the wicked.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will ##wash##note:x[<<Exod 30:19–20; Ps 73:13; Isa 1:16–18>>] mine hands in innocency: so will I compass thine altar, O LORD:
@ That I may publish with the voice of thanksgiving, and ##tell##note:x[<<Ps 71:17–19; 145:5>>] of all thy wondrous works.
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 27:4–6; 84:10; John 2:14–17>>], I have loved the habitation of thy house, and the place where thine honour dwelleth.note:tn[__where…__: Heb. of the tabernacle of thy honour]
@ Gather not my soul with sinners, nor my life with bloody men&#58;note:tn[__Gather…__: or, Take not away]note:tn[__bloody…__: Heb. men of blood]
@ In whose hands __is__ mischief, and their right hand is full of ##bribes##note:x[<<Exod 23:8; Deut 16:19>>].note:tn[__full…__: Heb. filled with]
@ But as for me, ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 26:1>>] walk in mine integrity: ##redeem##note:x[<<Ps 69:18>>] me, and be merciful unto me.
@ ##My##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; Ps 27:11; 40:2>>] foot standeth in an even place: in the congregations will I bless the LORD.

=== @27

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is My Salvation

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD __is__ my light and my ##salvation##note:x[<<Exod 15:2>>]; whom shall I fear? the LORD __is__ the strength of my life; ##of whom##note:x[<<Ps 118:6; Heb 13:6>>] shall I be afraid?
@ When the ##wicked##note:x[<<Ps 3:7; 22:16>>], __even__ mine enemies and my foes, came upon me to eat up my flesh, ##they##note:x[<<Ps 118:12>>] stumbled and fell.note:tn[__came…__: Heb. approached against me]
@ Though an ##host##note:x[<<Ps 3:6; 1 Pet 3:14>>] should encamp against me, my heart shall not fear: though ##war##note:x[<<Rom 8:35–37>>] should rise against me, in this __will__ I __be__ confident.
@ ##One##note:x[<<Ps 26:8>>] __thing__ have I desired of the LORD, that will I seek after; that I may ##dwell##note:x[<<Ps 23:6; 65:4>>] in the house of the LORD all the days of my life, to behold the beauty of the LORD, and to enquire in his temple.note:tn[__the beauty__: or, the delight]
@ ##For in##note:x[<<Ps 138:7>>] the time of trouble he shall ##hide##note:x[<<Prov 18:10>>] me in his pavilion: in the ##secret##note:x[<<Ps 31:20>>] of his tabernacle shall he hide me; he shall set me up upon a rock.
@ ##And##note:x[<<Ps 3:3>>] now shall mine head be lifted up ##above##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:49>>] mine enemies round about me: ##therefore##note:x[<<Heb 13:15>>] will I offer in his tabernacle sacrifices of ##joy##note:tn[Heb. shouting]; I will sing, yea, I will sing praises unto the LORD.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear, O LORD, __when__ I cry with my voice: have mercy also upon me, and answer me.##note:x[<<Ps 4:1; 5:2; 130:2–4>>]
@ __When thou saidst__, ##Seek##note:x[<<Ps 105:4; Isa 55:6–7>>] ye my face; my heart said unto thee, ##Thy##note:x[<<Jer 29:12–13>>] face, LORD, will I seek.note:tn[__When…__: or, My heart said unto thee, Let my face seek thy face, etc]
@ ##Hide##note:x[<<Ps 69:17; 102:2; 143:7>>] not thy face __far__ from me; put not thy servant away in anger: thou hast been my help; leave me not, neither forsake me, O God of my salvation.
@ ##When##note:x[<<Isa 49:15>>] my father and my mother forsake me, then ##the LORD##note:x[<<John 16:32>>] will ##take me up##note:x[<<Isa 40:11>>].note:tn[__take…__: Heb. gather me]
@ ##Teach##note:x[<<Ps 25:4–5; 86:11>>] me thy way, O LORD, and lead me in a plain path, because of ##mine enemies##note:x[<<Ps 5:8>>].note:tn[__a plain…__: Heb. a way of plainness]note:tn[__mine…__: Heb. those which observe me]
@ Deliver me not over unto the ##will##note:x[<<Ps 31:8; 41:11>>] of mine enemies: for ##false##note:x[<<Matt 26:59–60>>] witnesses are risen up against me, and such as breathe out cruelty.
@ __I had__ ##__fainted__##note:x[<<Ps 56:3; 2 Cor 4:16>>], unless I had believed to see the goodness of the LORD ##in the##note:x[<<Ps 142:5>>] land of the living.
@ ##Wait##note:x[<<Ps 31:24>>] on the LORD: ##be##note:x[<<Ps 31:24>>] of good courage, ##and##note:x[<<Isa 40:31>>] he shall strengthen thine heart: wait, I say, on the LORD.

=== @28

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is My Strength

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Unto thee will I cry, ##O##note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>] LORD my rock; be not silent ##to me##note:tn[Heb. from me]: lest, __if__ thou be silent to me, ##I become##note:x[<<Ps 88:4–6; 143:7>>] like them that go down into the pit.
@ Hear the voice of my supplications, ##when##note:x[<<Ps 141:2; 1 Tim 2:8>>] I cry unto thee, when I lift up my hands toward ##thy holy oracle##note:x[<<Ps 138:2>>].note:tn[__thy…__: or, the oracle of thy sanctuary]
@ Draw me not away with the wicked, and with the workers of iniquity, which ##speak##note:x[<<Ps 12:2; 55:21; Jer 9:8–9>>] peace to their neighbours, but mischief __is__ in their hearts.
@ ##Give##note:x[<<2 Tim 4:14; Rev 18:6>>] them according to their deeds, and according to the wickedness of their endeavours: give them after ##the work##note:x[<<Ps 109:17–21>>] of their hands; render to them their desert.
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Job 34:26–27; Isa 5:12; Rom 1:28>>] they regard not the works of the LORD, nor the operation of his hands, he shall destroy them, and not build them up.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed __be__ the LORD, because he hath heard the voice of my supplications.##note:x[<<Ps 31:21–22; 107:19–22; 118:5>>]
@ The LORD __is__ my ##strength##note:x[<<Eph 6:10>>] and my shield; my ##heart##note:x[<<Ps 56:3–4>>] trusted in him, and I am helped: ##therefore##note:x[<<Ps 30:11–12>>] my heart greatly rejoiceth; and with my song will I praise him.
@ The LORD __is__ ##their##note:tn[or, his] strength, and he __is__ the saving strength of ##his##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:13; Ps 20:6; Isa 61:1>>] anointed.note:tn[__saving…__: Heb. strength of salvations]
@ Save thy people, and ##bless##note:x[<<Deut 9:29>>] thine inheritance: note:x[<<Ps 78:71; Isa 40:11>>]##feed##note:tn[or, rule] them also, and lift them up for ever.

=== @29

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Ascribe Glory to the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Give##note:x[<<Ps 96:7–9; Jer 13:16–18; Rev 5:11–14>>] unto the LORD, O ye mighty, give unto the LORD glory and strength.note:tn[__ye mighty__: Heb. ye sons of the mighty]
@ ##Give##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:28–29>>] unto the LORD the ##glory##note:x[<<Ps 96:8>>] due unto his name; ##worship##note:x[<<Ps 27:4>>] the LORD in the beauty of holiness.note:tn[__the glory…__: Heb. the honour of his name]note:tn[__in…__: or, in his glorious sanctuary]
@ The voice of the LORD __is__ upon the waters: the God of glory ##thundereth##note:x[<<Exod 9:33; Job 37:2–5>>]: the LORD __is__ upon note:x[<<Ps 93:3–4>>]##many##note:tn[or, great] waters.
@ The voice of the LORD __is__ note:x[<<Ps 33:9; Jer 51:15–16>>]##powerful##note:tn[Heb. in power]; the voice of the LORD __is__ ##full of##note:x[<<Ezek 10:5>>] majesty.note:tn[__full…__: Heb. in majesty]
@ ##The voice of the LORD breaketh the cedars; yea, the LORD breaketh the cedars of Lebanon.##note:x[<<Isa 2:13>>]
@ He maketh them also to ##skip##note:x[<<Ps 114:4–7>>] like a calf; Lebanon and ##Sirion##note:x[<<Deut 3:9>>] like a young ##unicorn##note:x[<<Num 23:22>>].
@ The voice of the LORD ##divideth##note:tn[Heb. cutteth out] the ##flames##note:x[<<Exod 9:23; Ps 77:18; 144:5–6>>] of fire.
@ The voice of the LORD shaketh the wilderness; the LORD shaketh the wilderness of ##Kadesh##note:x[<<Num 13:26>>].
@ The voice of the LORD ##maketh##note:x[<<Job 39:1–3>>] the hinds to calve, and ##discovereth##note:x[<<Ezek 20:46–48>>] the forests: and ##in his temple##note:x[<<Ps 46:2–5>>] doth every one speak of __his__ glory.note:tn[__to calve__: or, to be in pain]note:tn[__doth…__: or, every whit of it uttereth, etc]
@ The LORD ##sitteth##note:x[<<Gen 6:17>>] upon the flood; yea, the LORD sitteth ##King##note:x[<<Ps 10:16; 1 Tim 1:17>>] for ever.
@ The LORD will ##give##note:x[<<Isa 40:31>>] strength unto his people; the LORD will ##bless##note:x[<<Num 6:24–27; 2 Thess 3:16>>] his people with peace.

=== @30

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm __and__ Song __at__ the dedication of the house of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== You Turned My Mourning into Dancing

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will ##extol##note:x[<<Dan 4:37>>] thee, O LORD; for thou hast lifted me up, and ##hast not##note:x[<<Ps 25:2; 35:19>>] made my foes to rejoice over me.
@ O LORD my God, I cried unto thee, ##and##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:5; Ps 103:3–4; 147:3>>] thou hast healed me.
@ O LORD, thou hast ##brought##note:x[<<Ps 16:10; 86:13; Isa 38:17–18>>] up my soul from the grave: thou hast kept me alive, that I should not go down to the pit.
@ ##Sing##note:x[<<Ps 97:12>>] unto the LORD, O ye saints of his, and give thanks ##at the remembrance##note:x[<<Ps 97:12>>] of his holiness.note:tn[__at…__: or, to the memorial]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Isa 54:7–8; 2 Cor 4:17>>] his anger __endureth but__ a moment; in his favour __is__ life: ##weeping##note:x[<<Ps 126:5–6>>] may endure for a night, but ##joy##note:tn[Heb. singing] __cometh__ in the morning.note:tn[__his anger…__: Heb. there is but a moment in his anger]note:tn[__for a night__: Heb. in the evening]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##And##note:x[<<Luke 12:19; 2 Cor 12:7>>] in my prosperity I said, ##I shall##note:x[<<Ps 119:117>>] never be moved.
@ LORD, ##by thy##note:x[<<Ps 30:5>>] favour ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 104:29; 143:7>>] hast made my mountain to stand strong: thou didst hide thy face, __and__ I was troubled.note:tn[__made…__: Heb. settled strength for my mountain]
@ I cried to thee, O LORD; and ##unto##note:x[<<Ps 34:6; 130:1–2; Phil 4:6–7>>] the LORD I made supplication.
@ ##What##note:x[<<Ps 6:5; 115:17–18; Isa 38:18>>] profit __is there__ in my blood, when I go down to the pit? Shall the dust praise thee? shall it declare thy truth?
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 143:1, 7–9>>], O LORD, and have mercy upon me: LORD, ##be thou##note:x[<<Ps 54:4>>] my helper.
@ Thou hast turned for me my mourning into ##dancing##note:x[<<Eccl 3:4; Jer 31:4, 13–14>>]: thou hast put off my sackcloth, and girded me with gladness;
@ To the end that ##__my__ glory##note:x[<<Ps 16:9>>] may sing praise to thee, ##and##note:x[<<Luke 19:40>>] not be silent. O LORD my God, ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 145:2>>] give thanks unto thee for ever.note:tn[__my glory__: that is, my tongue, or, my soul]

=== @31

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Into Your Hands I Commit My Spirit

[.parallel]
(<<Luke 23:44–49>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ In ##thee##note:x[<<Ps 25:2; 71:1–2; Rom 10:11>>], O LORD, do I put my trust; let me never be ashamed: deliver me in thy righteousness.
@ ##Bow##note:x[<<Ps 71:2; 86:1>>] down thine ear to me; ##deliver##note:x[<<Ps 102:2>>] me speedily: be thou my strong rock, for an house of defence to save me.note:tn[__my…__: Heb. to me for a rock of strength]
@ For thou __art__ my rock and my fortress; therefore ##for thy##note:x[<<Ps 79:9>>] name’s sake ##lead##note:x[<<Ps 25:5; 143:10–11>>] me, and guide me.
@ ##Pull##note:x[<<Ps 25:15; 57:6; 140:5>>] me out of the net that they have laid privily for me: for thou __art__ my strength.
@ ##Into##note:x[<<Luke 23:46; Acts 7:59>>] thine hand I commit my spirit: thou hast redeemed me, O LORD ##God##note:x[<<Deut 32:4>>] of truth.
@ I have ##hated##note:x[<<Ps 26:5>>] them that regard ##lying##note:x[<<Ps 24:4; Jer 10:8>>] vanities: but I trust in the LORD.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 49:13>>] be glad and rejoice in thy mercy: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 119:153>>] thou hast considered my trouble; thou hast ##known##note:x[<<Isa 63:9>>] my soul in adversities;
@ And hast not ##shut me##note:x[<<Deut 32:30>>] up into the hand of the enemy: thou hast ##set##note:x[<<Job 36:16; Ps 4:1>>] my feet in a large room.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Have mercy upon me, O LORD, for I am in trouble: ##mine##note:x[<<Ps 6:7; 88:9>>] eye is consumed with grief, __yea__, ##my soul##note:x[<<Ps 73:26>>] and my belly.
@ For ##my life##note:x[<<Ps 78:33; 88:15>>] is spent with grief, and my years with sighing: my strength faileth because of mine iniquity, and my ##bones##note:x[<<Ps 32:3–4>>] are consumed.
@ I was a reproach among all mine enemies, but ##especially##note:x[<<Ps 38:11; 88:8, 18>>] among my neighbours, and a fear to mine acquaintance: they that did see me without fled from me.
@ I am ##forgotten##note:x[<<Ps 88:4–5>>] as a dead man out of mind: I am like ##a broken vessel##note:x[<<Ps 2:9; 119:83>>].note:tn[__a broken…__: Heb. a vessel that perisheth]
@ For ##I have##note:x[<<Jer 20:10>>] heard the slander of many: ##fear##note:x[<<Lam 2:22>>] __was__ on every side: ##while##note:x[<<Matt 27:1>>] they took counsel together against me, they devised to take away my life.
@ But I trusted in thee, O LORD: I said, ##Thou##note:x[<<Ps 18:2; 43:5; 56:3–4>>] __art__ my God.
@ ##My times##note:x[<<Eccl 3:1–8; John 17:1; Acts 1:7>>] __are__ in thy hand: deliver me from the hand of mine enemies, and from them that persecute me.
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Ps 4:6; 80:3>>] thy face to shine upon thy servant: ##save##note:x[<<Ps 6:4>>] me for thy mercies’ sake.
@ ##Let me##note:x[<<Ps 25:2–3; 34:5>>] not be ashamed, O LORD; for I have called upon thee: let the ##wicked##note:x[<<Jer 20:11>>] be ashamed, __and__ let them be silent in the grave.note:tn[__silent…__: or, cut off for]
@ Let ##the lying##note:x[<<Isa 54:17>>] lips be put to silence; which speak ##grievous things##note:x[<<Ps 94:4; Jude 1:15>>] proudly and contemptuously against the righteous.note:tn[__grievous…__: Heb. a hard thing]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##__Oh__##note:x[<<Isa 64:4; Lam 3:23–25; 1 Cor 2:9>>] how great __is__ thy goodness, which thou hast laid up for them that fear thee; __which__ thou hast wrought for them that trust in thee before the sons of men!
@ Thou shalt ##hide##note:x[<<Ps 27:5; 32:7; 91:1–4>>] them in the secret of thy presence from the pride of man: thou shalt keep them secretly in a pavilion from the strife of tongues.
@ Blessed __be__ the LORD: for he hath shewed me his ##marvellous##note:x[<<Ps 17:7; 1 Pet 2:9>>] kindness in a note:x[<<1 Sam 23:7–13>>]##strong##note:tn[or, fenced] city.
@ For ##I said##note:x[<<Ps 116:11>>] in my haste, ##I am##note:x[<<Ps 88:16>>] cut off from before thine eyes: ##nevertheless##note:x[<<Heb 5:7>>] thou heardest the voice of my supplications when I cried unto thee.
@ ##O love##note:x[<<Ps 97:10>>] the LORD, all ye his ##saints##note:x[<<Ps 97:10>>]: __for__ the LORD preserveth the faithful, and ##plentifully##note:x[<<Ps 94:2>>] rewardeth the proud doer.
@ ##Be of##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; Isa 35:3–4>>] good courage, and he shall strengthen your heart, ##all ye##note:x[<<Ps 146:5>>] that hope in the LORD.

=== @32

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David, Maschil.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Joy of Forgiveness

[.parallel]
(<<Romans 4:1–12>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __is he whose__ ##transgression##note:x[<<Isa 44:22; Rom 4:6–8>>] __is__ forgiven, __whose__ sin __is__ ##covered##note:x[<<Ps 85:2>>].note:tn[__A Psalm…__: or, A Psalm of David giving instruction]
@ Blessed __is__ the man unto whom ##the LORD##note:x[<<2 Cor 5:19–21>>] imputeth not iniquity, and in ##whose##note:x[<<John 1:47; Rev 14:5>>] spirit __there is__ no guile.
@ ##When##note:x[<<Prov 28:13>>] I kept silence, my ##bones##note:x[<<Ps 38:3>>] waxed old through my ##roaring##note:x[<<Ps 38:8>>] all the day long.
@ For day and night thy ##hand##note:x[<<1 Sam 5:11; Ps 38:2–8; 39:10–11>>] was heavy upon me: my moisture is turned into the drought of summer. Selah.
@ I ##acknowledged##note:x[<<2 Sam 12:13; Prov 28:13>>] my sin unto thee, and mine iniquity have I not hid. I said, I will confess my transgressions unto the LORD; and thou ##forgavest##note:x[<<2 Sam 12:13>>] the iniquity of my sin. Selah.
@ For this shall every one that is godly ##pray##note:x[<<Isa 55:6; 2 Cor 6:2>>] unto thee in a time when thou mayest be found: surely ##in the floods##note:x[<<Isa 43:2>>] of great waters they shall not come nigh unto him.note:tn[__when…__: Heb. of finding]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou __art__ ##my##note:x[<<Ps 9:9; 27:5; 119:114>>] hiding place; thou shalt preserve me from trouble; thou shalt compass me about with songs of deliverance. Selah.
@ I will ##instruct##note:x[<<Matt 11:29>>] thee and teach thee in the way which thou shalt go: ##I will guide##note:x[<<Ps 33:18; Prov 3:5–6>>] thee with mine eye.note:tn[__guide…__: Heb. counsel thee, mine eye shall be upon thee]
@ ##Be ye##note:x[<<Prov 26:3>>] not as the horse, __or__ as the mule, __which__ have ##no##note:x[<<Job 35:11; Jer 4:22>>] understanding: whose mouth must be held in with bit and bridle, lest they come near unto thee.
@ ##Many##note:x[<<Prov 13:21>>] sorrows __shall be__ to the wicked: ##but##note:x[<<Prov 16:20; Isa 12:2–3>>] he that trusteth in the LORD, mercy shall compass him about.
@ ##Be glad##note:x[<<Ps 64:10; 97:12; Phil 4:4>>] in the LORD, and rejoice, ye righteous: and shout for joy, all __ye that are__ upright in heart.

=== @33

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise to the Creator

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 148:1–14>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Rejoice##note:x[<<Ps 32:11; Phil 4:4>>] in the LORD, O ye righteous: __for__ ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 147:1>>] is comely for the upright.
@ ##Praise##note:x[<<Ps 98:4–5; 144:9; 150:3–6>>] the LORD with harp: sing unto him with the psaltery __and__ an instrument of ten strings.
