@ Sing unto him ##a new##note:x[<<Ps 96:1; Isa 42:10; Eph 5:19>>] song; play skilfully with a loud noise.
@ For ##the word##note:x[<<Ps 12:6>>] of the LORD __is__ right; and ##all his##note:x[<<Deut 32:4; Titus 1:2>>] works __are done__ in truth.
@ ##He##note:x[<<Ps 11:7; 45:7>>] loveth righteousness and judgment: the ##earth##note:x[<<Ps 119:64>>] is full of the ##goodness##note:tn[or, mercy] of the LORD.
@ ##By the##note:x[<<Heb 11:3; 2 Pet 3:5>>] word of the LORD were the heavens made; and all the host of them by the ##breath##note:x[<<Job 33:4>>] of his mouth.
@ He gathereth the waters of the sea together as an ##heap##note:x[<<Exod 15:8; Josh 3:13, 16>>]: he layeth up the depth in storehouses.
@ Let all ##the earth##note:x[<<Ps 96:9–10; Rev 14:6–7; 15:4>>] fear the LORD: let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of him.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Gen 1:3; Ps 33:6; 148:5–6>>] he spake, and it was __done;__ he commanded, and it stood fast.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Job 5:12–13>>] note:x[<<Isa 44:25>>]##bringeth##note:tn[Heb. maketh frustrate] the counsel of the heathen to nought: ##he maketh##note:x[<<Ps 21:11>>] the devices of the people of none effect.
@ ##The counsel##note:x[<<Job 23:13; Prov 19:21; Isa 46:10>>] of the LORD standeth for ever, the thoughts of his heart to all generations.note:tn[__to all…__: Heb. to generation and generation]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 144:15>>] __is__ the nation whose God __is__ the LORD; __and__ the ##people##note:x[<<John 15:16; 1 Pet 2:9>>] __whom__ he hath chosen for his own inheritance.
@ The LORD ##looketh##note:x[<<Job 28:24; Ps 11:4>>] from heaven; he ##beholdeth##note:x[<<Ps 53:2>>] all the sons of men.
@ From the place of his habitation he looketh upon all the inhabitants of the earth.
@ He fashioneth their hearts alike; he ##considereth##note:x[<<Ps 44:21; Jer 32:19; Hos 7:2>>] all their works.
@ There is ##no king##note:x[<<Ps 44:3, 6–7>>] saved by the multitude of an host: a ##mighty##note:x[<<Jer 9:23>>] man is not delivered by much strength.
@ ##An horse##note:x[<<Ps 20:7; Prov 21:31>>] __is__ a vain thing for safety: neither shall he deliver __any__ by ##his great##note:x[<<Ps 147:10>>] strength.
@ Behold, ##the eye##note:x[<<Job 36:7; Ps 147:11; 1 Pet 3:12>>] of the LORD __is__ upon them that fear him, upon them that hope in his mercy;
@ To deliver their soul from death, and ##to keep##note:x[<<Job 5:19–22; Ps 37:19; Prov 10:3>>] them alive in famine.
@ Our ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; 130:5–6; Isa 40:31>>] waiteth for the LORD: he __is__ our help and our shield.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 13:5; Zech 10:7; John 16:22>>] our heart shall rejoice in him, because we have trusted in his holy name.
@ ##Let thy mercy, O LORD, be upon us, according as we hope in thee.##note:x[<<Ps 13:5; 119:76; Matt 9:29>>]

=== @34

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David, when he changed his behaviour before Abimelech; who drove him away, and he departed.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Taste and See That the LORD Is Good

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 21:8–15>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will bless the LORD at all times: his praise __shall__ continually __be__ in my mouth.##note:x[<<Ps 145:1–2; Col 3:17; 1 Thess 5:18>>]note:tn[__Abimelech__: or, Achish]
@ My soul shall ##make##note:x[<<Jer 9:24; 1 Cor 1:31>>] her boast in the LORD: ##the humble##note:x[<<Ps 119:74>>] shall hear __thereof__, and be glad.
@ O ##magnify##note:x[<<Ps 69:30; Luke 1:46>>] the LORD with me, and ##let us##note:x[<<Ps 148:1–14>>] exalt his name together.
@ I ##sought##note:x[<<Ps 18:6; 116:1–6; Matt 7:7>>] the LORD, and he heard me, and delivered me from all my fears.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 123:1–2; Heb 12:2>>] looked unto him, ##and were##note:x[<<Ps 18:28>>] lightened: and their faces were not ashamed.note:tn[__were lightened__: or, they flowed unto him]
@ ##This##note:x[<<Ps 3:4; 10:17>>] poor man cried, and the LORD heard __him__, and ##saved##note:x[<<Ps 34:17–19>>] him out of all his troubles.
@ ##The angel##note:x[<<Ps 91:11; Dan 6:22; Heb 1:14>>] of the LORD encampeth round about them that fear him, and delivereth them.
@ O ##taste##note:x[<<Ps 119:103; Heb 6:4–5; 1 Pet 2:2–3>>] and see that the LORD __is__ good: blessed __is__ the man __that__ trusteth in him.
@ O fear the LORD, ye his saints: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 23:1; Rom 8:32; Phil 4:19>>] __there is__ no want to them that fear him.
@ The young ##lions##note:x[<<Ps 104:21>>] do lack, and suffer hunger: ##but##note:x[<<Ps 84:11; Matt 6:32>>] they that seek the LORD shall not want any good __thing__.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Come, ye children, hearken unto me: ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 111:10; Prov 1:7; 2:1–9>>] teach you the fear of the LORD.
@ ##What##note:x[<<Deut 6:2; 30:20; 1 Pet 3:10–11>>] man __is he that__ desireth life, __and__ loveth __many__ days, that he may see good?
@ ##Keep##note:x[<<Jas 1:19; 3:5–10>>] thy tongue from evil, and thy lips from ##speaking##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:22>>] guile.
@ ##Depart##note:x[<<Ps 37:27; 1 Pet 3:11>>] from evil, and do good; ##seek##note:x[<<Heb 12:14>>] peace, and pursue it.
@ ##The##note:x[<<Ps 33:18; 1 Pet 3:12>>] eyes of the LORD __are__ upon the righteous, ##and##note:x[<<Ps 34:17>>] his ears __are open__ unto their cry.
@ The ##face##note:x[<<Jer 44:11>>] of the LORD __is__ against them that do evil, ##to cut##note:x[<<Prov 10:7; Jer 17:13>>] off the remembrance of them from the earth.
@ __The righteous__ ##cry##note:x[<<Ps 34:19; 145:18–20; Isa 65:24>>], and the LORD heareth, and delivereth them out of all their troubles.
@ The LORD ##__is__ nigh##note:x[<<Ps 145:18>>] ##unto them##note:x[<<Ps 147:3>>] that are of a broken heart; and saveth ##such as##note:x[<<Isa 57:15>>] be of a contrite spirit.note:tn[__unto…__: Heb. to the broken of heart]note:tn[__of a contrite…__: Heb. contrite of spirit]
@ ##Many##note:x[<<1 Pet 4:12–13>>] __are__ the afflictions of the righteous: ##but##note:x[<<Ps 34:6, 17>>] the LORD delivereth him out of them all.
@ ##He keepeth all his bones: not one of them is broken.##note:x[<<Ps 35:10; 91:12; John 19:36>>]
@ ##Evil##note:x[<<Ps 94:23; Isa 3:11>>] shall slay the wicked: and ##they##note:x[<<Ps 37:12–15>>] that hate the righteous shall be ##desolate##note:tn[or, guilty].
@ The LORD ##redeemeth##note:x[<<Ps 31:5; 103:4; 1 Pet 1:18–19>>] the soul of his servants: and none of them that trust in him shall be ##desolate##note:tn[or, guilty].

=== @35

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Contend with My Opponents, O LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Plead##note:x[<<Ps 43:1; 119:154; Jer 51:36>>] __my cause__, O LORD, with them that strive with me: fight against them that fight against me.
@ ##Take hold of shield and buckler, and stand up for mine help.##note:x[<<Exod 15:3; Deut 32:41–42; Isa 42:13>>]
@ Draw out also the spear, and ##stop##note:x[<<Ps 27:2>>] __the way__ against them that persecute me: ##say##note:x[<<Ps 51:12; Isa 12:2>>] unto my soul, I __am__ thy salvation.
@ Let them be ##confounded##note:x[<<Ps 35:26; 70:2–3>>] and put to shame that seek after my soul: let them be ##turned##note:x[<<Jer 46:5>>] back and brought to confusion that devise my hurt.
@ Let them be ##as chaff##note:x[<<Job 21:18; Ps 1:4; Isa 29:5>>] before the wind: and let the angel of the LORD chase __them__.
@ Let ##their##note:x[<<Ps 73:18; Prov 4:19; Jer 23:12>>] way be dark and slippery: and let the angel of the LORD persecute them.note:tn[__dark…__: Heb. darkness and slipperiness]
@ For ##without##note:x[<<John 15:25>>] cause have they ##hid##note:x[<<Ps 9:15; 140:5>>] for me their net __in__ a pit, __which__ without cause they have digged for my soul.
@ ##Let destruction##note:x[<<Luke 21:34>>] come upon him at unawares; and let his ##net##note:x[<<Ps 141:9–10; Prov 5:22>>] that he hath hid catch himself: into that very destruction let him fall.note:tn[__at…__: Heb. which he knoweth not of]
@ ##And my soul shall be joyful in the LORD: it shall rejoice in his salvation.##note:x[<<Isa 61:10>>]
@ All my bones shall say, LORD, ##who##note:x[<<Exod 15:11>>] __is__ like unto thee, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 109:31; 140:12>>] deliverest the poor from him that is too strong for him, yea, the poor and the needy from him that spoileth him?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##False witnesses##note:x[<<Ps 27:12; Matt 26:59–60>>] did rise up; they laid to my charge __things__ that I knew not.note:tn[__False…__: Heb. Witnesses of wrong]note:tn[__they…__: Heb. they asked me]
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 38:20; Jer 18:20; John 10:32>>] rewarded me evil for good __to__ the ##spoiling##note:tn[Heb. depriving] of my soul.
@ But as for me, ##when##note:x[<<Job 30:25; Ps 69:10–11; Matt 5:44>>] they were sick, my clothing __was__ sackcloth: I ##humbled##note:tn[or, afflicted] my soul with fasting; and my prayer returned into mine own bosom.
@ I ##behaved myself##note:tn[Heb. walked] as though __he had been__ my friend __or__ brother: ##I bowed##note:x[<<Luke 19:41–42>>] down heavily, as one that mourneth __for his__ mother.note:tn[__as though…__: Heb. as a friend, as a brother to me]
@ But in mine note:x[<<Jer 20:10>>]##adversity##note:tn[Heb. halting] they rejoiced, and gathered themselves together: __yea__, ##the abjects##note:x[<<Mark 14:65>>] gathered themselves together against me, and I knew __it__ not; they did tear __me__, and ceased not:
@ With hypocritical mockers in feasts, they ##gnashed##note:x[<<Job 16:9; Ps 37:12; Lam 2:16>>] upon me with their teeth.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Lord, how long wilt thou ##look##note:x[<<Hab 1:13>>] on? ##rescue##note:x[<<Ps 22:20–21; 57:4>>] my soul from their destructions, my ##darling##note:tn[Heb. only one] from the lions.
@ I will ##give##note:x[<<Ps 22:22–25; Heb 2:12>>] thee thanks in the great congregation: I will praise thee among ##much##note:tn[Heb. strong] people.
@ Let not them that are mine enemies ##wrongfully##note:tn[Heb. falsely] rejoice over me: __neither__ let them ##wink##note:x[<<Prov 6:13>>] with the eye ##that hate##note:x[<<Ps 69:4; John 15:25>>] me without a cause.
@ For they speak not peace: ##but##note:x[<<Jer 11:19; Matt 26:4>>] they devise deceitful matters against __them that are__ ##quiet##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:22–23>>] in the land.
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Ps 22:13; Luke 11:53–54>>], they opened their mouth wide against me, __and__ said, ##Aha##note:x[<<Ps 40:15>>], aha, our eye hath seen __it__.
@ ##__This__##note:x[<<Exod 3:7>>] thou hast seen, O LORD: ##keep##note:x[<<Ps 28:1>>] not silence: O Lord, ##be##note:x[<<Ps 10:1>>] not far from me.
@ ##Stir##note:x[<<Ps 7:6; 44:23>>] up thyself, and awake to my judgment, __even__ unto my cause, ##my God##note:x[<<Ps 89:26>>] and my Lord.
@ ##Judge##note:x[<<Ps 7:8; 26:1; 43:1>>] me, O LORD my God, according to thy righteousness; and let them not rejoice over me.
@ Let them not say in their hearts, Ah, so would ##we have##note:x[<<Lam 2:16>>] it: let them not say, We have swallowed him up.note:tn[__Ah…__: Heb. Ah, ah, our soul]
@ Let them be ##ashamed##note:x[<<Ps 132:18>>] and brought to confusion together that rejoice at mine hurt: let them be ##clothed##note:x[<<Job 8:22>>] with shame and dishonour that ##magnify##note:x[<<Ps 38:16>>] __themselves__ against me.
@ Let them ##shout##note:x[<<Ps 40:16>>] for joy, and be glad, that favour my righteous cause: yea, let them say continually, Let the LORD be magnified, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 149:4; Jer 32:40–41>>] hath pleasure in the prosperity of his servant.note:tn[__righteous…__: Heb. righteousness]
@ ##And my tongue shall speak of thy righteousness __and__ of thy praise all the day long.##note:x[<<Ps 71:24; 145:21>>]

=== @36

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __A Psalm__ of David the servant of the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Transgression of the Wicked

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The transgression of the wicked saith within my heart, __that there is__ ##no##note:x[<<Prov 16:6; Rom 3:18>>] fear of God before his eyes.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Deut 29:19; Ps 10:3; 49:18>>] flattereth himself in his own eyes, until his iniquity be found to be hateful.note:tn[__until…__: Heb. to find his iniquity to hate]
@ ##The words##note:x[<<Ps 55:21>>] of his mouth __are__ iniquity and deceit: he hath left off to be wise, __and__ to do good.
@ He ##deviseth##note:x[<<Prov 4:16; Mic 2:1>>] ##mischief##note:tn[or, vanity] upon his bed; he ##setteth##note:x[<<Isa 65:2>>] himself in a way __that is__ not good; he abhorreth not evil.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thy ##mercy##note:x[<<Ps 103:11; 108:4>>], O LORD, __is__ in the heavens; __and__ thy ##faithfulness##note:x[<<Ps 100:5>>] __reacheth__ unto the clouds.
@ Thy righteousness __is__ like the great mountains; thy ##judgments##note:x[<<Ps 77:19; Isa 40:28; Rom 11:33>>] __are__ a great deep: O LORD, thou preservest man and beast.note:tn[__the…__: Heb. the mountains of God]
@ ##How##note:x[<<Ps 86:5; 1 John 3:1>>] ##excellent##note:tn[Heb. precious] __is__ thy lovingkindness, O God! therefore the children of men ##put their##note:x[<<Ruth 2:12>>] trust under the shadow of thy wings.
@ They shall be ##abundantly##note:x[<<Ps 16:11; 65:4>>] satisfied with the fatness of thy house; ##and thou##note:x[<<Ps 16:11>>] shalt make them drink of the river of thy pleasures.note:tn[__abundantly…__: Heb. watered]
@ ##For##note:x[<<John 4:14>>] with thee __is__ the fountain of life: ##in thy##note:x[<<John 8:12; 1 John 1:7>>] light shall we see light.
@ O ##continue##note:tn[Heb. draw out at length] thy lovingkindness unto them ##that##note:x[<<Ps 9:10; Jer 24:7; Heb 8:11>>] know thee; and thy righteousness to the upright in heart.
@ Let not the ##foot##note:x[<<Ps 12:3–5; Isa 51:23>>] of pride come against me, and let not the ##hand##note:x[<<Ps 16:8>>] of the wicked remove me.
@ There are the workers of iniquity fallen: they are cast down, and ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 1:5>>] not be able to rise.

=== @37

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Delight Yourself in the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<1 Kings 2:1–9>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Fret##note:x[<<Ps 37:7; Prov 24:19>>] not thyself because of evildoers, ##neither##note:x[<<Prov 23:17>>] be thou envious against the workers of iniquity.
@ ##For they shall soon be cut down like the grass, and wither as the green herb.##note:x[<<Ps 90:5–6; 92:7; Jas 1:10–11>>]
@ ##Trust##note:x[<<Jer 17:7–8; 1 Cor 15:57–58>>] in the LORD, and do good; __so__ shalt thou dwell in the land, and ##verily##note:tn[Heb. in truth, or, stableness] thou shalt ##be fed##note:x[<<Matt 6:31–33>>].
@ Delight thyself also in the LORD; ##and##note:x[<<Ps 145:19; John 15:7, 16>>] he shall give thee the desires of thine heart.
@ ##Commit##note:x[<<Prov 16:3; Phil 4:6–7; 1 Pet 5:7>>] thy way unto the LORD; trust also in him; and he shall bring __it__ to pass.note:tn[__Commit…__: Heb. Roll thy way upon]
@ And ##he shall##note:x[<<Isa 54:17; Mic 7:8–9>>] bring forth thy righteousness as the ##light##note:x[<<Job 11:17>>], and thy judgment as the noonday.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Rest in##note:tn[Heb. Be silent to] the LORD, and ##wait##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; Heb 10:36–37; Jas 5:7–11>>] patiently for him: fret not thyself because of him who prospereth in his way, because of the man who bringeth wicked devices to pass.
@ ##Cease##note:x[<<Prov 14:29; 16:32; Jas 1:19–20>>] from anger, and forsake wrath: fret not thyself in any wise to do evil.
@ For evildoers shall be cut off: but those that wait upon the LORD, they shall ##inherit##note:x[<<Ps 25:13; 37:29; Isa 60:21>>] the earth.
@ For ##yet##note:x[<<Job 24:24; 1 Pet 4:7>>] a little while, and the ##wicked##note:x[<<Ps 37:35–36>>] __shall__ not __be:__ yea, thou shalt diligently consider his place, and it __shall__ not __be__.
@ But ##the meek##note:x[<<Matt 5:5; Gal 5:22–23>>] shall inherit the earth; and shall ##delight##note:x[<<Isa 26:3>>] themselves in the abundance of peace.
@ ##The wicked##note:x[<<Matt 26:16>>] note:x[<<Mic 2:1>>]##plotteth##note:tn[or, practiseth] against the just, and ##gnasheth##note:x[<<Ps 35:16>>] upon him with his teeth.
@ The Lord shall ##laugh##note:x[<<Ps 2:4; Prov 1:26>>] at him: for he seeth that ##his day##note:x[<<1 Sam 26:10>>] is coming.
@ The wicked have drawn out the sword, and have bent their bow, to cast down the poor and needy, __and__ to ##slay##note:x[<<Prov 29:27>>] such as be of upright conversation.note:tn[__such…__: Heb. the upright of way]
@ Their ##sword##note:x[<<Ps 7:14–15; 35:8>>] shall enter into their own heart, and their bows shall be broken.
@ ##A little that a righteous man hath __is__ better than the riches of many wicked.##note:x[<<Prov 15:16–17; 16:8; 1 Tim 6:6>>]
@ For the arms of the wicked shall be broken: but the ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 63:8; 145:14; Isa 41:10>>] upholdeth the righteous.
@ The LORD ##knoweth##note:x[<<Ps 1:6>>] the days of the upright: and ##their##note:x[<<Ps 103:17; Rom 6:23>>] inheritance shall be for ever.
@ They shall not be ashamed in the evil time: and in the ##days##note:x[<<Job 5:20–22; Ps 33:19; Prov 10:3>>] of famine they shall be satisfied.
@ ##But the##note:x[<<Ps 68:2; Luke 13:3>>] wicked shall perish, and the enemies of the LORD __shall be__ as the fat of lambs: they shall consume; into ##smoke##note:x[<<Ps 102:3>>] shall they consume away.note:tn[__the fat__: Heb. the preciousness]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The wicked ##borroweth##note:x[<<Prov 22:7>>], and payeth not again: but the ##righteous##note:x[<<Ps 112:5; Heb 13:16>>] sheweth mercy, and giveth.
@ For __such as be__ ##blessed##note:x[<<Prov 3:33>>] of him shall inherit the earth; and __they that be__ ##cursed##note:x[<<1 Cor 16:22>>] of him shall be ##cut off##note:x[<<Ps 37:9>>].
@ The ##steps##note:x[<<Ps 119:133; Prov 16:9>>] of a __good__ man are note:x[<<Prov 4:26>>]##ordered##note:tn[or, established] by the LORD: and he delighteth in his way.
@ ##Though##note:x[<<Ps 145:14; Prov 24:16>>] he fall, he shall not be utterly cast down: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 145:14>>] the LORD upholdeth __him with__ his hand.
@ I have been young, and __now__ am old; ##yet##note:x[<<Ps 37:28; Heb 13:5>>] have I not seen the righteous forsaken, ##nor his seed##note:x[<<Ps 25:13>>] begging bread.
@ __He is__ ##ever##note:tn[Heb. all the day] ##merciful##note:x[<<Ps 37:21; 112:5; Matt 5:7>>], and lendeth; and his seed __is__ blessed.
@ ##Depart##note:x[<<Ps 34:14; Prov 16:6>>] from evil, and ##do good##note:x[<<1 Thess 5:15>>]; and dwell for evermore.
@ For the LORD ##loveth##note:x[<<Ps 11:7>>] judgment, and forsaketh not his saints; they are preserved for ever: ##but##note:x[<<Ps 21:10; Prov 2:22>>] the seed of the wicked shall be cut off.
@ ##The righteous shall inherit the land, and dwell therein for ever.##note:x[<<Ps 37:9; Prov 2:21; 2 Pet 3:13>>]
@ ##The mouth of the righteous speaketh wisdom, and his tongue talketh of judgment.##note:x[<<Prov 15:7; 25:11–13; Col 4:6>>]
@ The ##law##note:x[<<Deut 6:6; Ps 40:8; Isa 51:7>>] of his God __is__ in his heart; none of his ##steps##note:tn[or, goings] shall slide.
@ The wicked watcheth the righteous, and seeketh to slay him.
@ The LORD ##will not##note:x[<<2 Pet 2:9>>] leave him in his hand, nor ##condemn##note:x[<<Ps 109:31; Rom 8:1>>] him when he is judged.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Wait##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; 37:9; Prov 20:22>>] on the LORD, and keep his way, and he shall exalt thee to inherit the land: when the wicked are cut off, thou shalt see __it__.
@ ##I have##note:x[<<Job 5:3; 21:7–17; Ps 73:3–11>>] seen the wicked in great power, and spreading himself like a green bay tree.note:tn[__a green…__: or, a green tree that groweth in his own soil]
@ ##Yet he passed away, and, lo, he __was__ not: yea, I sought him, but he could not be found.##note:x[<<Ps 37:10; Acts 12:22–23>>]
@ ##Mark the perfect __man__, and behold the upright: for the end of __that__ man __is__ peace.##note:x[<<Isa 32:17; Acts 7:59–60; 2 Tim 4:6–8>>]
@ ##But the transgressors shall be destroyed together: the end of the wicked shall be cut off.##note:x[<<Ps 52:5; Prov 14:32>>]
@ But the ##salvation##note:x[<<Isa 12:2>>] of the righteous __is__ of the LORD: __he is__ their ##strength##note:x[<<Ps 9:9; 91:15>>] in the time of trouble.
@ And ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 31:5; Dan 3:17>>] shall help them, and deliver them: he shall deliver them from the wicked, and save them, ##because##note:x[<<1 Chr 5:20>>] they trust in him.

=== @38

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David, to bring to remembrance.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Do Not Rebuke Me in Your Anger

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 6:1–10>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD, ##rebuke##note:x[<<Ps 6:1; Isa 54:8; Heb 12:5–11>>] me not in thy wrath: neither chasten me in thy hot displeasure.
@ For ##thine##note:x[<<Job 6:4>>] arrows stick fast in me, and ##thy hand##note:x[<<Ps 32:4>>] presseth me sore.
@ __There is__ no soundness in my flesh ##because##note:x[<<Ps 51:8>>] of thine anger; ##neither##note:x[<<Ps 6:2; 51:8>>] __is there any__ ##rest##note:tn[Heb. peace, or, health] in my bones because of my sin.
@ For ##mine##note:x[<<Ezra 9:6; Ps 40:12>>] iniquities are gone over mine head: ##as an##note:x[<<Matt 11:28>>] heavy burden they are too heavy for me.
@ ##My wounds##note:x[<<Isa 1:5–6>>] stink __and__ are corrupt because of my foolishness.
@ I am ##troubled##note:tn[Heb. wried]; I am ##bowed##note:x[<<Ps 35:14>>] down greatly; I go ##mourning##note:x[<<Job 30:28; Ps 42:9>>] all the day long.
@ For my loins are filled with a loathsome __disease:__ and __there is__ no soundness in my flesh.
@ I am feeble and sore broken: I have ##roared##note:x[<<Job 3:24; 30:28; Ps 32:3>>] by reason of the disquietness of my heart.
@ Lord, all my desire __is__ before thee; and my ##groaning##note:x[<<Ps 102:5; Rom 8:26–27; 2 Cor 5:2>>] is not hid from thee.
@ My heart panteth, my strength faileth me: as for ##the light##note:x[<<Ps 6:7; 69:3; 88:9>>] of mine eyes, it also is gone from me.note:tn[__is gone…__: Heb. is not with me]
@ My ##lovers##note:x[<<Job 19:13–17; Ps 31:11>>] and my friends stand aloof from my note:x[<<Luke 23:49>>]##sore##note:tn[Heb. stroke]; and my kinsmen stand afar off.note:tn[__my kinsmen__: or, my neighbours]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They also that seek after my life ##lay snares##note:x[<<2 Sam 17:1–3; Ps 140:5>>] __for me:__ and they that seek my hurt ##speak##note:x[<<Ps 35:20>>] mischievous things, and imagine deceits all the day long.
@ ##But I, as a deaf __man__, heard not; and __I was__ as a dumb man __that__ openeth not his mouth.##note:x[<<Ps 39:2, 9; Isa 53:7>>]
@ Thus I was as a man ##that heareth##note:x[<<Amos 5:13; Mark 15:3–5; John 8:6>>] not, and in whose mouth __are__ no reproofs.
@ For in thee, O LORD, ##do##note:x[<<Ps 39:7>>] I hope: thou wilt note:x[<<Ps 138:3>>]##hear##note:tn[or, answer], O Lord my God.note:tn[__in…__: or, thee do I wait for]
@ For I said, __Hear me__, lest __otherwise__ they should rejoice over me: when my ##foot##note:x[<<Deut 32:35; Ps 94:18>>] slippeth, they magnify __themselves__ against me.
@ For I __am__ ready ##to halt##note:x[<<Ps 35:15; Mic 4:6–7>>], and my ##sorrow##note:x[<<Ps 6:6>>] __is__ continually before me.note:tn[__to halt__: Heb. for halting]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Job 33:27; Ps 32:5; Prov 28:13>>] I will declare mine iniquity; I will be sorry for my sin.
@ But mine enemies __are__ lively, __and__ they are strong: and ##they that##note:x[<<Ps 35:19; Matt 10:22; John 15:18–25>>] hate me wrongfully are multiplied.note:tn[__are lively…__: Heb. being living, are strong]
@ They also that ##render##note:x[<<Ps 35:12; Jer 18:20>>] evil for good are mine adversaries; ##because##note:x[<<1 John 3:12>>] I follow __the thing that__ good __is__.
@ Forsake me not, O LORD: ##O my God##note:x[<<Ps 22:11, 19, 24>>], be not far from me.
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Ps 40:13>>] haste to help me, ##O Lord##note:x[<<Ps 27:1; Isa 12:2>>] my salvation.note:tn[__to…__: Heb. for my help]

=== @39

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __even__ to Jeduthun, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Watch My Ways

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I said, I will take heed to my ways, ##that I##note:x[<<Ps 141:3; Prov 21:23>>] sin not with my tongue: I will keep ##my mouth##note:x[<<Jas 1:26>>] with a bridle, while the wicked is before me.note:tn[__my mouth…__: Heb. a bridle, or, muzzle for my mouth]
@ ##I was##note:x[<<Matt 27:12–14>>] dumb with silence, I held my peace, __even__ from good; and ##my sorrow##note:x[<<Acts 4:20>>] was ##stirred##note:tn[Heb. troubled].
@ ##My heart was hot within me, while I was musing the fire burned: __then__ spake I with my tongue,##note:x[<<Jer 20:9; Ezek 3:14; Luke 24:32>>]
@ LORD, ##make##note:x[<<Job 14:13; Ps 90:12; 119:84>>] me to know mine end, and the measure of my days, what it __is; that__ I may know how frail I __am__.note:tn[__how…__: or, what time I have here]
@ ##Behold##note:x[<<Ps 90:9–10>>], thou hast made my days __as__ an handbreadth; and mine age __is__ ##as nothing##note:x[<<Ps 89:47>>] before thee: ##verily##note:x[<<Ps 144:4>>] every man at his best state __is__ altogether vanity. Selah.note:tn[__at…__: Heb. settled]
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Luke 12:20–21>>] every man walketh in ##a vain shew##note:x[<<Jas 4:14>>]: surely they are disquieted in vain: ##he heapeth##note:x[<<Luke 12:20–21>>] up __riches__, and knoweth not who shall gather them.note:tn[__a vain…__: Heb. an image]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ And now, Lord, what wait I for? my ##hope##note:x[<<Ps 38:15; 119:81; Rom 15:13>>] __is__ in thee.
@ ##Deliver##note:x[<<Ps 51:14; Mic 7:19>>] me from all my transgressions: ##make##note:x[<<Ps 44:13>>] me not the reproach of the foolish.
@ I was dumb, ##I opened not my mouth; because thou didst __it__##note:x[<<1 Sam 3:18; 2 Sam 16:10; Job 2:10>>].
@ ##Remove##note:x[<<Job 9:34; 13:21; Ps 25:16–17>>] thy stroke away from me: I am consumed by the ##blow##note:tn[Heb. conflict] of thine hand.
@ ##When##note:x[<<Heb 12:6>>] thou with rebukes dost correct man for iniquity, thou makest ##his beauty##note:x[<<Job 13:28; Isa 50:9>>] to consume away like a moth: surely every man __is__ vanity. Selah.note:tn[__his…__: Heb. that which is to be desired in him to melt away]
@ Hear my prayer, O LORD, and give ear unto my cry; ##hold##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:5>>] not thy peace at my tears: ##for I __am__##note:x[<<Heb 11:13; 1 Pet 2:11>>] a stranger with thee, __and__ a sojourner, as all my fathers __were__.
@ O ##spare##note:x[<<Job 10:20–21; 14:5–6>>] me, that I may recover strength, before I go hence, and ##be no##note:x[<<Job 14:10–12>>] more.

=== @40

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Waited Patiently for the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 70:1–5; Hebrews 10:1–18>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I waited##note:x[<<Ps 27:13–14; 37:7; Jas 5:7–11>>] patiently for the LORD; and he inclined unto me, and heard my cry.note:tn[__I waited…__: Heb. In waiting I waited]
@ He brought me up also out of an horrible pit, out of the miry clay, and ##set##note:x[<<Ps 27:5>>] my feet upon a rock, __and__ ##established##note:x[<<Ps 37:23; 119:133>>] my goings.note:tn[__an…__: Heb. a pit of noise]
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Ps 33:3>>] hath put a new song in my mouth, __even__ ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 103:1–5>>] unto our God: ##many##note:x[<<Ps 34:1–6>>] shall see __it__, and fear, and shall trust in the LORD.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 34:8; 84:11–12; Jer 17:7–8>>] __is__ that man that maketh the LORD his trust, and respecteth not the proud, nor such as turn aside to lies.
@ ##Many##note:x[<<Exod 15:11>>], O LORD my God, __are__ thy wonderful works __which__ thou hast done, and thy ##thoughts##note:x[<<Ps 139:17–18; Jer 29:11>>] __which are__ to us-ward: they cannot be reckoned up in order unto thee: __if__ I would declare and speak __of them__, they are more than can be numbered.note:tn[__they cannot…__: or, none can order them unto thee]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Sacrifice##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:22; Isa 1:11; Heb 10:5–12>>] and offering thou didst not desire; mine ears hast thou ##opened##note:tn[Heb. digged]: burnt offering and sin offering hast thou not required.
@ Then said I, ##Lo##note:x[<<Heb 10:7–9>>], I come: ##in the##note:x[<<Luke 24:44; John 5:39>>] volume of the book __it is__ written of me,
@ ##I delight##note:x[<<Jer 15:16; John 4:34; Rom 7:22>>] to do thy will, O my God: yea, thy law __is__ within my heart.note:tn[__within…__: Heb. in the midst of my bowels]
@ I have ##preached##note:x[<<Ps 22:22, 25; Mark 16:15–16>>] righteousness in the great congregation: lo, I have not refrained my lips, O LORD, thou knowest.
@ I have ##not hid##note:x[<<Acts 20:20–21; Rom 10:9–10>>] thy ##righteousness##note:x[<<Rom 1:16–17>>] within my heart; I have declared thy faithfulness and thy salvation: I have not concealed thy lovingkindness and thy truth from the great congregation.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Withhold not thou thy tender mercies from me, O LORD: ##let thy##note:x[<<Ps 23:6; 43:3; 57:3>>] lovingkindness and thy truth continually preserve me.
@ For innumerable evils have compassed me about: ##mine##note:x[<<Ps 38:4>>] iniquities have taken hold upon me, so that I am not able to look up; ##they are##note:x[<<Ps 69:4>>] more than the hairs of mine head: therefore my ##heart##note:x[<<Ps 73:26>>] ##faileth##note:tn[Heb. forsaketh] me.
@ ##Be##note:x[<<Ps 25:17–18>>] pleased, O LORD, to deliver me: O LORD, ##make##note:x[<<Ps 38:22; 70:1–5>>] haste to help me.
@ ##Let them be ashamed##note:x[<<Ps 35:4, 26; 71:13>>] and confounded together that seek after my soul to destroy it; let them be driven backward and put to shame that wish me evil.
@ Let them be ##desolate##note:x[<<Ps 69:24–25>>] for a reward of their shame that ##say##note:x[<<Ps 35:21, 25>>] unto me, Aha, aha.
@ Let ##all##note:x[<<Ps 35:27; 68:3>>] those that seek thee rejoice and be glad in thee: let such as love thy salvation ##say##note:x[<<Ps 35:27>>] continually, The LORD be magnified.
@ But ##I __am__ poor##note:x[<<Ps 70:5; Isa 41:17>>] and needy; __yet__ the Lord thinketh upon me: thou __art__ my ##help##note:x[<<Heb 13:6>>] and my deliverer; make no tarrying, O my God.

=== @41

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Victory over Betrayal

[.parallel]
(<<John 13:18–30>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Prov 14:21; 19:17; 2 Cor 9:8–14>>] __is__ he that considereth the poor: the LORD will deliver him in time of trouble.note:tn[__the poor__: or, the weak, or, sick]note:tn[__in time…__: Heb. in the day of evil]
@ The LORD will ##preserve##note:x[<<Jer 45:4–5>>] him, and keep him alive; __and__ he shall be ##blessed##note:x[<<1 Tim 4:8>>] upon the earth: and ##thou wilt not##note:x[<<Ps 27:12>>] deliver him unto the will of his enemies.note:tn[__thou…__: or, do not thou deliver]
@ The LORD will ##strengthen##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:5–6; Ps 73:26; Phil 2:26–27>>] him upon the bed of languishing: thou wilt ##make##note:tn[Heb. turn] all his bed in his sickness.
@ I said, LORD, be merciful unto me: ##heal##note:x[<<Ps 103:3; Hos 6:1; Jas 5:15–16>>] my soul; for I have sinned against thee.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Mine##note:x[<<Ps 102:8>>] enemies speak evil of me, When shall he die, and ##his name##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>] perish?
@ And if he come to see __me__, he ##speaketh##note:x[<<Ps 12:2; Prov 26:24–25; Luke 11:53–54>>] vanity: his heart gathereth iniquity to itself; __when__ he goeth abroad, he telleth __it__.
@ All that hate me ##whisper##note:x[<<Prov 16:28>>] together against me: against me do they devise my hurt.note:tn[__my…__: Heb. evil to me]
@ An evil disease, __say they__, cleaveth fast unto him: and __now__ that he lieth he shall rise up no more.note:tn[__An…__: Heb. A thing of Belial]
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Ps 55:12–14, 20–22>>], mine own familiar friend, in whom I trusted, ##which##note:x[<<John 13:18>>] did eat of my bread, hath lifted up __his__ heel against me.note:tn[__mine…__: Heb. the man of my peace]note:tn[__lifted…__: Heb. magnified]
@ But thou, O LORD, be merciful unto me, and raise me up, that I may requite them.
@ By this I know that thou favourest me, ##because##note:x[<<Ps 124:6; Jer 20:13; Col 2:15>>] mine enemy doth not triumph over me.
@ And as for me, thou upholdest me in mine integrity, and ##settest##note:x[<<Job 36:7; Ps 34:15; 73:23–24>>] me before thy face for ever.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 72:18–19; Eph 1:3; Rev 4:8>>] __be__ the LORD God of Israel from everlasting, and to everlasting. Amen, and Amen.

=== @42

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, Maschil, for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.major]
==== BOOK II

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 42—72>>)

[discrete.pericope]
==== As the Deer Pants for the Water

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ As the hart ##panteth##note:tn[Heb. brayeth] after the water brooks, ##so panteth##note:x[<<Ps 63:1–2; 143:6–7; Isa 26:8–9>>] my soul after thee, O God.note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm giving instruction of the sons, etc]
@ My soul ##thirsteth##note:x[<<Ps 63:1; John 7:37>>] for God, for the living God: ##when##note:x[<<Ps 84:10>>] shall I come and appear before God?
@ My tears have been my meat day and night, ##while##note:x[<<Ps 42:10; 79:10; 115:2>>] they continually say unto me, Where __is__ thy God?
@ When I remember these __things__, ##I pour##note:x[<<1 Sam 1:15–16; Ps 62:8>>] out my soul in me: for I had gone with the multitude, I went with them to the house of God, ##with the voice##note:x[<<Isa 30:29>>] of joy and praise, with a multitude that kept holyday.
@ note:x[<<Ps 61:2>>]Why art thou ##cast##note:tn[Heb. bowed] down, O my soul? and __why__ art thou disquieted in me? ##hope##note:x[<<Ps 37:7; Heb 10:36–37>>] thou in God: for I shall yet ##praise##note:tn[or, give thanks] him __for__ the help of his countenance.note:tn[__for the…__: or, his presence is salvation]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O my God, my soul is cast down within me: ##therefore##note:x[<<Jonah 2:7>>] will I remember thee ##from the##note:x[<<Ps 61:2>>] land of Jordan, and of the ##Hermonites##note:x[<<Deut 3:8–9>>], from the hill Mizar.note:tn[__the hill…__: or, the little hill]
@ Deep calleth unto deep at the noise of thy waterspouts: ##all thy##note:x[<<Ps 69:14–15; 88:7; Jonah 2:3>>] waves and thy billows are gone over me.
@ __Yet__ the LORD will command his lovingkindness in the daytime, and ##in the night##note:x[<<Job 35:10; Ps 63:6; 149:5>>] his song __shall be__ with me, __and__ my prayer unto the God of my life.
@ I will say unto ##God##note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>] my rock, Why hast thou forgotten me? ##why go##note:x[<<Ps 38:6; 43:2>>] I mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?
@ ##__As__ with##note:x[<<Ps 42:3>>] a ##sword##note:tn[or, killing] in my bones, mine enemies reproach me; ##while##note:x[<<Ps 42:3; Joel 2:17>>] they say daily unto me, Where __is__ thy God?
@ Why art thou ##cast down##note:x[<<Ps 42:5>>], O my soul? and why art thou disquieted within me? hope thou in God: for I shall yet praise him, __who is__ ##the health##note:x[<<Jer 30:17; 33:6>>] of my countenance, and my God.

=== @43

[discrete.pericope]
==== Send Out Your Light

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Judge##note:x[<<Ps 7:8; 26:1>>] me, O God, and ##plead##note:x[<<Ps 35:1>>] my cause against an ##ungodly##note:tn[or, unmerciful] nation: O deliver me from the deceitful and unjust man.note:tn[__the deceitful…__: Heb. a man of deceit and iniquity]
@ For thou __art__ ##the God##note:x[<<Ps 28:7; Eph 6:10>>] of my strength: why dost thou cast me off? ##why go##note:x[<<Ps 42:9>>] I mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?
@ O ##send##note:x[<<Ps 97:11; John 1:4>>] out thy light and thy truth: let them ##lead##note:x[<<Prov 3:5–6>>] me; let them bring me unto thy holy hill, and to thy tabernacles.
@ ##Then##note:x[<<Ps 66:13–15>>] will I go unto the altar of God, unto God ##my exceeding joy##note:x[<<Isa 61:10; Hab 3:17–18>>]: yea, upon the harp will I praise thee, O God my God.note:tn[__my exceeding…__: Heb. the gladness of my joy]
@ ##Why art thou cast down, O my soul? and why art thou disquieted within me? hope in God: for I shall yet praise him, __who is__ the##note:x[<<Ps 42:5, 11>>] health of my countenance, and my God.

=== @44

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician for the sons of Korah, Maschil.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Redeem Us, O God

[.parallel]
(<<Romans 8:35–39>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ We ##have heard##note:x[<<Exod 13:14–15; Ps 78:3–6; Joel 1:3>>] with our ears, O God, our fathers have told us, __what__ work thou didst in their days, in the times of old.note:tn[__Maschil__: or, of instruction]
@ __How__ thou didst ##drive out##note:x[<<Exod 15:17; Ps 78:55; 135:10–12>>] the heathen with thy hand, and plantedst them; __how__ thou didst afflict the people, and cast them out.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Deut 8:17–18; Josh 24:12>>] they got not the land in possession by their own sword, neither did their own arm save them: but thy right hand, and thine arm, and the light of thy countenance, ##because##note:x[<<Deut 7:7–8>>] thou hadst a favour unto them.
@ Thou art ##my King##note:x[<<Ps 74:12>>], O God: ##command##note:x[<<Ps 42:8>>] deliverances for Jacob.
@ ##Through thee##note:x[<<Ps 118:10–13>>] will we push down our enemies: through thy name will we ##tread##note:x[<<Ps 60:12; 108:13>>] them under that rise up against us.
@ ##For I will not trust in my bow, neither shall my sword save me.##note:x[<<Ps 20:7; 33:16–17; Hos 1:7>>]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Josh 1:5; 23:9–10; Ps 140:7>>] thou hast saved us from our enemies, and hast put them to shame that hated us.
@ ##In God##note:x[<<Ps 34:2; Jer 9:24; 1 Cor 1:29–31>>] we boast all the day long, and praise thy name for ever. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##But thou hast cast off, and put us to shame; and goest not forth with our armies.##note:x[<<Ps 60:1, 10; 74:1>>]
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Lev 26:17; Josh 7:8, 12>>] makest us to turn back from the enemy: and they which hate us spoil for themselves.
@ Thou hast ##given##note:x[<<Rom 8:36>>] us like sheep __appointed__ for meat; and hast ##scattered##note:x[<<Deut 4:27; 28:64>>] us among the heathen.note:tn[__like…__: Heb. as sheep of meat]
@ Thou ##sellest##note:x[<<Deut 32:30; Isa 52:3–4; Jer 15:13>>] thy people for nought, and dost not increase __thy wealth__ by their price.note:tn[__for…__: Heb. without riches]
@ Thou ##makest##note:x[<<Ps 79:4; 80:6; Jer 24:9>>] us a reproach to our neighbours, a scorn and a derision to them that are round about us.
@ note:x[<<Jer 24:9>>]Thou makest us a byword among the heathen, a shaking of the head among the people.
@ My confusion __is__ continually before me, and the shame of my face hath ##covered##note:x[<<Ps 69:7>>] me,
@ For the voice of him that reproacheth and blasphemeth; by reason of the ##enemy##note:x[<<Ps 8:2>>] and avenger.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##All this##note:x[<<Dan 9:13>>] is come upon us; yet have we not forgotten thee, neither have we dealt falsely in thy covenant.
@ Our ##heart##note:x[<<Ps 78:57>>] is not turned back, neither ##have##note:x[<<Ps 119:51, 157>>] our ##steps##note:tn[or, goings] declined from thy way;
@ Though thou hast sore broken us in the place of dragons, and covered us ##with the##note:x[<<Job 3:5; Ps 23:4>>] shadow of death.
@ If we have forgotten the name of our God, or ##stretched##note:x[<<Job 11:13>>] out our hands to a strange god;
@ ##Shall##note:x[<<Job 34:21–22; Jer 17:10; 23:24>>] not God search this out? for he knoweth the secrets of the heart.
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Rom 8:36>>], for thy sake are we ##killed##note:x[<<Ps 44:11; John 15:21>>] all the day long; we are counted as sheep for the slaughter.
@ ##Awake##note:x[<<Ps 7:6; 78:65>>], why sleepest thou, O Lord? arise, cast __us__ not off for ever.
@ ##Wherefore##note:x[<<Deut 32:20; Job 13:24>>] hidest thou thy face, __and__ forgettest our affliction and our oppression?
@ ##For our soul is bowed down to the dust: our belly cleaveth unto the earth.##note:x[<<Ps 119:25>>]
@ Arise for our help, and ##redeem##note:x[<<Ps 26:11; 130:7–8>>] us for thy mercies’ sake.note:tn[__for our…__: Heb. a help for us]

=== @45

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Shoshannim, for the sons of Korah, Maschil, A Song of loves.

[discrete.pericope]
==== My Heart Is Stirred by a Noble Theme

[.parallel]
(<<1 Kings 3:1–15; 2 Chronicles 1:1–13; Psalms 72:1–20>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ My heart is inditing ##a good##note:x[<<Job 33:3; Ps 49:3>>] matter: I speak of the things which I have made touching the king: my ##tongue##note:x[<<2 Sam 23:2>>] __is__ the pen of a ready writer.note:tn[__Maschil__: or, of instruction]note:tn[__is inditing__: Heb. boileth, or, bubbleth up]
@ Thou art ##fairer##note:x[<<Song 5:10–16>>] than the children of men: ##grace##note:x[<<Luke 4:22; John 7:46>>] is poured into thy lips: therefore God hath blessed thee for ever.
@ ##Gird##note:x[<<Isa 49:2; Heb 4:12; Rev 1:16>>] thy sword upon __thy__ thigh, O __most__ mighty, with thy glory and thy majesty.
@ And in thy majesty ##ride##note:x[<<Rev 6:2; 19:11>>] prosperously because of truth and meekness __and__ righteousness; and thy ##right##note:x[<<Ps 65:5>>] hand shall teach thee terrible things.note:tn[__ride…__: Heb. prosper thou, ride thou]
@ ##Thine##note:x[<<Num 24:8; Ps 21:12; 38:2>>] arrows __are__ sharp in the heart of the king’s enemies; __whereby__ the people fall under thee.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thy ##throne##note:x[<<Ps 93:2; Heb 1:8>>], ##O God##note:x[<<John 1:1>>], __is__ for ever and ever: the sceptre of thy kingdom __is__ a right sceptre.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Heb 1:9>>] lovest righteousness, and hatest wickedness: therefore God, ##thy God##note:x[<<Isa 61:1>>], hath anointed thee with the ##oil##note:x[<<Ps 21:6>>] of gladness above thy fellows.
@ ##All##note:x[<<Song 1:3; John 19:39; 2 Cor 2:14–16>>] thy garments __smell__ of myrrh, and aloes, __and__ cassia, out of the ivory palaces, whereby they have made thee glad.
@ Kings’ daughters __were__ among thy honourable women: ##upon##note:x[<<1 Kgs 2:19>>] thy right hand did stand the ##queen##note:x[<<Eph 5:26–27; Rev 21:9>>] in gold of Ophir.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hearken##note:x[<<Song 2:10–13>>], O daughter, and consider, and incline thine ear; ##forget##note:x[<<Gen 12:1; Matt 10:37>>] also thine own people, and thy father’s house;
@ ##So shall##note:x[<<Song 4:1–5>>] the king greatly desire thy beauty: for he __is__ thy ##Lord##note:x[<<Isa 54:5>>]; and ##worship##note:x[<<Ps 95:6>>] thou him.
@ ##And the##note:x[<<Isa 23:17–18>>] daughter of Tyre __shall be there__ with a gift; __even__ the ##rich##note:x[<<Ps 22:29; Isa 60:3>>] among the people shall intreat thy ##favour##note:tn[Heb. face].
@ note:x[<<Isa 61:10; Rev 19:7–8>>]The king’s daughter __is__ ##all glorious##note:x[<<Luke 11:40>>] within: her clothing __is__ of wrought gold.
@ ##She##note:x[<<2 Cor 11:2>>] shall be brought unto the king in ##raiment##note:x[<<Exod 28:39>>] of needlework: the ##virgins##note:x[<<Song 8:13>>] her companions that follow her shall be brought unto thee.
@ ##With##note:x[<<Rev 7:15–17>>] gladness and rejoicing shall they be brought: they shall enter into the king’s palace.
@ Instead of thy fathers shall be thy children, whom thou mayest make ##princes##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:9; Rev 1:6; 5:10>>] in all the earth.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 72:17–19; Mal 1:11>>] make thy name to be remembered in all generations: ##therefore##note:x[<<Isa 62:3>>] shall the people praise thee for ever and ever.

=== @46

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician for the sons of Korah, A Song upon Alamoth.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Is Our Refuge and Strength

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 18:13–16; 2 Chronicles 32:1–8>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ God __is__ our ##refuge##note:x[<<Ps 62:7–8; Prov 18:10>>] and strength, ##a very##note:x[<<Ps 145:18>>] present help in trouble.note:tn[__for__: or, of]
@ Therefore ##will##note:x[<<Ps 23:4; 27:3; Heb 13:6>>] not we fear, though the earth be removed, and though the mountains be carried into the midst of the sea;note:tn[__midst…__: Heb. heart of the seas]
@ __Though__ ##the waters##note:x[<<Ps 93:3–4; Jer 5:22>>] thereof roar __and__ be troubled, __though__ the ##mountains##note:x[<<Job 9:5–6>>] shake with the swelling thereof. Selah.
@ __There is__ ##a river##note:x[<<Ps 36:8–9; Ezek 47:1–12; Rev 22:1–3>>], the streams whereof shall make glad the city of God, the holy __place__ of the tabernacles of the most High.
@ ##God __is__##note:x[<<Joel 2:27; Zeph 3:15; Zech 2:5>>] in the midst of her; she shall not be moved: God shall help her, __and that__ right early.note:tn[__and…__: Heb. when the morning appeareth]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The heathen raged, the kingdoms were moved: he uttered his voice, the ##earth##note:x[<<Amos 9:5; Nah 1:5; Hab 3:5–6>>] melted.
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<Num 14:9; Rom 8:31>>] of hosts __is__ with us; the God of Jacob __is__ ##our refuge##note:x[<<Ps 9:9>>]. Selah.note:tn[__our…__: Heb. an high place for us]
@ ##Come##note:x[<<Ps 66:5; 92:4–6>>], behold the works of the LORD, what ##desolations##note:x[<<Isa 24:1>>] he hath made in the earth.
@ He ##maketh##note:x[<<Isa 2:4; Mic 4:3–4>>] wars to cease unto the end of the earth; he breaketh the bow, and cutteth the spear in sunder; he ##burneth##note:x[<<Mic 5:10>>] the chariot in the fire.
@ ##Be still##note:x[<<Hab 2:20>>], and ##know##note:x[<<Ps 100:3>>] that I __am__ God: ##I will be##note:x[<<Ezek 38:23>>] exalted among the heathen, I will be exalted in the earth.
@ The LORD of hosts __is__ with us; ##the God##note:x[<<Deut 33:27; Ps 46:1; Jer 16:19>>] of Jacob __is__ our refuge. Selah.

=== @47

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Clap Your Hands, All You Peoples

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O ##clap##note:x[<<Ps 98:4>>] your hands, all ye people; ##shout##note:x[<<Ps 98:4; Jer 31:7>>] unto God with the voice of triumph.note:tn[__for__: or, of]
@ For the LORD most high ##__is__ terrible##note:x[<<Ps 99:3>>]; __he is__ ##a great##note:x[<<Ps 95:3; Mal 1:14>>] King over all the earth.
@ He shall ##subdue##note:x[<<Ps 18:47; 81:14>>] the people under us, and the nations under ##our feet##note:x[<<1 Cor 15:25>>].
@ He shall ##choose##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:4>>] our inheritance for us, the ##excellency##note:x[<<Amos 6:8; Nah 2:2>>] of Jacob whom he loved. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##God##note:x[<<Ps 24:7–10; 68:33>>] is gone up ##with a shout##note:x[<<Ps 78:65>>], the LORD with the sound of a trumpet.
@ Sing praises ##to God##note:x[<<Ps 117:1–2; Isa 12:4–6>>], sing praises: sing praises unto ##our King##note:x[<<Matt 25:34>>], sing praises.
@ For God __is__ the ##King##note:x[<<Ps 47:8; Zech 14:9>>] of all the earth: ##sing##note:x[<<Col 3:16>>] ye praises ##with##note:tn[or, every one that hath] understanding.
@ God ##reigneth##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:31; Ps 93:1; Rev 19:6>>] over the heathen: God sitteth upon the throne of his holiness.
@ ##The princes##note:x[<<Gen 49:10; Ps 72:7–9>>] of the people are gathered together, __even__ the people of the God of Abraham: for the ##shields##note:x[<<Ps 89:18>>] of the earth __belong__ unto God: he is greatly exalted.note:tn[__princes…__: or, voluntary of the people are gathered unto the people of the God of Abraham]

=== @48

[.psalm-title]
A Song __and__ Psalm for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Broken Bondage

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Great __is__ the LORD, and greatly to be praised in the city of our God, __in__ the ##mountain##note:x[<<Isa 2:2–3; Mic 4:1; Zech 8:3>>] of his holiness.note:tn[__for__: or, of]
@ ##Beautiful##note:x[<<Ps 50:2; Lam 2:15>>] for situation, the joy of the whole earth, __is__ mount Zion, __on__ the sides of the north, ##the city##note:x[<<Matt 5:35>>] of the great King.
@ ##God is known in her palaces for a refuge.##note:x[<<Ps 125:1; Isa 37:33–36; Zech 2:4–5>>]
@ For, lo, the kings were assembled, they passed by together.
@ They saw __it, and__ so they marvelled; they were troubled, __and__ hasted away.
@ Fear took hold upon them there, __and__ pain, as of a woman in travail.
@ Thou ##breakest##note:x[<<Ezek 27:25–26>>] the ##ships##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:48>>] of Tarshish with an ##east##note:x[<<Jer 18:17>>] wind.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ As we have heard, so have we seen in the city of the LORD of hosts, in the city of our ##God##note:x[<<Ps 87:5; Mic 4:1>>]: God will establish it for ever. Selah.
@ note:x[<<Ps 40:10>>]We have ##thought##note:x[<<Ps 26:3; 77:10–11>>] of thy lovingkindness, O God, in the midst of thy temple.
@ ##According##note:x[<<Deut 28:58; Ps 113:3; Mal 1:11>>] to thy name, O God, so __is__ thy praise unto the ends of the earth: thy right hand is full of righteousness.
@ Let mount Zion rejoice, let the ##daughters##note:x[<<Ps 97:8>>] of Judah be glad, ##because##note:x[<<Rev 15:4; 18:20>>] of thy judgments.
@ Walk about Zion, and go round about her: tell the towers thereof.
@ Mark ye well her bulwarks, ##consider##note:tn[or, raise up] her palaces; ##that ye##note:x[<<Ps 71:18>>] may tell __it__ to the generation following.note:tn[__Mark…__: Heb. Set your heart to]
@ For ##this God##note:x[<<Ps 73:24>>] __is__ our God for ever and ever: he will be our ##guide##note:x[<<Ps 23:3–4; Isa 58:11>>] __even__ unto death.

=== @49

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Evanescence of Wealth

[.parallel]
(<<Ecclesiastes 5:8–20>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 78:1; Matt 11:15; Rev 2:7>>] this, all __ye__ people; give ear, all __ye__ inhabitants of the world&#58;note:tn[__for__: or, of]
@ ##Both low and high, rich and poor, together.##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:7–8; Ps 62:9; Jas 2:1–7>>]
@ My ##mouth##note:x[<<Prov 22:20–21>>] shall speak of wisdom; and the ##meditation##note:x[<<Ps 19:14; Matt 12:35>>] of my heart __shall be__ of understanding.
@ I will ##incline##note:x[<<Ps 78:2; Matt 13:35>>] mine ear to a ##parable##note:x[<<Num 23:7>>]: I will open my dark saying upon the harp.
@ Wherefore should I fear in the days of evil, __when__ the iniquity of my heels shall compass me about?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They that ##trust##note:x[<<Ps 52:7; 1 Tim 6:17>>] in their wealth, and ##boast##note:x[<<Jer 9:23>>] themselves in the multitude of their riches;
@ None __of them__ can by any means redeem his brother, nor ##give##note:x[<<Matt 16:26; 20:28; 1 Tim 2:6>>] to God a ransom for him:
@ (For the redemption of their soul __is__ precious, and it ceaseth for ever:)
@ ##That he##note:x[<<Ps 89:48; Eccl 8:8>>] should still live for ever, __and__ not ##see##note:x[<<Ps 16:10>>] corruption.
@ For he seeth __that__ wise men die, likewise the fool and the brutish person perish, and ##leave##note:x[<<Ps 39:6; Eccl 5:13–16; Luke 12:20>>] their wealth to others.
@ ##Their inward##note:x[<<Ps 5:9; 64:6>>] thought __is, that__ their houses __shall continue__ for ever, __and__ their dwelling places to all generations; ##they call##note:x[<<Gen 4:17>>] __their__ lands after their own names.note:tn[__all…__: Heb. generation and generation]
@ Nevertheless man __being__ ##in honour##note:x[<<Ps 39:5; 49:20>>] abideth not: he is like the ##beasts##note:x[<<Eccl 3:18–21>>] __that__ perish.
@ This their way __is__ their ##folly##note:x[<<Luke 12:20; 1 Cor 3:19>>]: yet their posterity approve their sayings. Selah.note:tn[__approve…__: Heb. delight in their mouth]
@ Like sheep ##they##note:x[<<Job 30:23>>] are laid in the grave; death shall feed on them; and the ##upright##note:x[<<Mal 4:3; 1 Cor 6:2>>] shall have dominion over them in the morning; and their ##beauty##note:tn[or, strength] shall consume in the grave from their dwelling.note:tn[__in the grave from…__: or, the grave being an habitation to every one of them]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But ##God##note:x[<<Ps 56:13; 73:24>>] will redeem my soul from the note:x[<<Ps 86:13>>]##power##note:tn[Heb. hand] of the grave: for he shall receive me. Selah.note:tn[__the grave__: or, hell]
@ ##Be not##note:x[<<Ps 37:1, 7>>] thou afraid when one is made rich, when the glory of his house is increased;
@ For when he dieth ##he shall##note:x[<<Job 27:19; 1 Tim 6:7>>] carry nothing away: his glory shall not descend after him.
@ Though while he lived he ##blessed##note:x[<<Deut 29:19; Luke 12:19>>] his soul: and __men__ will praise thee, when thou doest well to thyself.note:tn[__while…__: Heb. in his life]
@ He shall go ##to the generation##note:x[<<Gen 15:15>>] of his fathers; they shall ##never##note:x[<<Job 33:30; Ps 56:13>>] see light.note:tn[__He…__: Heb. The soul shall]
@ Man __that is__ in honour, and understandeth not, is like the beasts __that__ perish.

=== @50

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Mighty One Calls

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##mighty##note:x[<<Josh 22:22>>] God, __even__ the LORD, hath spoken, and ##called##note:x[<<Ps 113:3; Matt 25:32>>] the earth from the rising of the sun unto the going down thereof.note:tn[__of…__: or, for Asaph]
@ Out of Zion, the ##perfection##note:x[<<Ps 48:2>>] of beauty, ##God##note:x[<<Ps 80:1; Rev 21:23>>] hath shined.
@ Our God shall come, and shall not keep silence: ##a fire##note:x[<<Lev 10:2; Num 16:35; Dan 7:10>>] shall devour before him, and it shall be very tempestuous round about him.
@ He shall ##call##note:x[<<Deut 31:28; 32:1; Isa 1:2>>] to the heavens from above, and to the earth, that he may judge his people.
@ Gather my saints together unto me; those that have ##made##note:x[<<Exod 24:3–8; Matt 26:28; Heb 13:20>>] a covenant with me by sacrifice.
@ And the ##heavens##note:x[<<Ps 97:6; Rev 19:2>>] shall declare his righteousness: for ##God##note:x[<<Ps 75:7>>] __is__ judge himself. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 81:8; Jer 2:9>>], O my people, and I will speak; O Israel, and I will testify against thee: ##I __am__##note:x[<<Exod 20:2>>] God, __even__ thy God.
@ ##I will not reprove thee for thy sacrifices or thy burnt offerings, __to have been__ continually before me.##note:x[<<Isa 1:11–31; Hos 6:6; Heb 10:4–10>>]
@ ##I will take no bullock out of thy house, __nor__ he goats out of thy folds.##note:x[<<Mic 6:6–8; Acts 17:25; Heb 10:4–6>>]
@ For ##every##note:x[<<Ps 8:6–8; Jer 27:5–6>>] beast of the forest __is__ mine, __and__ the ##cattle##note:x[<<Ps 104:14>>] upon a thousand hills.
@ I ##know##note:x[<<Gen 1:20–22; Matt 6:26>>] all the fowls of the mountains: and the wild beasts of the field __are__ ##mine##note:tn[Heb. with me].
@ If I were hungry, I would not tell thee: for the ##world##note:x[<<Exod 19:5; Deut 10:14; Ps 24:1–2>>] __is__ mine, and the fulness thereof.
@ Will I eat the flesh of bulls, or drink the blood of goats?
@ ##Offer##note:x[<<Heb 13:15>>] unto God thanksgiving; and ##pay##note:x[<<Deut 23:21; Ps 56:12>>] thy vows unto the most High:
@ And ##call##note:x[<<Ps 91:15; 107:28>>] upon me in the day of trouble: I will ##deliver##note:x[<<Ps 34:3–4>>] thee, and thou shalt glorify me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But unto the ##wicked##note:x[<<Isa 55:6–7>>] God saith, What hast thou to do to declare my statutes, or __that__ ##thou shouldest##note:x[<<Ps 25:14; 78:36–38>>] take my covenant in thy mouth?
@ Seeing thou ##hatest##note:x[<<Prov 12:1; 2 Tim 4:3–4>>] instruction, and ##castest##note:x[<<Neh 9:26>>] my words behind thee.
@ When thou sawest a thief, then thou ##consentedst##note:x[<<Rom 1:32>>] with him, and hast been ##partaker##note:x[<<1 Tim 5:22>>] with adulterers.note:tn[__hast…__: Heb. thy portion was with]
@ Thou note:x[<<Jer 9:5>>]##givest##note:tn[Heb. sendest] thy mouth to evil, and thy ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 10:7; Rev 21:8>>] frameth deceit.
@ Thou sittest __and__ speakest against thy brother; thou ##slanderest##note:x[<<Lev 19:16>>] ##thine own##note:x[<<Matt 10:21>>] mother’s son.
@ These __things__ hast thou done, and ##I kept##note:x[<<Isa 57:11; Rom 2:4–5>>] silence; thou thoughtest that I was altogether __such an one__ as thyself: __but__ I ##will##note:x[<<Prov 29:1>>] reprove thee, and set __them__ in order before thine eyes.
@ Now consider this, ye that ##forget##note:x[<<Job 8:13; Ps 9:17; Hos 4:6>>] God, lest I tear __you__ in pieces, and __there be__ none to deliver.
@ Whoso offereth praise glorifieth me: and ##to him##note:x[<<Ps 24:4–5>>] that ##ordereth __his__ conversation##note:x[<<Jas 3:13>>] __aright__ will I shew the ##salvation##note:x[<<Ps 91:16>>] of God.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. that disposeth his way]

=== @51

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David, when Nathan the prophet came unto him, after he had gone in to Bath-sheba.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Create in Me a Clean Heart, O God

[.parallel]
(<<2 Samuel 12:1–12>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Have mercy upon me, O God, according to thy lovingkindness: according unto the multitude of thy tender mercies ##blot##note:x[<<Isa 43:25; 44:22; Acts 3:19>>] out my transgressions.
@ ##Wash##note:x[<<Ezek 36:25; Heb 10:21–22; 1 John 1:7–9>>] me throughly from mine iniquity, and cleanse me from my sin.
@ ##For I##note:x[<<Ps 38:18; Prov 28:13>>] acknowledge my transgressions: and ##my sin##note:x[<<Isa 59:12>>] __is__ ever before me.
@ ##Against##note:x[<<Gen 20:6; 2 Sam 12:13–14>>] thee, thee only, have I sinned, and done __this__ ##evil##note:x[<<Luke 15:21>>] in thy sight: that thou mightest be justified when thou speakest, __and__ be clear when thou judgest.
@ Behold, I was ##shapen##note:x[<<Job 14:4; Ps 58:3; Rom 5:12>>] in iniquity; and in sin did my mother conceive me.note:tn[__conceive…__: Heb. warm me]
@ Behold, thou desirest truth in the ##inward##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:7; Rom 7:22>>] parts: and ##in the hidden##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:4>>] __part__ thou shalt make me to know wisdom.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Purge me with hyssop, ##and##note:x[<<Heb 9:13–14; 1 John 1:7>>] I shall be clean: wash me, and I shall be ##whiter##note:x[<<Isa 1:18>>] than snow.
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Matt 5:4>>] me to hear joy and gladness; __that__ the ##bones##note:x[<<Job 5:17–18; Hos 6:1–2>>] __which__ thou hast broken may rejoice.
@ ##Hide##note:x[<<Jer 16:17; Mic 7:18–19>>] thy face from my sins, and ##blot##note:x[<<Col 2:14>>] out all mine iniquities.
@ ##Create##note:x[<<2 Cor 5:17>>] in me a ##clean##note:x[<<Ezek 11:19>>] heart, O God; and ##renew##note:x[<<Eph 4:22–24>>] a ##right##note:tn[or, constant] spirit within me.
@ ##Cast##note:x[<<2 Kgs 13:23>>] me not away from thy presence; and ##take##note:x[<<Isa 63:10–11>>] not thy ##holy##note:x[<<Eph 4:30>>] spirit from me.
@ Restore unto me the ##joy##note:x[<<Ps 13:5; Isa 61:10>>] of thy salvation; and ##uphold##note:x[<<Isa 41:10>>] me __with thy__ free spirit.
@ ##__Then__##note:x[<<Luke 22:32>>] will I teach transgressors thy ways; and sinners shall be ##converted##note:x[<<Acts 3:19; Jas 5:19–20>>] unto thee.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Deliver##note:x[<<2 Sam 12:9>>] me from ##bloodguiltiness##note:tn[Heb. bloods], O God, thou God of my salvation: __and__ my ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 35:28>>] shall sing aloud of thy righteousness.
@ O Lord, ##open##note:x[<<Ezek 29:21>>] thou my lips; and my ##mouth##note:x[<<Ps 63:3–5; Heb 13:15>>] shall shew forth thy praise.
@ For thou ##desirest##note:x[<<Hos 6:6>>] not sacrifice; else would I give __it:__ thou ##delightest##note:x[<<Ps 40:6; Heb 10:5–6>>] not in burnt offering.note:tn[__else…__: or, that I should]
@ The sacrifices of God __are__ ##a broken spirit##note:x[<<Ps 34:18; 147:3; Isa 66:2>>]: a broken and a contrite heart, O God, thou wilt not despise.
@ ##Do##note:x[<<Ps 102:16; 122:6–9>>] good in ##thy##note:x[<<Phil 2:13>>] good pleasure unto Zion: build thou the walls of Jerusalem.
@ Then shalt thou be ##pleased##note:x[<<Eph 5:2>>] with the ##sacrifices##note:x[<<Ps 4:5; Mal 3:3>>] of righteousness, with burnt offering and whole burnt offering: then shall they offer bullocks upon thine altar.

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 22:6–23>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]

@ ##Thy##note:x[<<Prov 6:16–19>>] tongue deviseth mischiefs; ##like##note:x[<<Ps 57:4; Prov 18:21>>] a sharp razor, working deceitfully.

@ Thou lovest all ##devouring##note:x[<<Jas 3:6–9>>] words, O __thou__ deceitful tongue.note:tn[__O thou…__: or, and the deceitful tongue]
@ ##God##note:x[<<Rev 21:8>>] shall likewise destroy thee for ever, he shall take thee away, and pluck thee out of __thy__ dwelling place, and ##root##note:x[<<Prov 2:22>>] thee out of ##the land##note:x[<<Ps 27:13>>] of the living. Selah.note:tn[__destroy…__: Heb. beat thee down]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]

@ Lo, __this is__ the man __that__ ##made##note:x[<<Jer 17:5>>] not God his strength; but ##trusted##note:x[<<Ps 49:6–20>>] in the abundance of his riches, __and__ ##strengthened##note:x[<<Eccl 8:8>>] himself in his ##wickedness##note:tn[or, substance].
@ But I __am__ ##like##note:x[<<Ps 1:3; 92:12–14; Hos 14:6–8>>] a green olive tree in the house of God: I trust in the mercy of God for ever and ever.
@ I will praise thee for ever, because thou hast done __it:__ and I will ##wait##note:x[<<Ps 130:5–6; Lam 3:25–26>>] on thy name; ##for __it is__##note:x[<<Ps 73:28>>] good before thy saints.

==== Cast Your Burden upon the LORD

(<<2 Samuel 17:15–29>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Give##note:x[<<Ps 64:1; 80:1; 1 Pet 3:12>>] ear to my prayer, O God; and hide not thyself from my supplication.note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]note:tn[__Maschil__: or, of instruction]
@ Attend unto me, and hear me: ##I mourn##note:x[<<Ps 38:6; 43:2; Isa 38:14>>] in my complaint, and make a noise;
@ Because of the voice of the enemy, because of the oppression of the wicked: ##for they##note:x[<<2 Sam 16:7–8; Ps 35:11>>] cast iniquity upon me, and in wrath they hate me.
@ ##My##note:x[<<Mark 14:33–34; 2 Cor 1:8–10>>] heart is sore pained within me: and the ##terrors##note:x[<<Ps 116:3>>] of death are fallen upon me.
@ ##Fearfulness##note:x[<<Ps 119:120>>] and trembling are come upon me, and horror hath ##overwhelmed##note:tn[Heb. covered] me.

@ ##Lo, __then__ would I wander far off, __and__ remain in the wilderness. Selah.##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:14; Jer 9:2; 37:12>>]
@ I would hasten my escape from the windy storm __and__ tempest.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Destroy, O Lord, __and__ divide their tongues: for ##I have##note:x[<<Jer 6:7>>] seen violence and strife in the city.
@ ##Day##note:x[<<Ps 59:14–15; John 18:28>>] and night they go about it upon the walls thereof: ##mischief##note:x[<<Isa 59:6–15>>] also and sorrow __are__ in the midst of it.
@ Wickedness __is__ in the midst thereof: ##deceit##note:x[<<Isa 59:7>>] and guile depart not from her streets.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 41:9>>] __it was__ not an enemy __that__ reproached me; ##then I##note:x[<<Matt 26:21–23; John 13:18>>] could have borne __it:__ neither __was it__ he that hated me __that__ did magnify __himself__ against me; then I would have hid myself from him:
@ But __it was__ thou, a man mine equal, ##my guide##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:12; Jer 9:4; Mic 7:5>>], and mine acquaintance.note:tn[__mine equal__: Heb. according to my rank]
@ We took sweet counsel together, __and__ ##walked##note:x[<<Ps 42:4; Ezek 33:31>>] unto the house of God in company.note:tn[__We…__: Heb. Who sweetened counsel]
@ ##Let death##note:x[<<Ps 59:13; 109:6–20; Acts 1:18–20>>] seize upon them, __and__ let them go down quick into ##hell##note:tn[or, the grave]: for wickedness __is__ in their dwellings, __and__ among them.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##As for me, I will call upon God; and the LORD shall save me.##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; 73:28; Luke 6:11–12>>]
@ ##Evening##note:x[<<Acts 3:1; Eph 6:18; 1 Thess 5:17>>], and morning, and at noon, will I pray, and cry aloud: and he shall hear my voice.
@ ##He hath##note:x[<<Ps 3:6–7; 57:3>>] delivered my soul in peace from the battle __that was__ against me: for ##there##note:x[<<Matt 26:53>>] were many with me.
@ God shall hear, and afflict them, ##even##note:x[<<Deut 33:27>>] he that abideth of old. Selah. Because they have ##no changes##note:x[<<Eccl 8:11; Zeph 1:12>>], therefore they fear not God.note:tn[__Because…__: or, With whom also there be no changes, yet they]
@ He hath ##put##note:x[<<Acts 12:1>>] forth his hands against such as be ##at peace##note:x[<<Ps 7:4>>] with him: he hath note:x[<<Ps 89:34>>]##broken##note:tn[Heb. profaned] his covenant.
@ ##__The words__##note:x[<<Ps 28:3; 57:4; Prov 5:3–4>>] of his mouth were smoother than butter, but war __was__ in his heart: his words were softer than oil, yet __were__ they drawn swords.
@ ##Cast##note:x[<<Ps 37:5; Phil 4:6–7; 1 Pet 5:7>>] thy ##burden##note:tn[or, gift] upon the LORD, and he shall sustain thee: he shall never suffer the righteous to be moved.
@ But thou, O God, shalt bring them down into the pit of destruction: ##bloody and deceitful men shall not live out half their days##note:x[<<Job 15:32; Ps 5:6; Prov 10:27>>]; but I will trust in thee.note:tn[__bloody…__: Heb. men of bloods and deceit]note:tn[__shall…__: Heb. shall not half their days]

=== @56

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Jonath-elem-rechokim, Michtam of David, when the Philistines took him in Gath.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Be Merciful to Me, O God

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 21:8–15>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<1 Sam 29:4>>]note:x[<<Ps 16:1; 59:1>>]Be merciful unto me, O God: for man would swallow me up; he fighting daily oppresseth me.note:tn[__Michtam…__: or, A golden Psalm of David]
@ Mine ##enemies##note:tn[Heb. observers] would daily swallow __me__ up: for __they be__ ##many##note:x[<<Ps 118:10–12>>] that fight against me, O thou most High.
@ ##What time I am afraid, I will trust in thee.##note:x[<<1 Sam 30:6; Ps 34:4; 2 Cor 7:5–6>>]
@ In God I will praise his word, ##in God I have##note:x[<<Ps 118:6; Rom 8:31–39; Heb 13:6>>] put my trust; I will not fear what flesh can do unto me.
@ Every day ##they##note:x[<<Isa 29:20–21; Matt 22:15; Luke 11:54>>] wrest my words: all their thoughts __are__ against me for evil.
@ They ##gather##note:x[<<Ps 59:3; 71:10; 140:2>>] themselves together, they hide themselves, they mark my steps, when they wait for my soul.
@ Shall they escape by iniquity? ##in __thine__##note:x[<<Ps 55:23>>] anger cast down the people, O God.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou tellest my wanderings: ##put##note:x[<<2 Kgs 20:5; Ps 39:12>>] thou my tears into thy bottle: ##__are they__##note:x[<<Mal 3:16>>] not in thy book?
@ ##When##note:x[<<Ps 118:11–13>>] I cry __unto thee__, then shall mine enemies turn back: this I know; ##for##note:x[<<Ps 46:11; Rom 8:31>>] God __is__ for me.
@ ##In God will I praise __his__ word: in the LORD will I praise __his__ word.##note:x[<<Matt 24:35>>]
@ In God have I put my trust: ##I will not##note:x[<<Ps 27:1; Isa 51:7–8, 12–13>>] be afraid what man can do unto me.
@ ##Thy##note:x[<<1 Sam 1:24–28>>] vows __are__ upon me, O God: ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 9:1–3>>] render praises unto thee.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 116:8>>] thou hast delivered my soul from death: __wilt__ not __thou deliver__ my feet from falling, that I may walk before God in ##the light##note:x[<<Job 33:30; Rev 21:23–24>>] of the living?

=== @57

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, Al-taschith, Michtam of David, when he fled from Saul in the cave.

[discrete.pericope]
==== In You My Soul Takes Refuge

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 22:1–5; Psalms 108:1–13; Psalms 142:1–7>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Be merciful unto me, O God, be merciful unto me: for my soul trusteth in thee: yea, in the ##shadow##note:x[<<Ps 17:7–8; 36:7; 91:4>>] of thy wings will I make my refuge, until __these__ calamities be overpast.note:tn[__Al-taschith__: or, Destroy not]note:tn[__Michtam__: or, A golden Psalm]
@ I will cry unto God most high; unto God ##that##note:x[<<Ps 138:8; Phil 1:6; Heb 13:21>>] performeth __all things__ for me.
@ He shall ##send##note:x[<<Ps 40:11; 43:3; 144:5–7>>] from heaven, and save me __from__ the reproach of him that would swallow me up. Selah. God shall send forth his mercy and his truth.note:tn[__from the…__: or, he reproacheth him that]
@ My soul __is__ ##among##note:x[<<Ps 58:6>>] lions: __and__ I lie __even among__ them that are set on fire, __even__ the sons of men, ##whose##note:x[<<Ps 58:6; Prov 30:14>>] teeth __are__ spears and arrows, and their tongue a sharp sword.
@ Be thou exalted, O God, above the heavens; __let__ ##thy glory##note:x[<<Ps 72:19; Isa 6:3; Hab 2:14>>] __be__ above all the earth.
@ They have prepared ##a net##note:x[<<Ps 7:15–16; 35:7–8; Mic 7:2>>] for my steps; my soul is bowed down: they have digged a pit before me, into the midst whereof they are fallen __themselves__. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##My##note:x[<<Ps 112:7>>] heart is ##fixed##note:tn[or, prepared], O God, my heart is fixed: ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 34:4; Eph 5:20>>] sing and give praise.
@ ##Awake##note:x[<<Judg 5:12>>] up, ##my glory##note:x[<<Ps 16:9; 30:12>>]; awake, psaltery and harp: I __myself__ will awake early.
@ ##I will praise thee, O Lord, among the people: I will sing unto thee among the nations.##note:x[<<Ps 18:49; 22:22–23; 96:3>>]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 36:5; 103:11; 108:4>>] thy mercy __is__ great unto the heavens, and thy truth unto the clouds.
@ ##Be thou exalted, O God, above the heavens: __let__ thy glory __be__ above all the earth.##note:x[<<Ps 8:1; 57:5; Rev 15:3–4>>]

=== @58

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, Al-taschith, Michtam of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Judges the Earth

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 57:1>>]##Do##note:x[<<Ps 72:1–4; Isa 11:3–5>>] ye indeed speak righteousness, O congregation? do ye judge uprightly, O ye sons of men?note:tn[__Al-taschith…__: or, Destroy not]note:tn[__Michtam__: or, A golden Psalm]
@ Yea, ##in heart##note:x[<<Eccl 3:16>>] ye work wickedness; ye ##weigh##note:x[<<Ps 94:20; Isa 10:1>>] the violence of your hands in the earth.
@ The wicked are ##estranged##note:x[<<Ps 51:5; Prov 22:15; Isa 48:8>>] from the womb: they go astray as soon as they be born, speaking lies.note:tn[__as soon…__: Heb. from the belly]
@ Their ##poison##note:x[<<Ps 140:3; Jas 3:8>>] __is__ like the poison of a serpent: __they are__ like the deaf ##adder##note:tn[or, asp] __that__ stoppeth her ear;note:tn[__like the poison__: Heb. according to the likeness, etc]
@ Which will not hearken to the voice of charmers, charming never so wisely.note:tn[__charming…__: or, be the charmer never so cunning]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Break their##note:x[<<Ps 3:7>>] teeth, O God, in their mouth: break out the great teeth of the ##young##note:x[<<Hos 5:14>>] lions, O LORD.
@ ##Let them melt away as waters __which__ run continually: __when__ he bendeth __his bow to shoot__ his arrows, let them be as cut in pieces.##note:x[<<Josh 7:5; Ps 112:10>>]
@ As a snail __which__ melteth, let __every one of them__ pass away: __like__ the ##untimely##note:x[<<Job 3:16; Eccl 6:3>>] birth of a woman, __that__ they may not see the sun.
@ Before your pots can feel the ##thorns##note:x[<<Ps 118:12; Eccl 7:6>>], he shall take them away ##as##note:x[<<Prov 10:25>>] with a whirlwind, both living, and in __his__ wrath.note:tn[__both…__: Heb. as living as wrath]
@ The ##righteous##note:x[<<Prov 11:10; Rev 11:17–18>>] shall rejoice when he seeth the vengeance: he shall ##wash##note:x[<<Ps 68:23>>] his feet in the blood of the wicked.
@ So that a man shall say, Verily __there is__ ##a reward for##note:x[<<Isa 3:10>>] the righteous: ##verily he##note:x[<<Ps 64:9; 67:4>>] is a God that judgeth in the earth.note:tn[__a reward…__: Heb. fruit of the, etc]

=== @59

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, Al-taschith, Michtam of David; when Saul sent, and they watched the house to kill him.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Deliver Me from My Enemies

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 19:1–24>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<1 Sam 19:11–24>>]note:x[<<Ps 57:1>>]##Deliver##note:x[<<Ps 18:48>>] me from mine enemies, O my God: defend me from them that rise up against me.note:tn[__Al-taschith…__: or, Destroy not]note:tn[__Michtam__: or, A golden Psalm]note:tn[__defend…__: Heb. set me on high]
@ Deliver me from the workers of iniquity, and ##save##note:x[<<Ps 26:9; 139:19>>] me from bloody men.
@ For, lo, ##they##note:x[<<Ps 56:6>>] lie in wait for my soul: the mighty are gathered against me; ##not##note:x[<<1 Sam 24:11; Ps 69:4>>] __for__ my transgression, nor __for__ my sin, O LORD.
@ They run and prepare themselves without __my__ fault: ##awake##note:x[<<Ps 35:23>>] to ##help##note:tn[Heb. meet] me, and behold.
@ Thou therefore, O LORD God of hosts, the God of Israel, awake to visit all the heathen: be not merciful to any wicked transgressors. Selah.
@ ##They return at evening: they make a noise like a dog, and go round about the city.##note:x[<<Ps 59:14>>]
@ Behold, they ##belch##note:x[<<Prov 15:2>>] out with their mouth: ##swords##note:x[<<Ps 57:4>>] __are__ in their lips: for ##who##note:x[<<Ps 10:11>>], __say they__, doth hear?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 2:4; 37:13; Prov 1:26>>], O LORD, shalt laugh at them; thou shalt have all the heathen in derision.
@ __Because of__ ##his strength##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; Isa 26:3–4>>] will I wait upon thee: for God __is__ my note:x[<<Ps 9:9>>]##defence##note:tn[Heb. high place].
@ ##The God##note:x[<<Ps 59:17; 1 Pet 5:10>>] of my mercy shall ##prevent##note:x[<<Ps 21:3>>] me: God shall let me see __my desire__ upon mine ##enemies##note:tn[Heb. observers].
@ ##Slay##note:x[<<Rev 9:6>>] them not, lest my people forget: ##scatter##note:x[<<Deut 4:27>>] them by thy power; and bring them down, O Lord ##our shield##note:x[<<Ps 3:3>>].
@ ##__For__ the##note:x[<<Prov 12:13; 18:7>>] sin of their mouth __and__ the words of their lips let them even be taken in their pride: and for ##cursing##note:x[<<Hos 4:2>>] and lying __which__ they speak.
@ ##Consume##note:x[<<Ps 7:9>>] __them__ in wrath, consume __them__, that they __may__ not __be:__ ##and let##note:x[<<Ps 83:18; Ezek 39:7>>] them know that God ruleth in Jacob unto the ends of the earth. Selah.
@ And ##at evening##note:x[<<Ps 22:16>>] let them return; __and__ let them make a noise like a dog, and go round about the city.
@ Let them ##wander##note:x[<<Job 15:23; Ps 109:10>>] up and down for meat, and grudge if they be not satisfied.note:tn[__for meat__: Heb. to eat]note:tn[__and grudge…__: or, if they be not satisfied, then they will stay all night]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 21:13>>] I will sing of thy power; yea, I will ##sing aloud##note:x[<<Rom 15:9>>] of thy mercy in the morning: ##for thou##note:x[<<Eph 3:20>>] hast been my defence and refuge in the day of my trouble.
@ Unto thee, ##O my##note:x[<<Ps 18:1; 46:1>>] strength, will I sing: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 59:9–10>>] God __is__ my defence, __and__ the God of my mercy.

=== @60

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Shushan-eduth, Michtam of David, to teach; when he strove with Aram-naharaim and with Aram-zobah, when Joab returned, and smote of Edom in the valley of salt twelve thousand.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Victory with God

[.parallel]
(<<2 Samuel 8:1–14; 1 Chronicles 18:1–13; Psalms 108:1–13>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<2 Sam 8:3, 12–13>>]##O God##note:x[<<Ps 44:9>>], thou hast cast us off, thou hast ##scattered##note:tn[Heb. broken] us, thou hast been displeased; O turn thyself to us again.note:tn[__Michtam__: or, A golden Psalm]
@ Thou hast made the earth to tremble; thou hast broken it: ##heal##note:x[<<2 Chr 7:14; Isa 30:26; Hos 6:1>>] the breaches thereof; for it shaketh.
@ Thou hast ##shewed##note:x[<<Ps 71:20>>] thy people hard things: thou hast made us ##to drink##note:x[<<Isa 51:17, 22>>] the wine of astonishment.
@ Thou hast given ##a banner##note:x[<<Exod 17:15; Ps 20:5; Isa 11:12>>] to them that fear thee, that it may be displayed because of the truth. Selah.
@ ##That##note:x[<<Ps 108:6–13>>] thy beloved may be delivered; ##save##note:x[<<Exod 15:6; Isa 41:10>>] __with__ thy right hand, and hear me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##God##note:x[<<Ps 89:35>>] hath spoken in his holiness; I will rejoice, I will divide ##Shechem##note:x[<<Gen 12:6>>], and mete out the ##valley##note:x[<<Josh 13:27>>] of Succoth.
@ Gilead __is__ mine, and Manasseh __is__ mine; Ephraim also __is__ the ##strength##note:x[<<Deut 33:17>>] of mine head; ##Judah##note:x[<<Gen 49:10>>] __is__ my lawgiver;
@ Moab __is__ my washpot; ##over##note:x[<<2 Sam 8:14>>] Edom will I cast out my shoe: Philistia, ##triumph##note:x[<<2 Sam 8:1; Ps 108:9–10>>] thou because of me.note:tn[__triumph…__: or, triumph thou over me: (by an irony)]
@ Who will bring me __into__ the ##strong city##note:x[<<2 Sam 12:26–31>>]? who will lead me into Edom?note:tn[__strong…__: Heb. city of strength?]
@ __Wilt__ not thou, O God, __which__ ##hadst##note:x[<<Ps 60:1; 108:11>>] cast us off? and __thou__, O God, __which__ ##didst##note:x[<<Josh 7:12>>] not go out with our armies?
@ ##Give##note:x[<<Ps 130:8>>] us help from trouble: for ##vain##note:x[<<Ps 108:12; 146:3>>] __is__ the help of man.note:tn[__help of man__: Heb. salvation, etc]
@ Through God ##we shall##note:x[<<Josh 1:9>>] do valiantly: for he __it is that__ shall ##tread##note:x[<<Ps 44:5; Isa 63:3>>] down our enemies.

=== @61

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Neginah, __A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== You Have Heard My Vows

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 55:1–2; 130:2; Phil 4:6>>] my cry, O God; attend unto my prayer.
@ ##From##note:x[<<Ps 139:9–10>>] the end of the earth will I cry unto thee, when my heart is overwhelmed: lead me to ##the rock##note:x[<<Ps 62:6; Isa 32:2>>] __that__ is higher than I.
@ For ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 116:2; 140:7>>] hast been a shelter for me, __and__ a ##strong##note:x[<<Prov 18:10>>] tower from the enemy.
@ I will ##abide##note:x[<<Ps 23:6; 27:4>>] in thy tabernacle for ever: I will note:x[<<Ps 17:8>>]##trust##note:tn[or, make my refuge] in the covert of thy wings. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ For thou, O God, ##hast heard##note:x[<<Ps 56:12>>] my vows: thou hast given __me__ the ##heritage##note:x[<<Ps 115:13; Mal 3:16–18>>] of those that fear thy name.
@ note:x[<<Ps 21:4, 6>>]Thou wilt prolong the king’s life: __and__ his years as ##many generations##note:x[<<Ps 89:36–37>>].note:tn[__Thou…__: Heb. Thou shalt add days to the days of the king]note:tn[__as…__: Heb. as generation and generation]
@ He shall ##abide##note:x[<<Ps 41:12>>] before God for ever: O ##prepare##note:x[<<Gen 24:27; Ps 40:11>>] mercy and truth, __which__ may preserve him.
@ So will I ##sing##note:x[<<Ps 145:1–2>>] praise unto thy name for ever, ##that I##note:x[<<Ps 65:1>>] may daily perform my vows.

=== @62

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, to Jeduthun, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Waiting on God

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 62:2>>]##Truly##note:tn[or, Only] ##my soul##note:x[<<Isa 40:31; Lam 3:25>>] ##waiteth##note:tn[Heb. is silent] upon God: from him __cometh__ my salvation.
@ ##He only##note:x[<<Ps 18:2; 62:6>>] __is__ my rock and my salvation; __he is__ my ##defence##note:tn[Heb. high place]; ##I shall##note:x[<<2 Cor 4:8–9>>] not be greatly moved.
@ ##How##note:x[<<Ps 82:2; Jer 4:14>>] long will ye ##imagine##note:x[<<Ps 140:2>>] mischief against a man? ye shall be slain all of you: as a bowing wall __shall ye be, and as__ a tottering fence.
@ They only consult to cast __him__ down from his excellency: they delight in lies: they ##bless##note:x[<<Ps 28:3; 55:21>>] with their mouth, but they curse note:x[<<Ps 5:9>>]##inwardly##note:tn[Heb. in their inward parts]. Selah.
@ My soul, ##wait##note:x[<<Ps 27:13–14; Lam 3:24–26; Mic 7:7>>] thou only upon God; for my expectation __is__ from him.
@ He only __is__ my ##rock##note:x[<<Ps 62:2>>] and my salvation: __he is__ my defence; ##I shall##note:x[<<Ps 16:8; 112:6>>] not be moved.
@ In God __is__ my salvation and my ##glory##note:x[<<Ps 3:3>>]: the ##rock##note:x[<<Ps 94:22; Isa 26:4>>] of my strength, __and__ my refuge, __is__ in God.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Trust##note:x[<<Isa 26:4>>] in him at all times; __ye__ people, ##pour##note:x[<<Lam 2:19; Phil 4:6>>] out your heart before him: God __is__ a refuge for us. Selah.
