@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Ps 39:5>>] men of low degree __are__ vanity, __and__ men of high degree __are__ a lie: to be laid in the balance, they __are__ ##altogether##note:tn[or, alike] ##__lighter__##note:x[<<Isa 40:15, 17>>] than vanity.
@ ##Trust##note:x[<<Jer 17:11>>] not in oppression, and become not vain in robbery: if ##riches##note:x[<<Ps 52:7; 1 Tim 6:17>>] increase, set not your heart __upon them__.
@ God hath ##spoken##note:x[<<Job 33:14>>] once; twice have I heard this; that note:x[<<Isa 26:4; Rev 19:1>>]##power##note:tn[or, strength] __belongeth__ unto God.
@ Also unto thee, O Lord, __belongeth__ ##mercy##note:x[<<Ps 103:8>>]: for thou ##renderest##note:x[<<Job 34:11; Matt 16:27>>] to every man according to his work.

=== @63

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David, when he was in the wilderness of Judah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Thirsting for God

[.parallel]
(<<2 Samuel 15:30–37>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O God, thou __art__ my God; early will I seek thee: my ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 42:1–2; 84:2; 143:6>>] thirsteth for thee, my flesh longeth for thee in a dry and ##thirsty##note:tn[Heb. weary] land, where no water is;note:tn[__where…__: without water]
@ ##To see##note:x[<<Ps 27:4; 105:4>>] thy power and thy glory, so __as__ I have seen thee ##in the##note:x[<<Ps 84:2–11>>] sanctuary.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Phil 1:23>>] thy lovingkindness __is__ better than life, my ##lips##note:x[<<Ps 51:15>>] shall praise thee.
@ ##Thus##note:x[<<Ps 104:33>>] will I bless thee while I live: ##I will lift##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:22–66; Ps 134:2>>] up my hands in thy name.
@ ##My soul##note:x[<<Ps 17:15; 36:7–9; Isa 25:6>>] shall be satisfied as __with__ ##marrow##note:tn[Heb. fatness] and fatness; and my mouth shall praise __thee__ with joyful lips:
@ ##When I remember thee upon my bed, __and__ meditate on thee in the __night__ watches.##note:x[<<Ps 42:8; 119:147–148; 149:5>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 54:3–4>>] thou hast been my help, ##therefore##note:x[<<Ps 61:4; 2 Cor 1:10>>] in the shadow of thy wings will I rejoice.
@ My soul followeth hard after thee: ##thy##note:x[<<Ps 18:35; 73:23; Isa 41:10>>] right hand upholdeth me.
@ But those __that__ ##seek##note:x[<<Ps 40:14>>] my soul, to destroy __it__, shall ##go##note:x[<<Ps 9:17; 55:15>>] into the lower parts of the earth.
@ ##They shall fall##note:x[<<Jer 18:21; Ezek 35:5>>] by the sword: they shall be ##a portion##note:x[<<Ezek 39:4>>] for foxes.note:tn[__They shall fall…__: Heb. They shall make him run out like water by the hands of]
@ But ##the king##note:x[<<Ps 21:1>>] shall rejoice in God; every one that ##sweareth##note:x[<<Deut 6:13; Isa 45:23>>] by him shall glory: but the mouth of them that speak lies shall be stopped.

=== @64

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Hurtful Tongue

[.parallel]
(<<James 3:1–12>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 130:1–2; 143:1–3>>] my voice, O God, in my prayer: ##preserve##note:x[<<Ps 34:4>>] my life from fear of the enemy.
@ ##Hide##note:x[<<Ps 31:20; 143:9>>] me from the ##secret##note:x[<<Ps 109:2–3>>] counsel of the wicked; from the insurrection of the workers of iniquity:
@ Who ##whet##note:x[<<Ps 57:4; Jer 9:3; Jas 3:6–8>>] their tongue like a sword, __and__ bend __their bows to shoot__ their arrows, __even__ bitter words:
@ That they may shoot in secret at ##the perfect##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:22–23>>]: suddenly do they shoot at him, and fear not.
@ They encourage themselves __in__ an evil ##matter##note:tn[or, speech]: they commune ##of laying snares##note:x[<<Ps 140:5>>] privily; they say, ##Who##note:x[<<Ps 10:11; Ezek 8:12>>] shall see them?note:tn[__of…__: Heb. to hide his snares]
@ They ##search##note:x[<<Matt 26:59>>] out iniquities; they accomplish a diligent search: ##both##note:x[<<Ps 5:9; Jer 17:9–10>>] the inward __thought__ of every one __of them__, and the heart, __is__ deep.note:tn[__they…__: or, we are consumed by that which they have throughly searched]note:tn[__a diligent…__: Heb. a search searched]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But ##God##note:x[<<Ps 7:12–13; 18:14>>] shall shoot at them __with__ an arrow; ##suddenly##note:x[<<Prov 6:15>>] shall they be wounded.note:tn[__shall they…__: Heb. their wound shall be]
@ So they shall make their own ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 140:9; Prov 12:13; 18:7>>] to fall upon themselves: all that see them shall flee away.
@ And all men shall ##fear##note:x[<<Ps 40:3; Jer 51:10>>], and shall declare the work of God; for ##they##note:x[<<Hos 14:9>>] shall wisely consider of his doing.
@ The ##righteous##note:x[<<Ps 32:11; 40:3>>] shall be glad in the LORD, and shall trust in him; and all the ##upright##note:x[<<1 Cor 1:30–31>>] in heart shall glory.

=== @65

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm __and__ Song of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise Awaits God in Zion

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise note:x[<<Ps 62:1>>]##waiteth##note:tn[Heb. is silent] for thee, O God, in Sion: and ##unto##note:x[<<Ps 76:11; 116:17>>] thee shall the vow be performed.
@ O ##thou##note:x[<<Isa 65:24; Jer 29:12–13; 1 John 5:14–15>>] that hearest prayer, unto thee shall all flesh come.
@ ##Iniquities##note:tn[Heb. Words, or, Matters of iniquities] ##prevail##note:x[<<Ps 38:4>>] against me: __as for__ our ##transgressions##note:x[<<Ps 79:9; Isa 6:7>>], thou shalt purge them away.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 84:4>>] __is the man whom__ thou ##choosest##note:x[<<Ps 106:4–5; Eph 1:4>>], and causest to approach __unto thee, that__ he may dwell in thy courts: we shall be satisfied with the goodness of thy house, __even__ of thy holy temple.
@ __By__ ##terrible##note:x[<<Deut 10:21; Ps 66:3>>] things in righteousness wilt thou answer us, O God of our salvation; __who art__ ##the confidence##note:x[<<Isa 45:22>>] of all the ends of the earth, and of them that are afar off __upon__ the sea:

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Which##note:x[<<Hab 3:6>>] by his strength setteth fast the mountains; __being__ ##girded##note:x[<<Ps 93:1>>] with power:
@ ##Which##note:x[<<Ps 89:9>>] stilleth the ##noise##note:x[<<Ps 93:3–4>>] of the seas, the noise of their waves, and the ##tumult##note:x[<<Isa 17:12–13>>] of the people.
@ They also that dwell in the uttermost parts are afraid at thy tokens: thou makest the ##outgoings##note:x[<<Ps 104:20–23; 136:8>>] of the morning and evening to note:x[<<Ps 148:3>>]##rejoice##note:tn[or, sing].
@ Thou ##visitest##note:x[<<Ps 104:13–14>>] the earth, and waterest it: thou greatly enrichest it with ##the river##note:x[<<Ps 46:4; Rev 22:1>>] of God, __which__ is full of water: thou preparest them corn, when thou hast so provided for it.note:tn[__and…__: or, after thou hadst made it to desire rain]
@ Thou waterest the ridges thereof abundantly: thou ##settlest##note:tn[or, causest rain to descend into] the furrows thereof: thou makest it soft with showers: thou ##blessest##note:x[<<Ps 147:8; 1 Cor 3:6–7>>] the springing thereof.note:tn[__makest…__: Heb. dissolvest it]
@ Thou ##crownest##note:x[<<Ps 5:12; 103:4>>] the year ##with##note:tn[Heb. of] thy goodness; and ##thy paths##note:x[<<Joel 2:21–26>>] drop fatness.
@ They ##drop##note:x[<<Job 38:26–27; Ps 104:10–13>>] __upon__ the pastures of the wilderness: and the little hills rejoice on every side.note:tn[__rejoice…__: Heb. are girded with joy]
@ The ##pastures##note:x[<<Acts 14:17>>] are clothed with flocks; the valleys also are covered over with corn; ##they shout##note:x[<<Isa 35:1–2; 55:12>>] for joy, they also sing.

=== @66

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Song __or__ Psalm.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Make a Joyful Noise

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 100:1–5>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Ps 98:4; 100:1>>] a joyful noise unto God, ##all ye lands##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:23–24>>]:note:tn[__all…__: Heb. all the earth]
@ ##Sing forth the honour of his name: make his praise glorious.##note:x[<<Ps 105:2–3; Isa 49:13; Rev 5:13>>]
@ Say unto God, ##How terrible##note:x[<<Ps 65:5>>] __art thou in__ thy works! ##through##note:x[<<Ps 18:44; 81:15>>] the greatness of thy power shall thine enemies submit themselves unto thee.note:tn[__submit…__: or, yield feigned obedience: Heb. lie]
@ ##All the earth shall worship thee, and shall sing unto thee; they shall sing __to__ thy name. Selah.##note:x[<<Ps 22:27; 96:1–2; 117:1>>]
@ ##Come##note:x[<<Num 23:23; Ps 126:1–3>>] and see the works of God: __he is__ terrible __in his__ doing toward the children of men.
@ ##He turned##note:x[<<Exod 14:21–22; Ps 78:13>>] the sea into dry __land:__ ##they##note:x[<<Josh 3:16>>] went through the flood on foot: there did we rejoice in him.
@ He ##ruleth##note:x[<<Matt 28:18>>] by his power for ever; ##his eyes##note:x[<<2 Chr 16:9; Ps 11:4>>] behold the nations: let not the rebellious exalt themselves. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O bless our God, ye people, and make the voice of his praise to be heard:
@ Which ##holdeth##note:tn[Heb. putteth] our soul in life, and ##suffereth##note:x[<<Ps 94:18; 112:6; 121:3>>] not our feet to be moved.
@ For thou, O God, ##hast proved us##note:x[<<Ps 17:3>>]: thou hast ##tried##note:x[<<Isa 48:10; 1 Pet 1:6–7>>] us, as silver is tried.
@ Thou ##broughtest##note:x[<<Lam 1:13>>] us into the net; thou laidst affliction upon our loins.
@ Thou hast ##caused##note:x[<<Isa 51:23>>] men to ride over our heads; we went ##through##note:x[<<Isa 43:1–2>>] fire and through water: ##but thou##note:x[<<Jas 5:11>>] broughtest us out into a ##wealthy##note:tn[Heb. moist] __place__.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will ##go into##note:x[<<Ps 100:4>>] thy house with burnt offerings: I will ##pay##note:x[<<Ps 56:12; Eccl 5:4>>] thee my vows,
@ Which my lips have note:x[<<Judg 11:35–36>>]##uttered##note:tn[Heb. opened], and my mouth hath spoken, ##when##note:x[<<1 Sam 1:11>>] I was in trouble.
@ I will offer unto thee burnt sacrifices of ##fatlings##note:tn[Heb. marrow], with the incense of rams; I will offer bullocks with goats. Selah.
@ ##Come##note:x[<<Ps 34:11; 71:18; Mal 3:16>>] __and__ hear, all ye that fear God, and I will declare what he hath done for my soul.
@ ##I cried##note:x[<<Ps 34:6; 116:1–2, 12>>] unto him with my mouth, and he was extolled with my tongue.
@ ##If I regard##note:x[<<Prov 15:29; 28:9; John 9:31>>] iniquity in my heart, the Lord will not hear __me:__
@ ##__But__ verily God hath heard __me;__ he hath attended to the voice of my prayer.##note:x[<<Ps 116:1–2; Lam 3:55–56; Heb 5:7>>]
@ ##Blessed __be__ God, which hath not turned away my prayer, nor his mercy from me.##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:14–15; Ps 51:11; 86:12–13>>]

=== @67

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician on Neginoth, A Psalm __or__ Song.

[discrete.pericope]
==== May God Cause His Face to Shine upon Us

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 4:1>>]God be merciful unto us, and bless us; __and__ ##cause##note:x[<<Ps 4:6; 2 Cor 4:6>>] his face to shine ##upon##note:tn[Heb. with] us; Selah.note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]
@ ##That##note:x[<<Ps 98:2–3>>] ##thy way##note:x[<<Acts 18:25>>] may be known upon earth, thy ##saving##note:x[<<Titus 2:11>>] health among all nations.
@ ##Let the people praise thee, O God; let all the people praise thee.##note:x[<<Ps 45:17; 67:5; 74:21>>]
@ ##O let##note:x[<<Rom 15:10–11>>] the nations be glad and sing for joy: ##for thou##note:x[<<Ps 9:8; 98:9>>] shalt judge the people righteously, and ##govern##note:tn[Heb. lead] the nations upon earth. Selah.
@ ##Let the people praise thee, O God; let all the people praise thee.##note:x[<<Ps 67:3; Matt 6:9–10>>]
@ ##__Then__##note:x[<<Lev 26:4; Ps 85:9–12>>] shall the earth yield her increase; __and__ God, __even__ ##our own##note:x[<<Exod 3:15>>] God, shall bless us.
@ God shall bless us; and ##all the##note:x[<<Ps 22:27; Isa 45:22>>] ends of the earth shall ##fear##note:x[<<Acts 13:26>>] him.

=== @68

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm __or__ Song of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Enemies Are Scattered

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Let ##God arise##note:x[<<Num 10:35; Isa 42:13–14; 51:9–10>>], let his enemies be scattered: let them also that hate him flee before him.note:tn[__before…__: Heb. from his face]
@ ##As smoke##note:x[<<Ps 37:20; Hos 13:3>>] is driven away, __so__ drive __them__ away: ##as wax##note:x[<<Mic 1:4>>] melteth before the fire, __so__ let the wicked perish at the presence of God.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 64:10; Rev 18:20>>] let the righteous be glad; let them ##rejoice##note:x[<<1 Thess 5:16>>] before God: yea, let them exceedingly rejoice.note:tn[__exceedingly…__: Heb. rejoice with gladness]
@ ##Sing unto God##note:x[<<Ps 66:4; Isa 12:4–6>>], sing praises to his name: extol him that ##rideth##note:x[<<Deut 33:26>>] upon the heavens by his name JAH, and rejoice before him.
@ ##A father##note:x[<<Ps 10:14; 82:3–4; Jer 49:11>>] of the fatherless, and a judge of the widows, __is__ God in his holy habitation.
@ ##God##note:x[<<Ps 107:10; 113:9>>] setteth the solitary in families: ##he bringeth##note:x[<<Ps 107:10>>] out those which are bound with chains: but the rebellious dwell in a dry __land__.note:tn[__in families__: Heb. in a house]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##O God##note:x[<<Exod 13:21; Judg 4:14>>], when thou wentest forth before thy people, when ##thou didst##note:x[<<Judg 5:4>>] march through the wilderness; Selah:
@ The earth shook, ##the heavens##note:x[<<Judg 5:4–5>>] also dropped at the presence of God: __even__ ##Sinai##note:x[<<Exod 19:16, 18>>] itself __was moved__ at the presence of God, the God of Israel.
@ Thou, O God, ##didst##note:x[<<Deut 11:14; Ps 65:9–13; Ezek 34:26>>] ##send##note:tn[Heb. shake out] a plentiful rain, whereby thou didst ##confirm##note:tn[Heb. confirm it] thine inheritance, when it was weary.
@ ##Thy congregation##note:x[<<Ps 74:19>>] hath dwelt therein: ##thou##note:x[<<Deut 26:9–10>>], O God, hast prepared of thy goodness for the poor.
@ The ##Lord##note:x[<<Ps 40:3>>] gave the word: great __was__ the note:x[<<Exod 15:20; Ps 68:25>>]##company##note:tn[Heb. army] of those that published __it__.
@ ##Kings##note:x[<<Josh 10:16; Judg 5:19>>] of armies did flee apace: and she that tarried at home divided the spoil.note:tn[__did…__: Heb. did flee, did flee]
@ Though ye have lien among the pots, __yet shall ye be as__ ##the wings##note:x[<<Ps 105:37>>] of a dove covered with silver, and her feathers with yellow gold.
@ When the Almighty scattered kings in it, it was __white__ ##as snow##note:x[<<Isa 1:18>>] in Salmon.note:tn[__in it…__: or, for her, she]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The hill ##of God##note:x[<<Ps 87:1–2>>] __is as__ the hill of Bashan; an high hill __as__ the hill of Bashan.
@ Why leap ye, ye high hills? __this is__ ##the hill##note:x[<<Deut 12:5; Ps 132:13–14; Rev 21:2–3>>] __which__ God desireth to dwell in; yea, the LORD will dwell __in it__ for ever.
@ The ##chariots##note:x[<<Deut 33:2; Dan 7:10; Rev 5:11>>] of God __are__ twenty thousand, __even__ thousands of angels: the Lord __is__ among them, __as in__ Sinai, in the holy __place__.note:tn[__even…__: or, even many thousands]
@ Thou hast ##ascended##note:x[<<Eph 4:8–10>>] on high, thou hast led captivity captive: thou hast ##received##note:x[<<Eph 4:8>>] gifts for men; yea, __for__ the rebellious also, ##that##note:x[<<2 Cor 6:16>>] the LORD God might dwell __among them__.note:tn[__for men__: Heb. in the man]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 103:1–22; Eph 1:3>>] __be__ the Lord, __who__ ##daily##note:x[<<Lam 3:23>>] loadeth us __with benefits, even__ the God of our salvation. Selah.
@ __He that is__ ##our God##note:x[<<Isa 12:2>>] __is__ the God of salvation; and ##unto##note:x[<<Deut 32:39; Rev 1:18>>] GOD the Lord __belong__ the issues from death.
@ But ##God##note:x[<<Ps 110:6; Hab 3:13>>] shall wound the head of his enemies, __and__ the hairy scalp ##of such##note:x[<<Ps 7:12>>] an one as goeth on still in his trespasses.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The Lord said, I will bring again from ##Bashan##note:x[<<Num 21:33>>], I will bring __my people__ again from ##the depths##note:x[<<Ezek 36:24>>] of the sea:
@ ##That##note:x[<<Ps 58:10>>] thy foot may be ##dipped##note:tn[or, red] in the blood of __thine__ enemies, __and__ ##the tongue##note:x[<<1 Kgs 21:19; 22:38>>] of thy dogs in the same.
@ They have seen thy goings, O God; __even__ the goings of my God, my King, in the sanctuary.
@ The singers went before, the players on instruments __followed__ after; ##among##note:x[<<Exod 15:20; Judg 11:34>>] __them were__ the damsels playing with timbrels.
@ Bless ye God in the congregations, __even__ the Lord, ##from the fountain##note:x[<<Deut 33:28; Isa 48:1>>] of Israel.note:tn[__from…__: or, ye that are of the fountain of Israel]
@ There __is__ ##little##note:x[<<1 Sam 9:21>>] Benjamin __with__ their ruler, the princes of Judah __and__ their council, the princes of Zebulun, __and__ the princes of Naphtali.note:tn[__and their…__: or, with their company]
@ Thy God hath ##commanded##note:x[<<Ps 71:3>>] thy strength: ##strengthen##note:x[<<Ps 138:8; Phil 1:6>>], O God, that which thou hast wrought for us.
@ ##Because##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:3>>] of thy temple at Jerusalem ##shall##note:x[<<1 Kgs 10:10; Ps 76:11>>] kings bring presents unto thee.
@ Rebuke the company of spearmen, the ##multitude##note:x[<<Ps 22:12–13>>] of the bulls, with the calves of the people, __till every one__ submit himself with pieces of silver: scatter thou the people __that__ delight in war.note:tn[__the company…__: or, the beasts of the reeds]note:tn[__scatter…__: or, he scattereth]
@ ##Princes##note:x[<<Isa 19:18–25; 45:14>>] shall come out of Egypt; ##Ethiopia##note:x[<<Zeph 3:10>>] shall soon stretch out her hands unto God.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Sing unto God, ##ye kingdoms##note:x[<<Deut 32:43; Ps 67:2–5; 117:1–2>>] of the earth; O sing praises unto the Lord; Selah:
@ To him that ##rideth##note:x[<<Ps 18:10; 68:4; 104:3>>] upon the heavens of heavens, __which were__ of old; lo, he doth send out his voice, __and that__ a mighty voice.note:tn[__send…__: Heb. give]
@ ##Ascribe##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:28–29; Rev 19:6>>] ye strength unto God: ##his excellency##note:x[<<2 Pet 1:17>>] __is__ over Israel, and his strength __is__ in the ##clouds##note:tn[or, heavens].
@ O God, __thou art__ terrible out of thy holy places: the God of Israel __is__ ##he that giveth##note:x[<<Ps 29:11; Phil 4:13; Col 1:11>>] strength and power unto __his__ people. Blessed __be__ God.

=== @69

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Shoshannim, __A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Waters Are up to My Neck

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Save me, O God; for ##the waters##note:x[<<Ps 69:14–15; Isa 43:2; Lam 3:54>>] are come in unto __my__ soul.
@ ##I sink##note:x[<<Ps 40:2; Jer 38:6>>] in deep mire, where __there is__ no standing: I am come into deep waters, where ##the floods##note:x[<<Matt 7:25>>] overflow me.note:tn[__deep mire__: Heb. the mire of depth]note:tn[__deep waters__: Heb. depth of waters]
@ ##I am##note:x[<<Ps 6:6>>] weary of my crying: my throat is dried: ##mine##note:x[<<Ps 119:82, 123>>] eyes fail while I wait for my God.
@ They that ##hate##note:x[<<John 15:25>>] me without a cause are more than the hairs of mine head: they that would destroy me, ##__being__##note:x[<<Ps 35:19; 109:3–5>>] mine enemies wrongfully, are mighty: then I restored __that__ which I took not away.
@ O God, thou knowest my foolishness; note:x[<<Ps 17:3>>]and my ##sins##note:tn[Heb. guiltiness] are not ##hid##note:x[<<Ps 38:9; Jer 16:17>>] from thee.
@ ##Let not##note:x[<<Ps 25:3; 35:26; Luke 24:19–21>>] them that wait on thee, O Lord GOD of hosts, be ashamed for my sake: let not those that seek thee be confounded for my sake, O God of Israel.
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 44:22; Jer 15:15>>] for thy sake I have borne reproach; ##shame##note:x[<<Isa 50:6>>] hath covered my face.
@ I am ##become##note:x[<<Ps 31:11; John 1:11; 7:5>>] a stranger unto my brethren, and an alien unto my mother’s children.
@ For the ##zeal##note:x[<<Ps 119:139; John 2:14–17>>] of thine house hath eaten me up; ##and the##note:x[<<Rom 15:3>>] reproaches of them that reproached thee are fallen upon me.
@ ##When I wept, __and chastened__ my soul with fasting, that was to my reproach.##note:x[<<Luke 7:33–34>>]
@ I made sackcloth also my garment; and ##I became##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:7; Jer 24:9>>] a proverb to them.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Acts 4:26–27>>] that sit in the gate speak against me; and ##I __was__##note:x[<<Ps 35:15–16>>] the song of the ##drunkards##note:tn[Heb. drinkers of strong drink].

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But as for me, ##my prayer##note:x[<<Ps 55:16–17>>] __is__ unto thee, O LORD, ##__in__ an##note:x[<<Isa 49:8; 2 Cor 6:2>>] acceptable time: O God, in the multitude of thy mercy hear me, in the truth of thy salvation.
@ ##Deliver##note:x[<<Jer 38:6–13>>] me ##out of##note:x[<<Ps 144:7>>] the mire, and ##let me##note:x[<<Ps 109:21>>] not sink: let me be delivered from them that hate me, and out of the deep waters.
@ Let not the ##waterflood##note:x[<<Isa 43:1–2; Jonah 2:2–7; Rev 12:15–16>>] overflow me, neither let the deep swallow me up, and let not the pit shut her mouth upon me.
@ Hear me, O LORD; ##for thy##note:x[<<Ps 63:3; 109:21>>] lovingkindness __is__ good: ##turn##note:x[<<Ps 25:16>>] unto me according to the multitude of thy tender mercies.
@ And ##hide##note:x[<<Ps 27:9; 102:2; Matt 27:46>>] not thy face from thy servant; for I am in trouble: hear me speedily.note:tn[__hear…__: Heb. make haste to hear me]
@ ##Draw##note:x[<<Ps 22:1, 19>>] nigh unto my soul, __and__ ##redeem##note:x[<<Ps 111:9>>] it: deliver me because of mine enemies.
@ Thou hast known ##my reproach##note:x[<<Isa 53:3; Heb 12:2; 1 Pet 2:23>>], and my shame, and my dishonour: mine adversaries __are__ all before thee.
@ Reproach hath broken my heart; and I am full of heaviness: and ##I looked##note:x[<<Isa 63:5>>] __for some__ to take pity, ##but __there__##note:x[<<Ps 142:4>>] __was__ none; and for ##comforters##note:x[<<Job 16:2>>], but I found none.note:tn[__to take…__: Heb. to lament with me]
@ They gave me also ##gall for my meat##note:x[<<Matt 27:34>>]; and in my thirst they gave me ##vinegar##note:x[<<Mark 15:23; Luke 23:36>>] to drink.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Let their table##note:x[<<Prov 1:32; Rom 11:8–10>>] become a snare before them: and __that which should have been__ for __their__ welfare, __let it become__ ##a trap##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:8>>].
@ Let ##their eyes##note:x[<<Isa 6:9–10>>] be darkened, that they see not; and ##make their##note:x[<<Dan 5:6; Rom 11:10>>] loins continually to shake.
@ ##Pour##note:x[<<Ps 79:6; Hos 5:10; Rev 16:1>>] out thine indignation upon them, and let thy wrathful anger take hold of them.
@ ##Let their##note:x[<<Jer 7:12–14; Matt 23:38; Acts 1:20>>] habitation be desolate; __and__ let none dwell in their tents.note:tn[__their habitation__: Heb. their palace]note:tn[__let none…__: Heb. let there not be a dweller]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Zech 1:15>>] they persecute __him__ ##whom##note:x[<<Isa 53:4, 10>>] thou hast smitten; and they talk to the grief of those whom thou hast wounded.note:tn[__those…__: Heb. thy wounded]
@ ##Add##note:x[<<Rom 1:28; Rev 22:10–11>>] iniquity unto their iniquity: and ##let them##note:x[<<Isa 26:10>>] not come into thy righteousness.note:tn[__iniquity unto…__: or, punishment of iniquity, etc]
@ Let them be ##blotted##note:x[<<Exod 32:32–33>>] out of the book of the living, and not ##be written##note:x[<<Luke 10:20; Phil 4:3>>] with the righteous.
@ But ##I __am__ poor##note:x[<<Ps 40:17; 109:22>>] and sorrowful: ##let thy##note:x[<<Ps 91:14–16>>] salvation, O God, set me up on high.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 28:7; 118:28–29>>] praise the name of God with a song, and will ##magnify##note:x[<<Ps 34:3>>] him with thanksgiving.
@ __This__ ##also shall##note:x[<<Ps 50:13–14, 23; Heb 13:15>>] please the LORD better than an ox __or__ bullock that hath horns and hoofs.
@ note:x[<<John 16:22>>]The ##humble##note:tn[or, meek] shall see __this, and__ be glad: and ##your heart##note:x[<<Ps 22:26; Isa 55:6–7>>] shall live that seek God.
@ For the LORD heareth the poor, and despiseth not ##his prisoners##note:x[<<Zech 9:11–12; Acts 5:18–19; Rev 2:10>>].
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Ps 96:11; 150:6; Isa 49:13>>] the heaven and earth praise him, the seas, and every thing that ##moveth##note:tn[Heb. creepeth] therein.
@ For ##God##note:x[<<Ps 51:18; Isa 44:26; 46:13>>] will save Zion, and will build the cities of Judah: that they may dwell there, and have it in possession.
@ ##The seed##note:x[<<Ps 102:28; Acts 2:39>>] also of his servants shall inherit it: and ##they##note:x[<<Jas 1:12>>] that love his name shall dwell therein.

=== @70

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, __A Psalm__ of David, to bring to remembrance.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Hurry, O LORD, to Help Me!

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 40:1–17; Psalms 141:1–10>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ __Make haste__, ##O God##note:x[<<Ps 40:13–17; 71:12; 143:7>>], to deliver me; make haste to help me, O LORD.note:tn[__to help…__: Heb. to my help]
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Ps 35:4, 26; Isa 41:11>>] them be ashamed and confounded that seek after my soul: let them be turned backward, and put to confusion, that desire my hurt.
@ Let them be turned ##back##note:x[<<Ps 40:15; Acts 1:18>>] for a reward of their shame that say, ##Aha, aha##note:x[<<Prov 24:17–18>>].
@ ##Let all those that seek thee rejoice and be glad in thee: and let such as love thy salvation say continually, Let God be magnified.##note:x[<<Ps 35:27; 40:16; Lam 3:25>>]
@ But ##I __am__##note:x[<<Ps 40:17>>] poor and needy: ##make##note:x[<<Ps 141:1>>] haste unto me, O God: thou __art__ my help and my deliverer; ##O LORD##note:x[<<Rev 22:20>>], make no tarrying.

=== @71

[discrete.pericope]
==== Be My Rock of Refuge

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ In thee, O LORD, ##do I##note:x[<<Ps 22:5; 25:2–3; 31:1–3>>] put my trust: let me never be put to confusion.
@ Deliver me ##in thy##note:x[<<Ps 31:1; 143:11>>] righteousness, and cause me to escape: ##incline##note:x[<<Ps 17:6>>] thine ear unto me, and save me.
@ Be thou ##my strong habitation##note:x[<<Ps 31:2–3; Prov 18:10>>], whereunto I may continually resort: thou hast given commandment to save me; for thou __art__ ##my rock##note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>] and my fortress.note:tn[__Be…__: Heb. Be thou to me for a rock of habitation]
@ Deliver me, O my God, ##out of the##note:x[<<Ps 59:1–2; 140:1–4>>] hand of the wicked, out of the hand of the unrighteous and cruel man.
@ ##For thou##note:x[<<Ps 39:7; 119:81; Jer 17:7>>] __art__ my hope, O Lord GOD: __thou art__ my trust from my youth.
@ ##By thee##note:x[<<Ps 22:9–10>>] have I been holden up from the womb: ##thou art##note:x[<<Ps 139:15–16; Jer 1:5>>] he that took me out of my mother’s bowels: my praise __shall be__ continually of thee.
@ I am ##as a wonder##note:x[<<Isa 8:18; Acts 4:13; 1 Cor 4:9>>] unto many; but thou __art__ my strong refuge.
@ ##Let my mouth be filled __with__ thy praise __and with__ thy honour all the day.##note:x[<<Ps 35:28; 71:15; 146:2>>]
@ ##Cast##note:x[<<Ps 71:18; Isa 46:4; 2 Tim 1:12>>] me not off in the time of old age; forsake me not when my strength faileth.
@ For mine enemies speak against me; ##and they##note:x[<<Ps 56:6>>] that lay wait for my soul ##take##note:x[<<Ps 83:3>>] counsel together,note:tn[__lay…__: Heb. watch, or, observe]
@ Saying, God hath forsaken him: persecute and take him; ##for __there__##note:x[<<Ps 7:2>>] __is__ none to deliver __him__.
@ ##O God##note:x[<<Ps 22:11; 35:22; 38:21–22>>], be not far from me: O my God, make haste for my help.
@ ##Let them be##note:x[<<Ps 71:24; Jer 20:11>>] confounded __and__ consumed that are adversaries to my soul; let them be ##covered##note:x[<<Ps 109:29>>] __with__ reproach and dishonour that seek my hurt.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 43:5; Lam 3:26; 1 John 3:3>>] I will hope continually, and will yet praise thee more and more.
@ ##My mouth##note:x[<<Ps 71:8>>] shall shew forth thy righteousness __and__ thy salvation ##all the day##note:x[<<Ps 35:28>>]; for ##I know##note:x[<<Ps 40:5>>] not the numbers __thereof__.
@ ##I will go##note:x[<<Ps 29:11; Phil 4:13>>] in the strength of the Lord GOD: I will make mention of ##thy righteousness##note:x[<<Phil 3:9>>], __even__ of thine only.
@ O God, ##thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 71:5; 119:9, 102>>] taught me from my youth: and hitherto have I declared thy wondrous works.
@ ##Now##note:x[<<Ps 71:9; Isa 46:4>>] also when I am old and grayheaded, O God, forsake me not; ##until I##note:x[<<Ps 78:4>>] have shewed thy strength unto __this__ generation, __and__ thy power to every one __that__ is to come.note:tn[__when…__: Heb. unto old age and gray hairs]note:tn[__thy strength__: Heb. thine arm]
@ ##Thy righteousness##note:x[<<Ps 36:5–6; 57:10>>] also, O God, __is__ very high, who hast done great things: O God, ##who __is__ like##note:x[<<Ps 35:10>>] unto thee!
@ __Thou__, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 60:3; 138:7>>] hast shewed me great and sore troubles, shalt ##quicken##note:x[<<Hos 6:1–2>>] me again, and shalt bring me up again from the depths of the earth.
@ Thou shalt ##increase##note:x[<<2 Sam 3:1>>] my greatness, and ##comfort##note:x[<<Ps 32:10; 2 Cor 1:4–5>>] me on every side.
@ I will also praise thee with the ##psaltery##note:x[<<Ps 92:1–3>>], __even__ thy truth, O my God: unto thee will I sing with the harp, ##O thou##note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:22; Ps 89:18>>] Holy One of Israel.note:tn[__the psaltery__: Heb. the instrument of psaltery]
@ ##My lips##note:x[<<Ps 104:33; Luke 1:46–47>>] shall greatly rejoice when I sing unto thee; and ##my soul##note:x[<<Ps 103:4>>], which thou hast redeemed.
@ My tongue also shall talk of thy righteousness all the day long: ##for they##note:x[<<Ps 18:37–43; 71:13; 1 Cor 15:25>>] are confounded, for they are brought unto shame, that seek my hurt.

=== @72

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ for Solomon.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Endow the King with Your Justice

[.parallel]
(<<1 Kings 3:1–15; 2 Chronicles 1:1–13; Psalms 45:1–17>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Jer 23:5–6>>]##Give##note:x[<<1 Chr 22:12–13; 2 Chr 1:10>>] the king thy judgments, O God, and thy righteousness unto the king’s son.note:tn[__for__: or, of]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Isa 32:1>>] judge thy people with righteousness, and thy poor with judgment.
@ The ##mountains##note:x[<<Isa 52:7>>] shall bring peace to the people, and the little hills, by righteousness.
@ ##He shall judge##note:x[<<Ps 109:31; Isa 11:4; Ezek 34:15–16>>] the poor of the people, he shall save the children of the needy, and shall break in pieces the oppressor.
@ They shall fear thee ##as long##note:x[<<Ps 89:36–37; Dan 7:14>>] as the sun and moon endure, throughout all generations.
@ He shall come down ##like##note:x[<<Deut 32:2; 2 Sam 23:4; Hos 6:3>>] rain upon the mown grass: as showers __that__ water the earth.
@ ##In his days##note:x[<<Isa 60:22; Mal 4:2>>] shall the righteous flourish; and ##abundance##note:x[<<Isa 2:4>>] of peace so long as the moon endureth.note:tn[__so long…__: Heb. till there be no moon]
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Exod 23:31; 1 Kgs 4:21–24; Zech 9:10>>] have dominion also from sea to sea, and from the river unto the ends of the earth.
@ They that dwell in the wilderness shall bow before him; and his enemies shall ##lick##note:x[<<Isa 49:23; Mic 7:17>>] the dust.
@ ##The kings of Tarshish and of the isles shall bring presents: the kings of Sheba and Seba shall offer gifts.##note:x[<<Ps 68:29; Isa 49:7; 60:6>>]
@ Yea, ##all kings##note:x[<<Isa 49:22–23; Rev 11:15>>] shall fall down before him: ##all nations##note:x[<<Ps 86:9>>] shall serve him.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Job 29:12; Ps 102:17; Rev 3:17–18>>] he shall deliver the needy when he crieth; the poor also, and __him__ that hath no helper.
@ He shall spare the poor and needy, and ##shall save##note:x[<<Ps 109:31; Ezek 34:16; Matt 5:3>>] the souls of the needy.
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Luke 1:68–75>>] redeem their soul from deceit and violence: and ##precious##note:x[<<Ps 116:15; 1 Thess 2:15–16>>] shall their blood be in his sight.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Rev 1:18>>] shall live, and ##to him##note:x[<<1 Kgs 10:14>>] shall be given of the gold of Sheba: prayer also shall be made for him continually; __and__ ##daily##note:x[<<Phil 2:11>>] shall he be praised.note:tn[__shall be given__: Heb. one shall give]
@ There shall be an handful of corn in the earth upon the top of the mountains; the fruit thereof shall shake like Lebanon: and __they__ of the city shall flourish like grass of the earth.
@ ##His name##note:x[<<Ps 89:36; Isa 7:14; Matt 1:23>>] shall ##endure##note:tn[Heb. be] for ever: his name shall be continued as long as the sun: and __men__ shall be blessed in him: all nations shall call him blessed.note:tn[__his name shall be…__: Heb. shall be as a son to continue his father’s name for ever]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:10; Ps 41:13; 106:48>>] __be__ the LORD God, the God of Israel, who only doeth wondrous things.
@ And blessed __be__ his glorious name for ever: ##and let##note:x[<<Num 14:21; Hab 2:14; Matt 6:10>>] the whole earth be filled __with__ his glory; Amen, and Amen.
@ ##The prayers##note:x[<<2 Sam 23:1; Job 31:40; Jer 51:64>>] of David the son of Jesse are ended.

=== @73

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.major]
==== BOOK III

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 73—89>>)

[discrete.pericope]
==== Surely God Is Good to Israel

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Truly##note:tn[or, Yet] ##God##note:x[<<Ps 84:11>>] __is__ good to Israel, __even__ to such as are note:x[<<Ps 51:10; Matt 5:8>>]##of##note:tn[or, for] a clean heart.note:tn[__of…__: Heb. clean of heart]
@ But as for me, my ##feet##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; Ps 116:8>>] were almost gone; my ##steps##note:x[<<Ps 94:18>>] had well nigh slipped.
@ For ##I was##note:x[<<Ps 37:7>>] envious at the foolish, __when__ I saw ##the prosperity##note:x[<<Job 21:7–15; Jer 12:1–2>>] of the wicked.
@ For __there are__ ##no##note:x[<<Job 21:23–25; Ps 17:14>>] bands in their death: but their strength __is__ ##firm##note:tn[Heb. fat].
@ ##They __are__##note:x[<<Job 21:9>>] not in trouble __as other__ men; neither are they plagued ##like##note:tn[Heb. with] __other__ men.note:tn[__in…__: Heb. in the trouble of other men]
@ Therefore pride compasseth them about as a chain; violence ##covereth##note:x[<<Ps 109:18; 1 Pet 5:5>>] them __as__ a garment.
@ Their ##eyes##note:x[<<Job 15:27; Ps 17:10; Jer 5:28>>] stand out with fatness: they have more than heart could wish.note:tn[__have…__: Heb. pass the thoughts of the heart]
@ They are corrupt, and ##speak wickedly##note:x[<<Ps 10:2>>] __concerning__ oppression: they ##speak loftily##note:x[<<Jude 1:16>>].
@ They ##set##note:x[<<Exod 5:2; Rev 13:6>>] their mouth against the heavens, and their ##tongue##note:x[<<Jas 3:6>>] walketh through the earth.
@ Therefore his people return hither: and waters of a full __cup__ are wrung out to them.
@ And they say, ##How##note:x[<<Ps 94:7; Zeph 1:12>>] doth God know? and ##is there##note:x[<<Hos 7:2>>] knowledge in the most High?
@ Behold, ##these##note:x[<<Ps 52:7>>] __are__ the ungodly, who prosper in the world; they increase __in__ riches.
@ ##Verily##note:x[<<Job 21:15; 34:9>>] I have cleansed my heart __in__ vain, and ##washed##note:x[<<Ps 26:6>>] my hands in innocency.
@ ##For all##note:x[<<Ps 94:12; Heb 12:5>>] the day long have I been plagued, and ##chastened##note:tn[Heb. my chastisement was] every morning.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ If I say, I will speak thus; behold, I should ##offend##note:x[<<Mal 2:8; Matt 18:6–7; Rom 14:15>>] __against__ the generation of thy children.
@ ##When##note:x[<<Eccl 8:17>>] I thought to know this, it __was__ too painful for me;note:tn[__too…__: Heb. labour in mine eyes]
@ ##Until##note:x[<<Ps 27:4; 77:13>>] I went into the sanctuary of God; ##__then__##note:x[<<Luke 12:20>>] understood I their end.
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Deut 32:35; Ps 35:6; Jer 23:12>>] thou didst set them in slippery places: thou castedst them down into destruction.
@ How are they __brought__ into desolation, as in a moment! ##they are##note:x[<<Prov 28:1; Dan 5:6>>] utterly consumed with terrors.
@ ##As a##note:x[<<Job 20:8>>] dream ##when##note:x[<<Ps 78:65>>] __one__ awaketh; __so__, O Lord, when thou awakest, thou shalt despise their image.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thus ##my heart##note:x[<<Ps 37:7; 73:3>>] was grieved, and I was pricked ##in my##note:x[<<Lam 3:13>>] reins.
@ ##So##note:x[<<Ps 92:6; Eccl 3:18>>] foolish __was__ I, and ##ignorant##note:tn[Heb. I knew not]: I was ##__as__ a##note:x[<<Isa 1:3>>] beast ##before##note:tn[Heb. with] thee.
@ ##Nevertheless##note:x[<<Matt 28:20>>] I __am__ continually with thee: ##thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 63:8; Isa 41:13>>] holden __me__ by my right hand.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Ps 32:8; Prov 3:5–6; Isa 58:11>>] shalt guide me with thy counsel, and afterward receive me __to__ glory.
@ ##Whom##note:x[<<Ps 63:3; Phil 3:8>>] have I in heaven __but thee__? and __there is__ ##none upon##note:x[<<Phil 3:8>>] earth __that__ I desire beside thee.
@ My flesh and my heart faileth: ##__but__##note:x[<<Ps 138:3; Isa 40:29–31; 2 Cor 12:9–10>>] God __is__ the ##strength##note:tn[Heb. rock] of my heart, and my portion for ever.
@ For, ##lo##note:x[<<Ps 119:155>>], they that are far from thee shall perish: thou hast destroyed all them ##that go##note:x[<<Exod 34:15; Jas 4:4>>] a whoring from thee.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Lam 3:25–26; Heb 10:19–22; Jas 4:8>>] __it is__ good for me to draw near to God: I have put my trust in the Lord GOD, that I may declare all thy works.

=== @74

[.psalm-title]
Maschil of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Why Have You Rejected Us Forever?

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 79:1–13; Jeremiah 52:1–11>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O God, why hast thou cast __us__ off for ever? __why__ doth thine anger ##smoke##note:x[<<Deut 29:20>>] against ##the sheep##note:x[<<Ps 79:13; 100:3>>] of thy pasture?note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm for Asaph to give instruction]
@ Remember thy congregation, __which__ thou hast ##purchased##note:x[<<Exod 15:16>>] of old; the ##rod##note:tn[or, tribe] of ##thine##note:x[<<Deut 32:9; Jer 10:16>>] inheritance, __which__ thou hast redeemed; this mount Zion, wherein thou hast dwelt.
@ Lift up thy feet unto ##the perpetual##note:x[<<Dan 9:17>>] desolations; __even__ ##all##note:x[<<Ps 79:1; Lam 1:10>>] __that__ the enemy hath done wickedly in the sanctuary.
@ ##Thine##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:17; Lam 2:7>>] enemies roar in the midst of thy congregations; ##they set##note:x[<<Matt 24:15>>] up their ensigns __for__ signs.
@ __A man__ was famous according as he had lifted up axes upon the thick trees.
@ ##But now they break down the carved work thereof at once with axes and hammers.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 6:18, 29, 32>>]
@ They have ##cast fire into thy sanctuary##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:9>>], they have defiled __by casting down__ the dwelling place of thy name to the ground.note:tn[__They have cast…__: Heb. They have sent thy sanctuary unto the fire]
@ They ##said##note:x[<<Ps 83:4>>] in their hearts, Let us ##destroy##note:tn[Heb. break] them together: they have burned up all the synagogues of God in the land.
@ We see not our signs: __there is__ ##no more##note:x[<<1 Sam 3:1; Amos 8:11>>] any prophet: neither __is there__ among us any that knoweth how long.
@ O God, how long shall the adversary reproach? shall the enemy blaspheme thy name for ever?
@ Why ##withdrawest##note:x[<<Lam 2:3>>] thou thy hand, even thy right hand? pluck __it__ out of thy bosom.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ For ##God##note:x[<<Ps 44:4; Isa 33:22>>] __is__ my King of old, working salvation in the midst of the earth.
@ Thou didst note:x[<<Exod 14:21; Ps 78:13>>]##divide##note:tn[Heb. break] the sea by thy strength: thou ##brakest##note:x[<<Isa 51:9–10>>] the heads of the ##dragons##note:tn[or, whales] in the waters.
@ Thou brakest the heads of ##leviathan##note:x[<<Job 41:1–34; Isa 27:1>>] in pieces, __and__ gavest him __to be__ ##meat##note:x[<<Ps 72:9>>] to the people inhabiting the wilderness.
@ Thou didst ##cleave##note:x[<<Exod 17:5–6; Num 20:11; Ps 105:41>>] the fountain and the flood: thou driedst up mighty rivers.note:tn[__mighty…__: Heb. rivers of strength]
@ ##The day##note:x[<<Gen 1:3–5; Ps 136:7–9>>] __is__ thine, the night also __is__ thine: thou hast ##prepared##note:x[<<Ps 136:7–9>>] the light and the sun.
@ Thou hast ##set##note:x[<<Deut 32:8; Acts 17:26>>] all the borders of the earth: thou hast note:x[<<Gen 8:22>>]##made##note:tn[Heb. made them] summer and winter.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Remember this, __that__ the enemy hath reproached, O LORD, and __that__ ##the foolish##note:x[<<Ps 39:8>>] people have blasphemed thy name.
@ note:x[<<Song 2:14; Matt 10:16>>]O deliver not the soul of thy turtledove unto the multitude __of the wicked:__ ##forget##note:x[<<Jas 2:5–6>>] not the congregation of thy poor for ever.
@ ##Have##note:x[<<Gen 17:7–8; Ps 106:45; Heb 8:10>>] respect unto the covenant: for the dark places of the earth are full of the habitations of cruelty.
@ ##O let not##note:x[<<Ps 12:5>>] the oppressed return ashamed: let the poor and needy praise thy name.
@ Arise, O God, plead thine own cause: ##remember##note:x[<<Ps 74:18; Isa 52:5>>] how the foolish man reproacheth thee daily.
@ Forget not the voice of thine enemies: the tumult of those that rise up against thee ##increaseth##note:tn[Heb. ascendeth] continually.

=== @75

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, Al-taschith, A Psalm __or__ Song of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Righteous Judgment

[.parallel]
(<<Romans 2:1–16>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Unto thee, O God, do we give thanks, __unto thee__ do we give thanks: ##for __that__##note:x[<<Ps 138:2; Jer 10:6>>] thy name is near thy ##wondrous##note:x[<<Deut 4:7>>] works declare.note:tn[__Al-taschith__: or, Destroy not]note:tn[__of__: or, for]
@ When I shall ##receive the congregation##note:x[<<Eccl 3:17; John 7:6; Acts 17:31>>] I will judge uprightly.note:tn[__receive…__: or, take a set time]
@ The earth and all the inhabitants thereof are dissolved: ##I bear##note:x[<<Heb 1:3>>] up the ##pillars##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:8>>] of it. Selah.
@ I said unto the fools, Deal not foolishly: and to the wicked, ##Lift##note:x[<<Zech 1:21>>] not up the horn:
@ Lift not up your horn on high: ##speak##note:x[<<2 Chr 30:8; Isa 48:4; Acts 7:51>>] __not with__ a stiff neck.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ For promotion __cometh__ neither from the east, nor from the west, nor from the ##south##note:tn[Heb. desert].
@ But God __is__ the judge: ##he putteth##note:x[<<2 Sam 6:21; Ps 113:7–8; Luke 1:52>>] down one, and setteth up another.
@ ##For in##note:x[<<Job 21:20; Ps 11:6; Jer 25:15>>] the hand of the LORD __there is__ a cup, and the wine is red; it is full of mixture; and he poureth out of the same: but the dregs thereof, all the wicked of the earth shall wring __them__ out, __and__ drink __them__.
@ But I will declare for ever; I will sing praises to the God of Jacob.
@ ##All the horns##note:x[<<Jer 48:25>>] of the wicked also will I cut off; ##__but__ the horns##note:x[<<Ps 89:17; 92:10>>] of the righteous shall be exalted.

=== @76

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician on Neginoth, A Psalm __or__ Song of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Name Is Great in Israel

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##In Judah##note:x[<<Acts 17:23>>] __is__ God known: ##his##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:10–12; Dan 4:1–2>>] name __is__ great in Israel.note:tn[__chief…__: or, overseer]note:tn[__of__: or, for]
@ In ##Salem##note:x[<<Gen 14:18>>] also is his tabernacle, and his ##dwelling##note:x[<<Ps 132:13–14; Isa 12:6>>] place in Zion.
@ ##There##note:x[<<Ps 46:9>>] brake he the arrows of the bow, the shield, and the sword, and the battle. Selah.
@ Thou __art__ more glorious __and__ excellent than the ##mountains##note:x[<<Ezek 19:1–4>>] of prey.
@ note:x[<<Isa 46:12>>]The stouthearted are spoiled, ##they##note:x[<<Ps 13:3; Nah 3:18>>] have slept their sleep: and none of the men of might have found their hands.
@ ##At thy##note:x[<<Exod 15:1, 21; Ps 80:16>>] rebuke, O God of Jacob, both the chariot and horse are cast into a dead sleep.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou, __even__ thou, __art__ to be feared: and ##who##note:x[<<Nah 1:6>>] may stand in thy sight ##when##note:x[<<Ps 2:12>>] once thou art angry?
@ Thou didst cause judgment to be heard from heaven; the earth feared, and was ##still##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:29–30>>],
@ When God arose to judgment, ##to save##note:x[<<Zeph 2:3; Matt 5:5>>] all the meek of the earth. Selah.
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Dan 3:19–20; Acts 4:26–28>>] the wrath of man shall praise thee: the ##remainder##note:x[<<Matt 24:22>>] of wrath shalt thou restrain.
@ ##Vow##note:x[<<Ps 50:14; Eccl 5:4–6>>], and pay unto the LORD your God: ##let all##note:x[<<Ps 68:29>>] that be round about him bring presents unto him that ought to be feared.note:tn[__unto him…__: Heb. to fear]
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Ps 68:12; Zeph 3:6>>] cut off the spirit of princes: __he is__ ##terrible##note:x[<<Isa 13:6–8>>] to the kings of the earth.

=== @77

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, to Jeduthun, A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== In the Day of Trouble I Sought the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 50:1>>]##I cried##note:x[<<Ps 3:4; 142:1–3>>] unto God with my voice, __even__ unto God with my voice; and he gave ear unto me.note:tn[__of__: or, for]
@ ##In the##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; Isa 26:9, 16>>] day of my trouble I sought the Lord: my ##sore##note:tn[Heb. hand] ran in the night, and ceased not: my soul refused to be comforted.
@ ##I remembered##note:x[<<Job 31:23>>] God, and was troubled: I complained, and my ##spirit##note:x[<<Ps 61:2; 142:2–3>>] was overwhelmed. Selah.
@ Thou ##holdest##note:x[<<Job 7:13–15; Ps 6:6>>] mine eyes waking: I am so troubled that I cannot speak.
@ ##I have considered the days of old, the years of ancient times.##note:x[<<Deut 32:7; Ps 143:5; Isa 51:9>>]
@ I call to remembrance ##my song##note:x[<<Ps 42:8>>] in the night: I ##commune##note:x[<<Ps 4:4>>] with mine own heart: ##and##note:x[<<Ps 139:23–24>>] my spirit made diligent search.
@ Will ##the Lord##note:x[<<Ps 89:46; Lam 3:31–32>>] cast off for ever? ##and will##note:x[<<Ps 85:1>>] he be favourable no more?
@ Is his mercy clean gone for ever? ##doth##note:x[<<Num 23:19; Rom 9:6>>] __his__ promise fail for evermore?note:tn[__for evermore__: Heb. to generation and generation?]
@ Hath ##God##note:x[<<Isa 49:14–15>>] forgotten to be gracious? hath he in anger shut up his tender mercies? Selah.
@ And I said, ##This __is__##note:x[<<Job 42:3; Ps 31:22; Mark 9:24>>] my infirmity: __but I will remember__ the years of the right hand of the most High.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will remember the works of the LORD: surely I will remember thy wonders of old.##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:12; Ps 28:5; Isa 5:12>>]
@ I will meditate also of all thy work, and ##talk##note:x[<<Ps 71:24; 105:2; 145:11>>] of thy doings.
@ ##Thy way##note:x[<<Ps 73:17>>], O God, __is__ in the sanctuary: ##who##note:x[<<Exod 15:11; Isa 40:25>>] __is so__ great a God as __our__ God?
@ Thou __art__ ##the God##note:x[<<Exod 15:11; Ps 136:4>>] that doest wonders: ##thou hast##note:x[<<Dan 6:27>>] declared thy strength among the people.
@ Thou hast ##with##note:x[<<Exod 6:6; Deut 9:29; Isa 63:9>>] __thine__ arm redeemed thy people, the sons of Jacob and Joseph. Selah.
@ ##The waters saw thee, O God, the waters saw thee; they were afraid: the depths also were troubled.##note:x[<<Exod 14:21; Josh 3:15–16; Hab 3:15>>]
@ The clouds poured out water: the skies sent out a sound: ##thine##note:x[<<Ps 18:14>>] arrows also went abroad.note:tn[__poured…__: Heb. were poured forth with water]
@ The ##voice##note:x[<<Rev 11:19>>] of thy thunder __was__ in the heaven: the ##lightnings##note:x[<<Ps 97:4>>] lightened the world: the earth trembled and shook.
@ Thy ##way##note:x[<<Neh 9:11; Ps 29:10>>] __is__ in the sea, and thy path in the great waters, and thy ##footsteps##note:x[<<Exod 14:28>>] are not known.
@ ##Thou leddest thy people like a flock by the hand of Moses and Aaron.##note:x[<<Exod 13:21; 14:19; Ps 78:52>>]

=== @78

[.psalm-title]
Maschil of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Open My Mouth in Parables

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 13:34–35>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 49:1–3; Isa 51:4; 55:3>>]Give ear, O my people, __to__ my law: incline your ears to the words of my mouth.note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm for Asaph to give instruction]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Matt 13:34–35>>] open my mouth in a parable: I will utter ##dark sayings##note:x[<<Matt 13:11–13; Mark 4:34>>] of old:
@ ##Which we have heard and known, and our fathers have told us.##note:x[<<Exod 12:26–27; 13:8; Ps 44:1>>]
@ ##We will##note:x[<<Deut 4:9; Joel 1:3>>] not hide __them__ from their children, ##shewing##note:x[<<Deut 11:19>>] to the generation to come the praises of the LORD, and his strength, and his wonderful works that he hath done.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Deut 4:45; Ps 81:5>>] established a testimony in Jacob, and appointed a law in Israel, which he commanded our fathers, ##that they##note:x[<<Eph 6:4>>] should make them known to their children:
@ ##That##note:x[<<Ps 102:18>>] the generation to come might know __them, even__ the children __which__ should be born; ##__who__##note:x[<<Joel 1:3>>] should arise and declare __them__ to their children:
@ That they might set their hope in God, and ##not forget##note:x[<<Deut 4:9>>] the works of God, but ##keep##note:x[<<Deut 5:29; Rev 14:12>>] his commandments:
@ And might not be ##as their##note:x[<<Exod 32:9>>] fathers, a stubborn and rebellious generation; a generation __that__ ##set not##note:x[<<Ps 78:37>>] their heart aright, and ##whose##note:x[<<Ps 78:37>>] spirit was not stedfast with God.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. that prepared not their heart]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##The children##note:x[<<Deut 1:41–44; Josh 17:16–18; 1 Sam 31:1>>] of Ephraim, __being__ armed, __and__ ##carrying##note:tn[Heb. throwing forth] bows, turned back in the day of battle.
@ ##They kept not the covenant of God, and refused to walk in his law;##note:x[<<Deut 31:16, 20; Judg 2:10–12>>]
@ ##And forgat his works, and his wonders that he had shewed them.##note:x[<<Deut 32:18; Ps 106:13>>]
@ Marvellous things did he in the sight of their fathers, in the land of Egypt, __in__ the field of ##Zoan##note:x[<<Num 13:22; Isa 19:11; Ezek 30:14>>].
@ He divided the sea, and caused them to pass through; and he made the waters to stand as an heap.
@ ##In the daytime also he led them with a cloud, and all the night with a light of fire.##note:x[<<Exod 13:21–22; 14:24; Ps 105:39>>]
@ ##He clave the rocks in the wilderness, and gave __them__ drink as __out of__ the great depths.##note:x[<<Exod 17:6; Num 20:11; Ps 105:41>>]
@ He brought streams also out of the rock, and caused waters to run down like rivers.
@ And they sinned yet more against him by provoking the most High in the wilderness.
@ And they tempted God in their heart ##by asking meat##note:x[<<Num 11:4; Ps 106:14–15>>] for their lust.
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Exod 16:8–10; Num 21:5>>], they spake against God; they said, ##Can God##note:x[<<Num 11:4>>] ##furnish##note:tn[Heb. order] a table in the wilderness?
@ Behold, ##he smote##note:x[<<Num 20:11>>] the rock, that the waters gushed out, and the streams overflowed; ##can he give##note:x[<<Num 11:21–23>>] bread also? can he provide flesh for his people?
@ Therefore ##the LORD##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:11; Jude 1:5>>] heard __this__, and was wroth: so ##a fire##note:x[<<Num 11:1–3>>] was kindled against Jacob, and anger also came up against Israel;
@ ##Because they believed not in God, and trusted not in his salvation:##note:x[<<Heb 3:12, 18–19; Jude 1:5>>]
@ Though he had commanded the clouds from above, and ##opened##note:x[<<Gen 7:11; Mal 3:10>>] the doors of heaven,
@ And ##had rained##note:x[<<Exod 16:4; Deut 8:3; Ps 105:40>>] down manna upon them to eat, and had given them of the corn of heaven.
@ ##Man##note:x[<<Ps 103:20>>] did eat angels’ food: ##he sent##note:x[<<Exod 16:8; Matt 14:20>>] them meat to the full.note:tn[__Man…__: or, Every one did eat the bread of the mighty]
@ ##He caused##note:x[<<Num 11:31>>] an east wind to blow in the heaven: and by his power he brought in the south wind.note:tn[__to blow__: Heb. to go]
@ ##He rained##note:x[<<Exod 16:12–13>>] flesh also upon them as dust, and feathered fowls like as the sand of the sea&#58;note:tn[__feathered…__: Heb. fowl of wing]
@ And he let __it__ fall in the midst of their camp, round about their habitations.
@ So they did eat, and were well filled: for he gave them their own desire;
@ They were not estranged from their lust. ##But##note:x[<<Num 11:33–34>>] while their meat __was__ yet in their mouths,
@ The wrath of God came upon them, and slew the fattest of them, and smote down the chosen __men__ of Israel.note:tn[__smote…__: Heb. made to bow]note:tn[__chosen…__: or, young men]
@ For all this ##they sinned##note:x[<<Ezek 20:13>>] still, and ##believed##note:x[<<Ps 78:22; John 12:37>>] not for his wondrous works.
@ Therefore their ##days##note:x[<<Num 14:29, 35; 26:64–65>>] did he consume in vanity, and their years in trouble.
@ ##When he slew them, then they sought him: and they returned and enquired early after God.##note:x[<<Num 21:7; Hos 5:15>>]
@ And they remembered that ##God __was__##note:x[<<Deut 32:4>>] their rock, and the high God ##their redeemer##note:x[<<Exod 15:13; Isa 41:14>>].
@ ##Nevertheless##note:x[<<Isa 29:13; Ezek 33:31>>] they did flatter him with their mouth, and they lied unto him with their tongues.
@ For ##their heart##note:x[<<Acts 8:21>>] was not right with him, neither were they ##stedfast##note:x[<<Ps 78:8>>] in his covenant.
@ ##But he##note:x[<<Num 14:18–20>>], __being__ full of compassion, forgave __their__ iniquity, and destroyed __them__ not: yea, ##many##note:x[<<Isa 48:9; Ezek 20:17>>] a time turned he his anger away, and did not stir up all his wrath.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Gen 6:3>>] remembered that they __were but__ flesh; ##a wind##note:x[<<Job 7:7; Jas 4:14>>] that passeth away, and cometh not again.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##How oft##note:x[<<Ps 95:8–10>>] did they ##provoke##note:tn[or, rebel against] him in the wilderness, __and__ ##grieve##note:x[<<Eph 4:30>>] him in the desert!
@ ##Yea##note:x[<<Num 14:22; Acts 7:39; Heb 3:8–11>>], they turned back and tempted God, and limited the Holy One of Israel.
@ They ##remembered##note:x[<<Ps 78:11; Jer 32:21>>] not his hand, __nor__ ##the day##note:x[<<Exod 14:12>>] when he delivered them from the enemy.note:tn[__from…__: or, from affliction]
@ How he had ##wrought##note:tn[Heb. set] his signs in Egypt, and his wonders in the field of Zoan:
@ And had turned their rivers into ##blood##note:x[<<Ps 105:29>>]; and their floods, that they could not drink.
@ He ##sent##note:x[<<Exod 8:21–24>>] divers sorts of flies among them, which devoured them; and frogs, which destroyed them.
@ He ##gave also##note:x[<<Exod 10:12–15>>] their increase unto the caterpiller, and their labour unto the locust.
@ He ##destroyed##note:tn[Heb. killed] their vines with hail, and their sycomore trees with ##frost##note:tn[or, great hailstones].
@ He gave up their cattle also to the hail, and their flocks to hot thunderbolts.note:tn[__gave…__: Heb. shut up]note:tn[__hot…__: or, lightnings]
@ He cast upon them the fierceness of his anger, wrath, and indignation, and trouble, by sending evil angels __among them__.
@ He made a way to his anger; ##he spared##note:x[<<Ezek 7:4; 2 Pet 2:4–5>>] not their soul from death, but gave their life over to the pestilence;note:tn[__He made…__: Heb. He weighed a path]note:tn[__life…__: or, beasts to the murrain]
@ And ##smote##note:x[<<Ps 135:8>>] all the firstborn in Egypt; the chief of __their__ strength in the ##tabernacles##note:x[<<Ps 105:23; 106:22>>] of Ham:
@ ##But##note:x[<<Neh 9:12; Ps 77:20; Isa 63:11–14>>] made his own people to go forth like sheep, and guided them in the wilderness like a flock.
@ And he led them on safely, ##so that##note:x[<<Exod 14:19–20>>] they feared not: ##but##note:x[<<Exod 14:27–28; 15:10>>] the sea ##overwhelmed##note:tn[Heb. covered] their enemies.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Exod 15:17>>] brought them to the border of his sanctuary, __even to__ this mountain, __which__ ##his right##note:x[<<Ps 44:3>>] hand had purchased.
@ note:x[<<Neh 9:22–25; Ps 44:2>>]He cast out the heathen also before them, and ##divided##note:x[<<Josh 13:7>>] them an inheritance by line, and made the tribes of Israel to dwell in their tents.
@ ##Yet they tempted and provoked the most high God, and kept not his testimonies:##note:x[<<Judg 2:11–12>>]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ezek 20:27–28>>] turned back, and dealt unfaithfully like their fathers: ##they were##note:x[<<Hos 7:16>>] turned aside like a deceitful bow.
@ For they provoked him to anger with ##their high##note:x[<<Lev 26:30>>] places, and ##moved##note:x[<<Deut 32:21; 1 Cor 10:22>>] him to jealousy with their graven images.
@ When God heard __this__, he was wroth, and ##greatly##note:x[<<Ps 106:40; Lam 2:7>>] abhorred Israel:
@ ##So that he forsook the tabernacle of Shiloh, the tent __which__ he placed among men;##note:x[<<Josh 18:1; 1 Sam 4:4–11; Jer 26:6–9>>]
@ And delivered ##his strength##note:x[<<Ps 132:8>>] into captivity, and his glory into the enemy’s hand.
@ He gave his people over also unto the sword; and was wroth with his inheritance.
@ The fire consumed their young men; and their ##maidens##note:x[<<Jer 7:34>>] were not given to marriage.note:tn[__given…__: Heb. praised]
@ Their ##priests##note:x[<<1 Sam 4:17>>] fell by the sword; and their ##widows##note:x[<<Job 27:15>>] made no lamentation.
@ ##Then##note:x[<<Ps 7:6; 44:23; Isa 51:9>>] the Lord awaked as one out of sleep, __and__ like a mighty man that shouteth by reason of wine.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<1 Sam 5:6>>] smote his enemies in the hinder parts: ##he put##note:x[<<Jer 23:40>>] them to a perpetual reproach.
@ Moreover he refused the tabernacle of Joseph, and chose not the tribe of Ephraim:
@ But chose the tribe of Judah, the ##mount##note:x[<<Ps 87:2>>] Zion which he loved.
@ And he built his sanctuary like high __palaces__, like the earth which he hath ##established##note:tn[Heb. founded] for ever.
@ He ##chose##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:11–12>>] David also his servant, and took him from the sheepfolds:
@ From ##following##note:tn[Heb. after] the ewes great with young he brought him to ##feed##note:x[<<2 Sam 5:2; 1 Chr 11:2>>] Jacob his people, and Israel his inheritance.
@ So he fed them ##according##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:4; Isa 11:2–4>>] to the integrity of his heart; and ##guided##note:x[<<2 Tim 2:15>>] them by the skilfulness of his hands.

=== @79

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prayer for Deliverance

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 74:1–23; Jeremiah 52:1–11>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 74:1>>]O God, the heathen are come into thine inheritance; thy ##holy##note:x[<<Lam 1:10>>] temple have they defiled; they ##have laid##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:19>>] Jerusalem on heaps.note:tn[__of__: or, for]
@ ##The dead bodies of thy servants have they given __to be__ meat unto the fowls of the heaven, the flesh of thy saints unto the beasts of the earth.##note:x[<<Jer 7:33; 16:4; 34:20>>]
@ Their blood have they shed like water round about Jerusalem; ##and __there__##note:x[<<Jer 14:16; 16:4>>] __was__ none to bury __them__.
@ We are ##become##note:x[<<Ps 80:6>>] a reproach to our neighbours, a scorn and derision to them that are round about us.
@ ##How long##note:x[<<Ps 74:1; 89:46>>], LORD? ##wilt##note:x[<<Ps 85:5>>] thou be angry for ever? shall thy jealousy burn like fire?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Pour out thy wrath ##upon##note:x[<<Jer 10:25>>] the heathen that have ##not known##note:x[<<2 Thess 1:8>>] thee, and upon the kingdoms that have ##not called##note:x[<<Ps 14:4>>] upon thy name.
@ ##For they##note:x[<<Jer 51:34–35; Zech 1:15>>] have devoured Jacob, and laid waste his dwelling place.
@ O ##remember##note:x[<<Isa 64:9>>] not against us former iniquities: let thy tender mercies speedily prevent us: for ##we are##note:x[<<Ps 116:6; 142:6>>] brought very low.note:tn[__former…__: or, the iniquities of them that were before us]
@ Help us, O God of our salvation, ##for the##note:x[<<2 Chr 14:11>>] glory of thy name: and deliver us, and ##purge##note:x[<<Ps 25:11>>] away our sins, ##for thy##note:x[<<Jer 14:7>>] name’s sake.
@ ##Wherefore##note:x[<<Ps 42:10>>] should the heathen say, Where __is__ their God? let him be known among the heathen in our sight __by__ the ##revenging##note:tn[Heb. vengeance] of the blood of thy servants __which is__ shed.
@ Let the ##sighing##note:x[<<Ps 102:20; Isa 42:7>>] of the prisoner come before thee; according to the greatness of thy power ##preserve thou those that are appointed to die##note:x[<<Ps 102:20>>];note:tn[__thy…__: Heb. thine arm]note:tn[__preserve…__: Heb. reserve the children of death]
@ And ##render##note:x[<<Gen 4:15; Lev 26:21; Jer 32:18>>] unto our neighbours sevenfold into their bosom their reproach, wherewith they have reproached thee, O Lord.
@ So we ##thy people##note:x[<<Ps 74:1; 95:7>>] and sheep of thy pasture will give thee thanks for ever: ##we will##note:x[<<Isa 43:21>>] shew forth thy praise to all generations.note:tn[__to all…__: Heb. to generation and generation]

=== @80

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Shoshannim-eduth, A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Hear Us, O Shepherd of Israel

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Give ear, O Shepherd ##of##note:tn[or, for] Israel, thou that ##leadest##note:x[<<Ps 77:20>>] Joseph like a flock; thou that dwellest __between__ the cherubims, ##shine##note:x[<<Ps 50:2; Rev 21:23>>] forth.
@ ##Before##note:x[<<Num 2:18–24>>] Ephraim and Benjamin and Manasseh ##stir up##note:x[<<Ps 35:23>>] thy strength, and come __and__ save us.note:tn[__come…__: Heb. come for salvation to us]
@ ##Turn us##note:x[<<Ps 85:4; Lam 5:21>>] again, O God, and ##cause##note:x[<<Num 6:25–26>>] thy face to shine; and we shall be saved.
@ O LORD God of hosts, ##how long##note:x[<<Ps 85:5>>] wilt thou be angry against the prayer of thy people?note:tn[__be…__: Heb. smoke]
@ ##Thou feedest them with the bread of tears; and givest them tears to drink in great measure.##note:x[<<Ps 42:3; 102:9; Isa 30:20>>]
@ Thou makest us a strife unto our neighbours: and ##our enemies##note:x[<<Ps 79:4>>] laugh among themselves.
@ ##Turn##note:x[<<Ps 51:10; 80:19>>] us again, O God of hosts, and cause thy face to shine; and ##we shall##note:x[<<Isa 64:5>>] be saved.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou hast brought ##a vine##note:x[<<Jer 2:21; Ezek 17:6>>] out of Egypt: ##thou hast cast##note:x[<<Ps 44:2>>] out the heathen, and planted it.
@ Thou ##preparedst##note:x[<<Josh 24:12>>] __room__ before it, and didst cause it to take deep root, and it filled the land.
@ The hills were covered with the shadow of it, and the boughs thereof __were like__ the goodly cedars.note:tn[__goodly…__: Heb. cedars of God]
@ ##She sent out her boughs unto the sea, and her branches unto the river.##note:x[<<Ps 72:8>>]
@ Why hast thou __then__ ##broken##note:x[<<Ps 89:40–41; Isa 5:5; Luke 20:16>>] down her hedges, so that all they which pass by the way do pluck her?
@ The boar out of the wood doth waste it, and the wild beast of the field doth devour it.
@ ##Return##note:x[<<Isa 63:15>>], we beseech thee, O God of hosts: ##look down##note:x[<<Isa 63:15>>] from heaven, and behold, and visit this vine;
@ And the ##vineyard##note:x[<<John 15:1>>] which thy right hand hath planted, and ##the branch##note:x[<<Zech 3:8; 6:12>>] __that__ thou madest strong for thyself.
@ __It is__ ##burned##note:x[<<John 15:6>>] with fire, __it is__ cut down: they ##perish##note:x[<<Ps 39:11>>] at the rebuke of thy countenance.
@ ##Let thy hand be upon the man of thy right hand, upon the son of man __whom__ thou madest strong for thyself.##note:x[<<Ps 89:21; 110:1; Dan 7:13–14>>]
@ ##So will##note:x[<<John 6:66–69>>] not we go back from thee: ##quicken##note:x[<<Ps 119:25, 40>>] us, and we will call upon thy name.
@ ##Turn us##note:x[<<Ps 80:3; Jer 3:22–23>>] again, O LORD God of hosts, ##cause##note:x[<<Ps 27:4>>] thy face to shine; and we shall be saved.

=== @81

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Gittith, __A Psalm__ of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Sing for Joy to God Our Strength

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Sing##note:x[<<Jer 31:7>>] aloud unto God our strength: ##make##note:x[<<Ps 66:1; 100:1–2>>] a joyful noise unto the God of Jacob.note:tn[__of Asaph__: or, for Asaph]
@ ##Take a psalm, and bring hither the timbrel, the pleasant harp with the psaltery.##note:x[<<Ps 92:3; 149:1–3; Col 3:16>>]
@ Blow up the trumpet in the ##new##note:x[<<Lev 23:24–25>>] moon, in the time appointed, on our ##solemn##note:x[<<Num 15:3; 2 Chr 2:4>>] feast day.
@ For this __was__ a statute for Israel, __and__ a law of the God of Jacob.
@ This he ordained in Joseph __for__ a testimony, when he went out ##through##note:tn[or, against] the land of Egypt: ##__where__##note:x[<<Deut 28:49; Ps 114:1; Jer 5:15>>] I heard a language __that__ I understood not.
@ ##I removed##note:x[<<Exod 6:6; Isa 9:4; 10:27>>] his shoulder from the burden: his hands were delivered from the pots.note:tn[__were…__: Heb. passed away]
@ Thou ##calledst##note:x[<<Exod 2:23>>] in trouble, and I delivered thee; I answered thee in the ##secret##note:x[<<Exod 19:19>>] place of thunder: I ##proved##note:x[<<Num 20:13>>] thee at the waters of ##Meribah##note:tn[or, Strife]. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 50:7; John 3:11>>], O my people, and I will testify unto thee: O Israel, ##if thou wilt##note:x[<<Exod 15:26>>] hearken unto me;
@ ##There shall##note:x[<<1 Cor 8:5–6>>] no ##strange##note:x[<<Deut 32:12; Isa 43:12>>] god be in thee; neither shalt thou worship any strange god.
@ ##I __am__##note:x[<<Exod 20:2>>] the LORD thy God, which brought thee out of the land of Egypt: ##open##note:x[<<John 7:37; 15:7>>] thy mouth wide, and I will fill it.
@ But my ##people##note:x[<<Jer 2:11–13; 7:23–24>>] would not hearken to my voice; and Israel ##would none##note:x[<<Deut 32:18>>] of me.
@ So ##I gave##note:x[<<Rom 1:24>>] them up unto their own hearts’ lust: __and__ ##they walked##note:x[<<Isa 30:1; Jer 7:24>>] in their own counsels.note:tn[__unto…__: or, to the hardness of their hearts, or, imagination]
@ ##Oh that##note:x[<<Deut 5:29; 10:12–13; Isa 48:18>>] my people had hearkened unto me, __and__ Israel had walked in my ways!
@ ##I should##note:x[<<Josh 23:13; Judg 2:20–23>>] soon have subdued their enemies, and ##turned##note:x[<<Amos 1:8>>] my hand against their adversaries.
@ ##The haters##note:x[<<Deut 7:10; Rom 1:30; 8:7>>] of the LORD should have submitted themselves unto him: but their time should have endured for ever.note:tn[__submitted…__: or, yielded feigned obedience: Heb. lied]
@ He should have ##fed##note:x[<<Deut 32:13–14; Ps 147:14>>] them also with the finest of the wheat: and with ##honey##note:x[<<1 Sam 14:25–26>>] out of the rock should I have satisfied thee.note:tn[__finest…__: Heb. fat of wheat]

=== @82

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Presides in the Divine Assembly

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<2 Chr 19:6–7>>]##God##note:x[<<2 Chr 19:6–7>>] standeth in the congregation of the mighty; he judgeth among ##the gods##note:x[<<John 10:35>>].note:tn[__of Asaph__: or, for Asaph]
@ How long will ye ##judge##note:x[<<Ps 58:1–2>>] unjustly, and ##accept##note:x[<<Deut 1:17; Prov 18:5>>] the persons of the wicked? Selah.
@ note:x[<<Ps 10:18>>]##Defend##note:tn[Heb. Judge] the poor and fatherless: ##do##note:x[<<Jer 22:3; Jas 1:27>>] justice to the afflicted and needy.
@ ##Deliver##note:x[<<Job 29:12; Prov 24:11–12>>] the poor and needy: ##rid##note:x[<<Job 5:15–16>>] __them__ out of the hand of the wicked.
@ They ##know not##note:x[<<Rom 1:28>>], neither will they understand; they ##walk##note:x[<<Prov 2:13>>] on in darkness: ##all the##note:x[<<Ps 11:3>>] foundations of the earth are out of course.note:tn[__out…__: Heb. moved]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I have said, Ye __are__ gods; and all of you __are__ children of the most High.##note:x[<<Ps 82:1; John 10:34–36>>]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Job 21:32; Ps 49:12; Ezek 31:14>>] ye shall die like men, and fall like one of the princes.
@ ##Arise##note:x[<<Ps 96:13>>], O God, judge the earth: for ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 2:8; Rev 11:15>>] shalt inherit all nations.

=== @83

[.psalm-title]
A Song __or__ Psalm of Asaph.

[discrete.pericope]
==== O God, Be Not Silent

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Keep##note:x[<<Ps 28:1; 35:22; 50:3>>] not thou silence, O God: hold not thy peace, and be not still, O God.note:tn[__of Asaph__: or, for Asaph]
@ ##For, lo##note:x[<<Matt 27:24>>], thine enemies make a tumult: and they ##that hate##note:x[<<Ps 81:15>>] thee have lifted up the head.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 64:2>>] have taken crafty counsel against thy people, and consulted against ##thy hidden##note:x[<<Ps 27:5; 31:20>>] ones.
@ ##They have said, Come, and let us cut them off from __being__ a nation; that the name of Israel may be no more in remembrance.##note:x[<<Exod 1:10; Esth 3:6–9; Jer 11:19>>]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 2:2; Isa 8:9–10; Rev 17:13>>] they have consulted together with one ##consent##note:tn[Heb. heart]: they are confederate against thee:
@ The tabernacles of Edom, and the Ishmaelites; of Moab, and the ##Hagarenes##note:x[<<1 Chr 5:10>>];
@ ##Gebal##note:x[<<Josh 13:5>>], and Ammon, and Amalek; the Philistines with the inhabitants of Tyre;
@ Assur also is joined with them: they have ##holpen##note:tn[Heb. been an arm to] ##the children##note:x[<<Deut 2:9>>] of Lot. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Do unto them ##as __unto__##note:x[<<Isa 9:4>>] the Midianites; as __to__ Sisera, as __to__ Jabin, at the brook of Kison:
@ __Which__ perished at Endor: they became ##__as__ dung##note:x[<<Zeph 1:17>>] for the earth.
@ Make their nobles like ##Oreb##note:x[<<Judg 7:25>>], and like Zeeb: yea, all their princes as Zebah, and as Zalmunna:
@ ##Who said, Let us take to ourselves the houses of God in possession.##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:11>>]
@ O my God, make them like a wheel; ##as the##note:x[<<Job 21:18; Ps 35:5; Isa 40:24>>] stubble before the wind.
@ ##As the fire##note:x[<<Mal 4:1>>] burneth a wood, and as ##the flame##note:x[<<Deut 32:22>>] setteth the mountains on fire;
@ ##So persecute them with thy tempest, and make them afraid with thy storm.##note:x[<<Job 9:17; Ps 50:3>>]
@ Fill their faces with shame; that they may seek thy name, O LORD.
@ ##Let them be confounded and troubled for ever; yea, let them be put to shame, and perish:##note:x[<<Ps 35:4>>]
@ ##That __men__##note:x[<<Ps 59:13; Ezek 38:23>>] may know that thou, ##whose##note:x[<<Exod 6:3>>] name alone __is__ JEHOVAH, __art__ the most high over all the earth.

=== @84

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician upon Gittith, A Psalm for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Better Is One Day in Your Courts

[.parallel]
(<<John 1:14–18>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##How##note:x[<<Ps 27:4; 36:8; 122:1>>] amiable __are__ thy tabernacles, O LORD of hosts!note:tn[__for the sons__: or, of the sons]
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 42:1–2; 63:1–2>>] longeth, yea, even fainteth for the courts of the LORD: my ##heart##note:x[<<Isa 26:9>>] and my flesh crieth out for the living God.
@ Yea, the ##sparrow##note:x[<<Ps 90:1; 91:1; Matt 8:20>>] hath found an house, and the swallow a nest for herself, where she may lay her young, __even__ thine altars, O LORD of hosts, my King, and my God.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 23:6; 27:4; 65:4>>] __are__ they that dwell in thy house: they will be still praising thee. Selah.
@ Blessed __is__ the man whose ##strength##note:x[<<Ps 28:7–8; Zech 10:12; 2 Cor 12:9>>] __is__ in thee; in whose heart __are__ the ways __of them__.
@ ##__Who__##note:x[<<John 16:33; Rom 5:3–5; 2 Cor 4:17>>] passing through the valley of Baca make it a well; the rain also ##filleth##note:tn[Heb. covereth] the pools.note:tn[__Baca…__: or, mulberry trees make him a well, etc]
@ ##They##note:x[<<Prov 4:18; Isa 40:31; 2 Cor 3:18>>] go from strength to strength, __every one of them__ in Zion appeareth before God.note:tn[__strength to…__: or, company to company]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD God of hosts, hear my prayer: give ear, O God of Jacob. Selah.
@ Behold, O God ##our##note:x[<<Gen 15:1; Ps 98:1>>] shield, and look upon ##the face##note:x[<<Ps 2:2>>] of thine anointed.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 27:4; 63:2>>] a day in thy courts __is__ better than a thousand. I had rather be a doorkeeper in the house of my God, than ##to dwell##note:x[<<Ps 26:8–10>>] in the tents of wickedness.note:tn[__I had…__: Heb. I would choose rather to sit at the threshold]
@ For the LORD God __is__ a sun and ##shield##note:x[<<Prov 2:7>>]: the LORD will give grace and glory: ##no##note:x[<<Ps 34:9–10>>] good __thing__ will he withhold from them that ##walk##note:x[<<Prov 2:7>>] uprightly.
@ O LORD of hosts, ##blessed##note:x[<<Ps 62:8; Isa 30:18; Jer 17:7–8>>] __is__ the man that trusteth in thee.

=== @85

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== You Showed Favor to Your Land

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ LORD, ##thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 14:7; Jer 30:18; Ezek 39:25>>] been ##favourable##note:tn[or, well pleased] unto thy land: thou hast brought back the captivity of Jacob.note:tn[__for the sons__: or, of the sons]
@ Thou hast ##forgiven##note:x[<<Ps 32:1; Mic 7:18; Col 2:13>>] the iniquity of thy people, thou hast covered all their sin. Selah.
@ Thou hast ##taken##note:x[<<Isa 12:1>>] away all thy wrath: thou hast ##turned##note:x[<<Deut 13:17>>] __thyself__ from the fierceness of thine anger.note:tn[__thyself…__: or, thine anger from waxing hot]
@ ##Turn us##note:x[<<Ps 80:3, 7; Lam 5:21>>], O God of our salvation, and cause thine anger toward us to cease.
@ Wilt thou be ##angry##note:x[<<Ps 79:5; 80:4>>] with us for ever? wilt thou draw out thine anger to all generations?
@ Wilt thou not ##revive##note:x[<<Ps 80:18; Isa 57:15; Hab 3:2>>] us again: that thy people may rejoice in thee?
@ ##Shew us thy mercy, O LORD, and grant us thy salvation.##note:x[<<Ps 50:23; 91:16; Jer 42:12>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will hear what God the LORD will speak: ##for he##note:x[<<Ps 29:11; John 14:27>>] will speak peace unto his people, and to his saints: ##but##note:x[<<Rev 3:19>>] let them not turn again to folly.
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Isa 46:13; John 7:17>>] his salvation __is__ nigh them that fear him; that ##glory##note:x[<<Zech 2:5>>] may dwell in our land.
@ ##Mercy##note:x[<<Ps 89:14; 100:5; John 1:17>>] and truth are met together; righteousness and peace have kissed __each other__.
@ ##Truth##note:x[<<Isa 45:8; John 14:6; 1 John 5:20–21>>] shall spring out of the earth; and righteousness shall look down from heaven.
@ Yea, ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 84:11; Jas 1:17>>] shall give __that which is__ good; and ##our land##note:x[<<Ps 67:6>>] shall yield her increase.
@ ##Righteousness##note:x[<<Ps 89:14>>] shall go before him; and ##shall set##note:x[<<John 13:34; 1 John 2:6>>] __us__ in the way of his steps.

=== @86

[.psalm-title]
A Prayer of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Tried but Trusting

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Bow##note:x[<<Ps 31:2; Dan 9:18>>] down thine ear, O LORD, hear me: ##for I __am__##note:x[<<Ps 40:17>>] poor and needy.note:tn[__A Prayer…__: or, A Prayer, being a Psalm of David]
@ ##Preserve##note:x[<<Ps 4:3; 119:94; John 17:11>>] my soul; for I __am__ ##holy##note:tn[or, one whom thou favourest]: O thou my God, save thy servant that trusteth in thee.
@ ##Be merciful##note:x[<<Ps 57:1>>] unto me, O Lord: ##for I##note:x[<<Ps 88:9; Eph 6:18>>] cry unto thee ##daily##note:tn[or, all the day].
@ ##Rejoice##note:x[<<Isa 66:13–14>>] the soul of thy servant: for unto thee, O Lord, ##do##note:x[<<Ps 25:1; 143:8>>] I lift up my soul.
@ For ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 145:8–9; Joel 2:13>>], Lord, __art__ good, and ready to forgive; and ##plenteous##note:x[<<Joel 2:13>>] in mercy unto all them that call upon thee.
@ ##Give ear, O LORD, unto my prayer; and attend to the voice of my supplications.##note:x[<<Ps 5:1–2; 17:1; 130:2>>]
@ ##In the day of my trouble I will call upon thee: for thou wilt answer me.##note:x[<<Ps 34:4–6; 50:15; Jonah 2:2>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Among##note:x[<<Exod 15:11; Ps 89:6>>] the gods __there is__ none like unto thee, O Lord; ##neither##note:x[<<Deut 3:24>>] __are there any works__ like unto thy works.
@ ##All##note:x[<<Ps 66:4; Isa 66:23; Rev 15:4>>] nations whom thou hast made shall come and worship before thee, O Lord; and shall glorify thy name.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 72:18>>] thou __art__ great, and doest wondrous things: thou __art__ ##God##note:x[<<Isa 37:16; Mark 12:29>>] alone.
@ ##Teach##note:x[<<Ps 5:8; 25:4>>] me thy way, O LORD; I will walk in thy truth: ##unite##note:x[<<Jer 32:38–39>>] my heart to fear thy name.
@ I will ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 146:1–2; Rev 19:5–6>>] thee, O Lord my God, ##with all##note:x[<<Deut 6:5>>] my heart: and I will glorify thy name for evermore.
@ For ##great##note:x[<<Ps 57:10; 103:8–12>>] __is__ thy mercy toward me: ##and thou##note:x[<<Ps 116:8>>] hast delivered my soul from the lowest ##hell##note:tn[or, grave].
@ ##O God##note:x[<<Ps 54:3>>], the proud are risen against me, and the ##assemblies##note:x[<<Matt 27:1–2>>] of ##violent##note:tn[Heb. terrible] __men__ have sought after my soul; and have not set thee before them.
@ ##But thou##note:x[<<Ps 103:8; 130:7; Eph 2:4–7>>], O Lord, __art__ a God full of compassion, and gracious, longsuffering, and plenteous in mercy and truth.
@ O turn unto me, and have mercy upon me; ##give##note:x[<<Isa 40:29–31; Eph 6:10; Phil 4:13>>] thy strength unto thy servant, and save the son of thine handmaid.
@ ##Shew##note:x[<<Ps 41:10–11>>] me a token for good; ##that they##note:x[<<Ps 71:9–13>>] which hate me may see __it__, and be ashamed: because ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 71:20–21>>], LORD, hast holpen me, and comforted me.

=== @87

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm __or__ Song for the sons of Korah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Loves the Gates of Zion

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##His##note:x[<<Isa 28:16; Eph 2:20–22>>] foundation __is__ in ##the holy##note:x[<<Isa 56:7>>] mountains.note:tn[__for the sons__: or, of the sons]
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Deut 12:5; Ps 78:67–69; 132:13–14>>] loveth the gates of Zion more than all the dwellings of Jacob.
@ ##Glorious##note:x[<<Ezek 36:11–38; 37:27–28; Rev 21:10–27>>] things are spoken of thee, O city of God. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will make mention of Rahab and Babylon to them that know me: behold Philistia, and ##Tyre##note:x[<<Ps 45:12>>], with Ethiopia; ##this __man__##note:x[<<Ps 68:31; Isa 19:23–25>>] was born there.
@ And ##of Zion##note:x[<<Isa 60:1–9; John 1:12–14>>] it shall be said, This and that man was born in her: and the ##highest##note:x[<<Rom 8:31>>] himself shall establish her.
@ The LORD shall count, ##when##note:x[<<Isa 4:3; Ezek 13:9; Luke 10:20>>] he writeth up the people, __that__ this __man__ was born there. Selah.
@ As well the singers as the players on instruments __shall be there:__ ##all my##note:x[<<Isa 12:3; John 1:16; Rev 22:17>>] springs __are__ in thee.

=== @88

[.psalm-title]
A Song __or__ Psalm for the sons of Korah, to the chief Musician upon Mahalath Leannoth, Maschil of Heman the Ezrahite.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Cry Out before You

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O ##LORD##note:x[<<Isa 12:2>>] God of my salvation, ##I have##note:x[<<Ps 22:2; Luke 18:7>>] cried day __and__ night before thee&#58;note:tn[__for the sons__: or, of the sons]note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm of Heman the Ezrahite, giving instruction]
@ ##Let my prayer come before thee: incline thine ear unto my cry;##note:x[<<Ps 141:1–2; Lam 3:8>>]
