@ For my ##soul##note:x[<<Lam 3:15–19; Mark 14:33–34>>] is full of troubles: and my ##life##note:x[<<Ps 107:18>>] draweth nigh unto the grave.
@ I am ##counted##note:x[<<Ps 28:1; 143:7; 2 Cor 1:9>>] with them that go down into the pit: I am as a man __that hath__ no strength:
@ Free among the dead, like the slain that lie in the grave, whom thou rememberest no more: and they are ##cut##note:x[<<Ps 31:22; Isa 53:8>>] off ##from##note:tn[or, by] thy hand.
@ Thou hast laid me in the ##lowest##note:x[<<Ps 86:13>>] pit, in darkness, in the ##deeps##note:x[<<Ps 69:15>>].
@ Thy wrath lieth hard upon me, and thou hast afflicted __me__ ##with##note:x[<<Ps 42:7>>] all thy waves. Selah.
@ Thou hast ##put##note:x[<<Ps 31:11>>] away mine acquaintance far from me; thou hast ##made##note:x[<<Isa 49:7>>] me an abomination unto them: __I am__ shut up, and I cannot come forth.
@ ##Mine eye mourneth by reason of affliction: LORD, I have##note:x[<<Ps 38:10>>] ##called##note:x[<<Ps 86:3>>] daily upon thee, I have ##stretched##note:x[<<Job 11:13>>] out my hands unto thee.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Wilt thou##note:x[<<Ps 6:5; 30:9>>] shew wonders to the dead? ##shall##note:x[<<Isa 26:19>>] the dead arise __and__ praise thee? Selah.
@ Shall thy lovingkindness be declared in the grave? __or__ thy faithfulness in destruction?
@ Shall thy wonders be known in the ##dark##note:x[<<Eccl 9:5>>]? and thy righteousness in the land of forgetfulness?
@ But unto thee have I cried, O LORD; ##and in##note:x[<<Ps 5:3; 119:147–148; Mark 1:35>>] the morning shall my prayer prevent thee.
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 43:2>>], why castest thou off my soul? __why__ ##hidest##note:x[<<Job 13:24; Ps 13:1>>] thou thy face from me?
@ I __am__ ##afflicted##note:x[<<Ps 73:14>>] and ready to die from __my__ youth up: ##__while__##note:x[<<Job 6:4; Luke 22:44>>] I suffer thy terrors I am distracted.
@ Thy fierce wrath goeth over me; thy terrors have cut me off.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Ps 22:16>>] came round about me ##daily##note:tn[or, all the day] like water; they compassed me about together.
@ ##Lover##note:x[<<Job 19:12–15; Ps 38:11; 88:8>>] and friend hast thou put far from me, __and__ mine acquaintance into darkness.

=== @89

[.psalm-title]
Maschil of Ethan the Ezrahite.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Sing of His Love Forever

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 101:1>>] sing of the mercies of the LORD for ever: with my mouth will I make known ##thy faithfulness##note:x[<<Ps 89:8; Lam 3:23>>] to all generations.note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm for Ethan the Ezrahite, to give instruction]note:tn[__to all…__: Heb. to generation and generation]
@ For I have said, ##Mercy##note:x[<<Neh 9:31; Ps 36:5>>] shall be built up for ever: thy faithfulness shalt thou establish in the very heavens.
@ I have ##made##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:10–16; Ps 89:28>>] a covenant with my chosen, I have ##sworn##note:x[<<Ps 132:11>>] unto David my servant,
@ ##Thy seed will I establish for ever, and build up thy throne to all generations. Selah.##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:12–16; Ps 132:12; Luke 1:32–33>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ And the ##heavens##note:x[<<Ps 19:1; 97:6; Isa 44:23>>] shall praise thy wonders, O LORD: thy faithfulness also in the congregation of the saints.
@ ##For who##note:x[<<Ps 71:19; 86:8; Jer 10:6>>] in the heaven can be compared unto the LORD? __who__ among the sons of the mighty can be likened unto the LORD?
@ ##God is greatly to be feared in the assembly of the saints, and to be had in reverence of all __them that are__ about him.##note:x[<<Ps 76:7–11; Jer 10:7; Heb 12:28–29>>]
@ O LORD God of hosts, who __is__ ##a strong##note:x[<<Jer 32:17>>] LORD ##like##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:2; Ps 71:19>>] unto thee? or to thy faithfulness round about thee?
@ ##Thou rulest the raging of the sea: when the waves thereof arise, thou stillest them.##note:x[<<Ps 65:7; 107:25–29; Mark 4:41>>]
@ Thou hast broken ##Rahab##note:tn[or, Egypt] in pieces, as one that is slain; thou hast scattered thine enemies with thy strong arm.note:tn[__thy…__: Heb. the arm of thy strength]
@ ##The heavens __are__ thine, the earth also __is__ thine: __as for__ the world and the fulness thereof, thou hast founded them.##note:x[<<Gen 1:1; 1 Chr 29:11; Ps 115:16>>]note:tn[__the fulness…__: or, all it containeth]
@ The ##north##note:x[<<Job 26:7>>] and the south thou hast created them: ##Tabor##note:x[<<Josh 19:22>>] and ##Hermon##note:x[<<Josh 12:1>>] shall rejoice in thy name.
@ Thou hast ##a mighty arm##note:x[<<Ps 62:11>>]: strong is thy hand, __and__ high is thy right hand.note:tn[__a…__: Heb. an arm with might]
@ ##Justice##note:x[<<Ps 45:6–7; 97:2>>] and judgment __are__ the note:x[<<Prov 16:12>>]##habitation##note:tn[or, establishment] of thy throne: mercy and truth shall go before thy face.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __is__ the people that know the joyful sound: they shall walk, O LORD, ##in the light##note:x[<<Job 29:3; Ps 4:6; 44:3>>] of thy countenance.
@ In thy ##name##note:x[<<Ps 29:7; Phil 4:4>>] shall they rejoice all the day: and in thy ##righteousness##note:x[<<Phil 3:9>>] shall they be exalted.
@ For thou __art__ the glory of their strength: and in thy favour ##our horn##note:x[<<Ps 75:10; 92:10; 148:14>>] shall be exalted.
@ For ##the LORD __is__##note:x[<<Ps 47:9>>] our defence; and the ##Holy##note:x[<<Ps 71:22>>] One of Israel __is__ our ##king##note:x[<<Isa 33:22>>].note:tn[__the LORD…__: or, our shield is of the LORD, and our king is of the Holy One of Israel]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Then thou spakest in vision to thy holy one, and saidst, ##I have laid##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:18>>] help upon __one that is__ mighty; I have ##exalted##note:x[<<1 Kgs 11:34>>] __one__ chosen out of the people.
@ ##I have found David my servant; with my holy oil have I anointed him:##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:1, 12–13; Isa 61:1–3>>]
@ ##With##note:x[<<Ps 18:32–39; Isa 42:1>>] whom my hand shall be established: ##mine##note:x[<<Ps 89:13>>] arm also shall strengthen him.
@ The ##enemy##note:x[<<1 Chr 17:9>>] shall not exact upon him; nor the son of wickedness afflict him.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:9; 22:40–44>>] beat down his foes before his face, and plague them that hate him.
@ ##But my##note:x[<<2 Cor 1:20>>] faithfulness and my mercy __shall be__ with him: and ##in my##note:x[<<Ps 89:16–17; 91:14>>] name shall his horn be exalted.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 80:11>>] set his hand also in the sea, and his right hand in the rivers.
@ He shall cry unto me, ##Thou##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:14>>] __art__ my father, my God, and the ##rock##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:47; Ps 95:1>>] of my salvation.
@ ##Also##note:x[<<Ps 2:7; Col 1:15, 18>>] I will make him __my__ firstborn, higher than the kings of the earth.
@ My ##mercy##note:x[<<Isa 55:3>>] will I keep for him for evermore, and my covenant shall stand fast with him.
@ ##His seed##note:x[<<Ps 89:4, 36>>] also will I make __to endure__ for ever, and his throne as the ##days##note:x[<<Deut 11:21>>] of heaven.
@ ##If##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:14; 1 Chr 28:9>>] his children forsake my law, and walk not in my judgments;
@ If they ##break##note:tn[Heb. profane] my statutes, and keep not my commandments;
@ ##Then will I visit their transgression with the rod, and their iniquity with stripes.##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:14; Prov 3:11–12; Heb 12:6–11>>]
@ ##Nevertheless##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:15; Isa 54:8–10; Lam 3:31–32>>] my lovingkindness will I not utterly take from him, nor suffer my faithfulness to fail.note:tn[__will…__: Heb. I will not make void from him]note:tn[__to fail__: Heb. to lie]
@ My covenant will I not break, ##nor##note:x[<<Num 23:19; Matt 24:35; Jas 1:17>>] alter the thing that is gone out of my lips.
@ ##Once##note:x[<<Ps 132:11; Heb 6:13>>] have I sworn by my holiness ##that I will not lie##note:x[<<Titus 1:2>>] unto David.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. if I lie]
@ His ##seed##note:x[<<Ps 89:29; Isa 59:21>>] shall endure for ever, ##and##note:x[<<Ps 72:5>>] his throne as the sun before me.
@ It shall be established for ##ever##note:x[<<Gen 1:14–18; Ps 104:19; Jer 31:35–36>>] as the moon, and __as__ a faithful witness in heaven. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##But##note:x[<<1 Chr 28:9>>] thou hast cast off ##and##note:x[<<Deut 32:19>>] abhorred, thou hast been wroth with thine anointed.
@ Thou hast made void the covenant of thy servant: thou hast ##profaned##note:x[<<Ps 74:7; Lam 5:16>>] his crown __by casting it__ to the ground.
@ Thou hast ##broken##note:x[<<Ps 80:12>>] down all his hedges; thou hast ##brought##note:x[<<Lam 2:2, 5>>] his strong holds to ruin.
@ All that pass by the way spoil him: he is a reproach to his neighbours.
@ Thou hast set up the right hand of his adversaries; thou hast made all his enemies to rejoice.
@ Thou hast also turned the edge of his sword, and hast not made him to stand in the battle.
@ Thou hast made his ##glory##note:tn[Heb. brightness] to cease, and cast his throne down to the ground.
@ The days of his youth hast ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 44:15; 109:29>>] shortened: thou hast covered him with shame. Selah.
@ ##How##note:x[<<Ps 79:5>>] long, LORD? wilt thou hide thyself for ever? shall ##thy wrath##note:x[<<Ps 78:63>>] burn like fire?
@ ##Remember##note:x[<<Job 7:7; 10:9>>] how short my time is: ##wherefore##note:x[<<Job 14:1>>] hast thou made all men in vain?
@ ##What##note:x[<<Eccl 9:5>>] man __is he that__ liveth, and ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 49:15>>] not ##see death##note:x[<<Heb 11:5>>]? shall he deliver his soul from the hand of the grave? Selah.
@ Lord, where __are__ thy former lovingkindnesses, __which__ ##thou##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:15>>] swarest unto David in thy truth?
@ ##Remember, Lord, the reproach of thy servants; __how__ I do bear in my bosom __the reproach of__ all the mighty people;##note:x[<<Ps 69:9; 74:18, 22>>]
@ Wherewith thine enemies have reproached, O LORD; wherewith they have reproached the footsteps of thine anointed.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 41:13; 72:18–19; 1 Tim 1:17>>] __be__ the LORD for evermore. Amen, and Amen.

=== @90

[.psalm-title]
A Prayer of Moses the man of God.

[discrete.major]
==== BOOK IV

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 90—106>>)

[discrete.pericope]
==== From Everlasting to Everlasting

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Lord##note:x[<<Deut 33:27; Ps 71:3; 1 John 4:16>>], thou hast been our dwelling place in all generations.note:tn[__A Prayer…__: or, A Prayer, being a Psalm of Moses]note:tn[__in…__: Heb. in generation and generation]
@ Before the mountains were brought forth, ##or ever##note:x[<<Gen 1:1>>] thou hadst formed the earth and the world, ##even from##note:x[<<Ps 102:24–27; Heb 13:8>>] everlasting to everlasting, thou __art__ God.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Gen 3:19; Ps 104:29; Eccl 12:7>>] turnest man to destruction; and sayest, Return, ye children of men.
@ ##For##note:x[<<2 Pet 3:8>>] a thousand years in thy sight __are but__ as yesterday when it is past, ##and __as__##note:x[<<Matt 24:43; Luke 12:38>>] a watch in the night.note:tn[__when…__: or, when he hath passed them]
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Job 22:16>>] carriest them away as with a flood; they are ##__as__ a sleep##note:x[<<Ps 73:20>>]: in the ##morning##note:x[<<Isa 40:6>>] __they are__ like grass __which__ groweth up.note:tn[__groweth…__: or, is changed]
@ ##In the morning it flourisheth, and groweth up; in the evening it is cut down, and withereth.##note:x[<<Job 14:2; Ps 92:7>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##For we##note:x[<<Ps 39:11>>] are consumed by thine anger, and by thy wrath are we troubled.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Jer 16:17; 23:24>>] hast set ##our##note:x[<<Eccl 12:14>>] iniquities before thee, our secret __sins__ in the light of thy countenance.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 78:33>>] all our days are passed away in thy wrath: we spend our years as ##a tale##note:x[<<Ps 39:5>>] __that is told__.note:tn[__passed…__: Heb. turned away]note:tn[__as a…__: or, as a meditation]
@ The days of our years __are__ threescore years and ten; and if by reason of strength __they be__ fourscore years, ##yet##note:x[<<2 Sam 19:35>>] __is__ their strength labour and sorrow; ##for##note:x[<<Ps 78:39; Jas 4:14>>] it is soon cut off, and we fly away.note:tn[__The days…__: Heb. As for the days of our years, in them are seventy years]
@ Who knoweth the power of thine anger? even according to thy fear, __so is__ thy wrath.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##So##note:x[<<Ps 39:4; John 9:4; Eph 5:16–17>>] teach __us__ to number our days, that we may ##apply##note:tn[Heb. cause to come] __our__ hearts unto wisdom.
@ Return, O LORD, how long? and ##let it##note:x[<<Deut 32:36; Ps 106:45; 135:14>>] repent thee concerning thy servants.
@ O satisfy us early with thy mercy; ##that we##note:x[<<Ps 23:6; 85:6; 86:4>>] may rejoice and be glad all our days.
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; 126:5–6; Isa 40:1–2>>] us glad according to the days __wherein__ thou hast afflicted us, __and__ the years __wherein__ we have seen evil.
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Ps 44:1; Hab 3:2>>] thy work appear unto thy servants, and thy glory unto their children.
@ And let the beauty of the LORD our God be upon us: and ##establish##note:x[<<Prov 16:3; 1 Cor 3:7; 2 Thess 2:16–17>>] thou the work of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish thou it.

=== @91

[discrete.pericope]
==== You Are My Refuge and My Fortress

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ He that ##dwelleth##note:x[<<Ps 27:5; 32:7>>] in the secret place of the most High shall ##abide##note:tn[Heb. lodge] ##under##note:x[<<Ps 36:7>>] the shadow of the Almighty.
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 18:2; Prov 18:10>>] say of the LORD, __He is__ my refuge and my fortress: my God; ##in him##note:x[<<Isa 26:3–4>>] will I trust.
@ Surely he shall deliver thee from the ##snare##note:x[<<Ps 124:7; 141:9>>] of the fowler, ##__and__ from##note:x[<<Job 5:10–22>>] the noisome pestilence.
@ He shall ##cover##note:x[<<Ps 17:8; 57:1; 61:4>>] thee with his feathers, and under his wings shalt thou trust: his truth __shall be thy__ shield and buckler.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Job 5:19–27; Isa 43:2>>] shalt not be afraid for the ##terror##note:x[<<Prov 3:23–25>>] by night; __nor__ for the arrow __that__ flieth by day;
@ __Nor__ for the ##pestilence##note:x[<<Ps 121:5–6>>] __that__ walketh in darkness; __nor__ for the ##destruction##note:x[<<Num 16:48; Matt 24:6–7>>] __that__ wasteth at noonday.
@ ##A thousand shall fall at thy side, and ten thousand at thy right hand; __but__ it shall not come nigh thee.##note:x[<<Gen 7:23; Exod 12:12–13; Ps 32:6>>]
@ ##Only##note:x[<<Ps 37:34; 92:11; Prov 3:25–26>>] with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Because thou hast made the LORD, __which is__ my refuge, __even__ the ##most High##note:x[<<Ps 71:3; 90:1; 91:1>>], thy habitation;
@ ##There##note:x[<<Ps 121:7; Prov 12:21>>] shall no evil befall thee, ##neither##note:x[<<Deut 7:15>>] shall any plague come nigh thy dwelling.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 34:7; Luke 4:10–11; Heb 1:14>>] he shall give his angels charge over thee, to keep thee in all thy ways.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Isa 63:9>>] shall bear thee up in __their__ hands, ##lest##note:x[<<Ps 37:24; Prov 3:23>>] thou dash thy foot against a stone.
@ Thou shalt ##tread##note:x[<<1 Sam 17:37; Dan 6:22>>] upon the lion and note:x[<<Mark 16:18>>]##adder##note:tn[or, asp]: the young lion and the dragon shalt thou trample under feet.
@ Because he hath ##set##note:x[<<John 14:23; Jas 1:12>>] his love upon me, therefore will I deliver him: I will set him on high, because he hath ##known##note:x[<<Ps 9:10>>] my name.
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Isa 65:24; Jer 33:3>>] call upon me, and I will answer him: ##I __will be__##note:x[<<Ps 138:7>>] with him in trouble; I will deliver him, and honour him.
@ ##With long life##note:x[<<Ps 21:4; Prov 3:2, 16>>] will I satisfy him, and shew him my salvation.note:tn[__long…__: Heb. length of days]

=== @92

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm __or__ Song for the sabbath day.

[discrete.pericope]
==== How Great Are Your Works!

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ __It is a__ ##good##note:x[<<Ps 107:1, 21–22; 147:1>>] __thing__ to give thanks unto the LORD, and to sing praises unto thy name, O most High:
@ To ##shew##note:x[<<Ps 89:1–2; Lam 3:22–23; John 1:17>>] forth thy lovingkindness in the morning, and thy faithfulness every night,note:tn[__every…__: Heb. in the nights]
@ Upon an ##instrument##note:x[<<2 Chr 23:5; Ps 33:2; 68:25>>] of ten strings, and upon the psaltery; upon the harp with a solemn sound.note:tn[__the harp…__: or, the solemn sound with the harp]note:tn[__a solemn…__: Heb. Higgaion]
@ ##For thou, LORD, hast made me glad through thy work: I will triumph in the works of thy hands.##note:x[<<Ps 126:3; Isa 65:13–14; 2 Cor 2:14>>]
@ ##O LORD##note:x[<<Ps 40:5>>], how great are thy works! __and__ thy ##thoughts##note:x[<<Isa 55:8–9; Rom 11:33–34>>] are very deep.
@ ##A brutish##note:x[<<Ps 73:22; 94:8; 1 Cor 2:14>>] man knoweth not; neither doth a fool understand this.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ When the ##wicked##note:x[<<Ps 37:38>>] spring as the grass, and when all the ##workers##note:x[<<Ps 73:18–20>>] of iniquity do flourish; ##__it is__ that##note:x[<<Ps 37:38>>] they shall be destroyed for ever:
@ But thou, LORD, ##__art most__##note:x[<<Exod 18:11; Ps 83:18>>] high for evermore.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Ps 37:20; 89:10>>], lo, thine enemies, O LORD, for, lo, thine enemies shall perish; all the workers of iniquity shall be scattered.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 89:17>>] my horn shalt thou exalt like __the horn of__ an unicorn: ##I shall##note:x[<<Ps 23:5; 45:7>>] be anointed with fresh oil.
@ ##Mine eye also shall see __my desire__ on mine enemies, __and__ mine ears shall hear __my desire__ of the wicked that rise up against me.##note:x[<<Ps 54:7; 59:10; 91:8>>]
@ The ##righteous##note:x[<<Ps 52:8; Hos 14:5–6>>] shall flourish like the palm tree: he shall grow like a ##cedar##note:x[<<Ps 104:16>>] in Lebanon.
@ ##Those##note:x[<<Eph 3:17>>] that be planted in the house of the LORD ##shall flourish##note:x[<<Isa 61:3; 2 Pet 3:18>>] in the courts of our God.
@ They shall still bring forth fruit ##in old age##note:x[<<Ps 71:18; Isa 46:4; Jer 17:8>>]; they shall be fat and ##flourishing##note:tn[Heb. green];
@ To shew that the LORD __is__ upright: __he is__ ##my rock##note:x[<<Ps 18:2; 62:6>>], ##and##note:x[<<Rom 9:14>>] __there is__ no unrighteousness in him.

=== @93

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 99:1–9>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:12; Ps 96:10>>] reigneth, he is clothed with majesty; the LORD is clothed with strength, __wherewith__ he hath girded himself: the ##world##note:x[<<Ps 96:10>>] also is stablished, that it cannot be moved.
@ ##Thy##note:x[<<Ps 45:6; Prov 8:22–23>>] throne __is__ established of old: ##thou##note:x[<<Ps 90:2>>] __art__ from everlasting.note:tn[__of old__: Heb. from then]
@ ##The floods##note:x[<<Ps 69:1–2; Isa 17:12–13>>] have ##lifted##note:x[<<Ps 96:11>>] up, O LORD, the floods have lifted up their voice; the floods lift up their waves.
@ The LORD on high __is__ ##mightier##note:x[<<Job 38:11; Ps 65:7; 89:9>>] than the noise of many waters, __yea, than__ the mighty waves of the sea.
@ ##Thy##note:x[<<Ps 19:7–8; 119:111, 129>>] testimonies are very sure: holiness becometh thine house, O LORD, for ever.note:tn[__for ever__: Heb. to length of days]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Will Not Forget His People

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD God, to whom vengeance belongeth; ##O God##note:x[<<Deut 32:35; Isa 35:4; Nah 1:2>>], to whom vengeance belongeth, shew thyself.note:tn[__God…__: Heb. God of revenges]note:tn[__shew…__: Heb. shine forth]
@ ##Lift##note:x[<<Ps 7:6>>] up thyself, ##thou##note:x[<<2 Cor 5:10>>] judge of the earth: ##render##note:x[<<Ps 31:23>>] a reward to the proud.
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Jer 12:1–2; Rev 6:10>>], how long shall ##the wicked##note:x[<<Job 20:5>>], how long shall the wicked triumph?
@ __How long__ ##shall##note:x[<<Ps 31:18>>] they utter __and__ speak hard things? __and__ all the workers of iniquity ##boast##note:x[<<Ps 52:1>>] themselves?
@ They ##break##note:x[<<Isa 3:15>>] in pieces thy people, O LORD, and afflict thine heritage.
@ ##They slay the widow and the stranger, and murder the fatherless.##note:x[<<Isa 10:2; Ezek 22:7>>]
@ Yet ##they say##note:x[<<Ps 10:11–13; Isa 29:15; Luke 18:3–4>>], The LORD shall not see, neither shall the God of Jacob regard __it__.
@ Understand, ye ##brutish##note:x[<<Ps 49:10; 92:6>>] among the people: and __ye__ ##fools##note:x[<<Titus 3:3>>], when will ye be wise?
@ ##He that planted##note:x[<<Exod 4:11; Prov 20:12>>] the ear, shall he not ##hear##note:x[<<Jer 23:23–24>>]? he that formed the eye, shall he not see?
@ He that ##chastiseth##note:x[<<Jer 10:25>>] the heathen, shall not he correct? he that ##teacheth man##note:x[<<Job 35:11; Isa 28:26>>] knowledge, __shall not he know__?
@ ##The LORD knoweth the thoughts of man, that they __are__ vanity.##note:x[<<Job 11:11–12; Rom 1:21–22; 1 Cor 1:19>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Job 5:17; Prov 3:11; 1 Cor 11:32>>] __is__ the man whom thou chastenest, O LORD, and teachest him out of thy law;
@ That thou mayest give him rest from the days of adversity, ##until the pit##note:x[<<Ps 9:15; 55:23; 2 Pet 2:9>>] be digged for the wicked.
@ ##For##note:x[<<1 Sam 12:22; Ps 37:28; Rom 11:1–2>>] the LORD will not cast off his people, neither will he forsake his inheritance.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 125:3; Mic 7:9>>] judgment shall return unto righteousness: ##and all##note:x[<<Ps 37:34>>] the upright in heart shall follow it.note:tn[__shall follow…__: Heb. shall be after it]
@ Who will ##rise up##note:x[<<Judg 5:23; 3 John 1:8>>] for me against the evildoers? __or__ who will ##stand up##note:x[<<John 7:50–51>>] for me against the workers of iniquity?
@ ##Unless##note:x[<<Ps 118:13; 124:1–2>>] the LORD __had been__ my help, my soul had ##almost##note:tn[or, quickly] ##dwelt##note:x[<<Ps 31:17>>] in silence.
@ When I said, ##My foot##note:x[<<Ps 119:116–117; 121:3; Isa 41:10>>] slippeth; thy mercy, O LORD, held me up.
@ ##In the multitude of my thoughts within me thy comforts delight my soul.##note:x[<<Ps 61:2; 77:2–10; 2 Cor 1:4–5>>]
@ Shall the ##throne##note:x[<<Eccl 3:16>>] of iniquity have fellowship with thee, which ##frameth##note:x[<<Ps 58:2; Isa 10:1>>] mischief by a law?
@ They ##gather##note:x[<<Ps 59:3; Matt 27:1>>] themselves together against the soul of the righteous, and ##condemn##note:x[<<Prov 17:15>>] the innocent blood.
@ ##But##note:x[<<Ps 27:1–3; 59:9>>] the LORD is my defence; and my God __is__ ##the rock##note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>] of my refuge.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Ps 7:16; Prov 2:22>>] shall bring upon them their own iniquity, and shall ##cut them##note:x[<<Prov 14:32>>] off in their own wickedness; __yea__, the LORD our God shall cut them off.

@ ##Let us##note:x[<<Ps 100:4; Jer 31:12–13>>] come before his presence with thanksgiving, and make a joyful noise unto him with ##psalms##note:x[<<Ps 105:2>>].note:tn[__come…__: Heb. prevent his face]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ For he __is__ our God; and we __are__ the people of his pasture, and the sheep of his hand. ##To day##note:x[<<Heb 3:15; 4:7>>] ##if ye##note:x[<<Rev 3:20>>] will hear his voice,
@ Harden not your heart, as ##in the##note:x[<<Exod 17:7; Num 20:13; Heb 3:8–9>>] ##provocation##note:tn[Heb. contention], __and__ as __in__ the day of temptation in the wilderness:

@ Unto whom ##I sware##note:x[<<Num 14:23; Heb 3:11; 4:3>>] in my wrath that they should not enter into my rest.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. if they enter into my rest]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<Ps 96:10–11; 99:1; Col 1:13>>] reigneth; let the earth rejoice; let the multitude of isles be glad __thereof__.note:tn[__multitude…__: Heb. many, or, great isles]

@ ##The heavens##note:x[<<Ps 19:1; 50:6>>] declare his righteousness, and ##all the##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:47>>] people see his glory.

@ Ye that love the LORD, ##hate##note:x[<<Ps 34:14; Prov 8:13; Rom 12:9>>] evil: he preserveth the souls of his saints; he delivereth them out of the hand of the wicked.
@ ##Light##note:x[<<Prov 4:18; John 12:46>>] is ##sown##note:x[<<Jas 5:7–11>>] for the righteous, and gladness for the upright in heart.

=== @101

A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Set No Worthless Thing before My Eyes

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will sing##note:x[<<Ps 89:1; Rev 19:1–3>>] of mercy and judgment: ##unto thee##note:x[<<Ps 71:22–23>>], O LORD, will I sing.
@ I will ##behave##note:x[<<Ps 119:106>>] myself wisely in ##a perfect##note:x[<<1 Kgs 9:4>>] way. O when wilt thou come unto me? I will ##walk##note:x[<<1 Tim 3:4–5>>] within my house with a perfect heart.
@ I will ##set##note:x[<<Job 31:1; Ps 119:37; Jas 1:13–15>>] no wicked thing before mine eyes: I hate the work of them that turn aside; __it__ shall not cleave to me.note:tn[__wicked…__: Heb. thing of Belial]
@ ##A froward##note:x[<<Prov 8:13; 11:20>>] heart shall depart from me: I will not ##know##note:x[<<2 Cor 6:14–16>>] a wicked __person__.
@ ##Whoso##note:x[<<Ps 15:3; 1 Cor 5:11>>] privily slandereth his neighbour, him will I cut off: him that hath ##an high##note:x[<<Ps 18:27>>] look and a proud heart will not I suffer.
@ ##Mine##note:x[<<Ps 119:63; Luke 12:43–44>>] eyes __shall be__ upon the faithful of the land, ##that they##note:x[<<Rev 21:3>>] may dwell with me: he that walketh in a perfect way, he shall serve me.note:tn[__in a…__: or, perfect in the way]
@ ##He that worketh##note:x[<<2 Sam 4:10–12; Prov 29:12; Acts 5:1–10>>] deceit shall not dwell within my house: he that telleth lies shall not tarry in my sight.note:tn[__shall not tarry__: Heb. shall not be established]
@ I will ##early##note:x[<<Ps 75:10; Prov 16:12; Jer 21:12>>] destroy all the wicked of the land; that I may cut off all wicked doers from the city of the LORD.

=== @102

[.psalm-title]
A Prayer of the afflicted, when he is overwhelmed, and poureth out his complaint before the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Prayer of the Afflicted

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 61:2>>]##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 130:1–2>>] my prayer, O LORD, and ##let my##note:x[<<Ps 18:6>>] cry come unto thee.note:tn[__of__: or, for]
@ ##Hide##note:x[<<Ps 27:9; 69:17; Isa 8:17>>] not thy face from me in the day __when__ I am in trouble; incline thine ear unto me: in the day __when__ I call answer me speedily.
@ For ##my days##note:x[<<Jas 4:14>>] are consumed like smoke, and ##my bones##note:x[<<Ps 31:10; Lam 1:13>>] are burned as an hearth.note:tn[__like…__: or, (as some read) into smoke]
@ My heart is smitten, and ##withered##note:x[<<Ps 37:2; Isa 40:7>>] like grass; ##so that##note:x[<<Ezra 10:6>>] I forget to eat my bread.
@ By reason of ##the voice##note:x[<<Job 19:20; Prov 17:22; Lam 4:8>>] of my groaning my bones cleave to my ##skin##note:tn[or, flesh].
@ I am ##like##note:x[<<Isa 38:14>>] ##a pelican##note:x[<<Zeph 2:14; Rev 18:2>>] of the wilderness: I am like an owl of the desert.
@ I ##watch##note:x[<<Ps 77:4>>], and am as a sparrow ##alone##note:x[<<Lam 3:28–30>>] upon the house top.
@ ##Mine##note:x[<<Ps 69:9–10; Rom 15:3>>] enemies reproach me all the day; __and__ they that are ##mad##note:x[<<Acts 26:11>>] against me are sworn against me.
@ For I have eaten ashes like bread, and ##mingled##note:x[<<Ps 42:3>>] my drink with weeping,
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 38:3>>] of thine indignation and thy wrath: for ##thou hast##note:x[<<2 Cor 4:9>>] lifted me up, and cast me down.
@ ##My days##note:x[<<Job 14:2; Ps 109:23; 144:4>>] __are__ like a shadow that declineth; and I am withered like grass.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ But ##thou##note:x[<<Lam 5:19>>], O LORD, shalt endure for ever; and ##thy remembrance##note:x[<<Exod 3:15; Ps 135:13>>] unto all generations.
@ ##Thou##note:x[<<Ps 44:26; Isa 60:1>>] shalt arise, __and__ have mercy upon Zion: for the time to favour her, yea, ##the set##note:x[<<Gal 4:4>>] time, is come.
@ ##For thy servants take pleasure in her stones, and favour the dust thereof.##note:x[<<Neh 4:2>>]
@ ##So the heathen shall fear the name of the LORD, and all the kings of the earth thy glory.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:43; Ps 138:4; Isa 60:3–22>>]
@ ##When##note:x[<<Ps 147:2; Jer 33:7>>] the LORD shall build up Zion, ##he shall##note:x[<<Isa 60:1–2>>] appear in his glory.
@ ##He will##note:x[<<Neh 1:6; Ps 9:18>>] regard the prayer of the destitute, and ##not despise##note:x[<<Ps 22:24>>] their prayer.
@ ##This##note:x[<<Rom 15:4>>] shall be written for the generation to come: and ##the people##note:x[<<Ps 22:30–31; Isa 43:21>>] which shall be created shall praise the LORD.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Deut 26:15; Ps 14:2>>] hath looked down from the height of his sanctuary; from heaven did the LORD behold the earth;
@ ##To hear##note:x[<<Ps 79:11; 146:7; Isa 61:1–3>>] the groaning of the prisoner; to loose those that are appointed to death;note:tn[__those…__: Heb. the children of death]
@ ##To declare the name of the LORD in Zion, and his praise in Jerusalem;##note:x[<<Ps 22:22>>]
@ ##When the people are gathered together, and the kingdoms, to serve the LORD.##note:x[<<Isa 49:22–23; 60:3–22; Zech 8:20–23>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ He ##weakened##note:tn[Heb. afflicted] my strength in the way; he shortened my days.
@ ##I said##note:x[<<Ps 39:13>>], O my God, take me not away in the midst of my days: ##thy years##note:x[<<Hab 1:12>>] __are__ throughout all generations.
@ ##Of old hast thou laid the foundation of the earth: and the heavens __are__ the work of thy hands.##note:x[<<Gen 1:1; Exod 20:11; Jer 32:17>>]
@ ##They shall##note:x[<<Isa 34:4; 51:6; Luke 21:33>>] perish, but thou shalt ##endure##note:tn[Heb. stand]: yea, all of them shall wax old like a garment; as a vesture shalt thou change them, and they shall be changed:
@ But ##thou __art__##note:x[<<Mal 3:6; Heb 13:8; Jas 1:17>>] the same, and thy years shall have no end.
@ ##The children##note:x[<<Ps 45:16–17; 69:35–36>>] of thy servants shall continue, and their seed shall be established before thee.

=== @103

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Bless the LORD, O My Soul

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Bless##note:x[<<Ps 146:1–2>>] the LORD, O my soul: and ##all that##note:x[<<Ps 57:7–11; 1 Cor 14:15>>] is within me, __bless__ his holy name.
@ Bless the LORD, O my soul, and ##forget not##note:x[<<Ps 105:5; 116:12; Isa 63:7>>] all his benefits:
@ Who forgiveth all thine iniquities; who ##healeth##note:x[<<Exod 15:26; Isa 53:5; Jer 17:14>>] all thy diseases;
@ Who ##redeemeth##note:x[<<Ps 56:13; 71:23>>] thy life from destruction; who ##crowneth##note:x[<<Ps 103:12>>] thee with lovingkindness and tender mercies;
@ Who ##satisfieth##note:x[<<Ps 107:9; 1 Tim 6:17>>] thy mouth with good __things; so that__ ##thy youth##note:x[<<Isa 40:31>>] is renewed like the eagle’s.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD ##executeth##note:x[<<Ps 9:9; 12:5; Prov 14:31>>] righteousness and judgment for all that are oppressed.
@ ##He made##note:x[<<Neh 9:14; Ps 77:20>>] known his ways unto Moses, ##his acts##note:x[<<Ps 147:19>>] unto the children of Israel.
@ The LORD __is__ ##merciful##note:x[<<Exod 34:6–7; Ps 86:15>>] and gracious, ##slow##note:x[<<Joel 2:13>>] to anger, and plenteous in mercy.note:tn[__plenteous…__: Heb. great in mercy]
@ He will not always chide: ##neither##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Isa 57:16; Mic 7:18–19>>] will he keep __his anger__ for ever.
@ He hath not ##dealt##note:x[<<Ezra 9:13; Neh 9:31; Lam 3:22>>] with us after our sins; nor rewarded us according to our iniquities.
@ For ##as the##note:x[<<Ps 36:5; 57:10>>] heaven is high above the earth, __so__ great is ##his mercy##note:x[<<Luke 1:50>>] toward them that fear him.note:tn[__as…__: Heb. according to the height of the heaven]
@ As far as the east is from the west, ##__so__ far##note:x[<<Isa 43:25; Mic 7:18; 1 John 1:7>>] hath he removed our transgressions from us.
@ ##Like##note:x[<<Luke 11:11–12; 15:21–22; Heb 12:5–11>>] as a father pitieth __his__ children, __so__ the LORD pitieth them that fear him.
@ For ##he knoweth##note:x[<<Ps 78:38–39>>] our frame; he remembereth that ##we __are__ dust##note:x[<<Job 10:9; Eccl 12:7>>].
@ __As for__ man, ##his days##note:x[<<Isa 40:6–8; Jas 1:10–11; 1 Pet 1:24>>] __are__ as grass: as a flower of the field, so he flourisheth.
@ For ##the wind##note:x[<<Isa 40:7>>] passeth over it, and ##it is gone##note:x[<<Job 14:10>>]; ##and the##note:x[<<Job 20:9>>] place thereof shall know it no more.note:tn[__it is…__: Heb. it is not]
@ But ##the mercy##note:x[<<Ps 100:5; Eph 2:4–7; 2 Tim 1:9>>] of the LORD __is__ from everlasting to everlasting upon them that fear him, and his righteousness unto children’s children;
@ ##To such##note:x[<<Deut 7:9; Ps 132:12>>] as keep his covenant, and to those that ##remember##note:x[<<Matt 28:20>>] his commandments to do them.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD hath ##prepared##note:x[<<Ps 9:7>>] his throne in the heavens; and ##his kingdom##note:x[<<Ps 47:2; Phil 2:9–10>>] ruleth over all.
@ ##Bless##note:x[<<Ps 148:2>>] the LORD, ye his angels, that excel in strength, that ##do his##note:x[<<Luke 1:19; Heb 1:14>>] commandments, hearkening unto the voice of his word.note:tn[__that excel…__: Heb. mighty in strength]
@ Bless ye the LORD, ##all __ye__ his hosts##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:19; Luke 2:13>>]; __ye__ ##ministers##note:x[<<Neh 9:6>>] of his, that do his pleasure.
@ Bless the LORD, ##all his works##note:x[<<Ps 145:10; Isa 42:10–12; 49:13>>] in all places of his dominion: bless the LORD, O my soul.

=== @104

[discrete.pericope]
==== How Many Are Your Works, O LORD!

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Bless##note:x[<<Ps 103:22>>] the LORD, O my soul. O LORD my God, thou ##art very great##note:x[<<Ps 145:3>>]; thou art clothed with ##honour##note:x[<<Ps 96:6>>] and majesty.
@ Who coverest __thyself__ ##with light##note:x[<<Dan 7:9>>] as __with__ a garment: who ##stretchest##note:x[<<Isa 40:22; 45:12>>] out the heavens like a curtain:
@ ##Who layeth##note:x[<<Ps 18:10–11; Amos 9:6>>] the beams of his chambers in the waters: who maketh the clouds his chariot: who ##walketh##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:11>>] upon the wings of the wind:
@ ##Who maketh##note:x[<<Heb 1:7>>] his angels spirits; his ##ministers##note:x[<<2 Kgs 2:11; 6:17>>] a flaming fire:
@ ##__Who__ laid the foundations of the earth##note:x[<<Job 26:7; Ps 24:2; 33:9>>], __that__ it should not be removed for ever.note:tn[__Who…__: Heb. He hath founded the earth upon her bases]
@ ##Thou coveredst it with the deep as __with__ a garment: the waters stood above the mountains.##note:x[<<Gen 7:19; 2 Pet 3:5>>]
@ ##At thy##note:x[<<Gen 8:1; Prov 8:28; Mark 4:39>>] rebuke they fled; at the voice of thy thunder they hasted away.
@ ##They go up##note:x[<<Gen 8:5>>] by the mountains; they go down by the valleys unto the place which thou hast founded for them.note:tn[__They go up…__: or, The mountains ascend, the valleys descend]
@ Thou ##hast set##note:x[<<Gen 9:11–15; Job 26:10; 38:10–11>>] a bound that they may not pass over; that they turn not again to cover the earth.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 107:35; Isa 35:7; 41:18>>]##He##note:tn[Heb. Who] sendeth the springs into the valleys, __which__ ##run##note:tn[Heb. walk] among the hills.
@ ##They give##note:x[<<Ps 145:16>>] drink to every beast of the field: the wild asses ##quench##note:tn[Heb. break] their thirst.
@ By them shall ##the fowls##note:x[<<Ps 50:11>>] of the heaven have their habitation, __which__ ##sing##note:tn[Heb. give a voice] among the branches.
@ He ##watereth##note:x[<<Ps 147:8; Jer 10:13; 14:22>>] the hills from his chambers: the earth is satisfied with the fruit of thy works.
@ He ##causeth##note:x[<<Ps 145:15–16; 147:8–9>>] the grass to grow for the cattle, and herb for the service of man: ##that he##note:x[<<Ps 136:25>>] may bring forth food out of the earth;
@ And ##wine##note:x[<<Ps 23:5; Eccl 10:19>>] __that__ maketh glad the heart of man, __and__ ##oil to make __his__##note:x[<<Eccl 9:7>>] face to shine, and bread __which__ strengtheneth man’s heart.note:tn[__oil…__: Heb. to make his face shine with oil, or, more than oil]
@ ##The trees of the LORD are full __of sap;__ the cedars of Lebanon, which he hath planted;##note:x[<<Num 24:6; Ps 29:5; 92:2>>]
@ Where ##the birds##note:x[<<Ps 104:12>>] make their nests: __as for__ the stork, the fir trees __are__ her house.
@ The high hills __are__ a refuge for ##the wild goats##note:x[<<Job 39:1>>]; __and__ the rocks for ##the conies##note:x[<<Prov 30:26>>].

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##He appointed the moon for seasons: the sun knoweth his going down.##note:x[<<Gen 1:14–18; Ps 136:7–9; Jer 31:35>>]
@ Thou ##makest##note:x[<<Ps 74:16; Isa 45:7>>] darkness, and it is night: wherein all the beasts of the forest do creep __forth__.note:tn[__beasts…__: Heb. beasts thereof do trample on the forest]
@ ##The young##note:x[<<Job 38:39; Ps 34:10>>] lions roar after their prey, and ##seek##note:x[<<Joel 1:20>>] their meat from God.
@ The sun ariseth, they gather themselves together, and lay them down in their dens.
@ ##Man goeth forth unto his work and to his labour until the evening.##note:x[<<Gen 3:19; Eccl 5:12; Eph 4:28>>]
@ O LORD, ##how##note:x[<<Ps 40:5>>] manifold are thy works! ##in wisdom##note:x[<<Jer 10:12>>] hast thou made them all: ##the earth##note:x[<<Ps 50:10–12>>] is full of thy riches.
@ __So is__ ##this great##note:x[<<Ps 95:4–5>>] and wide sea, wherein __are__ things creeping innumerable, both small and great beasts.
@ ##There go##note:x[<<Ps 107:23>>] the ships: __there is__ that ##leviathan##note:x[<<Ps 74:14; Isa 27:1>>], __whom__ thou hast ##made##note:tn[Heb. formed] to play therein.
@ ##These wait all upon thee; that thou mayest give __them__ their meat in due season.##note:x[<<Job 38:41; Ps 136:25; 147:9>>]
@ __That__ thou givest them they gather: thou openest thine hand, they are filled with good.
@ Thou hidest thy face, they are troubled: ##thou takest##note:x[<<Job 34:14–15; Ps 146:4; Eccl 12:7>>] away their breath, they die, and return to their dust.
@ Thou ##sendest##note:x[<<Job 33:4; Ezek 37:9>>] forth thy spirit, they are created: and thou ##renewest##note:x[<<Rev 21:5>>] the face of the earth.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##The glory##note:x[<<Eph 3:21; 2 Tim 4:18>>] of the LORD shall ##endure##note:tn[Heb. be] for ever: the LORD shall ##rejoice##note:x[<<Gen 1:31>>] in his works.
@ He ##looketh##note:x[<<Ps 97:4–5>>] on the earth, and it trembleth: ##he toucheth##note:x[<<Exod 19:18; Ps 144:5>>] the hills, and they smoke.
@ ##I will sing unto the LORD as long as I live: I will sing praise to my God while I have my being.##note:x[<<Ps 63:4; 145:1–2; 146:2>>]
@ My ##meditation##note:x[<<Ps 1:2>>] of him shall be sweet: ##I will be##note:x[<<Hab 3:17–18; Phil 4:4>>] glad in the LORD.
@ Let the ##sinners##note:x[<<Ps 37:38; 68:1–2>>] be consumed out of the earth, and let the wicked be no more. ##Bless##note:x[<<Ps 103:22>>] thou the LORD, O my soul. Praise ye the LORD.

=== @105

[discrete.pericope]
==== Tell of His Wonders

[.parallel]
(<<1 Chronicles 16:7–22>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O give thanks unto the LORD; ##call##note:x[<<Isa 12:4; Rom 10:13>>] upon his name: ##make known##note:x[<<Isa 12:4>>] his deeds among the people.
@ ##Sing unto##note:x[<<Ps 98:5; Isa 12:5–6>>] him, sing psalms unto him: ##talk ye##note:x[<<Ps 119:27>>] of all his wondrous works.
@ ##Glory##note:x[<<1 Cor 1:29; Gal 6:14>>] ye in his holy name: ##let the heart##note:x[<<Lam 3:25>>] of them rejoice that seek the LORD.
@ ##Seek##note:x[<<Amos 5:4–6; Zeph 2:2–3>>] the LORD, and his strength: ##seek his face##note:x[<<Ps 27:8>>] evermore.
@ note:x[<<Ps 119:13>>]##Remember##note:x[<<Deut 32:7; Ps 77:11>>] his marvellous works that he hath done; his wonders, and the judgments of his mouth;
@ O ##ye seed##note:x[<<Isa 44:1–2>>] of Abraham his servant, ye children of Jacob ##his chosen##note:x[<<John 15:16; 1 Pet 2:9>>].
@ He __is__ ##the LORD##note:x[<<Exod 20:2; Deut 26:17–18>>] our God: his ##judgments##note:x[<<Isa 26:9>>] __are__ in all the earth.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##He hath remembered##note:x[<<Ps 111:5; Luke 1:72–74>>] his covenant for ever, the word __which__ he commanded to ##a thousand##note:x[<<Deut 7:9>>] generations.
@ ##Which __covenant__ he made with Abraham, and his oath unto Isaac;##note:x[<<Gen 17:2; 22:16–17; 26:3>>]
@ And confirmed the same unto Jacob for a law, __and__ to Israel __for__ ##an everlasting##note:x[<<Gen 17:7–8; 2 Sam 23:5>>] covenant:
@ Saying, ##Unto thee##note:x[<<Gen 13:15; 15:18>>] will I give the land of Canaan, the note:x[<<Ps 78:55>>]##lot##note:tn[Heb. cord] of your inheritance:
@ When they were __but__ ##a few##note:x[<<Gen 34:30; Deut 7:7>>] men in number; yea, very few, ##and strangers##note:x[<<Heb 11:9>>] in it.
@ When they went from one nation to another, from __one__ kingdom to another people;
@ ##He suffered no man to do them wrong: yea, he reproved kings for their sakes;##note:x[<<Gen 35:5; Exod 7:16–17>>]
@ __Saying__, ##Touch##note:x[<<Gen 26:11; Zech 2:8>>] not mine anointed, ##and do##note:x[<<Gen 20:7>>] my prophets no harm.
@ ##Moreover##note:x[<<Gen 41:54>>] he called for a famine upon the land: he ##brake##note:x[<<Isa 3:1; Ezek 4:16>>] the whole staff of bread.
@ ##He sent##note:x[<<Gen 50:20>>] a man before them, __even__ ##Joseph##note:x[<<Gen 37:36; Acts 7:9>>], __who__ was sold for a servant:
@ ##Whose##note:x[<<Gen 39:20; 40:15; Acts 16:24>>] feet they hurt with fetters: he was laid in iron&#58;note:tn[__he…__: Heb. his soul came into iron]
@ Until the time that ##his word##note:x[<<Gen 41:11–16; Prov 21:1; Acts 7:10>>] came: the word of the LORD tried him.
@ ##The king sent and loosed him; __even__ the ruler of the people, and let him go free.##note:x[<<Gen 41:14>>]
@ He ##made##note:x[<<Gen 41:40–44>>] him lord of his house, and ruler of all his ##substance##note:tn[Heb. possession]:
@ To bind his princes at his pleasure; and teach his senators wisdom.
@ ##Israel##note:x[<<Acts 7:11–15>>] also came into Egypt; and Jacob sojourned in ##the land##note:x[<<Ps 78:51; 106:22>>] of Ham.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Deut 26:5; Acts 7:17>>] increased his people greatly; and made them stronger than their enemies.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##He turned##note:x[<<Gen 15:13; Exod 9:16>>] their heart ##to hate##note:x[<<Acts 7:19>>] his people, to deal subtilly with his servants.
@ He ##sent##note:x[<<Exod 3:10>>] Moses his servant; __and__ Aaron whom he had chosen.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Neh 9:10–11; Ps 78:43–51>>] shewed his signs among them, and wonders in the land of Ham.note:tn[__his…__: Heb. words of his signs]
@ He sent darkness, and made it dark; and they ##rebelled##note:x[<<Ps 99:7>>] not against his word.
@ ##He turned their waters into blood, and slew their fish.##note:x[<<Exod 7:20–21>>]
@ Their land brought forth frogs in abundance, in the chambers of their kings.
@ He spake, and ##there##note:x[<<Exod 8:21–24>>] came divers sorts of flies, __and__ lice in all their coasts.
@ He gave ##them hail for rain##note:x[<<Ps 78:47–48>>], __and__ flaming fire in their land.note:tn[__them…__: Heb. their rain hail]
@ He smote their vines also and their fig trees; and brake the trees of their coasts.
@ He spake, and ##the locusts##note:x[<<Exod 10:12–15; Ps 78:46; Joel 2:25>>] came, and caterpillers, and that without number,
@ And did eat up all the herbs in their land, and devoured the fruit of their ground.
@ He smote also all the firstborn in their land, the chief of all their strength.
@ He ##brought##note:x[<<Exod 3:22; 12:35–36; Acts 13:17>>] them forth also with silver and gold: and __there was__ not one feeble __person__ among their tribes.
@ Egypt was ##glad##note:x[<<Exod 12:33>>] when they departed: ##for##note:x[<<Josh 2:9>>] the fear of them fell upon them.
@ He ##spread##note:x[<<Neh 9:12; Ps 78:14; Isa 4:5>>] a cloud for a covering; and fire to give light in the night.
@ __The people__ ##asked##note:x[<<Ps 78:18>>], and he brought quails, and satisfied them with the bread of heaven.
@ He ##opened##note:x[<<Exod 17:6; Num 20:11; 1 Cor 10:4>>] the rock, and the waters gushed out; they ran in the dry places __like__ a river.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Gen 12:7; Exod 2:24; Luke 1:72–73>>] remembered his holy promise, __and__ Abraham his servant.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Acts 7:36; 13:17>>] brought forth his people ##with joy##note:x[<<Isa 35:10>>], __and__ his chosen with ##gladness##note:tn[Heb. singing]:
@ And ##gave##note:x[<<Josh 24:13; Ps 78:55>>] them the lands of the heathen: and they ##inherited##note:x[<<Deut 6:10–11>>] the labour of the people;
@ ##That##note:x[<<Deut 4:40; Eph 2:8–10; Titus 2:14>>] they might observe his statutes, and keep his laws. Praise ye the LORD.note:tn[__Praise…__: Heb. Hallelujah]

=== @106

[discrete.pericope]
==== Give Thanks to the LORD, for He Is Good

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise ye the LORD. ##O give##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:34; Ps 136:1; 1 Thess 5:18>>] thanks unto the LORD; for __he is__ good: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever.note:tn[__Praise…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ Who can ##utter##note:x[<<Rom 11:33; Eph 1:19>>] the mighty acts of the LORD? __who__ can shew forth all his praise?
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<John 13:17; Jas 1:25>>] __are__ they that keep judgment, __and__ he that ##doeth##note:x[<<Ps 15:2>>] righteousness at all times.
@ ##Remember##note:x[<<Ps 25:7; 119:132; Luke 23:42>>] me, O LORD, with the favour __that thou bearest unto__ thy people: O visit me with thy salvation;
@ That I ##may see##note:x[<<Ps 105:6, 43; John 15:16>>] the good of thy chosen, that I may rejoice in the gladness of thy nation, that I may glory with thine inheritance.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##We have sinned with our fathers, we have committed iniquity, we have done wickedly.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:47; Neh 9:16; Ps 78:8>>]
@ Our fathers understood not thy wonders in Egypt; ##they##note:x[<<Ps 78:42>>] remembered not the multitude of thy mercies; ##but##note:x[<<Exod 14:11–12>>] provoked __him__ at the sea, __even__ at the Red sea.
@ Nevertheless ##he saved##note:x[<<Ezek 20:9, 14>>] them for his name’s sake, ##that he##note:x[<<Exod 9:16>>] might make his mighty power to be known.
@ ##He rebuked##note:x[<<Ps 18:15; 78:13; Nah 1:4>>] the Red sea also, and it was dried up: so he led them through the depths, as through the wilderness.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Exod 14:30>>] saved them from the hand of him that hated __them__, and ##redeemed##note:x[<<Ps 107:2; Mic 6:4>>] them from the hand of the enemy.
@ ##And the waters covered their enemies: there was not one of them left.##note:x[<<Exod 14:13; 15:5; Ps 78:53>>]
@ ##Then believed they his words; they sang his praise.##note:x[<<Exod 14:31>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##They soon forgat##note:x[<<Exod 15:24; 16:2; Ps 78:11>>] his works; they waited not for his counsel&#58;note:tn[__They soon…__: Heb. They made haste, they forgat]
@ ##But##note:x[<<Num 11:4; 1 Cor 10:6>>] lusted exceedingly in the wilderness, and ##tempted##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:9>>] God in the desert.note:tn[__lusted…__: Heb. lusted a lust]
@ And ##he gave##note:x[<<Num 11:31–34; Isa 10:16>>] them their request; but sent leanness into their soul.
@ They ##envied##note:x[<<Num 16:1>>] Moses also in the camp, __and__ Aaron the saint of the LORD.
@ ##The earth opened and swallowed up Dathan, and covered the company of Abiram.##note:x[<<Num 16:29–33; 26:10; Deut 11:6>>]
@ ##And a fire was kindled in their company; the flame burned up the wicked.##note:x[<<Num 16:35–40; Heb 12:29>>]
@ They made a calf in Horeb, and worshipped the molten image.
@ ##Thus##note:x[<<Jer 2:11; Rom 1:22–23>>] they changed their glory ##into##note:x[<<Exod 20:4–5>>] the similitude of an ox that eateth grass.
@ They ##forgat##note:x[<<Ps 78:11–12, 42–51; 106:13>>] God their saviour, which had done great things in Egypt;
@ ##Wondrous##note:x[<<Ps 78:51; 105:23>>] works in the land of Ham, __and__ terrible things by the Red sea.
@ Therefore ##he said##note:x[<<Deut 9:13–14, 25>>] that he would destroy them, had not Moses his chosen ##stood##note:x[<<Ezek 22:30>>] before him in the breach, to turn away his wrath, lest he should destroy __them__.
@ Yea, ##they despised##note:x[<<Num 14:31>>] ##the pleasant land##note:x[<<Ezek 20:6>>], ##they believed##note:x[<<Deut 1:32>>] not his word&#58;note:tn[__the pleasant…__: Heb. a land of desire]
@ But ##murmured##note:x[<<Deut 1:26–27>>] in their tents, __and__ ##hearkened##note:x[<<Num 14:22; Ps 95:7–9>>] not unto the voice of the LORD.
@ ##Therefore##note:x[<<Num 14:28–35; Ps 95:11>>] he ##lifted##note:x[<<Ezek 20:15>>] up his hand against them, to overthrow them in the wilderness&#58;note:tn[__to overthrow__: Heb. to make them fall]
@ To overthrow their seed also among the nations, and ##to scatter##note:x[<<Lev 26:33; Ps 44:11>>] them in the lands.note:tn[__To overthrow__: Heb. To make them fall]
@ They ##joined##note:x[<<Num 25:1–3, 5; Hos 9:10>>] themselves also unto Baal-peor, and ate the sacrifices of the dead.
@ Thus they provoked __him__ to anger with their inventions: and ##the plague##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:8>>] brake in upon them.
@ ##Then stood up Phinehas, and executed judgment: and __so__ the plague was stayed.##note:x[<<Num 25:6–8, 14–15; Josh 7:12>>]
@ ##And that was counted unto him for righteousness unto all generations for evermore.##note:x[<<Num 25:11–13>>]
@ They ##angered##note:x[<<Num 20:2; Ps 81:7>>] __him__ also at the waters of strife, ##so that##note:x[<<Num 20:23–24>>] it went ill with Moses for their sakes:
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Num 20:10–11>>] they provoked his spirit, so that he spake unadvisedly with his lips.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They ##did not##note:x[<<Judg 1:21>>] destroy the nations, ##concerning##note:x[<<Deut 7:2, 16>>] whom the LORD commanded them:
@ ##But##note:x[<<Judg 1:27–36>>] were mingled among the heathen, and ##learned##note:x[<<1 Cor 5:6; 15:33>>] their works.
@ And they served their idols: ##which##note:x[<<Exod 23:33; Deut 7:16; Judg 2:3>>] were a snare unto them.
@ Yea, ##they sacrificed##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:3; 17:17; Jer 7:31>>] their sons and their daughters unto devils,
@ And ##shed##note:x[<<Deut 21:9; 2 Kgs 21:16>>] innocent blood, __even__ the blood of their sons and of their daughters, whom they sacrificed unto the idols of Canaan: and ##the land##note:x[<<Num 35:33>>] was polluted with blood.
@ Thus were they ##defiled##note:x[<<Ezek 20:18>>] with their own works, and ##went##note:x[<<Lev 17:7; Num 15:39>>] a whoring with their own inventions.
@ Therefore was ##the wrath##note:x[<<Judg 2:14>>] of the LORD kindled against his people, insomuch that he abhorred ##his own##note:x[<<Deut 9:29>>] inheritance.
@ And ##he gave##note:x[<<Judg 2:14; Neh 9:27–38>>] them into the hand of the heathen; and they that hated them ruled over them.
@ Their enemies also oppressed them, and they were brought into subjection under their hand.
@ ##Many##note:x[<<Judg 2:16–18>>] times did he deliver them; but they provoked __him__ ##with their##note:x[<<Ps 1:1; 81:12>>] counsel, and were brought low for their iniquity.note:tn[__brought…__: or, impoverished, or, weakened]
@ ##Nevertheless he regarded their affliction, when he heard their cry:##note:x[<<Judg 3:9; 4:3>>]
@ ##And he##note:x[<<2 Kgs 13:23; Ps 105:8>>] remembered for them his covenant, and ##repented##note:x[<<Exod 32:14>>] according to the multitude of his mercies.
@ ##He made them also to be pitied of all those that carried them captives.##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:50; Ezra 9:9; Jer 42:12>>]
@ ##Save us##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:35>>], O LORD our God, and gather us from among the heathen, ##to give##note:x[<<2 Cor 2:14; Rev 7:10–12>>] thanks unto thy holy name, __and__ to triumph in thy praise.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 41:13; 89:52>>] __be__ the LORD God of Israel from everlasting to everlasting: and let all the people say, Amen. Praise ye the LORD.note:tn[__Praise…__: Heb. Hallelujah]

=== @107

[discrete.major]
==== BOOK V

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 107—150>>)

[discrete.pericope]
==== Thanksgiving for Deliverance

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 8:23–27; Mark 4:35–41; Luke 8:22–25>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O ##give##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:34; 2 Chr 7:3; Ps 106:1>>] thanks unto the LORD, for __he is__ good: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever.
@ ##Let the##note:x[<<Gal 3:13; 1 Pet 1:18–19>>] redeemed of the LORD say __so__, whom he hath redeemed ##from##note:x[<<Luke 1:74>>] the hand of the enemy;
@ And ##gathered##note:x[<<Ps 106:47; Isa 43:5–6; Jer 29:14>>] them out of the lands, from the east, and from the west, from the north, and from the ##south##note:tn[Heb. sea].
@ They ##wandered##note:x[<<Deut 32:10; Ezek 34:6, 12>>] in the wilderness in a solitary way; they found no city to dwell in.
@ Hungry ##and##note:x[<<Mark 8:2–3>>] thirsty, their soul fainted in them.
@ ##Then##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; 91:15; Jer 29:12–14>>] they cried unto the LORD in their trouble, __and__ he delivered them out of their distresses.
@ And ##he led##note:x[<<Ezra 8:21–23; Ps 77:20; Jer 31:9>>] them forth by the right way, that they might go to a city of habitation.
@ ##Oh that __men__##note:x[<<Ps 107:31>>] would ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 147:1>>] the LORD __for__ his goodness, and __for__ ##his wonderful##note:x[<<Dan 4:2–3>>] works to the children of men!
@ ##For he satisfieth the longing soul, and filleth the hungry soul with goodness.##note:x[<<Ps 34:10; Jer 31:25; Matt 5:6>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Such ##as sit##note:x[<<Matt 4:16; Luke 1:79>>] in darkness and in the shadow of death, __being__ ##bound##note:x[<<Rom 6:20–21>>] in affliction and iron;
@ ##Because##note:x[<<Ps 106:43>>] they rebelled against the words of God, and ##contemned##note:x[<<Prov 1:25; Rom 1:28>>] the counsel of the most High:
@ Therefore ##he brought##note:x[<<Exod 2:23; 5:18–19>>] down their heart with labour; they fell down, ##and __there__##note:x[<<Ps 22:11>>] __was__ none to help.
@ ##Then they cried unto the LORD in their trouble, __and__ he saved them out of their distresses.##note:x[<<Ps 18:6; 107:6, 28>>]
@ He ##brought##note:x[<<Isa 42:16; Eph 5:8>>] them out of darkness and the shadow of death, and ##brake##note:x[<<Ps 116:16>>] their bands in sunder.
@ ##Oh that __men__ would praise the LORD __for__ his goodness, and __for__ his wonderful works to the children of men!##note:x[<<Ps 107:21>>]
@ ##For he hath broken the gates of brass, and cut the bars of iron in sunder.##note:x[<<Judg 16:3; Isa 45:1–2; Mic 2:13>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Fools ##because##note:x[<<Lam 3:39>>] of their transgression, and because of their iniquities, are afflicted.
@ Their soul ##abhorreth##note:x[<<Job 33:19–22>>] all manner of meat; ##and they##note:x[<<Ps 9:13; 88:3>>] draw near unto the gates of death.
@ ##Then they cry unto the LORD in their trouble, __and__ he saveth them out of their distresses.##note:x[<<Ps 34:4–6; 78:34–35>>]
@ ##He sent##note:x[<<Ps 147:15; Matt 8:8>>] his word, and ##healed##note:x[<<Ps 103:3–4>>] them, and delivered __them__ from their destructions.
@ ##Oh that __men__ would praise the LORD __for__ his goodness, and __for__ his wonderful works to the children of men!##note:x[<<Ps 107:8>>]
@ And let them ##sacrifice##note:x[<<Ps 50:14>>] the sacrifices of thanksgiving, and ##declare##note:x[<<Ps 9:11; 118:17>>] his works with ##rejoicing##note:tn[Heb. singing].

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They that go down to the sea in ships, that do business in great waters;
@ These see the works of the LORD, and his wonders in the deep.
@ For ##he commandeth##note:x[<<Ps 148:8; Jonah 1:4>>], and ##raiseth##note:tn[Heb. maketh to stand] the stormy wind, which ##lifteth##note:x[<<Matt 8:24>>] up the waves thereof.
@ They mount up to the heaven, they go down again to the depths: ##their soul##note:x[<<Ps 22:14; 119:28>>] is melted because of trouble.
@ They reel to and fro, and ##stagger##note:x[<<Job 12:25; Isa 19:14; 29:9>>] like a drunken man, and are at their wits’ end. note:tn[__are…__: Heb. all their wisdom is swallowed up]
@ ##Then they cry unto the LORD in their trouble, and he bringeth them out of their distresses.##note:x[<<Ps 107:13, 19; Matt 8:25>>]
@ ##He maketh the storm a calm, so that the waves thereof are still.##note:x[<<Ps 65:7; Matt 8:26; Luke 8:23–25>>]
@ Then are they glad because they be quiet; so ##he bringeth##note:x[<<John 6:21>>] them unto their desired haven.
@ ##Oh that __men__##note:x[<<Ps 103:2; 107:8>>] would praise the LORD __for__ his goodness, and __for__ his wonderful works to the children of men!
@ Let them ##exalt##note:x[<<Ps 46:10>>] him also ##in the congregation##note:x[<<Ps 22:22, 25>>] of the people, and praise him in the assembly of the elders.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ He ##turneth##note:x[<<Isa 42:15; 50:2>>] rivers into a wilderness, and the watersprings into dry ground;
@ ##A fruitful##note:x[<<Gen 13:10>>] land into note:x[<<Gen 14:3>>]##barrenness##note:tn[Heb. saltiness], for the wickedness of them that dwell therein.
@ He ##turneth##note:x[<<Ps 114:8; Isa 35:6–7; 41:17–19>>] the wilderness into a standing water, and dry ground into watersprings.
@ And ##there he##note:x[<<Luke 1:53>>] maketh the hungry to dwell, that they may prepare ##a city##note:x[<<Ps 107:7; Acts 17:26>>] for habitation;
@ And ##sow##note:x[<<Isa 37:30>>] the fields, and plant vineyards, ##which may##note:x[<<1 Cor 3:7; 2 Cor 9:10>>] yield fruits of increase.
@ ##He blesseth##note:x[<<Gen 12:2; 17:20; Exod 1:7>>] them also, so that they are multiplied greatly; and suffereth not their cattle to decrease.
@ Again, they are minished and brought low through ##oppression##note:x[<<Exod 2:23–24; 2 Kgs 10:32>>], affliction, and sorrow.
@ He ##poureth##note:x[<<Job 12:21, 24>>] contempt upon princes, and ##causeth##note:x[<<Job 12:24>>] them to wander in the ##wilderness##note:tn[or, void place], __where there is__ no way.
@ Yet ##setteth##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:8; Esth 8:15–17>>] he the poor on high ##from##note:tn[or, after] affliction, and ##maketh##note:x[<<Job 21:11>>] __him__ families like a flock.
@ The ##righteous##note:x[<<Job 22:19>>] shall see __it__, and rejoice: and all ##iniquity##note:x[<<Ps 63:11; Rom 3:19>>] shall stop her mouth.
@ Whoso ##__is__ wise##note:x[<<Ps 64:9; Jer 9:12; Hos 14:9>>], and will observe these __things__, even they shall understand the lovingkindness of the LORD.

=== @108

[.psalm-title]
A Song __or__ Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel’s Kingdom Blessing

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 57:1–11; Psalms 60:1–12>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O God, ##my heart##note:x[<<Ps 57:7–11>>] is fixed; ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 104:33>>] sing and give praise, even with ##my glory##note:x[<<Ps 145:21>>].
@ ##Awake##note:x[<<Ps 33:2; 69:30; 81:2>>], psaltery and harp: I __myself__ will awake early.
@ I will ##praise##note:x[<<Ps 22:22, 27; 117:1>>] thee, O LORD, among the people: and I will sing praises unto thee among the nations.
@ For ##thy mercy##note:x[<<Ps 36:5; 103:11; Eph 2:4–7>>] __is__ great above the heavens: and thy truth __reacheth__ unto the ##clouds##note:tn[or, skies].
@ ##Be thou##note:x[<<Ps 57:5; 148:13>>] exalted, O God, above the heavens: and ##thy glory##note:x[<<Matt 6:9–10>>] above all the earth;

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##That thy##note:x[<<Deut 33:12; Ps 60:5–12>>] beloved may be delivered: save __with__ thy right hand, ##and answer me##note:x[<<2 Chr 32:20–22>>].
@ God hath spoken in his holiness; ##I will rejoice##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:8>>], I will divide Shechem, and mete out the valley of Succoth.
@ Gilead __is__ mine; Manasseh __is__ mine; Ephraim also __is__ the strength of mine head; Judah __is__ my lawgiver;
@ Moab __is__ my washpot; over Edom will I cast out my shoe; over Philistia will I triumph.
@ Who will bring me into the strong city? ##who will lead##note:x[<<Isa 63:1–6>>] me into Edom?
@ __Wilt__ not __thou__, O God, ##__who__ hast##note:x[<<Ps 44:9>>] cast us off? and wilt not thou, O God, ##go forth##note:x[<<2 Chr 13:12>>] with our hosts?
@ Give us help from trouble: ##for vain##note:x[<<Ps 146:3–5; Isa 2:22; Jer 17:5–8>>] __is__ the help of man.
@ ##Through##note:x[<<Ps 18:29–34; 118:6–13>>] God we shall do valiantly: for he __it is that__ shall ##tread##note:x[<<Ps 60:12>>] down our enemies.

=== @109

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Song of the Slandered

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Hold##note:x[<<Ps 28:1; 83:1>>] not thy peace, ##O God##note:x[<<Deut 10:21>>] of my praise;
@ For ##the mouth##note:x[<<Ps 31:13, 18>>] of the wicked and the mouth ##of the deceitful##note:x[<<Prov 6:17>>] are opened against me: they have spoken against me with a lying tongue.note:tn[__of the deceitful__: Heb. of deceit]note:tn[__are opened__: Heb. have opened themselves]
@ They compassed me about also with words of hatred; and ##fought##note:x[<<Ps 35:7; 69:4>>] against me without a cause.
@ ##For my##note:x[<<Ps 35:7; 38:20>>] love they are my adversaries: ##but I##note:x[<<Ps 69:12–13>>] __give myself unto__ prayer.
@ And ##they##note:x[<<Prov 17:13>>] have rewarded me evil for good, and hatred for my love.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Set thou##note:x[<<Matt 27:4>>] a wicked man over him: ##and let##note:x[<<Zech 3:1>>] note:x[<<Matt 5:25>>]##Satan##note:tn[or, an adversary] stand at his right hand.
@ When he shall be judged, let him ##be condemned##note:x[<<Gal 3:10>>]: ##and let##note:x[<<Prov 21:27; 28:9>>] his prayer become sin.note:tn[__be condemned__: Heb. go out guilty, or, wicked]
@ Let ##his days##note:x[<<Ps 55:23>>] be few; __and__ let ##another##note:x[<<Acts 1:16–26>>] take his ##office##note:tn[or, charge].
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Exod 22:24; Jer 18:21>>] his children be fatherless, and his wife a widow.
@ ##Let his children be continually vagabonds, and beg: let them seek __their bread__ also out of their desolate places.##note:x[<<Gen 4:12–14; Ps 37:25>>]
@ Let the ##extortioner##note:x[<<Job 5:5>>] catch all that he hath; and let the strangers spoil his labour.
@ Let there be none to extend mercy unto him: neither let there be any to favour his fatherless children.
@ ##Let his##note:x[<<Job 18:19; Ps 37:28>>] posterity be cut off; __and__ in the generation following let ##their name##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>] be blotted out.
@ ##Let the##note:x[<<Exod 20:5>>] iniquity of his fathers be remembered with the LORD; and let not the sin of his mother be ##blotted##note:x[<<Neh 4:5; Jer 18:23>>] out.
@ Let them be ##before##note:x[<<Ps 90:8>>] the LORD continually, that he may ##cut off##note:x[<<Job 18:17; Ps 34:16>>] the memory of them from the earth.
@ Because that he remembered not to shew mercy, but persecuted the poor and needy man, that he might even ##slay##note:x[<<Ps 34:18>>] the broken in heart.
@ ##As he loved cursing, so let it come unto him: as he delighted not in blessing, so let it be far from him.##note:x[<<Ps 52:4–5; Prov 14:14; Matt 7:2>>]
@ ##As he##note:x[<<Ps 73:6>>] clothed himself with cursing like as with his garment, ##so let##note:x[<<Num 5:22>>] it come into his bowels like water, and like oil into his bones.note:tn[__into his bowels__: Heb. within him]
@ Let it be unto him ##as the garment##note:x[<<Ps 35:26; 109:29; 132:18>>] __which__ covereth him, and for a girdle wherewith he is girded continually.
@ __Let__ this __be__ the reward of mine adversaries from the LORD, and of them that speak evil against my soul.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##But do##note:x[<<Ps 25:11; 31:3; 79:9–10>>] thou for me, O GOD the Lord, for thy name’s sake: because thy mercy __is__ good, deliver thou me.
@ ##For I##note:x[<<Ps 40:17; 86:1>>] __am__ poor and needy, ##and my##note:x[<<Ps 102:4>>] heart is wounded within me.
@ I am gone like the shadow when it declineth: ##I am tossed##note:x[<<Exod 10:19>>] up and down as the locust.
@ My ##knees##note:x[<<Ps 22:14; Matt 4:2; Heb 12:12>>] are weak through fasting; and my flesh faileth of fatness.
@ I became also ##a reproach##note:x[<<Ps 69:19–20; Rom 15:3>>] unto them: ##__when__ they##note:x[<<Ps 22:6–7>>] looked upon me they shaked their heads.
@ ##Help##note:x[<<Ps 119:86; Heb 5:7>>] me, O LORD my God: O ##save me##note:x[<<Ps 57:1>>] according to thy mercy:
@ ##That they may know that this __is__ thy hand; __that__ thou, LORD, hast done it.##note:x[<<Exod 8:19; 1 Kgs 18:36–37; Job 37:7>>]
@ ##Let them##note:x[<<Num 23:20; Ps 109:17>>] curse, but bless thou: when they arise, let them be ashamed; ##but let##note:x[<<John 16:22>>] thy servant rejoice.
@ Let mine adversaries ##be clothed##note:x[<<Ps 35:26; 132:18; Jer 20:11>>] with shame, and let them cover themselves with their own confusion, as with a mantle.
@ I will ##greatly##note:x[<<Ps 22:22>>] praise the LORD with my mouth; yea, ##I will praise##note:x[<<Ps 35:18; 111:1>>] him among the multitude.
@ ##For he##note:x[<<Ps 16:8; 73:23; 121:5>>] shall stand at the right hand of the poor, to save __him__ from those that condemn his soul.note:tn[__those…__: Heb. the judges of]

=== @110

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Faithful Messiah

[.parallel]
(<<Genesis 14:17–24; Hebrews 5:1–10>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD said unto my Lord, Sit thou at my right hand, ##until##note:x[<<1 Cor 15:25; Heb 1:13; 10:12–13>>] I make thine enemies thy footstool.
@ The LORD shall send ##the rod##note:x[<<2 Cor 10:4–5>>] of thy strength out of Zion: rule thou in the midst of thine enemies.
@ ##Thy##note:x[<<Judg 5:2; Heb 13:21>>] people __shall be__ willing in the day of thy power, in the ##beauties##note:x[<<Titus 2:14>>] of holiness from the womb of the morning: thou hast the dew of thy youth.note:tn[__from…__: or, more than the womb of the morning: thou shalt have, etc]
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<Heb 5:6>>] hath sworn, and will not repent, ##Thou##note:x[<<Gen 14:18; Heb 7:17>>] __art__ a priest for ever after the order of Melchizedek.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The Lord ##at thy##note:x[<<Ps 16:8>>] right hand shall ##strike##note:x[<<Rev 17:12–14>>] through kings ##in the day##note:x[<<Rom 2:5>>] of his wrath.
@ He shall ##judge##note:x[<<Isa 2:4; Mic 4:3>>] among the heathen, he shall fill __the places__ with the dead bodies; he shall ##wound##note:x[<<Ps 68:21>>] the heads over ##many##note:tn[or, great] countries.
@ ##He shall##note:x[<<Judg 7:5–6>>] drink of the brook in the way: ##therefore##note:x[<<Heb 2:9–10>>] shall he ##lift##note:x[<<Ps 27:6>>] up the head.

=== @111

[discrete.pericope]
==== Majestic Is His Work

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Praise ye the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 106:1>>]. ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 138:1>>] praise the LORD with __my__ whole heart, in the ##assembly##note:x[<<Ps 149:1>>] of the upright, and __in__ the congregation.note:tn[__Praise ye…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ The ##works##note:x[<<Ps 104:24>>] of the LORD __are__ great, ##sought##note:x[<<Ps 104:24>>] out of all them ##that have##note:x[<<Prov 24:14>>] pleasure therein.
@ His work __is__ ##honourable##note:x[<<Ps 145:4–5, 10–12; Eph 3:10>>] and glorious: and his righteousness endureth for ever.
@ He hath made his wonderful works to be remembered: the LORD __is__ ##gracious##note:x[<<Ps 86:5; 103:8; Isa 63:7>>] and full of compassion.
@ He hath given ##meat##note:tn[Heb. prey] unto them that fear him: ##he will##note:x[<<Ps 105:8; Luke 1:72>>] ever be mindful of his covenant.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ He hath ##shewed##note:x[<<Josh 10:13–14>>] his people the power of his works, ##that he##note:x[<<Ps 2:8; 80:8>>] may give them the heritage of the heathen.
@ The ##works##note:x[<<Deut 32:4; Ps 89:14>>] of his hands __are__ verity and judgment; ##all his##note:x[<<Ps 19:7>>] commandments __are__ sure.
@ ##They##note:x[<<Matt 5:18>>] stand fast for ever and ever, __and__ ##__are__ done##note:x[<<Ps 19:9>>] in truth and uprightness.note:tn[__stand…__: Heb. are established]
@ He ##sent##note:x[<<Luke 1:68; Titus 2:14>>] redemption unto his people: he hath commanded his covenant for ever: ##holy##note:x[<<Ps 99:3>>] and reverend __is__ his name.
@ The ##fear##note:x[<<Job 28:28; Prov 1:7; 9:10>>] of the LORD __is__ the beginning of wisdom: a good understanding have all they that do ##__his commandments__##note:tn[Heb. them]__:__ his praise endureth for ever.note:tn[__a good…__: or, good success]

=== @112

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Blessed Fear of the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 128:1–6>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise ye the LORD. ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 128:1; Isa 50:10; Luke 1:50>>] __is__ the man __that__ feareth the LORD, __that__ delighteth greatly in his commandments.note:tn[__Praise…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ ##His seed shall be mighty upon earth: the generation of the upright shall be blessed.##note:x[<<Gen 17:7; Ps 37:26; Prov 20:7>>]
@ ##Wealth##note:x[<<Prov 3:16; 15:6; Matt 6:33>>] and riches __shall be__ in his house: and his righteousness endureth for ever.
@ Unto the upright ##there ariseth##note:x[<<Job 11:17; Ps 97:11>>] light in the darkness: ##__he is__ gracious##note:x[<<Col 3:12–13>>], and full of compassion, and righteous.
@ A ##good##note:x[<<Prov 2:20>>] man ##sheweth##note:x[<<Ps 37:25–26>>] favour, and lendeth: ##he will##note:x[<<Eph 5:15>>] guide his affairs with ##discretion##note:tn[Heb. judgment].

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Ps 62:2; 125:1>>] he shall not be moved for ever: ##the righteous##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>] shall be in everlasting remembrance.
@ He ##shall not##note:x[<<Prov 1:33>>] be afraid of evil tidings: his ##heart##note:x[<<Ps 57:7; Isa 26:3–4>>] is fixed, trusting in the LORD.
@ His ##heart##note:x[<<Ps 31:24>>] __is__ established, he shall not be afraid, ##until he##note:x[<<Ps 59:10; 91:8>>] see __his desire__ upon his enemies.
@ ##He##note:x[<<2 Cor 9:9>>] hath ##dispersed##note:x[<<Heb 13:16>>], he hath given to the poor; his righteousness endureth for ever; his ##horn##note:x[<<Ps 75:10>>] shall be exalted with honour.
@ The ##wicked##note:x[<<Luke 13:28>>] shall see __it__, and be grieved; he shall gnash with his teeth, and ##melt##note:x[<<Ps 58:7–8>>] away: the ##desire##note:x[<<Prov 10:28>>] of the wicked shall perish.

=== @113

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Exalts the Humble

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 1:1–8>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise ye the LORD. ##Praise, O##note:x[<<Ps 135:1–3, 20; Eph 5:19–20>>] ye servants of the LORD, praise the name of the LORD.note:tn[__Praise ye…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ ##Blessed be the name of the LORD from this time forth and for evermore.##note:x[<<Dan 2:20>>]
@ note:x[<<Ps 86:9; Isa 59:19; Mal 1:11>>]From the rising of the sun unto the going down of the same the LORD’s name __is__ to be praised.
@ The LORD __is__ ##high##note:x[<<Ps 97:9; 99:2>>] above all nations, __and__ ##his glory##note:x[<<Ps 8:1>>] above the heavens.
@ Who __is__ ##like##note:x[<<Ps 89:6; Isa 40:25; Jer 10:6>>] unto the LORD our God, who dwelleth on high,note:tn[__dwelleth…__: Heb. exalteth himself to dwell]
@ Who ##humbleth##note:x[<<Ps 11:4>>] __himself__ to behold __the things that are__ in heaven, and ##in the earth##note:x[<<Ps 138:6; Isa 57:15>>]!
@ He ##raiseth##note:x[<<Ps 107:41; Luke 1:52–53>>] up the poor out of the dust, __and__ lifteth the ##needy##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:7–8>>] out of the dunghill;
@ ##That he may set __him__ with princes, __even__ with the princes of his people.##note:x[<<Gen 41:41; Ps 45:16; Phil 2:8–11>>]
@ He ##maketh##note:x[<<Gen 21:5–7; 30:22–23; Ps 68:6>>] the barren woman to keep house, __and to be__ a joyful mother of children. Praise ye the LORD.note:tn[__to keep…__: Heb. to dwell in an house]

=== @114

==== A Psalm of Exodus

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ When ##Israel##note:x[<<Exod 12:41–42; 13:3>>] went out of Egypt, the house of Jacob from ##a people##note:x[<<Ps 81:5>>] of strange language;
@ ##Judah was his sanctuary, __and__ Israel his dominion.##note:x[<<Exod 25:8>>]
@ The ##sea##note:x[<<Exod 14:21; Ps 77:16; Hab 3:8>>] saw __it__, and fled: Jordan was driven back.
@ ##The mountains skipped like rams, __and__ the little hills like lambs.##note:x[<<Exod 19:18; 20:18; Judg 5:4–5>>]
@ ##What __ailed__ thee, O thou sea, that thou fleddest? thou Jordan, __that__ thou wast driven back?##note:x[<<Hab 3:8>>]
@ Ye mountains, __that__ ye skipped like rams; __and__ ye little hills, like lambs?
@ ##Tremble##note:x[<<Job 26:11; Ps 104:32; Jer 5:22>>], thou earth, at the presence of the Lord, at the presence of the God of Jacob;
@ ##Which turned the rock __into__ a standing water, the flint into a fountain of waters.##note:x[<<Num 20:11; Deut 8:15; Ps 107:35>>]

=== @115

==== To Your Name Be the Glory

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 135:1–21>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Not ##unto us##note:x[<<Ps 79:9–10; Isa 48:11; Ezek 20:14>>], O LORD, not unto us, but unto thy name give glory, for thy mercy, __and__ for thy truth’s sake.
@ ##Wherefore should the heathen say, Where __is__ now their God?##note:x[<<Ps 42:3, 10; 79:10>>]
@ But our God __is__ in the heavens: ##he hath##note:x[<<Ps 135:6; Isa 46:10; Dan 4:35>>] done whatsoever he hath pleased.
@ ##Their idols##note:x[<<Deut 4:28; Isa 46:6–7; Jer 10:3–5>>] __are__ silver and gold, the work of men’s hands.
@ They have mouths, but they speak not: eyes have they, but they see not:
@ They have ears, but they hear not: noses have they, but they smell not:
@ They have hands, but they handle not: feet have they, but they walk not: neither speak they through their throat.
@ ##They that make them are like unto them; __so is__ every one that trusteth in them.##note:x[<<Ps 135:18; Jer 10:8; Hab 2:18–19>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O ##Israel##note:x[<<Ps 118:2–4>>], ##trust##note:x[<<Ps 62:8>>] thou in the LORD: he __is__ ##their help##note:x[<<Prov 30:5>>] and their shield.

@ ##Ye that fear the LORD, trust in the LORD: he __is__ their help and their shield.##note:x[<<Ps 147:11; Prov 14:26; Acts 10:35>>]
@ The LORD ##hath##note:x[<<Ps 136:23; Isa 44:21>>] been mindful of us: he will bless __us;__ he will bless ##the house of Israel##note:x[<<Eph 1:3>>]; he will bless the house of Aaron.
@ ##He will bless##note:x[<<Ps 112:1; 128:1; Luke 1:50>>] them that fear the LORD, __both__ small ##and##note:tn[Heb. with] great.
@ The ##LORD##note:x[<<Jer 33:22>>] shall increase ##you##note:x[<<Acts 2:39; 3:25>>] more and more, you and your children.
@ Ye __are__ ##blessed##note:x[<<Gen 14:19>>] of the LORD which ##made##note:x[<<Gen 1:1; Ps 96:5>>] heaven and earth.
@ The ##heaven##note:x[<<Ps 89:11>>], __even__ the heavens, __are__ the LORD’s: ##but the earth##note:x[<<Gen 1:28–30; Jer 27:5–6>>] hath he given to the children of men.
@ The ##dead##note:x[<<Ps 6:5; 88:10–12; Isa 38:18–19>>] praise not the LORD, neither any that go down into silence.
@ ##But we will bless the LORD from this time forth and for evermore. Praise the LORD.##note:x[<<Ps 113:2; 145:21; Dan 2:20>>]

==== The LORD Has Heard My Voice

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I ##love##note:x[<<1 John 4:19; 5:2–3>>] the LORD, ##because##note:x[<<Ps 66:19–20>>] he hath heard my voice __and__ my supplications.

@ The ##sorrows##note:x[<<Ps 18:4–6; 88:6–7>>] of death compassed me, and the pains of hell gat hold upon me: ##I found##note:x[<<Ps 38:6>>] trouble and sorrow.note:tn[__gat…__: Heb. found me]
@ Then ##called##note:x[<<Ps 118:5>>] I upon the name of the LORD; ##O LORD##note:x[<<Ps 22:20; 143:6–9>>], I beseech thee, deliver my soul.
@ ##Gracious##note:x[<<Ps 103:8>>] __is__ the LORD, ##and righteous##note:x[<<Ps 119:137; 145:17>>]; yea, our God __is__ merciful.
@ The LORD ##preserveth##note:x[<<Ps 19:7; Matt 11:25>>] the simple: ##I was##note:x[<<Ps 142:6>>] brought low, and he helped me.
@ Return unto ##thy rest##note:x[<<Jer 6:16; Matt 11:28–29>>], O my soul; for the LORD hath ##dealt##note:x[<<Ps 13:6>>] bountifully with thee.
@ ##For thou##note:x[<<Ps 56:13; 86:13>>] hast delivered my soul from death, ##mine##note:x[<<Rev 21:4>>] eyes from tears, __and__ my feet from falling.
@ I will ##walk##note:x[<<Luke 1:75>>] before the LORD ##in the land##note:x[<<Ps 27:13>>] of the living.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I believed##note:x[<<2 Cor 4:13; Heb 11:1>>], ##therefore##note:x[<<Prov 21:28>>] have I spoken: I was greatly afflicted:

@ ##I will take##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:21>>] the cup of salvation, and ##call##note:x[<<Ps 105:1; 116:2>>] upon the name of the LORD.
@ I will ##pay my vows##note:x[<<Ps 22:25; 116:18; Jonah 2:9>>] unto the LORD now in the presence of all his people.
@ ##Precious##note:x[<<1 Sam 25:29; Ps 37:32–33; 72:14>>] in the sight of the LORD __is__ the death of his saints.
@ O LORD, ##truly##note:x[<<Ps 86:16; 143:12>>] I __am__ thy servant; I __am__ thy servant, __and__ ##the son##note:x[<<Ps 86:16>>] of thine handmaid: thou hast loosed my bonds.
@ I will offer to thee ##the sacrifice##note:x[<<Lev 7:12; Ps 50:14; 107:22>>] of thanksgiving, and will call upon the name of the LORD.
