=== @118

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is on My Side

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##O give thanks unto the LORD; for __he is__ good: because his mercy __endureth__ for ever.##note:x[<<1 Chr 16:34; Ps 106:1; 107:1>>]
@ ##Let Israel now say, that his mercy __endureth__ for ever.##note:x[<<Ps 115:9–11>>]
@ Let the house of Aaron now say, that his mercy __endureth__ for ever.
@ ##Let them now that fear the LORD say, that his mercy __endureth__ for ever.##note:x[<<Ps 22:23; Rev 19:5>>]
@ I ##called##note:x[<<Ps 18:6; 120:1>>] upon the LORD in distress: the LORD answered me, __and__ ##__set me__##note:x[<<Ps 18:19>>] in a large place.note:tn[__in distress__: Heb. out of distress]
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Ps 56:4; Rom 8:31; Heb 13:6>>] __is__ on my side; I will not fear: what can man do unto me?note:tn[__on…__: Heb. for me]
@ The LORD ##taketh##note:x[<<Ps 54:4>>] my part with them that help me: ##therefore##note:x[<<Ps 54:7; 59:10>>] shall I see __my desire__ upon them that hate me.
@ ##__It is__ better to trust in the LORD than to put confidence in man.##note:x[<<Ps 40:4; 62:8–9; Jer 17:5–7>>]
@ __It is__ better to trust in the LORD ##than to put##note:x[<<Ps 146:3–5; Isa 31:1; Ezek 29:7>>] confidence in princes.
@ All nations compassed me about: but in the name of the LORD will I destroy them.note:tn[__destroy…__: Heb. cut them off]
@ ##They compassed me about; yea, they compassed me about: but in the name of the LORD I will destroy them.##note:x[<<1 Sam 23:26; Ps 22:12–16; 88:17>>]
@ They compassed me about ##like bees##note:x[<<Deut 1:44>>]; they are quenched as the fire of thorns: for ##in the name##note:x[<<1 Sam 17:45; Ps 20:5>>] of the LORD I will ##destroy##note:tn[Heb. cut down] them.
@ ##Thou hast thrust sore at me that I might fall: but the LORD helped me.##note:x[<<Ps 18:17–18; Mic 7:8; Matt 4:1–11>>]
@ The LORD ##__is__ my strength##note:x[<<Exod 15:2–6; Ps 18:2; Isa 12:2>>] and song, and is become my salvation.
@ The ##voice##note:x[<<Acts 2:46–47>>] of rejoicing and salvation __is__ in the tabernacles of the righteous: ##the right##note:x[<<Ps 60:12; 89:13>>] hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.
@ The ##right hand##note:x[<<Exod 15:6>>] of the LORD is exalted: the right hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.
@ I shall not ##die##note:x[<<Isa 38:16–20; John 11:4>>], but live, and ##declare##note:x[<<Ps 71:17–18>>] the works of the LORD.
@ The LORD hath ##chastened##note:x[<<Job 5:17–18; 1 Cor 11:32; 2 Cor 6:9>>] me sore: but he hath not given me over unto death.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Open##note:x[<<Isa 26:2; Rev 22:14>>] to me the gates of righteousness: ##I will go##note:x[<<Ps 9:13–14>>] into them, __and__ I will praise the LORD:
@ ##This gate##note:x[<<Ps 24:7; Isa 26:2; Rev 21:24–27>>] of the LORD, into which the righteous shall enter.
@ ##I will praise thee: for thou hast heard me,##note:x[<<Ps 116:1>>] ##and art##note:x[<<Exod 15:2; Ps 118:14>>] become my salvation.
@ ##The stone##note:x[<<Matt 21:42; Luke 20:17; 1 Pet 2:4–8>>] __which__ the builders refused is become the head __stone__ of the corner.
@ note:x[<<Eph 1:19–22>>]This is the LORD’s doing; ##it __is__##note:x[<<Job 5:9; Acts 13:41>>] marvellous in our eyes.note:tn[__the LORD’s…__: Heb. from the LORD]
@ This __is__ the day __which__ the LORD hath made; ##we will##note:x[<<Neh 8:10; Ps 84:10; Isa 58:13>>] rejoice and be glad in it.
@ ##Save##note:x[<<Ps 20:9; 69:13>>] now, I beseech thee, O LORD: O LORD, I beseech thee, ##send now##note:x[<<Ps 90:17>>] prosperity.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Matt 21:9; 23:39; Luke 19:38>>] __be__ he that cometh in the name of the LORD: we have blessed you out of the house of the LORD.
@ God __is__ the LORD, which hath ##shewed##note:x[<<Ps 18:28; John 8:12; 1 Pet 2:9>>] us light: bind the sacrifice with cords, __even__ unto the horns of the altar.
@ Thou __art__ ##my God##note:x[<<Exod 15:2; Ps 146:2; Isa 25:1>>], and I will praise thee: __thou art__ my God, I will exalt thee.
@ ##O give thanks unto the LORD; for __he is__ good: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever.##note:x[<<Ezra 3:11; Ps 103:17; 118:1>>]

=== @119

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __א ALEPH.__

[discrete.pericope]
==== Your Word Is a Lamp to My Feet

[discrete.subsection]
===== ALEPH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Luke 11:28; Jas 1:25>>] __are__ the ##undefiled##note:tn[or, perfect, or, sincere] in the way, who ##walk##note:x[<<Ezek 11:20>>] in the law of the LORD.
@ Blessed __are__ they that keep his testimonies, __and that__ ##seek##note:x[<<Deut 4:29; Ps 119:10; Jer 29:13>>] him with the whole heart.
@ ##They also do no iniquity: they walk in his ways.##note:x[<<1 John 3:9; 5:18>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Thou hast commanded __us__ to keep thy precepts diligently.##note:x[<<Matt 28:20; John 14:21; 1 John 5:3>>]
@ ##O that my ways were directed to keep thy statutes!##note:x[<<Ps 51:10; 2 Thess 3:5; Heb 13:21>>]
@ Then ##shall I##note:x[<<Job 22:26; Ps 119:80; 1 John 2:28>>] not be ashamed, when I have respect unto all thy commandments.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 119:171>>] praise thee with uprightness of heart, ##when##note:x[<<Ps 119:12; John 6:45>>] I shall have learned thy righteous judgments.note:tn[__thy…__: Heb. judgments of thy righteousness]
@ I will keep thy statutes: ##O forsake##note:x[<<Ps 38:21–22; 51:11; Phil 4:13>>] me not utterly.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ב BETH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== BETH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Wherewithal ##shall##note:x[<<2 Tim 2:22>>] a young man cleanse his way? ##by taking##note:x[<<2 Tim 3:15–17; Jas 1:21–25>>] heed __thereto__ according to thy word.
@ With ##my whole##note:x[<<2 Chr 15:15; Ps 119:2, 34>>] heart have I sought thee: O let me not wander from thy commandments.
@ ##Thy word##note:x[<<Ps 37:31; 40:8; Col 3:16>>] have I hid in mine heart, that I might not sin against thee.
@ Blessed __art__ thou, O LORD: ##teach##note:x[<<Ps 119:64; Luke 24:45; 1 John 2:27>>] me thy statutes.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ With my lips have ##I declared##note:x[<<Ps 37:30; 40:9–10; 71:15–18>>] all the judgments of thy mouth.
@ I have ##rejoiced##note:x[<<Ps 119:162; Jer 15:16; Matt 13:44>>] in the way of thy testimonies, as __much as__ in all riches.
@ I will ##meditate##note:x[<<Ps 1:2; 119:148; Jas 1:25>>] in thy precepts, and have respect unto thy ways.
@ I will ##delight##note:x[<<Ps 119:24, 47>>] myself in thy statutes: I will ##not forget##note:x[<<Prov 3:1>>] thy word.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ג GIMEL.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== GIMEL

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Deal##note:x[<<Ps 13:6>>] bountifully with thy servant, __that__ ##I may live##note:x[<<Eph 2:4–5; Titus 2:11–12>>], and keep thy word.
@ note:x[<<Isa 32:3; Acts 26:18; Eph 1:17–18>>]##Open##note:tn[Heb. Reveal] thou mine eyes, that I may behold wondrous things out of thy law.
@ I __am__ ##a stranger##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:15; Ps 39:12; Heb 11:13–16>>] in the earth: hide not thy commandments from me.
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 42:1; 63:1; 84:2>>] breaketh for the longing __that it hath__ unto thy judgments at all times.
@ Thou hast ##rebuked##note:x[<<1 Pet 5:5>>] the proud __that are__ ##cursed##note:x[<<Ps 119:10, 118>>], which do err from thy commandments.
@ ##Remove##note:x[<<Ps 39:8; 119:39>>] from me reproach and contempt; ##for I have##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:20>>] kept thy testimonies.
@ ##Princes##note:x[<<Ps 2:1–2>>] also did sit __and__ speak against me: __but__ thy servant did meditate in thy statutes.
@ Thy ##testimonies##note:x[<<Ps 119:92>>] also __are__ my delight __and__ ##my counsellors##note:x[<<Col 3:16; 2 Tim 3:15–17>>].note:tn[__my counsellors__: Heb. men of my counsel]

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ד DALETH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== DALETH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Col 3:2>>] cleaveth unto the dust: ##quicken##note:x[<<Ps 119:159>>] thou me ##according##note:x[<<Deut 30:6>>] to thy word.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I have declared my ways, and thou heardest me: ##teach##note:x[<<Ps 25:4; 27:11; 86:11>>] me thy statutes.
@ Make me to understand the way of thy precepts: ##so shall I talk##note:x[<<Ps 71:17; 105:2; 145:5–6>>] of thy wondrous works.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 22:14>>] ##melteth##note:tn[Heb. droppeth] for heaviness: ##strengthen##note:x[<<Ps 27:14; Phil 4:13>>] thou me according unto thy word.
@ ##Remove##note:x[<<Prov 30:8; 1 John 1:8; 2:4>>] from me the way of lying: and grant me thy law graciously.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I have ##chosen##note:x[<<Ps 119:29; Prov 1:29; John 3:19–21>>] the way of truth: thy judgments have I laid __before me__.
@ I have ##stuck##note:x[<<Prov 23:23; John 8:31>>] unto thy testimonies: O LORD, ##put me##note:x[<<Rom 5:5>>] not to shame.
@ I will ##run##note:x[<<Heb 12:1>>] the way of thy commandments, when thou shalt ##enlarge##note:x[<<1 Kgs 4:29; 2 Cor 3:17>>] my heart.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ה HE.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== HE

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Teach##note:x[<<Ps 119:26–27; Isa 54:13>>] me, O LORD, the way of thy statutes; and ##I shall keep##note:x[<<1 John 2:27>>] it __unto__ the end.
@ ##Give me##note:x[<<Prov 2:5–6; Jas 1:5>>] understanding, and ##I shall##note:x[<<Matt 7:24>>] keep thy law; yea, I shall observe it with __my__ whole heart.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Make me##note:x[<<Ezek 36:26–27>>] to go in ##the path##note:x[<<Ps 23:3; Prov 4:11>>] of thy commandments; for therein do I delight.
@ Incline my heart unto thy testimonies, ##and not to##note:x[<<Mark 7:21–22; Luke 12:15; Heb 13:5>>] covetousness.
@ ##Turn##note:x[<<Prov 4:25; Isa 33:15; 1 John 2:16>>] away mine eyes from beholding vanity; __and__ quicken thou me in thy way.note:tn[__Turn…__: Heb. Make to pass]
@ ##Stablish##note:x[<<2 Cor 1:20>>] thy word unto thy servant, ##who __is devoted__##note:x[<<Ps 147:11; Jer 32:39–41>>] to thy fear.
@ ##Turn##note:x[<<Ps 119:22; 1 Tim 3:7; Titus 2:8>>] away my reproach which I fear: for thy judgments __are__ good.
@ Behold, ##I have##note:x[<<Ps 119:20; 2 Cor 7:1>>] longed after thy precepts: ##quicken##note:x[<<John 10:10>>] me in thy righteousness.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ו VAU.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== WAW

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 69:16; 119:58, 76–77>>]Let thy mercies come also unto me, O LORD, __even__ thy salvation, according to thy word.
@ ##So shall##note:x[<<Matt 27:63>>] I ##have wherewith##note:x[<<2 Sam 16:7–8>>] to answer him that reproacheth me: ##for I trust##note:x[<<Acts 27:25>>] in thy word.note:tn[__So…__: or, So shall I answer him that reproveth me in a thing]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ And ##take not##note:x[<<Eph 1:13>>] the word of truth utterly out of my mouth; ##for I have##note:x[<<Ps 9:4; 1 Pet 2:23>>] hoped in thy judgments.
@ So shall I ##keep##note:x[<<Ps 119:33–34; Rev 7:15; 22:11>>] thy law continually for ever and ever.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##And I will##note:x[<<Ps 119:133; Jas 1:25>>] walk at liberty: ##for I seek##note:x[<<Ps 119:94>>] thy precepts.note:tn[__at liberty__: Heb. at large]
@ I will ##speak##note:x[<<Matt 10:18–19; Acts 26:1–2>>] of thy testimonies also before kings, and ##will not##note:x[<<Rom 1:16>>] be ashamed.
@ And I will delight myself in thy commandments, ##which##note:x[<<Ps 119:48, 97, 127>>] I have loved.
@ My hands also will I lift up ##unto thy##note:x[<<Matt 7:21>>] commandments, which I have loved; ##and I will##note:x[<<Ps 1:2; 119:15>>] meditate in thy statutes.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ז ZAIN.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== ZAYIN

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Remember##note:x[<<Ps 106:4, 45>>] the word unto thy servant, ##upon which##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:25>>] thou hast caused me to hope.
@ ##This##note:x[<<Ps 94:19; Rom 15:4>>] __is__ my comfort in my affliction: ##for thy##note:x[<<John 6:63>>] word hath quickened me.
@ The ##proud##note:x[<<Jer 20:7>>] have had me greatly in derision: ##__yet__ have##note:x[<<Job 23:11; Ps 119:157>>] I not declined from thy law.
@ I ##remembered##note:x[<<Ps 77:11–12; 105:5>>] thy judgments of old, O LORD; and have comforted myself.
@ ##Horror##note:x[<<Ezra 9:3; 10:6; Ps 119:158>>] hath taken hold upon me because of the wicked that forsake thy law.
@ ##Thy statutes have been my songs in the house of my pilgrimage.##note:x[<<Gen 47:9; Heb 11:13–16>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I have remembered thy name, O LORD, in the ##night##note:x[<<Ps 42:8; 63:6; Isa 26:9>>], and have kept thy law.
@ This I had, ##because##note:x[<<Ps 119:165; 1 John 3:19–24>>] I kept thy precepts.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ח CHETH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== HETH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ __Thou art__ ##my portion##note:x[<<Ps 16:5; 142:5; Lam 3:24>>], O LORD: I have said that I would keep thy words.
@ ##I intreated##note:x[<<Ps 119:10; Heb 10:22>>] thy ##favour##note:tn[Heb. face] with __my__ whole heart: ##be merciful##note:x[<<Ps 119:41>>] unto me according to thy word.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I ##thought##note:x[<<Lam 3:40; Ezek 18:28; 2 Cor 13:5>>] on my ways, and turned my feet unto thy testimonies.
@ I ##made##note:x[<<Ps 95:7–8; Eccl 9:10; Gal 1:16>>] haste, and delayed not to keep thy commandments.
@ note:x[<<Hos 6:9>>]The ##bands##note:tn[or, companies] of the wicked have robbed me: ##__but__ I##note:x[<<Ps 119:176; Rom 12:17–21>>] have not forgotten thy law.
@ At ##midnight##note:x[<<Ps 42:8; Mark 1:35; Acts 16:25>>] I will rise to give thanks unto thee because of thy righteous judgments.
@ I __am__ ##a companion##note:x[<<Ps 101:6; Prov 13:20; Mal 3:16–18>>] of all __them__ that fear thee, and of them that keep thy precepts.
@ The ##earth##note:x[<<Ps 33:5>>], O LORD, is full of thy mercy: ##teach##note:x[<<Ps 119:12; Matt 11:29>>] me thy statutes.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ט TETH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== TETH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou hast ##dealt well##note:x[<<Ps 13:6; 30:11; 119:17>>] with thy servant, O LORD, according unto thy word.
@ ##Teach me##note:x[<<1 Kgs 3:9; Prov 2:1–9; Phil 1:9>>] good judgment and knowledge: for I have believed thy commandments.
@ Before I was afflicted I went astray: ##but now##note:x[<<Ps 119:71; Jer 31:18–19; Hos 5:15>>] have I kept thy word.
@ Thou __art__ ##good##note:x[<<Ps 86:5; 106:1>>], and doest good; ##teach##note:x[<<Ps 119:12>>] me thy statutes.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##proud##note:x[<<Job 13:4; Ps 109:2–3; Matt 5:11–12>>] have forged a lie against me: __but__ I will keep thy precepts with __my__ whole heart.
@ Their ##heart is as fat##note:x[<<Ps 17:10; 73:7; Acts 28:27>>] as grease; __but__ I delight in thy law.
@ __It is__ ##good##note:x[<<Ps 119:67; 1 Cor 11:32; Heb 12:10–11>>] for me that I have been afflicted; that I might learn thy statutes.
@ The law of thy mouth __is__ ##better##note:x[<<Ps 19:10; 119:127; Prov 8:10–11>>] unto me than thousands of gold and silver.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __י JOD.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== YODH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Thy hands##note:x[<<Ps 111:10; 138:8; 139:14–16>>] have made me and fashioned me: give me understanding, that I may learn thy commandments.
@ They that ##fear thee##note:x[<<Ps 34:2–6; 66:16; 119:79>>] will be glad when they see me; because I have hoped in thy word.
@ I know, O LORD, that thy judgments __are__ note:x[<<Rom 3:4–5>>]##right##note:tn[Heb. righteousness], and __that__ ##thou in##note:x[<<Heb 12:10–11; Rev 3:19>>] faithfulness hast afflicted me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Let, I pray thee, thy ##merciful##note:x[<<Ps 86:5; 106:4–5; 2 Cor 1:3–5>>] kindness be for my comfort, according to thy word unto thy servant.note:tn[__for…__: Heb. to comfort me]
@ Let ##thy tender##note:x[<<Ps 119:41; Lam 3:22–23>>] mercies come unto me, that I may live: ##for thy##note:x[<<Ps 119:174>>] law __is__ my delight.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Let ##the proud##note:x[<<Ps 119:51>>] be ashamed; for they dealt perversely with me ##without##note:x[<<Ps 25:3; John 15:25>>] a cause: __but__ I will meditate in thy precepts.
@ ##Let those##note:x[<<Ps 119:74; 142:7>>] that fear thee turn unto me, and those that have known thy testimonies.
@ Let my heart be ##sound##note:x[<<Deut 26:16; 2 Chr 12:14>>] in thy statutes; ##that I be##note:x[<<1 John 2:28>>] not ashamed.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __כ CAPH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== KAPH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ My soul ##fainteth##note:x[<<Ps 73:26; 84:2; 119:40>>] for thy salvation: __but__ I hope in thy word.
@ Mine ##eyes##note:x[<<Ps 69:3; 119:123; Prov 13:12>>] fail for thy word, saying, When wilt thou comfort me?
@ For I am become ##like a bottle in the smoke##note:x[<<Job 30:30>>]; ##__yet__ do I##note:x[<<Ps 119:176>>] not forget thy statutes.
@ ##How##note:x[<<Ps 90:12>>] many __are__ the days of thy servant? ##when##note:x[<<Rev 6:10–11>>] wilt thou execute judgment on them that persecute me?

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##The proud##note:x[<<Ps 35:7; Prov 16:27; Jer 18:20>>] have digged pits for me, which __are__ not after thy law.
@ All thy commandments __are__ ##faithful##note:tn[Heb. faithfulness]: ##they##note:x[<<Ps 35:19; 119:78>>] persecute me wrongfully; ##help##note:x[<<Ps 70:5>>] thou me.
@ They had ##almost##note:x[<<Matt 10:28>>] consumed me upon earth; ##but I forsook##note:x[<<Ps 119:61>>] not thy precepts.
@ ##Quicken##note:x[<<Ps 119:25, 40>>] me after thy lovingkindness; ##so shall I##note:x[<<Ps 25:10>>] keep the testimony of thy mouth.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ל LAMED.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== LAMEDH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##For ever##note:x[<<Matt 5:18; 24:34–35; 1 Pet 1:25>>], O LORD, thy word is settled in heaven.
@ Thy ##faithfulness##note:x[<<Mic 7:20>>] __is__ ##unto all generations##note:x[<<Ps 89:1–2; 100:5>>]: thou hast established the earth, and it ##abideth##note:tn[Heb. standeth].note:tn[__unto…__: Heb. to generation and generation]
@ ##They continue this##note:x[<<Ps 148:5–6; Isa 48:13; Jer 33:25>>] day according to thine ordinances: for all __are__ thy servants.
@ Unless ##thy law##note:x[<<Ps 119:143; Rom 15:4>>] __had been__ my delights, ##I should##note:x[<<Prov 6:22–23>>] then have perished in mine affliction.
@ I ##will never##note:x[<<Ps 119:16, 50; John 6:63>>] forget thy precepts: for with them thou hast quickened me.
@ ##I __am__ thine##note:x[<<Ps 86:2; Isa 44:2; Zeph 3:17>>], save me; for I have sought thy precepts.
@ The ##wicked##note:x[<<Ps 119:69>>] have waited for me to destroy me: ##__but__ I##note:x[<<Ps 119:31, 167>>] will consider thy testimonies.
@ ##I have seen##note:x[<<Eccl 2:11; Matt 24:35>>] an end of all perfection: __but__ thy commandment __is__ exceeding broad.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __מ MEM.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== MEM

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##O how##note:x[<<Ps 1:2; 119:159; Prov 2:10>>] love I thy law! it __is__ my meditation all the day.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou ##through##note:x[<<Ps 119:104; Prov 2:6; Col 3:16>>] thy commandments hast made me wiser than mine enemies: for they __are__ ever with me.note:tn[__they…__: Heb. it is ever with me]
@ I have more understanding ##than all##note:x[<<Matt 11:25; 13:11>>] my teachers: ##for thy##note:x[<<Ps 119:24>>] testimonies __are__ my meditation.
@ I ##understand##note:x[<<Job 12:12>>] more than the ancients, ##because##note:x[<<Ps 111:10; Jas 3:13>>] I keep thy precepts.
@ I have ##refrained##note:x[<<Ps 119:104; Prov 1:15; 1 Pet 3:10–11>>] my feet from every evil way, that I might keep thy word.
@ I have not ##departed##note:x[<<Ps 18:21; Prov 5:7>>] from thy judgments: ##for thou##note:x[<<1 John 2:27>>] hast taught me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ How ##sweet##note:x[<<Ps 19:10; Prov 8:11; 24:13–14>>] are thy words unto my ##taste##note:tn[Heb. palate]! __yea, sweeter__ than honey to my mouth!
@ Through thy precepts I get understanding: ##therefore##note:x[<<Ps 119:128; Prov 8:13>>] I hate every ##false way##note:x[<<Prov 14:12>>].

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __נ NUN.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== NUN

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thy ##word##note:x[<<Ps 43:3; Prov 6:23>>] __is__ a note:x[<<Ps 18:28>>]##lamp##note:tn[or, candle] unto my feet, and a light unto my path.
@ I have ##sworn##note:x[<<Neh 10:29; Ps 56:12; 66:13–14>>], and I will perform __it__, that I will keep thy righteous judgments.
@ I am ##afflicted##note:x[<<Ps 34:19>>] very much: ##quicken##note:x[<<Ps 119:25; 143:11>>] me, O LORD, according unto thy word.
@ ##Accept##note:x[<<Num 29:39; Hos 14:2; Heb 13:15>>], I beseech thee, the freewill offerings of my mouth, O LORD, and teach me thy judgments.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##My soul##note:x[<<Judg 12:3>>] __is__ continually in my hand: yet do I not forget thy law.
@ The ##wicked##note:x[<<Ps 140:5; 141:9>>] have laid a snare for me: ##yet I erred##note:x[<<Ps 119:95>>] not from thy precepts.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Thy testimonies##note:x[<<Ps 16:5; 1 Pet 1:4>>] have I taken as an heritage for ever: ##for they##note:x[<<Jer 15:16>>] __are__ the rejoicing of my heart.
@ I have ##inclined##note:x[<<Ps 119:36; 141:4>>] mine heart to perform thy statutes alway, __even unto__ ##the end##note:x[<<Ps 119:33>>].note:tn[__to perform__: Heb. to do]

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ס SAMECH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== SAMEKH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I ##hate##note:x[<<Isa 55:7; Jer 4:14>>] __vain__ thoughts: but thy law do I love.
@ Thou __art__ ##my hiding##note:x[<<Ps 32:7; 91:1–2; Isa 32:2>>] place and my shield: I hope in thy word.
@ ##Depart##note:x[<<Ps 6:8; Matt 7:23; 1 Cor 15:33>>] from me, ye evildoers: for I will keep the commandments of my God.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Uphold me according unto thy word, that I may live: ##and let me##note:x[<<Ps 25:2; Rom 5:5; 1 Pet 2:6>>] not be ashamed of my hope.
@ ##Hold##note:x[<<Ps 71:6; Jude 1:24>>] thou me up, and I shall be safe: ##and I will##note:x[<<Ps 119:6>>] have respect unto thy statutes continually.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thou hast trodden down all them that ##err##note:x[<<Ps 119:10, 21>>] from thy statutes: for their deceit __is__ falsehood.
@ Thou puttest away all the wicked of the earth __like__ dross: therefore I love thy testimonies.note:tn[__puttest…__: Heb. causest to cease]
@ ##My flesh##note:x[<<Isa 66:2; Dan 10:8–11; Hab 3:16>>] trembleth for fear of thee; and I am afraid of thy judgments.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ע AIN.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== AYIN

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I have##note:x[<<2 Sam 8:15; Ps 18:20–24>>] done judgment and justice: leave me not to mine oppressors.
@ Be ##surety##note:x[<<Heb 7:22>>] for thy servant for good: ##let not##note:x[<<Ps 119:21>>] the proud oppress me.
@ ##Mine eyes fail for thy salvation, and for the word of thy righteousness.##note:x[<<Ps 130:6>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Deal##note:x[<<Ps 119:76–77; Dan 9:18>>] with thy servant according unto thy mercy, and ##teach##note:x[<<Ps 119:12>>] me thy statutes.
@ ##I __am__ thy##note:x[<<Ps 116:16>>] servant; ##give##note:x[<<Ps 119:34; Jas 3:13–17>>] me understanding, that I may know thy testimonies.
@ __It is__ time for __thee__, LORD, to work: __for__ ##they##note:x[<<Jer 8:8; Hab 1:4; Mal 2:8>>] have made void thy law.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Therefore ##I love##note:x[<<Ps 19:10; Prov 3:13–18; Matt 13:45–46>>] thy commandments above gold; yea, above fine gold.
@ Therefore ##I esteem##note:x[<<Ps 19:7–8; Rom 7:22>>] all __thy__ precepts __concerning__ all __things to be__ right; ##__and__ I##note:x[<<Ps 119:104>>] hate every false way.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __פ PE.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== PE

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Thy ##testimonies##note:x[<<Ps 119:18; 139:6>>] __are__ wonderful: therefore ##doth##note:x[<<Ps 25:10>>] my soul keep them.
@ The ##entrance##note:x[<<Ps 119:105; Prov 6:23; 2 Cor 4:6>>] of thy words giveth light; it giveth understanding unto the simple.
@ I ##opened##note:x[<<Ps 42:1; 119:20; 1 Pet 2:2>>] my mouth, and panted: for I longed for thy commandments.
@ ##Look##note:x[<<Ps 25:18; 119:124>>] thou upon me, and be merciful unto me, ##as thou usest to do unto those##note:x[<<Ps 106:4>>] that love thy name.note:tn[__as thou…__: Heb. according to the custom toward those, etc]
@ ##Order##note:x[<<Ps 17:5; 119:116>>] my steps in thy word: and ##let not##note:x[<<Ps 19:13>>] any iniquity have dominion over me.
@ ##Deliver me from the oppression of man: so will I keep thy precepts.##note:x[<<Ps 56:1–2; 119:122>>]
@ ##Make##note:x[<<Num 6:25–26; Ps 4:6; 80:7>>] thy face to shine upon thy servant; and teach me thy statutes.
@ ##Rivers of waters run down mine eyes, because they keep not thy law.##note:x[<<Ps 119:53; Jer 9:1; Ezek 9:4>>]

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __צ TZADDI.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== TZADE

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ezra 9:15; Ps 145:17; Jer 12:1>>]Righteous __art__ thou, O LORD, and upright __are__ thy judgments.
@ Thy ##testimonies##note:x[<<Ps 19:7–9; 119:86, 144>>] __that__ thou hast commanded __are__ ##righteous##note:tn[Heb. righteousness] and very ##faithful##note:tn[Heb. faithfulness].
@ My ##zeal##note:x[<<Ps 69:9; John 2:17>>] hath consumed me, because mine enemies have forgotten thy words.note:tn[__consumed…__: Heb. cut me off]
@ Thy word __is__ very note:x[<<Ps 12:6; 19:8; Prov 30:5>>]##pure##note:tn[Heb. tried, or, refined]: therefore thy servant loveth it.
@ I __am__ ##small##note:x[<<Ps 22:6; 2 Cor 8:9; Jas 2:5>>] and despised: __yet__ do not I forget thy precepts.
@ Thy righteousness __is__ an everlasting righteousness, ##and thy##note:x[<<Ps 19:9; 119:151; John 17:17>>] law __is__ the truth.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Trouble##note:x[<<Ps 119:107>>] and anguish have taken hold on me: ##__yet__ thy##note:x[<<Job 23:12>>] commandments __are__ my delights.note:tn[__taken…__: Heb. found me]
@ The ##righteousness##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:23–25>>] of thy testimonies __is__ everlasting: ##give me##note:x[<<Ps 119:169; 2 Cor 4:6>>] understanding, and I shall live.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ק KOPH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== KOPH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I ##cried##note:x[<<Ps 102:1; 119:10; Jer 29:13>>] with __my__ whole heart; hear me, O LORD: I will keep thy statutes.
@ I cried unto thee; save me, and I shall keep thy testimonies.note:tn[__and I…__: or, that I may keep]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I prevented##note:x[<<Ps 5:3; Mark 1:35>>] the dawning of the morning, and cried: I ##hoped##note:x[<<Ps 130:5>>] in thy word.
@ Mine ##eyes##note:x[<<Ps 63:6; Lam 2:19; Luke 6:12>>] prevent the __night__ watches, that I might meditate in thy word.
@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 5:2–3; 55:2>>] my voice ##according unto##note:x[<<Isa 63:7>>] thy lovingkindness: O LORD, quicken me according to thy judgment.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They ##draw nigh##note:x[<<Ps 27:2>>] that follow after mischief: they are far from thy law.
@ Thou __art__ ##near##note:x[<<Ps 139:2; 145:18>>], O LORD; and ##all##note:x[<<Ps 119:142>>] thy commandments __are__ truth.
@ Concerning ##thy testimonies##note:x[<<Ps 111:7–8; Eccl 3:14; Luke 21:33>>], I have known of old that thou hast founded them for ever.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ר RESH.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== RESH

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Consider##note:x[<<Ps 9:13; Lam 5:1>>] mine affliction, and deliver me: ##for I##note:x[<<Ps 119:176>>] do not forget thy law.
@ ##Plead##note:x[<<1 Sam 24:15; Ps 35:1; Jer 50:34>>] my cause, and deliver me: quicken me according to thy word.
@ ##Salvation##note:x[<<Job 5:4; Eph 2:17–18>>] __is__ far from the wicked: ##for they##note:x[<<Rom 3:11>>] seek not thy statutes.
@ ##Great##note:tn[or, Many] ##__are__ thy##note:x[<<Ps 86:13, 15>>] tender mercies, O LORD: quicken me according to thy judgments.
@ Many __are__ my persecutors and mine enemies; ##__yet__ do I##note:x[<<Ps 119:51; 1 Cor 15:58>>] not decline from thy testimonies.
@ ##I beheld the transgressors, and was grieved; because they kept not thy word.##note:x[<<Ps 119:53>>]
@ ##Consider##note:x[<<Ps 119:97, 153>>] how I love thy precepts: quicken me, O LORD, according to thy lovingkindness.
@ ##Thy word __is__ true __from__ the beginning##note:x[<<Prov 30:5; 2 Tim 3:16>>]: ##and every one##note:x[<<Ps 119:144>>] of thy righteous judgments __endureth__ for ever.note:tn[__Thy word…__: Heb. The beginning of thy word is true]

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ש SCHIN.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== SIN and SHIN

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Princes##note:x[<<1 Sam 24:9–15; 26:18; Ps 119:157>>] have persecuted me without a cause: but my heart standeth in awe of thy word.
@ I ##rejoice##note:x[<<Ps 119:72, 111; Jer 15:16>>] at thy word, as one that findeth great spoil.
@ I ##hate##note:x[<<Ps 119:128; Prov 6:16–19; Rom 12:9>>] and abhor lying: __but__ thy law do I love.
@ ##Seven times##note:x[<<Ps 55:17; 119:62>>] a day do I praise thee ##because##note:x[<<Ps 97:8>>] of thy righteous judgments.
@ ##Great##note:x[<<Prov 3:1–2; Isa 32:17; John 14:27>>] peace have they which love thy law: and nothing shall offend them.note:tn[__nothing…__: Heb. they shall have no stumblingblock]
@ ##LORD##note:x[<<Gen 49:18; Ps 119:81, 174>>], I have hoped for thy salvation, and done thy commandments.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Heb 10:16>>] hath kept thy testimonies; ##and I love##note:x[<<Ps 40:8; Rom 7:22>>] them exceedingly.
@ I have kept thy precepts and thy testimonies: ##for all my##note:x[<<Ps 44:20–21; 139:3; Prov 5:21>>] ways __are__ before thee.

[discrete.acrostic]
==== __ת TAU.__

[discrete.subsection]
===== TAW

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Let my cry##note:x[<<Ps 18:6>>] come near before thee, O LORD: ##give me##note:x[<<Ps 119:144; Jas 1:5>>] understanding according to thy word.
@ Let my supplication come before thee: ##deliver me##note:x[<<Ps 119:41>>] according to thy word.
@ ##My lips##note:x[<<Ps 71:17, 23–24; 119:7>>] shall utter praise, when thou hast taught me thy statutes.
@ My ##tongue##note:x[<<Ps 37:30; Eph 4:29>>] shall speak of thy word: ##for all thy##note:x[<<Rom 7:14>>] commandments __are__ righteousness.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Let##note:x[<<Isa 41:10–14>>] thine hand help me; for I have chosen thy precepts.
@ I have ##longed##note:x[<<Ps 119:166>>] for thy salvation, O LORD; ##and thy law##note:x[<<Ps 119:16, 47>>] __is__ my delight.
@ ##Let my##note:x[<<Ps 51:14–15; 118:18–19; Isa 38:19>>] soul live, and it shall praise thee; and let thy judgments help me.
@ I have ##gone astray##note:x[<<Isa 53:6; Luke 15:4–7>>] like a lost sheep; ##seek##note:x[<<Luke 19:10>>] thy servant; for I do not forget thy commandments.

=== @120

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== In My Distress I Cried to the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ In ##my distress##note:x[<<Ps 18:6; 118:5; Jonah 2:2>>] I cried unto the LORD, and he heard me.
@ Deliver my soul, O LORD, ##from lying lips##note:x[<<Ps 140:1–3>>], __and__ from a deceitful tongue.
@ What shall be given unto thee? or what shall be ##done##note:tn[Heb. added] unto thee, thou false tongue?note:tn[__What shall be given…__: or, What shall the deceitful tongue give unto thee? or, what shall is profit thee?]
@ ##Sharp##note:x[<<Prov 18:21; Jas 3:5–8>>] ##arrows##note:x[<<Prov 19:9>>] of the mighty, with coals of juniper.note:tn[__Sharp…__: or, It is as the sharp arrows of the mighty man, with coals of juniper]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Gen 25:13>>]Woe is me, that I sojourn in ##Mesech##note:x[<<Gen 10:2; Ezek 27:13>>], __that__ I dwell in the tents of Kedar!
@ My ##soul##note:x[<<Ps 57:4; Ezek 2:6; Matt 10:16>>] hath long dwelt with him that hateth peace.
@ I __am__ ##__for__ peace##note:x[<<Ps 34:14; Matt 5:9; Heb 12:14>>]: but when I speak, they __are__ for war.note:tn[__for peace__: or, a man of peace]

=== @121

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Lift Up My Eyes to the Hills

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 120:1>>]##I will##note:x[<<Jer 3:23>>] ##lift up##note:x[<<Ps 123:1>>] mine eyes unto the hills, from whence cometh my help.note:tn[__I will…__: or, Shall I lift up mine eyes to the hills? whence should my help come?]
@ ##My help##note:x[<<Ps 124:8; Isa 41:13; Heb 13:6>>] __cometh__ from the LORD, which made heaven and earth.
@ He ##will not##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; Prov 3:23, 26>>] suffer thy foot to be moved: he that keepeth thee will not slumber.
@ Behold, ##he that##note:x[<<Ps 32:7–8; 127:1; Isa 27:3>>] keepeth Israel shall neither slumber nor sleep.
@ The LORD __is__ thy keeper: the LORD __is__ ##thy shade##note:x[<<Exod 13:21; Isa 25:4>>] ##upon##note:x[<<Ps 16:8>>] thy right hand.
@ ##The sun##note:x[<<Ps 91:5–10; Isa 49:10; Rev 7:16>>] shall not smite thee by day, nor the moon by night.
@ The LORD shall ##preserve##note:x[<<Ps 91:9–10; 2 Tim 4:18>>] thee from all evil: ##he shall##note:x[<<Ps 41:2>>] preserve thy soul.
@ The LORD shall preserve ##thy going out##note:x[<<Deut 28:6; Prov 2:8; 3:6>>] and thy coming in from this time forth, and even for evermore.

=== @122

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Pray for the Peace of Jerusalem

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I ##was glad##note:x[<<Ps 84:10>>] when they said unto me, ##Let us go##note:x[<<Isa 2:3; Mic 4:2>>] into the house of the LORD.
@ ##Our feet shall stand within thy gates, O Jerusalem.##note:x[<<2 Chr 6:6; Ps 84:7; 100:4>>]
@ Jerusalem is ##builded##note:x[<<2 Sam 5:9; Eph 2:20–21; 4:4–7>>] as a city that is compact together:
@ ##Whither##note:x[<<Deut 16:16; Ps 132:13>>] the tribes go up, the tribes of the LORD, unto the testimony of Israel, ##to give##note:x[<<Ps 66:13–16>>] thanks unto the name of the LORD.
@ For ##there##note:x[<<Deut 17:8; 2 Chr 19:8>>] are set thrones of judgment, the thrones of the house of David.note:tn[__are…__: Heb. do sit]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Pray##note:x[<<Ps 51:18; 137:6–7>>] for the peace of Jerusalem: ##they shall##note:x[<<Gen 12:3>>] prosper that love thee.
@ ##Peace##note:x[<<Isa 54:13; John 14:27; Jas 3:18>>] be within thy walls, __and__ prosperity within thy palaces.
@ note:x[<<Ps 16:3; 42:4; 119:63>>]For my brethren and companions’ sakes, I will now say, Peace __be__ within thee.
@ Because of ##the house##note:x[<<Ps 26:8; 84:10>>] of the LORD our God ##I will seek##note:x[<<Neh 2:10>>] thy good.

=== @123

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Lift Up My Eyes to You

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 121:1>>]Unto thee ##lift I##note:x[<<Ps 121:1; 141:8>>] up mine eyes, O thou that dwellest in the heavens.
@ Behold, as the eyes of servants __look__ unto the hand of their masters, __and__ as the eyes of a maiden unto the hand of her mistress; ##so our eyes##note:x[<<Ps 40:1–3; 130:5–6; Lam 3:25–26>>] __wait__ upon the LORD our God, until that he have mercy upon us.
@ ##Have mercy##note:x[<<Ps 57:1; Luke 18:11–13>>] upon us, O LORD, have mercy upon us: ##for we are##note:x[<<Ps 89:50–51>>] exceedingly filled with contempt.
@ Our soul is exceedingly filled ##with the scorning##note:x[<<Job 12:5; Ps 119:51; Acts 17:32>>] of those that are at ease, __and__ with the contempt of the proud.

=== @124

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Our Help Is in the Name of the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ If __it had not been__ ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 54:4; 56:9; Rom 8:31>>] who was on our side, now may Israel say;
@ If __it had not been__ the LORD who was on our side, ##when men##note:x[<<Ps 22:12–13, 16; 37:32>>] rose up against us:
@ ##Then they##note:x[<<Esth 3:12–13; Ps 35:25; 57:3>>] had swallowed us up quick, when their wrath was kindled against us:
@ Then ##the waters##note:x[<<Ps 69:15; Rev 12:15–16; 17:15>>] had overwhelmed us, the stream had gone over our soul:
@ Then the proud waters had gone over our soul.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __be__ the LORD, ##who hath not##note:x[<<Exod 15:9–10; Ps 118:13; 145:5–6>>] given us __as__ a prey to their teeth.
@ Our soul is escaped ##as a bird##note:x[<<Ps 91:3; Prov 6:5; 2 Tim 2:26>>] out of the snare of the fowlers: the snare is broken, and we are escaped.
@ ##Our help __is__ in the name of the LORD, who made heaven and earth.##note:x[<<Ps 121:2; 146:5–6; Acts 4:24>>]

=== @125

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Surrounds His People

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ They ##that trust##note:x[<<Ps 62:2; Prov 3:5–6; Jer 17:7–8>>] in the LORD __shall be__ as mount Zion, __which__ cannot be removed, __but__ abideth for ever.
@ As the mountains __are__ round about Jerusalem, so ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 4:5; Zech 2:5; John 10:28–29>>] __is__ round about his people from henceforth even for ever.
@ For ##the rod##note:x[<<Prov 22:8; Isa 14:5–6; 1 Cor 10:13>>] of the wicked shall not rest upon the lot of the righteous; lest the righteous put forth their hands unto iniquity.note:tn[__the wicked__: Heb. wickedness]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Do good##note:x[<<Ps 51:18>>], O LORD, unto __those that be__ good, and __to them that are__ ##upright##note:x[<<Ps 84:11; 119:80>>] in their hearts.
@ As for such as turn aside unto their ##crooked##note:x[<<Prov 2:15; Isa 59:8>>] ways, the LORD shall lead them forth with the workers of iniquity: __but__ ##peace##note:x[<<Ps 128:6>>] __shall be__ upon Israel.

=== @126

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Zion’s Captives Restored

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ When the LORD ##turned again##note:x[<<Ps 85:1; Hos 6:11; Joel 3:1>>] the captivity of Zion, we were like them that dream.note:tn[__turned…__: Heb. returned the returning]
@ ##Then was##note:x[<<Job 8:21; Isa 35:10>>] our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing: ##then said##note:x[<<Zech 8:22–23>>] they among the heathen, The LORD hath done great things for them.note:tn[__hath…__: Heb. hath magnified to do with them]
@ ##The LORD hath done great things for us; __whereof__ we are glad.##note:x[<<Isa 51:9–11; 66:14; Luke 1:49>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Turn again##note:x[<<Ps 85:4; 126:1; Hos 1:11>>] our captivity, O LORD, as the streams in the south.
@ They ##that sow##note:x[<<Joel 2:23; Matt 5:4; John 16:20–22>>] in tears shall reap in ##joy##note:tn[or, singing].
@ He ##that goeth##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Gal 6:7–8>>] forth and weepeth, bearing precious seed, ##shall doubtless##note:x[<<Isa 9:2–3>>] come again with rejoicing, bringing his sheaves __with him__.note:tn[__precious…__: or, seed basket]

=== @127

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees for Solomon.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Children Are a Heritage from the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Except ##the LORD##note:x[<<Prov 16:9; 21:30–31; 1 Cor 3:7>>] build the house, they labour in vain that build it: except the LORD keep the city, the watchman waketh __but__ in vain.note:tn[__for…__: or, of Solomon]note:tn[__that…__: Heb. that are builders of it in it]
@ __It is__ vain for you to rise up early, to sit up late, to eat the bread of sorrows: ##__for__ so he##note:x[<<Ps 3:5; 4:8; Eccl 5:12>>] giveth his beloved sleep.
@ Lo, ##children##note:x[<<Deut 28:4; 1 Sam 1:27; Ps 128:3–4>>] __are__ an heritage of the LORD: __and__ the fruit of the womb __is his__ reward.
@ As ##arrows##note:x[<<Jer 50:9>>] __are__ in the hand of a mighty man; ##so __are__ children##note:x[<<Prov 17:6; 31:28>>] of the youth.
@ ##Happy##note:x[<<Job 42:12–16>>] __is__ the man that hath his quiver full of them: ##they shall##note:x[<<Prov 27:11>>] not be ashamed, but they shall speak with the enemies in the gate.note:tn[__his…__: Heb. filled his quiver with them]note:tn[__speak…__: or, subdue, or, destroy]

=== @128

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Blessed Fear of the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 112:1–10>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __is__ ##every one##note:x[<<Ps 103:13; 112:1>>] that feareth the LORD; that ##walketh##note:x[<<Ps 119:1>>] in his ways.
@ For ##thou shalt eat##note:x[<<Isa 62:8; 65:21–23>>] the labour of thine hands: happy __shalt__ thou __be__, ##and __it shall__##note:x[<<Isa 3:10>>] __be__ well with thee.
@ Thy wife __shall be__ as ##a fruitful vine##note:x[<<Gen 49:22; Ezek 19:10>>] by the sides of thine house: thy children like ##olive plants##note:x[<<Ps 52:8>>] round about thy table.
@ Behold, that thus shall the man be blessed that feareth the LORD.
@ The LORD shall ##bless thee##note:x[<<Ps 20:2; 118:26; 134:3>>] out of Zion: and thou shalt see the good of Jerusalem all the days of thy life.
@ Yea, ##thou shalt see##note:x[<<Gen 50:23; Job 42:16>>] thy children’s children, __and__ ##peace##note:x[<<Isa 66:12>>] upon Israel.

=== @129

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Cords of the Wicked

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 124:1>>]Many a time have they afflicted me ##from##note:x[<<Jer 2:2>>] my youth, ##may##note:x[<<Ps 124:1>>] Israel now say&#58;note:tn[__Many…__: or, Much]
@ Many a time have they afflicted me from my youth: ##yet they have##note:x[<<Ps 34:19; Matt 16:18; Rev 12:8–9>>] not prevailed against me.note:tn[__Many…__: or, Much]
@ ##The plowers##note:x[<<Ps 141:7; Isa 51:23>>] plowed upon my back: they made long their furrows.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Neh 9:33; Lam 3:22; Dan 9:7>>] __is__ righteous: he hath cut asunder the cords of the wicked.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Let them all ##be confounded##note:x[<<Isa 37:35>>] and turned back that hate Zion.
@ Let them be ##as the grass##note:x[<<Ps 37:2>>] __upon__ the housetops, which withereth afore it groweth up:

@ Neither do they which go by say, ##The blessing##note:x[<<Ruth 2:4; Ps 118:26>>] of the LORD __be__ upon you: we bless you in the name of the LORD.

=== @130

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Out of the Depths

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Out of##note:x[<<Ps 42:7; 69:14–15; Heb 5:7>>] the depths have I cried unto thee, O LORD.
@ Lord, hear my voice: ##let thine ears##note:x[<<2 Chr 6:40; Neh 1:11; Ps 61:1–2>>] be attentive to the voice of my supplications.
@ If thou, LORD, ##shouldest mark##note:x[<<Ps 143:2; John 8:7–9; Rom 3:20–24>>] iniquities, O Lord, who shall stand?
@ ##But __there__##note:x[<<Ps 86:5; Isa 55:7>>] __is__ forgiveness with thee, ##that thou mayest##note:x[<<Jer 33:8–9>>] be feared.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I wait##note:x[<<Ps 33:20; 62:5; Isa 30:18>>] for the LORD, my soul doth wait, and in his word do I hope.
@ My soul ##__waiteth__##note:x[<<Ps 63:6; 119:147>>] for the Lord more than they that watch for the morning: __I say, more than__ they that watch for the morning.note:tn[__I say…__: or, which watch unto]
@ ##Let Israel##note:x[<<Ps 40:3>>] hope in the LORD: ##for with##note:x[<<Eph 1:7–8; Rev 5:9>>] the LORD __there is__ mercy, and with him __is__ plenteous redemption.
@ And ##he shall redeem##note:x[<<Matt 1:21; Rom 6:14; Titus 2:14>>] Israel from all his iniquities.

=== @131

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Have Stilled My Soul

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ LORD, my heart is not haughty, nor mine eyes lofty: ##neither##note:x[<<Rom 12:16>>] do I ##exercise##note:x[<<Job 42:3; Ps 139:6>>] myself in great matters, or in things too ##high##note:tn[Heb. wonderful] for me.note:tn[__exercise…__: Heb. walk]
@ Surely I have behaved and ##quieted##note:x[<<Ps 42:11>>] ##myself##note:tn[Heb. my soul], ##as a child##note:x[<<Mark 10:15; 1 Cor 14:20>>] that is weaned of his mother: my soul __is__ even as a weaned child.

=== @132

[.psalm-title]
A Song of degrees.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Has Chosen Zion

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Ps 120:1; 121:1; 129:1>>]LORD, remember David, __and__ all his afflictions:
@ How he sware unto the LORD, __and__ vowed unto ##the mighty##note:x[<<Gen 49:24>>] __God__ of Jacob;
@ Surely ##I will not##note:x[<<Hag 1:4; Matt 6:33>>] come into the tabernacle of my house, nor go up into my bed;
@ I will not ##give sleep##note:x[<<Prov 6:4>>] to mine eyes, __or__ slumber to mine eyelids,
@ Until I find out a place for the LORD, ##an habitation##note:x[<<Eph 2:22>>] for the mighty __God__ of Jacob.note:tn[__an habitation__: Heb. habitations]
@ Lo, we heard of it ##at Ephratah##note:x[<<1 Sam 17:12; Mic 5:2>>]: ##we found##note:x[<<1 Sam 7:1>>] it in the fields of the wood.
@ We ##will go##note:x[<<Ps 5:7>>] into his tabernacles: we will ##worship##note:x[<<Ps 99:5; Lam 2:1>>] at his footstool.
@ ##Arise##note:x[<<Num 10:35–36; 2 Chr 6:41–42; Ps 68:1>>], O LORD, into thy rest; thou, and the ark of thy strength.
@ Let ##thy priests##note:x[<<Job 29:14; Rev 19:8>>] be clothed with righteousness; and let thy saints shout for joy.
@ For thy servant David’s sake turn not away the face of thine anointed.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The LORD hath ##sworn##note:x[<<Ps 89:3–4>>] __in__ truth unto David; he will not turn from it; ##Of the fruit##note:x[<<2 Sam 7:12; Acts 2:30>>] of thy ##body##note:tn[Heb. belly] will I set upon thy throne.
@ If thy children will keep my covenant and my testimony that I shall teach them, their children shall also sit upon thy throne for evermore.
@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 78:68–69; Heb 12:22>>] hath chosen Zion; ##he hath desired##note:x[<<Ps 68:16>>] __it__ for his habitation.
@ This __is__ ##my rest##note:x[<<Ps 132:8>>] for ever: ##here will##note:x[<<Ps 76:2; 135:21>>] I dwell; for I have desired it.
@ I will ##abundantly##note:tn[or, surely] ##bless her provision##note:x[<<Lev 26:4–5; Ps 147:14; Prov 3:9–10>>]: I will satisfy her poor with bread.
@ I will also ##clothe##note:x[<<2 Chr 6:41; Ps 132:9; Isa 61:10>>] her priests with salvation: and her saints shall shout aloud for joy.
@ There ##will I make##note:x[<<Ezek 29:21; Luke 1:69>>] the horn of David to bud: ##I have ordained##note:x[<<2 Chr 21:7>>] a ##lamp##note:tn[or, candle] for mine anointed.
@ ##His enemies##note:x[<<Job 8:22; Ps 35:26>>] will I clothe with shame: ##but upon##note:x[<<Luke 1:32–33>>] himself shall his crown flourish.

@ ##__It is__ like##note:x[<<Prov 27:9>>] the precious ointment upon the head, ##that ran down##note:x[<<Exod 30:25–30; Lev 8:12>>] upon the beard, __even__ Aaron’s beard: that went down to the skirts of his garments;
@ As the dew of Hermon, __and as the dew__ that descended upon the mountains of Zion: ##for there the LORD##note:x[<<Lev 25:21; Deut 28:8; Ps 42:8>>] commanded the blessing, __even__ life for evermore.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Praise ye the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 33:1–2; 96:1–4>>]. ##Praise ye the name##note:x[<<Ps 7:17>>] of the LORD; praise __him__, O ye servants of the LORD.
@ Ye that stand in the house of the LORD, in ##the courts##note:x[<<Ps 92:13; 116:19>>] of the house of our God,

[.poetry%hardbreaks]

@ ##Whatsoever##note:x[<<Ps 115:3; Dan 4:35; Matt 28:18>>] the LORD pleased, __that__ did he in heaven, and in earth, in the seas, and all deep places.

@ Who ##smote##note:x[<<Exod 12:12; Ps 78:51; 105:36>>] the firstborn of Egypt, both of man and beast.note:tn[__both…__: Heb. from man unto beast]
@ __Who__ sent tokens and wonders into the midst of thee, O Egypt, ##upon Pharaoh##note:x[<<Ps 136:15>>], and upon all his servants.

@ ##Thy name##note:x[<<Exod 3:15; Ps 102:12>>], O LORD, __endureth__ for ever; __and__ thy memorial, O LORD, throughout all generations.note:tn[__throughout…__: Heb. to generation and generation]
@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 7:8; 50:4>>] will judge his people, and ##he will repent##note:x[<<Deut 32:36>>] himself concerning his servants.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ The ##idols##note:x[<<Ps 115:4–8; Isa 37:19; Jer 10:3–11>>] of the heathen __are__ silver and gold, the work of men’s hands.
@ They have mouths, but they speak not; ##eyes have they##note:x[<<Matt 13:14–16>>], but they see not;
@ They have ears, but they hear not; neither is there __any__ breath in their mouths.
@ ##They that##note:x[<<Ps 115:8; Isa 44:18–20; Jer 10:8>>] make them are like unto them: __so is__ every one that trusteth in them.
@ Bless the LORD, O house of Israel: bless the LORD, O house of Aaron:

@ Blessed be the LORD ##out of Zion##note:x[<<Ps 134:3>>], ##which dwelleth##note:x[<<Ps 132:13–14>>] at Jerusalem. Praise ye the LORD.

@ ##To him which led his people through the wilderness: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever.##note:x[<<Exod 13:18; 15:22; Deut 8:15>>]
@ ##To him which smote great kings: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever:##note:x[<<Ps 135:10–11>>]

@ ##Sihon king of the Amorites: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever:##note:x[<<Deut 29:7>>]
@ ##And Og the king of Bashan: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever:##note:x[<<Num 21:33>>]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]

@ O give thanks unto ##the God of heaven##note:x[<<Ps 136:1–3; Jonah 1:9>>]: for his mercy __endureth__ for ever.

=== @137

==== By the Rivers of Babylon

(<<Ezekiel 1:1–3>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Lam 2:10; Ezek 3:15>>]By ##the rivers##note:x[<<Ezek 1:1>>] of Babylon, there we sat down, yea, we wept, when we remembered Zion.

@ If I do not remember thee, ##let my tongue##note:x[<<Ps 22:15; Isa 41:17; Ezek 3:26>>] cleave to the roof of my mouth; if I prefer not Jerusalem above my chief joy.note:tn[__my chief…__: Heb. the head of my joy]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]

=== @138

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Thankful Heart

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##I will praise##note:x[<<Ps 86:12–13; 111:1; 1 Cor 14:15>>] thee with my whole heart: before the gods will I sing praise unto thee.
@ I will worship toward thy holy temple, and praise thy name for thy lovingkindness and for thy truth: ##for thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 56:10; Isa 42:21; Matt 5:18>>] magnified thy word above all thy name.
@ ##In the day##note:x[<<Ps 18:6; 34:4–6>>] when I cried thou answeredst me, __and__ ##strengthenedst##note:x[<<1 Pet 5:10>>] me __with__ strength in my soul.
@ ##All the kings##note:x[<<Ps 72:11; 102:15>>] of the earth shall praise thee, O LORD, ##when they hear##note:x[<<Ps 22:22>>] the words of thy mouth.
@ Yea, they shall sing in the ways of the LORD: ##for great##note:x[<<Ps 21:5; Rev 4:11; 7:12>>] __is__ the glory of the LORD.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Though##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:7–8; Ps 113:5–6; Isa 57:15>>] the LORD __be__ high, yet hath he respect unto the lowly: but the proud he knoweth afar off.
@ ##Though I walk##note:x[<<Ps 23:3–4>>] in the midst of trouble, ##thou wilt##note:x[<<Ps 71:20–21>>] revive me: thou shalt stretch forth thine hand against the wrath of mine enemies, ##and thy right##note:x[<<Isa 41:10>>] hand shall save me.
@ The LORD will ##perfect##note:x[<<Ps 57:2; Phil 1:6; 1 Thess 5:24>>] __that which__ concerneth me: thy mercy, O LORD, __endureth__ for ever: forsake not the works of thine own hands.

=== @139

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== You Have Searched Me and Known Me

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ O LORD, ##thou hast##note:x[<<Jer 12:3; 17:9–10; Heb 4:13>>] searched me, and known __me__.
@ Thou ##knowest##note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:27; Isa 37:28>>] my downsitting and mine uprising, thou ##understandest##note:x[<<Matt 9:4>>] my thought afar off.
@ Thou note:x[<<Job 31:4>>]##compassest##note:tn[or, winnowest] ##my path##note:x[<<Gen 28:10–17>>] and my lying down, ##and art acquainted##note:x[<<Jer 23:24>>] __with__ all my ways.
@ For ##__there is__ not##note:x[<<Ps 19:14>>] a word in my tongue, __but__, lo, O LORD, ##thou knowest##note:x[<<Ps 50:19–21; Heb 4:12–13>>] it altogether.
@ Thou hast ##beset me##note:x[<<Deut 33:27; Job 23:8–9>>] behind and before, ##and laid##note:x[<<Rev 1:17>>] thine hand upon me.
@ __Such__ ##knowledge##note:x[<<Job 42:3; Ps 40:5; Rom 11:33>>] __is__ too wonderful for me; it is high, I cannot __attain__ unto it.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Whither shall I go from thy spirit? or whither shall I flee from thy presence?##note:x[<<Jer 23:23–24; Jonah 1:3, 10>>]
@ If ##I ascend##note:x[<<Amos 9:2–4>>] up into heaven, thou __art__ there: if I make my bed ##in hell##note:x[<<Job 34:21–22; Jonah 2:2>>], behold, thou __art there__.
@ __If__ I take ##the wings##note:x[<<Ps 19:6>>] of the morning, __and__ dwell in the uttermost parts of the sea;
@ ##Even there shall thy hand lead me, and thy right hand shall hold me.##note:x[<<Ps 63:8; 73:23; Isa 41:13>>]
@ If I say, ##Surely##note:x[<<Ps 94:7; Jer 23:24>>] the darkness shall cover me; ##even the night##note:x[<<Job 12:22>>] shall be light about me.
@ Yea, ##the darkness##note:x[<<Exod 20:21; Job 34:22; Dan 2:22>>] hideth not from thee; but the night shineth as the day: the darkness and the light __are__ both alike __to thee__.note:tn[__hideth…__: Heb. darkeneth not]note:tn[__the darkness and…__: Heb. as is the darkness, so is the light]
@ For thou hast possessed my reins: thou hast ##covered me##note:x[<<Job 31:15; Isa 44:2; Jer 1:5>>] in my mother’s womb.
@ I will praise thee; ##for I am fearfully##note:x[<<Gen 1:26–27>>] __and__ wonderfully made: ##marvellous##note:x[<<Ps 104:24; 111:2>>] __are__ thy works; and __that__ my soul knoweth right well.note:tn[__right…__: Heb. greatly]
@ My ##substance##note:tn[or, strength, or, body] was not hid from thee, ##when I##note:x[<<Ps 139:13>>] was made in secret, __and__ curiously wrought ##in the lowest##note:x[<<Ps 63:9>>] parts of the earth.
@ Thine eyes did see my substance, yet being unperfect; and ##in thy book##note:x[<<Ps 56:8; Mal 3:16; Rev 20:12>>] all __my members__ were written, __which__ in continuance were fashioned, when __as yet there was__ none of them.note:tn[__all…__: Heb. all of them]note:tn[__which…__: or, what days they should be fashioned]

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ How ##precious##note:x[<<Ps 40:5; Isa 55:8–9; Jer 29:11>>] also are thy thoughts unto me, O God! how great is the sum of them!
@ __If__ I should count them, they are more in number than the sand: ##when I awake##note:x[<<Ps 3:5; 139:3; 1 Thess 5:10>>], I am still with thee.
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Ps 5:6; Isa 11:4>>] thou wilt slay the wicked, O God: ##depart from##note:x[<<Ps 119:115>>] me therefore, ye bloody men.
@ ##For they speak##note:x[<<Ps 74:18; Jude 1:15>>] against thee wickedly, __and__ ##thine##note:x[<<Exod 20:7>>] enemies take __thy name__ in vain.
@ ##Do not I##note:x[<<Ps 31:6; Rev 2:2>>] hate them, O LORD, that hate thee? ##and am not##note:x[<<Ps 119:158>>] I grieved with those that rise up against thee?
@ I hate them with perfect hatred: I count them mine enemies.
@ ##Search me##note:x[<<Ps 26:2>>], O God, and ##know##note:x[<<Job 31:6; 1 Pet 1:7>>] my heart: try me, and know my thoughts:
@ ##And see##note:x[<<Jer 17:9–10>>] if __there be any__ wicked way in me, ##and lead##note:x[<<Ps 25:8–9; 143:10>>] me in the way everlasting.note:tn[__wicked…__: Heb. way of pain, or, grief]

=== @140

[.psalm-title]
To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Rescue Me from Evil Men

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Deliver##note:x[<<Ps 71:4>>] me, O LORD, from the evil man: preserve me from the ##violent man##note:x[<<Ps 18:48; 140:4>>];note:tn[__violent…__: Heb. man of violences]
@ Which ##imagine##note:x[<<Ps 36:4; 64:5–6>>] mischiefs in __their__ heart; ##continually##note:x[<<Ps 56:6>>] are they gathered together __for__ war.
@ They have sharpened their tongues ##like a serpent##note:x[<<2 Cor 11:3>>]; ##adders##note:x[<<Ps 58:4; Rom 3:13–14>>]’ poison __is__ under their lips. Selah.
@ ##Keep me##note:x[<<Ps 36:11; 71:4>>], O LORD, from the hands of the wicked; preserve me from the violent man; who have purposed to ##overthrow##note:x[<<Ps 17:5>>] my goings.
@ ##The proud##note:x[<<Ps 35:7; 142:3; Jer 18:22>>] have hid a snare for me, and cords; they have spread a net by the wayside; they have set gins for me. Selah.
@ ##I said unto##note:x[<<Ps 16:2; 31:14; 119:57>>] the LORD, Thou __art__ my God: hear the voice of my supplications, O LORD.
@ O GOD the Lord, ##the strength##note:x[<<Ps 18:1–2; 62:7; 89:26>>] of my salvation, thou hast covered my head in the day of battle.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Grant not##note:x[<<2 Sam 15:31; Job 5:12–13; Ps 27:12>>], O LORD, the desires of the wicked: further not his wicked device; __lest__ they exalt themselves. Selah.note:tn[__lest…__: or, let them not be exalted]
@ __As for__ the head of those that compass me about, ##let the mischief##note:x[<<Ps 7:16; 94:23; Prov 18:7>>] of their own lips cover them.
@ Let ##burning coals##note:x[<<Ps 11:6; 21:9>>] fall upon them: let them be cast into the fire; ##into deep##note:x[<<Rev 20:15>>] pits, that they rise not up again.
@ Let not note:x[<<Prov 17:20; 18:21>>]an ##evil##note:x[<<Ps 34:21>>] speaker be established in the earth: evil shall hunt the violent man to overthrow __him__.note:tn[__an…__: or, an evil speaker (Heb. a man of tongue), a wicked man of violence, be established in the earth: let him be hunted to his overthrow]
@ I know that ##the LORD##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:45, 49; Ps 9:4>>] will maintain the cause of the afflicted, __and__ the right of the poor.
@ ##Surely##note:x[<<Isa 3:10>>] the righteous shall give thanks unto thy name: ##the upright##note:x[<<Ps 16:11; John 17:24>>] shall dwell in thy presence.

=== @141

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Come Quickly to Me

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 70:1–5>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ LORD, I cry unto thee: ##make haste##note:x[<<Ps 40:13; 70:5; 71:12>>] unto me; give ear unto my voice, when I cry unto thee.
@ Let my prayer be set forth before thee ##__as__ incense##note:x[<<Rev 5:8; 8:3–4>>]; __and__ ##the lifting##note:x[<<1 Tim 2:8>>] up of my hands __as__ the evening sacrifice.note:tn[__set…__: Heb. directed]
@ ##Set a watch##note:x[<<Ps 39:1; Mic 7:5; Jas 1:26>>], O LORD, before my mouth; keep the door of my lips.
@ ##Incline not##note:x[<<Ps 119:36; Isa 63:17; Matt 6:13>>] my heart to __any__ evil thing, to practise wicked works with men that work iniquity: and let me not eat of their dainties.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Let ##the righteous##note:x[<<Prov 27:5–6; Gal 6:1; Rev 3:19>>] smite me; __it shall be__ a kindness: and let him reprove me; __it shall be__ an excellent oil, __which__ shall not break my head: for yet my prayer also __shall be__ in their calamities.note:tn[__me; it shall be a…__: or, me kindly, and reprove me; let not their precious oil break, etc]
@ When their judges are overthrown in stony places, they shall hear my words; for they are sweet.
@ Our ##bones##note:x[<<Rom 8:36>>] are scattered at the grave’s mouth, as when one cutteth and cleaveth __wood__ upon the earth.
@ But ##mine eyes##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:12; Ps 123:1–2>>] __are__ unto thee, O GOD the Lord: in thee is my trust; ##leave not my soul destitute##note:x[<<Isa 41:17>>].note:tn[__leave…__: Heb. make not my soul bare]
@ Keep me ##from the snares##note:x[<<Ps 119:110; 140:5; Luke 20:20>>] __which__ they have laid for me, and the gins of the workers of iniquity.
@ Let ##the wicked##note:x[<<Ps 7:15–16; 35:8; 140:9>>] fall into their own nets, whilst that I withal ##escape##note:tn[Heb. pass over].

=== @142

[.psalm-title]
Maschil of David; A Prayer when he was in the cave.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Lift My Voice to the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<1 Samuel 22:1–5; Psalms 57:1–11>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<1 Chr 4:10; Ps 57:1>>]I cried unto the LORD ##with my voice##note:x[<<Ps 141:1>>]; with my voice unto the LORD did I make my supplication.note:tn[__Maschil…__: or, A Psalm of David, giving instruction]
@ I ##poured out##note:x[<<Isa 26:16; Rom 8:26>>] my complaint before him; ##I shewed##note:x[<<Phil 4:6–7>>] before him my trouble.
@ When ##my spirit##note:x[<<Ps 61:2>>] was overwhelmed within me, then thou knewest my path. ##In the way##note:x[<<Ps 140:5; 141:9>>] wherein I walked have they privily laid a snare for me.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I looked on __my__ right hand, and beheld, ##but __there was__##note:x[<<Job 19:13–19; Ps 31:11; 88:18>>] no man that would know me: refuge failed me; no man cared for my soul.note:tn[__I looked…__: or, Look on the right hand, and see]note:tn[__failed…__: Heb. perished from me]note:tn[__cared…__: Heb. sought after]
@ I cried unto thee, O LORD: I said, ##Thou __art__##note:x[<<2 Tim 4:17>>] my refuge __and__ ##my portion##note:x[<<Ps 16:5>>] ##in the land##note:x[<<Ps 27:13>>] of the living.
@ Attend unto my cry; ##for I am##note:x[<<Ps 79:8; 116:6; 136:23>>] brought very low: deliver me from my persecutors; for they are stronger than I.
@ Bring ##my soul##note:x[<<Ps 143:11; 146:7>>] out of prison, that I may praise thy name: ##the righteous##note:x[<<Ps 7:6–7>>] shall compass me about; for thou shalt deal bountifully with me.

=== @143

[.psalm-title]
A Psalm of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Stretch Out My Hands to You

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Hear my prayer, O LORD, give ear to my supplications: in ##thy faithfulness##note:x[<<Ps 31:1; 71:2; Dan 9:16>>] answer me, __and__ in thy righteousness.
@ And enter not into judgment with thy servant: for ##in thy sight##note:x[<<Job 25:4; Eccl 7:20; Rom 3:20>>] shall no man living be justified.
@ For ##the enemy##note:x[<<Ps 7:1–2; 17:9–13>>] hath persecuted my soul; he hath ##smitten##note:x[<<Ps 7:5>>] my life down to the ground; he hath made me to dwell in darkness, as those that have been long dead.
@ Therefore ##is my spirit##note:x[<<Ps 77:3; 142:3>>] overwhelmed within me; my heart within me is desolate.
@ I ##remember##note:x[<<Ps 77:5–6, 10–12; 111:4>>] the days of old; I meditate on all thy works; I muse on the work of thy hands.
@ I ##stretch forth##note:x[<<Job 11:13; Ps 88:9>>] my hands unto thee: ##my soul##note:x[<<Ps 63:1>>] __thirsteth__ after thee, as a thirsty land. Selah.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Hear me speedily, O LORD: my spirit faileth: ##hide not##note:x[<<Ps 27:9; 69:17>>] thy face from me, lest I be like ##unto them##note:x[<<Ps 28:1>>] that go down into the pit.note:tn[__lest…__: or, for I am become like, etc]
@ ##Cause me##note:x[<<Ps 25:4–5; 32:8; Prov 3:5–6>>] to hear thy lovingkindness in the morning; for in thee do I trust: cause me to know the way wherein I should walk; for I lift up my soul unto thee.
@ Deliver me, O LORD, from mine enemies: I ##flee unto thee##note:x[<<Ps 61:3–4; 142:5; Prov 18:10>>] to hide me.note:tn[__flee…__: Heb. hide me with thee]
@ ##Teach##note:x[<<Ps 25:4–5; Heb 13:21>>] me to do thy will; for thou __art__ my God: ##thy spirit##note:x[<<John 14:26>>] __is__ good; lead me into the land of uprightness.
@ ##Quicken##note:x[<<Ps 119:25; Eph 2:4–5>>] me, O LORD, for thy name’s sake: ##for thy righteousness##note:x[<<Ps 31:1>>]’ sake bring my soul out of trouble.
@ And ##of thy mercy##note:x[<<Ps 54:5>>] cut off mine enemies, and destroy all them that afflict my soul: ##for I __am__ thy##note:x[<<Ps 116:16; 119:94>>] servant.

=== @144

[.psalm-title]
__A Psalm__ of David.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Blessed Be the LORD, My Rock

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Blessed __be__ the LORD note:x[<<Ps 18:2>>]my ##strength##note:tn[Heb. rock], which ##teacheth##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:35; Ps 18:34>>] my hands to war, __and__ my fingers to fight&#58;note:tn[__to war…__: Heb. to the war, etc]
@ My goodness, and ##my fortress##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:2–3; Jer 16:19>>]; my high tower, and my deliverer; my shield, and __he__ in whom I trust; ##who subdueth##note:x[<<Ps 18:47>>] my people under me.note:tn[__My goodness__: or, My mercy]
@ LORD, ##what __is__ man##note:x[<<Job 7:17; Ps 8:4; Heb 2:6>>], that thou takest knowledge of him! __or__ the son of man, that thou makest account of him!
@ ##Man##note:x[<<Ps 39:5–6>>] is like to vanity: ##his days##note:x[<<Ps 102:11; 109:23>>] __are__ as a shadow that passeth away.
@ ##Bow##note:x[<<Ps 18:9>>] thy heavens, O LORD, and come down: ##touch##note:x[<<Ps 104:32>>] the mountains, and they shall smoke.
@ ##Cast forth##note:x[<<Ps 18:13–14>>] lightning, and scatter them: ##shoot out##note:x[<<Deut 32:23, 42>>] thine arrows, and destroy them.
@ ##Send##note:x[<<Ps 18:16>>] thine ##hand from##note:tn[Heb. hands from] above; rid me, and ##deliver me##note:x[<<Ps 69:1–2; Rev 17:15>>] out of great waters, from the hand of strange children;
@ Whose ##mouth##note:x[<<Ps 12:2; 41:6; 62:4>>] speaketh vanity, and their right hand __is__ a right hand of falsehood.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will ##sing a new##note:x[<<Ps 33:2–3; 40:3; 149:1>>] song unto thee, O God: upon a psaltery __and__ an instrument of ten strings will I sing praises unto thee.
@ __It is he__ ##that giveth##note:x[<<Ps 18:50; 33:16–18>>] ##salvation##note:tn[or, victory] unto kings: ##who delivereth##note:x[<<Ps 140:7>>] David his servant from the hurtful sword.
@ Rid me, ##and deliver me##note:x[<<Ps 144:7–8>>] from the hand of strange children, whose mouth speaketh vanity, and their right hand __is__ a right hand of falsehood:
@ That our sons __may be__ ##as plants##note:x[<<Ps 128:3; Isa 44:3–5>>] grown up in their youth; __that__ our daughters __may be__ as corner stones, ##polished##note:tn[Heb. cut] __after__ ##the similitude##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:3–6>>] of a palace:
@ __That__ ##our garners##note:x[<<Lev 26:10; Deut 28:8>>] __may be__ full, affording all manner of store: __that__ ##our sheep##note:x[<<Deut 7:13–14>>] may bring forth thousands and ten thousands in our streets&#58;note:tn[__all…__: Heb. from kind to kind]
@ __That__ our oxen __may be__ strong to labour; __that there be__ ##no breaking in##note:x[<<Zech 8:3–5>>], nor going out; that __there be__ no complaining in our streets.note:tn[__strong…__: Heb. able to bear burdens, or, loaden with flesh]
@ Happy __is that__ people, that is in such a case: ##__yea__, happy##note:x[<<Ps 33:12; 146:5; Eph 1:3>>] __is that__ people, whose God __is__ the LORD.

=== @145

[.psalm-title]
David’s __Psalm__ of praise.

[discrete.pericope]
==== I Will Exalt You, My God and King

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ I will extol thee, ##my God##note:x[<<Ps 45:6; 47:6–8; Matt 25:34>>], O king; and I will bless thy name for ever and ever.
@ ##Every day##note:x[<<Rev 7:15>>] will I bless thee; and I will praise thy name for ever and ever.
@ ##Great##note:x[<<Job 5:9; Ps 147:5>>] __is__ the LORD, and greatly to be praised; ##and his greatness __is__ unsearchable##note:x[<<Rom 11:33>>].note:tn[__and his…__: Heb. and of his greatness there is no search]
@ One ##generation##note:x[<<Deut 6:7; Ps 71:18; 78:3–7>>] shall praise thy works to another, and shall declare thy mighty acts.
@ I ##will speak##note:x[<<Isa 12:4>>] of the glorious honour of thy majesty, and of thy wondrous note:x[<<Ps 72:18>>]##works##note:tn[Heb. things, or, words].
@ ##And __men__##note:x[<<Dan 3:28–29; Hab 2:14>>] shall speak of the might of thy terrible acts: and note:x[<<Ps 107:31–32>>]I will ##declare##note:tn[Heb. declare it] thy greatness.
@ They shall ##abundantly##note:x[<<Isa 63:7; 2 Cor 9:11–12>>] utter the memory of thy great goodness, and shall ##sing##note:x[<<Ps 36:10>>] of thy righteousness.
@ The ##LORD __is__ gracious##note:x[<<Num 14:18; Ps 86:5; 103:8>>], and full of compassion; slow to anger, and of great mercy.note:tn[__of great…__: Heb. great in mercy]
@ The LORD __is__ ##good##note:x[<<Jonah 4:11; Nah 1:7; Matt 5:45>>] to all: and his tender mercies __are__ over all his works.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##All thy##note:x[<<Ps 19:1; 103:22>>] works shall praise thee, O LORD; and thy saints shall bless thee.
@ They shall speak of ##the glory##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:11–12; Ps 45:6–7; 93:1–2>>] of thy kingdom, and talk of thy power;
@ To ##make known##note:x[<<Rev 12:10>>] to the sons of men his mighty acts, and the glorious majesty of his kingdom.
@ Thy ##kingdom##note:x[<<Isa 9:7; Dan 7:14; 1 Tim 1:17>>] __is__ an everlasting kingdom, and thy dominion __endureth__ throughout all generations.note:tn[__an…__: Heb. a kingdom of all ages]
@ The LORD ##upholdeth##note:x[<<Ps 37:24; 94:18>>] all that fall, and ##raiseth up##note:x[<<Ps 146:8>>] all __those that be__ bowed down.
@ ##The eyes##note:x[<<Ps 104:27; 136:25; Acts 17:25>>] of all wait upon thee; and thou givest them their meat in due season.note:tn[__wait…__: or, look unto]
@ Thou ##openest##note:x[<<Ps 104:28; 107:9; 132:15>>] thine hand, and satisfiest the desire of every living thing.
@ The LORD __is__ ##righteous##note:x[<<Deut 32:4; Ps 103:6; Zeph 3:5>>] in all his ways, and ##holy##note:tn[or, merciful, or, bountiful] in all his works.
@ The LORD __is__ ##nigh unto##note:x[<<Deut 4:7; Ps 34:18>>] all them that ##call upon##note:x[<<Jer 29:12–13>>] him, to all that call upon him in truth.
@ He will ##fulfil##note:x[<<Ps 37:4; John 15:7; Eph 3:16–20>>] the desire of them that fear him: he also will hear their cry, and will save them.
@ The LORD ##preserveth##note:x[<<Exod 20:6; John 10:27–29; Rom 8:28–30>>] all them that love him: but all the wicked will he destroy.
@ ##My mouth##note:x[<<Ps 51:15; 71:8>>] shall speak the praise of the LORD: and ##let all flesh##note:x[<<Ps 150:6>>] bless his holy name for ever and ever.

=== @146

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise the LORD, O My Soul

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise ye the LORD. ##Praise the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 103:1, 22; 104:35>>], O my soul.note:tn[__Praise ye…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ ##While I live##note:x[<<Ps 63:4; 104:33; 145:1–2>>] will I praise the LORD: I will sing praises unto my God while I have any being.
@ ##Put##note:x[<<Ps 118:8–9; Isa 2:22; Jer 17:5–6>>] not your trust in princes, __nor__ in the son of man, in whom __there is__ no ##help##note:tn[or, salvation].
@ ##His breath##note:x[<<Gen 2:7; Ps 104:29>>] goeth forth, ##he returneth##note:x[<<Eccl 12:7>>] to his earth; in that very day his thoughts perish.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Happy##note:x[<<Ps 144:15>>] __is he__ that __hath__ the God of Jacob for his help, ##whose##note:x[<<Ps 71:5; Jer 17:7–8>>] hope __is__ in the LORD his God:
@ Which ##made heaven##note:x[<<Ps 136:5–6; Rev 14:7>>], and earth, ##the sea##note:x[<<Exod 20:11>>], and all that therein __is:__ which keepeth truth for ever:
@ Which ##executeth##note:x[<<Ps 103:6>>] judgment for the oppressed: which giveth food to the hungry. The LORD ##looseth##note:x[<<Ps 68:6; Isa 61:1>>] the prisoners:
@ The LORD ##openeth##note:x[<<Matt 9:30>>] __the eyes of__ the blind: the LORD ##raiseth##note:x[<<Ps 145:14>>] them that are bowed down: the LORD ##loveth##note:x[<<Ps 11:7>>] the righteous:
@ The LORD ##preserveth##note:x[<<Deut 10:18–19; Ps 68:5>>] the strangers; he relieveth the fatherless and widow: but ##the way##note:x[<<Ps 147:6>>] of the wicked he turneth upside down.
@ The LORD shall ##reign##note:x[<<Exod 15:18; Ps 10:16; Rev 11:15>>] for ever, __even__ thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Praise ye the LORD.

=== @147

[discrete.pericope]
==== It Is Good to Sing Praises

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise ye the LORD: ##for __it is__ good##note:x[<<Ps 135:3>>] to sing praises unto our God; for __it is__ pleasant; __and__ praise is comely.
@ The LORD doth ##build##note:x[<<Ps 51:18>>] up Jerusalem: ##he##note:x[<<Deut 30:3; Isa 56:8>>] gathereth together the outcasts of Israel.
@ He ##healeth##note:x[<<Job 5:18; Isa 61:1; Jer 33:6>>] the broken in heart, and bindeth up their ##wounds##note:tn[Heb. griefs].
@ ##He##note:x[<<Ps 8:3; 148:3; Isa 40:26>>] telleth the number of the stars; he calleth them all by __their__ names.
@ ##Great##note:x[<<Ps 96:4>>] __is__ our Lord, and of great power: ##his understanding __is__ infinite##note:x[<<Ps 139:17–18; Isa 40:28>>].note:tn[__his…__: Heb. of his understanding there is no number]
@ The LORD ##lifteth up##note:x[<<Ps 25:9; 146:8–9; 1 Pet 5:6>>] the meek: he casteth the wicked down to the ground.
@ ##Sing unto the LORD with thanksgiving; sing praise upon the harp unto our God:##note:x[<<Ps 47:6–7; 68:32; 95:1–2>>]
@ Who covereth the heaven with clouds, who ##prepareth##note:x[<<Job 5:10; Ps 65:9–13>>] rain for the earth, who maketh grass to grow upon the mountains.
@ ##He giveth to the beast his food, __and__ to the young ravens which cry.##note:x[<<Job 38:41; Ps 104:27–28; Matt 6:26>>]
@ He ##delighteth##note:x[<<Ps 20:7; 33:16–18>>] not in the strength of the horse: ##he taketh##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:7>>] not pleasure in the legs of a man.
@ The LORD ##taketh##note:x[<<Zeph 3:17; Mal 3:16–17>>] pleasure in them that ##fear##note:x[<<Ps 33:18>>] him, in those that hope in his mercy.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise the LORD, O Jerusalem; ##praise thy God##note:x[<<Ps 135:19–21; Isa 12:6; Joel 2:23>>], O Zion.
@ For he hath strengthened the bars of thy gates; he hath ##blessed##note:x[<<Ps 115:14–15; 128:3–6; Isa 44:3–5>>] thy children within thee.
@ ##He maketh peace##note:x[<<Ps 29:11; Isa 60:17–18>>] __in__ thy borders, __and__ filleth thee with the ##finest of the wheat##note:x[<<Ps 81:16>>].note:tn[__He…__: Heb. Who maketh thy border peace]note:tn[__finest…__: Heb. fat of wheat]
@ He ##sendeth##note:x[<<Job 37:12; Ps 33:9; 107:20>>] forth his commandment __upon__ earth: his word runneth very swiftly.
@ He ##giveth##note:x[<<Job 37:6; Ps 148:8>>] snow like wool: he ##scattereth##note:x[<<Job 38:29>>] the hoarfrost like ashes.
@ He ##casteth##note:x[<<Job 38:22–23; Ps 78:47–48>>] forth his ice like morsels: ##who can stand##note:x[<<Job 38:29–30>>] before his cold?
@ ##He sendeth out his word, and melteth them: he causeth his wind to blow, __and__ the waters flow.##note:x[<<Job 6:16–17; 37:10; Ps 147:15>>]
@ He ##sheweth##note:x[<<Ps 78:5; Mal 4:4>>] his word unto Jacob, ##his statutes##note:x[<<Mal 4:4>>] and his judgments unto Israel.note:tn[__his word__: Heb. his words]
@ He hath ##not dealt so##note:x[<<Deut 4:32–34; Rom 3:1–2; Eph 2:12>>] with any nation: and __as for his__ judgments, they have not known them. Praise ye the LORD.

=== @148

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise the LORD from the Heavens

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 33:1–22>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Praise ye the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 89:5; Luke 2:13–14; Rev 19:1–6>>]. Praise ye the LORD from the heavens: praise him in the heights.note:tn[__Praise ye the LORD. Praise__: Heb. Hallelujah, etc]
@ Praise ye him, ##all his angels##note:x[<<Ps 103:20–21; Isa 6:2–4>>]: praise ye him, all his hosts.
@ Praise ye him, ##sun##note:x[<<Ps 8:1–3; 19:1–6; 136:7–9>>] and moon: praise him, all ye stars of light.
@ Praise him, ye ##heavens##note:x[<<1 Kgs 8:27>>] of heavens, and ye ##waters##note:x[<<Gen 1:7>>] that __be__ above the heavens.
@ Let them praise the name of the LORD: ##for he##note:x[<<Gen 1:6; Ps 33:6–9; Rev 4:11>>] commanded, and they were created.
@ ##He hath also##note:x[<<Ps 89:37; Jer 31:35–36; 33:25>>] stablished them for ever and ever: he hath made a decree which shall not pass.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ Praise the LORD from the earth, ##ye dragons##note:x[<<Gen 1:21; Ps 74:13–14; 104:25–26>>], and all deeps:
@ ##Fire##note:x[<<Ps 147:15–18>>], and hail; snow, and vapour; stormy wind fulfilling his word:
@ ##Mountains##note:x[<<Isa 44:23; 49:13; 55:12–13>>], and all hills; fruitful trees, and all cedars:
@ ##Beasts##note:x[<<Gen 1:20–25; Ps 103:22; 150:6>>], and all cattle; creeping things, and flying fowl&#58;note:tn[__flying…__: Heb. birds of wing]
@ ##Kings##note:x[<<Ps 102:15; Isa 60:3; Rev 21:24>>] of the earth, and all people; princes, and all judges of the earth:
@ Both ##young men##note:x[<<Jer 31:13; Titus 2:4–6>>], and maidens; old men, and children:
@ Let them praise the name of the LORD: ##for his name##note:x[<<Ps 8:1, 9>>] alone is ##excellent##note:tn[Heb. exalted]; his ##glory##note:x[<<Ps 113:4>>] __is__ above the earth and heaven.
@ He also ##exalteth##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:1; Ps 75:10>>] the horn of his people, the praise of all his saints; __even__ of the children of Israel, ##a people##note:x[<<Deut 4:7>>] near unto him. Praise ye the LORD.

=== @149

[discrete.pericope]
==== Sing to the LORD a New Song

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 98:1–9; Isaiah 42:10–17>>)

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Praise ye the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 148:1>>]. ##Sing##note:x[<<Ps 33:3; Rev 5:9>>] unto the LORD a new song, __and__ his praise in the congregation of saints.note:tn[__Praise…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ Let Israel ##rejoice##note:x[<<Ps 100:1–3; Isa 54:5>>] in him that made him: ##let the##note:x[<<Isa 52:7>>] children of Zion be joyful in their King.note:tn[__in him…__: Heb. in his Makers]
@ Let them praise his name ##in the dance##note:x[<<Exod 15:20; 2 Sam 6:16; Jer 31:13>>]: let them sing praises unto him with the timbrel and harp.note:tn[__in…__: or, with the pipe]
@ For the LORD ##taketh pleasure##note:x[<<Ps 35:27; 147:11>>] in his people: he will ##beautify##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:4>>] the meek with salvation.
@ Let ##the saints##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:8>>] be joyful in glory: let them ##sing##note:x[<<Job 35:10; Ps 42:8>>] aloud upon their beds.

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ note:x[<<Heb 4:12; Rev 1:16>>]__Let__ ##the high##note:x[<<Rev 19:6>>] __praises__ of God __be__ in their ##mouth##note:tn[Heb. throat], and a twoedged sword in their hand;
@ ##To execute vengeance upon the heathen, __and__ punishments upon the people;##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:2–3; Zech 14:17–19; Rev 19:11–21>>]
@ ##To bind their kings with chains, and their nobles with fetters of iron;##note:x[<<Josh 10:23–24; 12:7; Judg 1:6–7>>]
@ To execute upon them the judgment written: ##this honour##note:x[<<Ps 148:14; 1 Cor 6:2–3>>] have all his saints. Praise ye the LORD.

=== @150

[discrete.pericope]
==== Let Everything That Has Breath Praise the LORD

[.poetry%hardbreaks]
@ ##Praise ye the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 149:1>>]. Praise God ##in his sanctuary##note:x[<<Ps 116:18–19; 134:2>>]: praise him in the firmament of his power.note:tn[__Praise ye…__: Heb. Hallelujah]
@ Praise him for his mighty acts: praise him ##according##note:x[<<Deut 3:24; Ps 145:3; Jer 32:17–19>>] to his excellent greatness.
@ Praise him ##with the sound##note:x[<<Ps 81:2–3>>] of the ##trumpet##note:tn[or, cornet]: praise him with ##the psaltery##note:x[<<Ps 33:2; 149:3>>] and harp.
@ Praise him ##with the timbrel##note:x[<<Exod 15:20>>] and note:x[<<Ps 149:3>>]##dance##note:tn[or, pipe]: praise him with ##stringed##note:x[<<Isa 38:20>>] instruments and organs.
@ Praise him upon ##the loud cymbals##note:x[<<1 Chr 15:16, 19; 25:1>>]: praise him upon the high sounding cymbals.
@ ##Let every thing##note:x[<<Ps 103:22; 145:10; Rev 5:13>>] that hath breath praise the LORD. Praise ye the LORD.
